Start Here

Orca 是什么？

What is Orca?

一分钟看懂：Orca 给谁用，什么时候该打开它。

Orca 是一个桌面 IDE，用来把多个 AI 编程 Agent 并排跑起来。每项任务有自己的工作树、自己的 Agent 终端、自己的浏览器标签。活可以同时分给 Claude Code、Codex、Cursor CLI 以及同类工具。你不用先 git stash，也不用在分支之间来回倒手，思路不会断。

什么时候用 Orca

  • 想让三个 Agent 同时修同一个 bug，再挑出更好的那个。
  • 想在发出去之前，认真看一遍 AI 写出的 diff。
  • 已经在为 Claude Code、Codex 或 Cursor CLI 付费，希望有一个地方把它们调度在一起。
  • 想让 Agent 在远程跑：走 SSH、跑在远程 Orca 服务器上，或按需起一台虚拟机。IDE 仍留在你自己的机器上。
  • 想要一名有名字、有记忆、能跑自动化的 AI 队友，别人可以从 Slack、GitHub 或 Linear 联系到它。见 Bots。

给谁用

Orca 是给已经靠写代码吃饭的人用的。你仍然是写代码的那个人，AI 只是助力。它默认你会读 diff、在意提交，也会把工作树保持干净。如果你要的是无代码工具，Orca 不是这类工具。

Orca 不做什么

  • 不是模型。 它跑的是你已经在用的 Agent。继续用你已经有的 Claude、Codex 或 OpenCode 订阅。
  • 不是 git 的替代品。 每个工作树都是一份真正的 Git 工作树。你可以 cd 进去，随时用普通 git。
  • 不是托管的 VPS 产品。 默认跑在你的桌面上。远程计算用的是你自己掌控的机器和云账号：SSH 目标、远程 Orca 服务器，或云虚拟机 / 按工作区准备的环境。

下一步

先看安装，再走一遍第一次跑三个 Agent。后者是这套文档里最重要的一页。准备把 Agent 挪出笔记本时，从 Orca 的几种运行方式 开始。

下一页：安装

A 60-second pitch: who Orca is for and when to reach for it.

Orca is a desktop IDE for running multiple AI coding agents side by side. Every task gets its own git worktree, its own agent terminal, and its own browser tab — so you can fan out work across Claude Code, Codex, Cursor CLI, and friends without stashing, branch-juggling, or losing flow.

When to use Orca

  • You want three agents trying the same bug in parallel and to pick the winner.
  • You want to review AI-generated diffs seriously before you ship them.
  • You already pay for Claude Code, Codex, or Cursor CLI and want one place to orchestrate them.
  • You want agents to run remotely — over SSH, on a self-hosted Orca server, or in an on-demand VM — without giving up your IDE.
  • You want an AI teammate with its own name, memory, and automations that people can reach from Slack, GitHub, or Linear. See Bots.

Who it's for

Orca is designed for people who already write code for a living and want to use AI as leverage — not as a replacement. It assumes you read diffs, care about commits, and keep a worktree tidy. If you're looking for a no-code tool, Orca is not that.

What Orca is not

  • Not a model. Orca runs agents you already use — bring your own Claude, Codex, or OpenCode subscription.
  • Not a git replacement. Every worktree is a real git worktree. You can cd in and use plain git whenever you want.
  • Not a hosted VPS product. Orca runs on your desktop by default. Remote compute uses machines and cloud accounts you control — SSH targets, self-hosted Orca servers, or Cloud VMs / per-workspace environments.
Next steps

Head to Install, then walk through Your first 3-agent session — the single most important page in these docs. When you're ready to move agents off the laptop, start with Ways to run Orca.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs

下一章Install

Start Here

安装

Install

下载 macOS、Windows 或 Linux 版，以及怎么拿到 RC 构建。

下载

Orca 是桌面应用。按系统直接下载：

  • macOS： Apple Silicon · Intel
  • Windows： 安装包
  • Linux： AppImage · .deb · .rpm
  • 更早的版本：GitHub Releases。

窄屏上不显示上面的直链。把下载链接发到你的邮箱，再到 macOS、Windows 或 Linux 上打开。输入框占位是 Your email，按钮是 Send download link。

Homebrew（macOS）

Orca 也以 Homebrew cask 发布。每个稳定版出来，这个 cask 会自动跟上：

brew install --cask stablyai/orca/orca

brew upgrade --cask orca 会装上新的稳定版。这个 cask 只跟踪稳定通道。要 RC 构建，用上面的 GitHub Releases 链接，或走应用内的 Check for Updates（检查更新）。做法写在更新一节。

第一次打开

第一次启动时，Orca 会：

  • 向你要主目录的访问权限，这样它才能添加仓库。
  • 如果这些东西已经存在，提议导入 ~/.claude、~/.codex，以及 Ghostty 的终端设置。
  • 停在一块空的起始页上，让你添加第一个仓库。

更新

Orca 在 stable（稳定）通道上检查更新。想让它自己下载、安装并重新打开，到 Settings → General → About 打开 Automatic updates（自动更新）。这项默认是关的。

自动安装会按你设置的检查频率、更新通道和延迟来做。下面几种情况它会先等，不立刻装：

  • 安装会关掉一个正在运行、或正在启动的应用内终端
  • WSL 迁移还在进行
  • 窗口状态存不下来

稳定版是确认过才发的。RC（release candidate，候选发布版）会先带上新功能，常常一天一版。

应用里不能把更新通道永久切到 RC。要对 Check for Updates 按住修饰键再点。入口在 Settings → General → Updates，或应用菜单 / Help 菜单：

修饰键 效果
Shift+click 把最新的 RC 预发布版也纳入检查
Cmd+click（macOS）/ Ctrl+click（Windows / Linux） 最新带 perf 标签的预发布版
Option+click（仅 macOS） 选一个已通过 Orca 兼容性检查的本地 macOS 构建

任何构建也都可以从 GitHub Releases 直接下载。

对这次更新不满意

旧版本一直放在 GitHub Releases。退回旧版时，Orca 不会强行把你的工作树数据降级。

各系统注意点

macOS

需要 macOS 13.4 或更高版本。更老的 Mac 会停在应用内更新器所能给到的最后一个兼容版本。macOS 13.0–13.3 可以先升级系统，再升级 Orca。安装包已签名，并经过 Apple 公证。第一次打开时，系统仍可能让你确认一次。基于 Electron 的应用会出现这种情况。

Windows

默认 shell 可以在 Settings → Terminal 里选 PowerShell 或 CMD。多数人会选 PowerShell。随应用附带的 orca 命令是原生程序，不需要 Visual C++ Redistributable。

Linux

希望 Orca 自己更新，就选 AppImage。.deb 和 .rpm 会告诉你有新版本，并给出对应包管理器的安装命令。运行那条命令之前先退出 Orca。Linux 上的命令行名字是 orca-ide，免得和 GNOME 的 Orca 屏幕阅读器撞名。

上一页：Orca 是什么？
下一页：第一次跑三个 Agent

Download Orca for macOS, Windows, or Linux, and opt into RC builds.

Download

  • macOS: Apple Silicon · Intel

  • Windows: installer

  • Linux: AppImage · .deb · .rpm

  • Older versions: GitHub Releases.

Homebrew (macOS)

Orca is also published as a Homebrew cask, auto-bumped on every stable release:

brew install --cask stablyai/orca/orca

brew upgrade --cask orca picks up new stable builds. The cask tracks the stable channel — for RC builds, use the GitHub Releases links above or the in-app Check for Updates flow described under Updates.

First launch

On first launch Orca will:

  • Ask for access to your home directory so it can add repos.
  • Offer to import ~/.claude, ~/.codex, and Ghostty terminal settings if present.
  • Drop you on an empty landing screen where you add your first repo.

Updates

Orca checks for updates on the stable channel. To download, install, and relaunch automatically, enable Automatic updates under Settings → General → About; it is off by default. Automatic installation follows your check cadence, channel, and update delay. Orca waits if installation would terminate a live or launching in-process terminal, a WSL move is active, or window state cannot be saved.

Stable releases are vetted; RC (release candidate) builds ship new features first, often daily.

There is no permanent in-app opt-in for the RC channel. Modifier clicks on Check for Updates (Settings → General → Updates, or the app / Help menu):

ModifierEffect
Shift+clickInclude the latest RC prerelease
Cmd+click (macOS) / Ctrl+click (Windows/Linux)Latest perf-tagged prerelease
Option+click (macOS only)Pick a validated local macOS build that passes Orca’s compatibility checks

You can still download any build directly from the GitHub Releases page.

Don't like the current update

Older versions are always available on the GitHub Releases page. Orca will not force-downgrade your worktree data if you go back.

Platform notes

macOS

Orca requires macOS 13.4 or later. Older Macs remain on their last compatible version through the in-app updater; macOS 13.0–13.3 users can update macOS before upgrading Orca. Orca is signed and notarized. On first launch, macOS may still ask you to confirm — that's normal for Electron-based apps.

Windows

The default shell can be set to PowerShell or CMD under Settings → Terminal. Most users want PowerShell. The bundled orca command is a native program, so it runs without the Visual C++ Redistributable.

Linux

Choose the AppImage if you want Orca to update itself. The .deb and .rpm packages report available updates and give you the package-manager command to install them; quit Orca before running that command. The Linux CLI is named orca-ide so it does not conflict with the GNOME Orca screen reader.

上一章What is Orca? 下一章Your first 3-agent session

Start Here

第一次跑三个 Agent

Your first 3-agent session

从一块空的应用开始，五分钟内让三个 Agent 并行跑起来。

这是全套文档里最重要的一页。走到最后，你会有三个 Agent 并行处理同一件事，各自走一种做法，并且已经发出一个 PR。

1. 添加仓库

点侧栏上的 Add Repo，选一个本机上已经检出的仓库。Orca 会读这个仓库的 git 状态，把默认分支记成 base ref（基准引用）。之后每个新工作树都从这条基准引用分出去。

以后可以在这个仓库自己的设置里改基准引用。

2. 新建工作树

点仓库名旁边的 +。输入任务名。fix-login-race 这样即可。留空的话，Orca 会用一种海洋生物的名字来命名。

启动器打开时，会预先选中你的 default agent（默认 Agent）。默认项在 Settings → Agents 里改。你也可以开一个空终端，先不跑 Agent。固定在侧栏上的工作树会更显眼，你真正常用的那些就一直看得到。

再选一条起始引用（start-from），也就是这条分支从哪条引用拉出来。多数时候就是你的基准引用（origin/main）。从任意分支或某一次提交开始也可以。

Orca 会在它管理的目录里建一棵真正的工作树，检出分支，然后打开。

3. 选一个 Agent

在工作区启动器里选，或先开终端，再用里面的 agent combobox（Agent 下拉框）。可以选 Claude Code、Codex、Cursor CLI，或任何受支持的 Agent。Orca 会在这个工作树里把它启动起来。受支持的 Agent 可以用对话界面（Chat UI）：初始设置时选 Chat，或之后在 Settings 里打开 Chat。

4. 让三个 Agent 做同一件事

把第 2、3 步再做两遍。现在有三个工作树：

  • fix-login-race → Claude Code
  • fix-login-race-2 → Codex
  • fix-login-race-3 → Cursor CLI

把同一段提示词分别贴进去。三条分支，三份 diff，同一段提示词。让它们各自做。

5. 分屏，三个一起看

把某个工作树的标签拖到窗格的右缘或下缘，窗格会分裂。三个 Agent 可以同时看着。详见标签、窗格与分屏布局。

6. 选一个，看 diff，提交并推送

Agent 停下来之后，打开每个工作树的 diff 查看器。用 批注 AI diff 留下行内评论，再发回给离目标最近的那个 Agent。

提交和推送可以直接在 Orca 里做，见 在 Orca 里提交并推送。另外两个工作树可以一键删掉，对应分支会一起删掉。

就这些

这条链路是 add → worktree → agent → split → diff → ship：添加仓库，建工作树，选 Agent，分屏，看 diff，提交并推送。Orca 的用法就是这一条。文档里的其他页面，都是把其中某一步讲透。

上一页：安装
下一页：从 Conductor 迁移

From empty app to three agents running in parallel in under five minutes.

This is the single most important page in the docs. By the end you'll have three agents running in parallel on three different approaches to the same task, with one PR shipped.

1. Add a repository

Click Add Repo on the sidebar and point Orca at a local checkout. Orca reads the repo's git state and picks up your default branch as its base ref — the ref every new worktree branches from.

You can change the base ref later under the repo's settings.

2. Create a worktree

Click the + next to the repo name. Type a task name ("fix-login-race" is fine — if you leave it blank, Orca names it after a marine creature).

The launcher opens with your default agent preselected (configurable under Settings → Agents) or an empty terminal if you'd rather start cold. Pinned worktrees stand out in the sidebar so the ones you actually live in stay visible.

Pick a start-from ref. Most of the time this is your base ref (origin/main), but you can start from any branch or commit.

Orca creates a real git worktree under its managed directory, checks out the branch, and opens it.

3. Pick an agent

Choose an agent in the workspace launcher, or open a terminal and use its agent combobox. Pick Claude Code, Codex, Cursor CLI, or any of the supported agents. Orca launches the agent in the worktree; supported agents can use Chat UI when you choose Chat during setup or in Settings.

4. Race three agents on the same task

Now repeat steps 2–3 twice more. You now have three worktrees:

  • fix-login-race → Claude Code
  • fix-login-race-2 → Codex
  • fix-login-race-3 → Cursor CLI

Paste the same prompt into each. Three branches. Three diffs. Same prompt. Let them work.

5. Split panes so you can watch them all

Drag a worktree's tab to the right or bottom edge of a pane to split. You can watch all three agents at once. See Tabs, panes & split layouts.

6. Pick a winner, review the diff, ship it

Once agents settle, open each worktree's diff view. Use Annotate AI Diff to leave inline comments and send them back to whichever agent got closest.

Commit and push directly from Orca — see Commit & push from Orca. The other two worktrees can be deleted with one click; their branches go with them.

That's it

This flow — add → worktree → agent → split → diff → ship — is the whole of Orca. Every other page in these docs is a deeper look at one of those steps.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/first-session

上一章Install 下一章Migrate from Conductor

Start Here

从 Conductor 迁过来

Migrate from Conductor

在 Orca 里打开现有的 Conductor 工作树，把安装脚本迁过来，并接上原来的 Agent 会话。用过 Conductor Cloud 的，要在 Cloud 关闭前先把那边的工作存下来。

Conductor 和 Orca 是同一套做法：每项任务有自己的分支，分支上有一份 Git 工作树，里面跑着 Agent。Conductor 的工作区就是普通的 Git 工作树，Orca 就地打开。文件不复制、不搬走，未提交的改动留在原地。

时间：Conductor Cloud 在 2026 年 11 月 20 日关闭。Conductor 桌面应用支持到 2027 年初。

用过 Conductor Cloud？

云端工作区不在你的 Mac 上，Orca 现在还看不到。先做完文末的迁走 Conductor Cloud 上的工作，再做下面的步骤。Conductor Cloud 从 2026 年 11 月 13 日起只读，2026 年 11 月 20 日开始删除云端数据。

对照

Conductor Orca
Workspace 工作树：自己的分支、文件和 Agent 终端
~/conductor/workspaces/<repo>/<name> 原位置显示在 Orca 里（默认隐藏）。新建的 Orca 工作区放在 Orca 的工作区文件夹
conductor.json 里的 setup 和 archive 脚本 orca.yaml 里的 scripts.setup 和 scripts.archive
Run 按钮和动作 快速命令：点一次，打开一个终端标签并运行
CONDUCTOR_ROOT_PATH ORCA_ROOT_PATH。setup 和 archive 脚本里，Orca 仍会设置 CONDUCTOR_ROOT_PATH
CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_PATH ORCA_WORKTREE_PATH
CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_NAME ORCA_WORKSPACE_NAME
CONDUCTOR_PORT 没有。Orca 不按工作树分配端口
Archive workspace 删除这棵工作树
diff、PR 和检查 diff 查看器、提交与推送、GitHub
Chat 对话界面。在 Settings → Agents → Open agents as → Chat 打开
Conductor Cloud 你自己掌控的机器上的远程 Orca 服务器，或云虚拟机
iOS 应用 iOS 和 Android 上的 Orca 手机端

1. 添加仓库

先安装 Orca。然后点侧栏顶部的 Add project（添加项目），选 Conductor 用过的同一份检出。不要新克隆一份。Conductor 的工作区是那份检出的工作树，Orca 靠它才能找到。

不确定是哪一份检出时，在任意一个 Conductor 工作区里运行：

git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir

输出是一个以 .git 结尾的路径。包含它的那个文件夹，就是要添加的检出。

2. 显示 Conductor 工作树

Orca 默认隐藏不是它自己创建的工作树。要自己打开显示。

  1. 打开侧栏里该项目的操作菜单，选 Show hidden worktrees（显示隐藏的工作树）。它们已经默认显示时，这一项写成 Hide non-Orca worktrees（隐藏非 Orca 工作树）。
  2. 在 Non-Orca worktrees（非 Orca 工作树）里，给每个仍显示为隐藏的 Conductor 工作区点 Show（显示）。它们通常在 ~/conductor/workspaces/ 下。较旧的 Conductor 会放在仓库里的 .conductor/ 文件夹。

每一棵都保留原来的路径、分支和未提交改动。Orca 不会对它们再跑一遍 setup，它们已经配好了。

列表里缺了某个工作区时，在第 1 步的检出里运行 git worktree list，确认它属于这个仓库。

不要在 Conductor 里归档这些工作区

在 Conductor 里归档会删掉文件夹。这个文件夹现在也是你的 Orca 工作树。退出 Conductor 即可。

3. 迁走脚本

交给 Agent

在这个仓库的一棵工作树里，把下面这段交给 Agent：

Convert this repo's Conductor config (conductor.json or .conductor/settings.toml) into orca.yaml,
following https://orca.dev/docs/migrate-from-conductor. Map CONDUCTOR_* variables to ORCA_*,
drop CONDUCTOR_PORT, and list the run actions so I can save them as Quick Commands.

要自己改：setup 和 archive 脚本选下面两种之一（两种都做了，看后面的说明），再把 run 动作存成快速命令。

做法 A：保存检测到的命令（只对你）

项目有 conductor.json、还没有 Orca 的 setup 时，侧栏会出现 Add a setup script（添加安装脚本）卡片，上面写着 Found a setup command in conductor.json。点 Save local setup（保存本地安装脚本）。Orca 把 setup 和 archive 存成 Settings → Repository 里的本地命令。之后这个项目每棵新工作树都会跑 setup，删除前会跑 archive。run 脚本不会存进来，要另存成快速命令。.conductor/settings.toml 不会被读取，里面的命令要手抄。

做法 B：提交 orca.yaml（给整个团队）

把脚本放进仓库根目录的 orca.yaml：

scripts:
  setup: |
    bun install
    cp "$ORCA_ROOT_PATH/.env" .env
  archive: |
    docker compose down
  • setup 在 Orca 创建新工作树之后立刻运行。
  • archive 在 Orca 删除工作树之前运行。

把 orca.yaml 合并进默认分支，再拉进第 1 步的那份检出。新工作树从它分出的那条分支读取 setup。删除时从那份检出读取 archive。完整生命周期见工作树。

两种都做了？

如果先保存了卡片、后才加上 orca.yaml，项目的 Command source & orca.yaml（命令来源与 orca.yaml）会是 Local only（仅本地）。这个值会忽略 orca.yaml 里的脚本。到 Settings → Repository 把它改成 orca.yaml only（仅 orca.yaml）。这里不要用 Run both（两个都跑）：本地命令和 orca.yaml 会接连各跑一遍，迁过来的同一条命令会执行两次。

Run 按钮和动作：快速命令

Conductor 的 Run 按钮和具名动作，变成快速命令。每条存一次：一个标签，加上命令，例如 bun dev 或 bun test。入口是 Settings → Quick Commands 里的 Add Command（添加命令），或工作树标签栏上快速命令菜单里的 + Command（+ 命令）。范围选 Project（本项目），就只在这个仓库的工作树里出现。选 Global（全局），每个仓库都有。

在任意工作树的标签栏点快速命令按钮即可运行。Orca 会在这棵工作树里新开一个终端标签并执行。右键一个终端，可以把命令插入进去，而不是新开标签。同一份列表也在 Orca 手机端，在远程 Orca 服务器上的工作树里同样可用。

变量和端口

脚本如果读 CONDUCTOR_ROOT_PATH，可以继续用。Orca 把它设成和 ORCA_ROOT_PATH 相同的路径。把 CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_PATH 换成 ORCA_WORKTREE_PATH（或者直接用当前目录）。把 CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_NAME 换成 ORCA_WORKSPACE_NAME。

Orca 不会给每棵工作树预留端口，去掉 --port $CONDUCTOR_PORT。多数开发服务器会自己挑一个空闲端口。

在 setup 里复制 .env 仍然有效。也可以把被 Git 忽略的文件列进 .worktreeinclude，Orca 会把它们复制进每棵新工作树。见共享目录与被 Git 忽略的文件。

4. 接上原来的 Agent

Claude Code、Codex、Cursor CLI，以及其他支持的 Agent，继续用你已经有的订阅和密钥。

把对话界面调成 Conductor 那种聊天

Orca 默认在各自的终端 CLI 里打开 Agent。想要接近 Conductor 的聊天，带富文本、画面和图片，打开 Settings → Agents，在 Open agents as（打开 Agent 的方式）里选 Chat（对话）。之后支持的 Agent（包括 Claude Code 和 Codex）的新会话会以对话打开。如果有的 Agent 仍停在终端，是因为你以前给它选过 CLI。在确认里点 Set all to Chat（全部改为对话）。这一页的 Installed（已安装）列表里，仍可以给每个 Agent 单独选 Chat 或 CLI。见对话界面。

继续一段 Conductor 对话

选中工作树，打开右侧栏的 Agents 标签（Agent 会话历史），找到会话，点 Resume（继续）。Orca 会列出 Agent CLI 存在惯用位置的每份记录，例如 Claude Code 的 ~/.claude。要把 Claude Code 或 Codex 的对话改成聊天继续，改从该会话的菜单选 Resume in New Chat（在新对话中继续）。工作树路径没变，在它的终端里运行 claude --resume 也可以。Claude Code 默认会删掉超过 30 天的会话，更早的聊天可能已经没有了。

想留一份 Conductor 聊天的副本，用 Conductor 的 Settings → Account → Export data（导出数据）。Orca 不能导入那份导出。

5. 停用 Conductor

  1. 退出 Conductor，把它移到废纸篓。
  2. 只要还有工作树留在 ~/conductor/workspaces/（或仓库的 .conductor/ 文件夹）里，就保留这些目录。删掉它们等于删掉你的工作。
  3. 一棵旧工作树用完后，从 Orca 里删除它。Orca 会跑你的 archive 脚本，删掉文件夹；分支如果已经完全合并，也会删掉。未合并的分支会留下，见保留下来的分支。
  4. 最后一棵旧工作树消失后，在每个仓库里运行 git worktree prune。然后可以删除 ~/conductor/workspaces/ 和各个 .conductor/ 文件夹。

删除 ~/conductor/ 之前先看一眼

如果在 Orca 里添加的某个项目就在 ~/conductor/ 下面（Transfer to local（转到本机）之后很常见），这个文件夹就是它所有工作树依赖的仓库。留下它，或者先把工作挪到另一份克隆。

迁走 Conductor Cloud 上的工作

2026 年 11 月 13 日起，Conductor Cloud 只读：可以读聊天、导出数据，Agent 不再接收消息。2026 年 11 月 20 日它关闭，Conductor 开始删除云端数据。

Orca 不托管机器。云端的工作要迁到你的 Mac，或迁到一台你掌控、一直开着的机器。

先把工作存下来

能在 11 月 13 日之前做完就做完。

  1. 推送每条分支。 提交全部内容，包括新文件（git add -A），然后推送。.env 这类被 Git 忽略的文件不会被推上去，另外复制到安全的地方。
  2. 导出聊天。 在 Conductor 里打开 Settings → Account → Export data（导出数据）。这是云端聊天唯一的一份副本。

在 Mac 上接着做

  1. 在 Conductor 里右键云端工作区，选 Transfer to local（转到本机）。工作区和它的聊天会移到你的 Mac。
  2. 接着做上面的第 1 到第 4 步。

转过来的工作区，可能挂在 Conductor 自己放在 ~/conductor/ 下的那份仓库上，而不是你的克隆。在里面运行 git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir。路径如果在 ~/conductor/ 下，就把那个文件夹添加为项目。分支已经推送过的话，也可以在常用的克隆里新建一棵工作树，在 New Workspace 编写框的 Branch from（从这条分出）里选那条远程分支。

合上笔记本，Agent 继续跑

以前云端工作区会在你离开后继续跑。在 Orca 里，把 Agent 放到你自己的机器上。见 Orca 的几种运行方式：

  • 远程 Orca 服务器。 让 Orca 留在 Mac mini、家里的服务器或一台云虚拟机上运行，再从笔记本或手机连上去。笔记本睡眠时，Agent 继续跑。
  • SSH。 工作树在任意 SSH 主机上跑，界面留在笔记本上。
  • 云虚拟机。 给每棵工作树一台你已经在用的沙箱或虚拟机，例如 Fly、Modal 或 Vercel Sandbox。

在那台机器上安装并登录 git 和各个 Agent CLI。笔记本上的登录不会跟着过去。然后从已推送的分支创建工作树，告诉 Agent 从哪里接上。例如：Continue the work on this branch. Read the last commits and the open PR first.

迁走例行任务

把每条 Conductor routine 做成一条 Orca 自动化，创建在远程 Orca 服务器上。笔记本关掉之后，它仍按时间表运行。

相关

  • 第一次跑三个 Agent
  • 工作树
  • Agent 会话历史
  • Orca 的几种运行方式
  • 故障排除

Open your Conductor worktrees in Orca, move your setup scripts, and resume your agent sessions — including Conductor Cloud work before Cloud shuts down.

Conductor and Orca share the same model: every task gets its own git worktree on its own branch, with an agent working inside it. Conductor workspaces are ordinary git worktrees, so Orca opens them in place. Nothing is copied or moved, and uncommitted changes stay where they are.

Timeline: Conductor Cloud shuts down on November 20, 2026, and the Conductor desktop app is supported until early 2027.

Used Conductor Cloud?

Cloud workspaces aren't on your Mac, so Orca can't see them yet. Before the steps below, save that work with Move your Conductor Cloud work at the end of this page. Conductor Cloud becomes read-only on November 13, 2026 and starts deleting cloud data on November 20, 2026.

At a glance

ConductorOrca
WorkspaceWorktree — its own branch, files, and agent terminals
~/conductor/workspaces/<repo>/<name>Shown in Orca where they are (hidden by default); new Orca workspaces go in Orca's workspace folder
conductor.json setup and archive scriptsscripts.setup and scripts.archive in orca.yaml
Run button and actionsQuick Commands — one click opens a terminal tab and runs it
CONDUCTOR_ROOT_PATHORCA_ROOT_PATH — Orca still sets CONDUCTOR_ROOT_PATH for setup and archive scripts
CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_PATHORCA_WORKTREE_PATH
CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_NAMEORCA_WORKSPACE_NAME
CONDUCTOR_PORTNone — Orca doesn't assign ports
Archive workspaceDelete the worktree
Diff, PRs, and checksDiff viewer, Commit & push, GitHub
ChatChat UI — turn it on in Settings → Agents → Open agents as → Chat
Conductor CloudA Remote Orca Server or Cloud VMs on machines you control
iOS appOrca mobile for iOS and Android

1. Add your repos

Install Orca, then click Add project at the top of the sidebar and pick the same checkout Conductor used — not a fresh clone. Conductor's workspaces are worktrees of that checkout, and Orca finds them through it.

Not sure which checkout that is? Run this inside any Conductor workspace:

git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir

It prints a path ending in .git. The folder containing it is the checkout to add.

2. Show your Conductor worktrees

Orca hides worktrees it didn't create until you choose to show them.

  1. Open the project's actions menu in the sidebar and choose Show hidden worktrees (it reads Hide non-Orca worktrees when they're shown by default).
  2. In Non-Orca worktrees, click Show next to each Conductor workspace still listed as hidden. They usually live under ~/conductor/workspaces/, or in a .conductor/ folder inside the repo on older Conductor versions.

Each one keeps its path, branch, and uncommitted changes. Orca doesn't run setup on them — they're already set up.

If a workspace is missing from the list, run git worktree list in the checkout from step 1 to confirm it belongs to that repo.

Don't archive these in Conductor

Archiving a workspace in Conductor deletes its folder, which is now also your Orca worktree. Quit Conductor instead.

3. Move your scripts

Let an agent do it

Open a worktree in the repo and ask your agent:

Convert this repo's Conductor config (conductor.json or .conductor/settings.toml) into orca.yaml,
following https://orca.dev/docs/migrate-from-conductor. Map CONDUCTOR_* variables to ORCA_*,
drop CONDUCTOR_PORT, and list the run actions so I can save them as Quick Commands.

To do it by hand, pick one of the two options below for setup and archive scripts (if you do both, see the note after them), then save your run actions as Quick Commands.

Option A: save the detected command (just you)

When a project has a conductor.json and no Orca setup yet, the sidebar shows an Add a setup script card: Found a setup command in conductor.json. Click Save local setup. Orca saves the setup and archive scripts as local commands in Settings → Repository, then runs setup in every new worktree in that project and archive before deleting one. The run script is skipped — save it as a Quick Command — and .conductor/settings.toml isn't read, so copy its commands by hand.

Option B: commit orca.yaml (your whole team)

Put the scripts in orca.yaml at the repo root:

scripts:
  setup: |
    bun install
    cp "$ORCA_ROOT_PATH/.env" .env
  archive: |
    docker compose down
  • setup runs in each new worktree right after Orca creates it.
  • archive runs before Orca deletes a worktree.

Merge orca.yaml to your default branch and pull it into the checkout from step 1. New worktrees read setup from the branch they start from, and deletes read archive from that checkout. See Worktrees for the full lifecycle.

Did both?

If you saved the card before adding orca.yaml, the project's Command source & orca.yaml setting (Settings → Repository) is Local only, which ignores orca.yaml scripts. Set it to orca.yaml only. Avoid Run both here — it runs the local commands and the orca.yaml scripts back to back, so the same migrated commands would run twice.

Run button and actions: Quick Commands

Conductor's Run button and named actions become Quick Commands. Save each one once — a label and the command, such as bun dev or bun test — with Add Command in Settings → Quick Commands, or + Command in the Quick Commands menu on a worktree's tab bar. Choose Project scope to show it only in this repo's worktrees, or Global for every repo.

Run one from the Quick Commands button in any worktree's tab bar: Orca opens a fresh terminal tab in that worktree and runs it. Right-click a terminal to insert a command into it instead. The same list is on Orca mobile, and it works in worktrees on a Remote Orca Server.

Variables and ports

Scripts that read CONDUCTOR_ROOT_PATH keep working — Orca sets it to the same path as ORCA_ROOT_PATH. Replace CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_PATH with ORCA_WORKTREE_PATH (or just use the current directory) and CONDUCTOR_WORKSPACE_NAME with ORCA_WORKSPACE_NAME.

Orca doesn't reserve ports per worktree, so drop --port $CONDUCTOR_PORT. Most dev servers pick a free port on their own.

Copying .env in setup works, but you can list gitignored files in .worktreeinclude instead and Orca copies them into every new worktree. See Shared directories & gitignored files.

4. Pick up your agents

Claude Code, Codex, Cursor CLI, and the other supported agents use the subscriptions and keys you already have.

Turn on Chat UI for the Conductor chat look

Orca opens agents in their own terminal CLI by default. For a chat view like Conductor's, with rich messages, visuals, and images, open Settings → Agents and under Open agents as choose Chat. New sessions for supported agents, including Claude Code and Codex, then open as chats. If some agents stay in the terminal because you chose CLI for them before, click Set all to Chat in the confirmation. You can still pick Chat or CLI for each agent in that page's Installed list. See Chat UI.

Resume a Conductor conversation

To pick up a Conductor conversation, select the worktree, open the right sidebar's Agents tab (Agent Session History), find the session, and click Resume. Orca lists every transcript the agent CLI saved in its usual place, like ~/.claude for Claude Code. To continue a Claude Code or Codex conversation as a chat, choose Resume in New Chat from the session's menu instead. Because the worktree keeps its path, claude --resume in its terminal works too. Claude Code removes sessions older than 30 days by default, so older chats may be gone.

To keep a copy of every Conductor chat, use Conductor's Settings → Account → Export data. Orca can't import that export.

5. Retire Conductor

  1. Quit Conductor and move it to the Trash.
  2. Keep ~/conductor/workspaces/ (or the repo's .conductor/ folder) while any of your worktrees still live there — deleting it deletes your work.
  3. When you finish with an old worktree, delete it from Orca. Orca runs your archive script, removes the folder, and deletes the branch if it's fully merged (unmerged branches are kept — see Preserved branches).
  4. After the last old worktree is gone, run git worktree prune in each repo. Then you can delete ~/conductor/workspaces/ and any .conductor/ folders.
Check before deleting ~/conductor/

If a project you added in Orca lives under ~/conductor/ (common after Transfer to local), that folder is the repository every one of its worktrees depends on. Keep it, or move your work to another clone first.

Move your Conductor Cloud work

On November 13, 2026, Conductor Cloud becomes read-only: you can read chats and export data, but agents stop taking messages. On November 20, 2026, it shuts down and Conductor starts deleting cloud data.

Orca doesn't host machines. Cloud work moves to your Mac, or to an always-on machine you control.

Save your work

Do this before November 13 if you can.

  1. Push every branch. Commit everything, including new files (git add -A), and push. Gitignored files like .env aren't pushed — copy them somewhere safe.
  2. Export your chats. In Conductor, open Settings → Account → Export data. It's your only copy of cloud chats.

Continue on your Mac

  1. In Conductor, right-click the cloud workspace and choose Transfer to local. This moves the workspace and its chats to your Mac.
  2. Follow steps 1–4 above.

A transferred workspace can be attached to Conductor's own copy of the repo under ~/conductor/ instead of your clone. Run git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir inside it. If the path is under ~/conductor/, add that folder as the project. Or, since the branch is pushed, create a new worktree in your usual clone and choose that remote branch under Branch from in the New Workspace composer.

Keep agents running with your laptop closed

Cloud workspaces kept working while you were away. In Orca, run agents on a machine you own — see Ways to run Orca:

  • Remote Orca Server — keep Orca running on a Mac mini, home server, or cloud VM, and connect from your laptop or phone. Agents keep going when your laptop sleeps.
  • SSH — run worktrees on any SSH host while the UI stays on your laptop.
  • Cloud VMs — give each worktree its own sandbox or VM from a provider you already use, like Fly, Modal, or Vercel Sandbox.

Install and sign in to git and your agent CLIs on that machine; your laptop's logins don't carry over. Then create a worktree from your pushed branch and tell the agent where to pick up — for example, "Continue the work on this branch. Read the last commits and the open PR first."

Move your routines

Rebuild each Conductor routine as an Orca automation, created on your Remote Orca Server so it runs on schedule while your laptop is off.

Related

  • Your first 3-agent session

  • Agent session history
  • Ways to run Orca
  • Troubleshooting

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/migrate-from-conductor

上一章Your first 3-agent session 下一章Worktrees

Start Here · The Orca Model

工作树

Worktrees

Orca 把每个功能或每个 bug 变成一棵自己的 Git 工作树。

Orca 以工作树为基本单位。它不是在一份检出上切分支、再 stash。每项任务通过 git worktree 在磁盘上得到一份自己的仓库副本。几个 Agent 可以并行，因为它们不会改到彼此的文件。

这套模型

  • 每个仓库有一条基准引用，通常是 origin/main。
  • 每棵工作树有一条起始引用，也就是它从哪里分出。
  • 每棵工作树有自己的分支、磁盘上的文件，以及自己的 Agent 终端。
  • 删除工作树会同时去掉目录和分支（有确认）。交给 git 删检出，聊天和文件操作不会被卡住。git 删不完检出时，工作区仍留在列表里，可以重试。失败情况允许时，Orca 提供 Force Delete（强制删除）。它是主按钮加箭头菜单：菜单里有 Always force delete（以后都强制删除），会记住这个选择，以后删除工作区都按它来。git 因为本地分支可能还有未合并的提交而留下它时，Orca 可以给出一步复核。见保留下来的分支。

一个功能的生命周期

  1. 创建。 填任务名，选起始引用，可选附上 GitHub / Linear / Jira / GitLab 链接。
  2. 工作。 Agent 终端、编辑器标签、浏览器标签、终端窗格，都限定在这棵工作树里。
  3. 审阅。 对照起始引用看 diff，使用批注 AI diff 和归属。
  4. 交付。 提交、推送、打开 PR、等检查，都在里面完成。
  5. 归档或删除。 点一下去掉工作树和分支。

创建在后台进行

提交 Create Worktree（创建工作树）对话框后，对话框马上关闭。git fetch 和 git worktree add 在后台继续，你可以接着用 Orca。新工作树出现在侧栏，带一行进度。工作树的标签会显示实时的准备状态，直到检出完成，并打开你选的起始界面。选了 Agent，就把那个 Agent 作为第一个标签启动。Blank Terminal（空白终端）则放进一个普通 shell。创建还在进行时，可以切到其他工作树、看进度，或从标签内的面板取消。大仓库里创建仍然快。在后台完成的创建，不会把你从已经切过去的工作区拉回来。创建失败时，面板显示错误，并带 Retry（重试）。

起始引用选择器

创建工作树时，New Workspace 编写框里的 Branch from（从这条分出）决定起始引用。可以是：

  • 仓库的基准引用（最快的一条路）。
  • 另一条本地分支。适合把工作叠在正在评审的 PR 上。
  • 某个提交的 SHA。
  • 一条已有的远程分支。Orca 会先 fetch，再检出。

共享目录与被 Git 忽略的文件

刚建好的工作树是一份干净检出。放在被 Git 忽略路径里的依赖、缓存和本地密钥，要重新准备才会有。

Orca 用三种互补的办法补上：

  1. 工作树共享路径（每个仓库一份，在 Settings → Repository）。路径从主检出放进每棵新工作树。macOS 上能用 APFS 克隆复制就用它，否则用符号链接。
  2. orca.yaml 里的 worktree.sharedDirectories。 随仓库提交的一份被 Git 忽略的目录列表，用同样的方式共享（符号链接 / 共享，不是复制）。适合 node_modules、.cache 这种可以重建的大目录。条目必须是主检出里已有的目录，并且被 Git 忽略。已跟踪或缺失的路径会跳过。
  3. 仓库根目录的 .worktreeinclude。 列出要复制（不是符号链接）进每棵新工作树的被 Git 忽略的文件或目录，让每棵工作树拥有自己的那一份。常见条目：.env、.vscode/ 下的本地配置。允许空行和 # 注释。目前只支持字面路径。glob 和取反会跳过，并给出警告。已跟踪、缺失，或没有被 Git 忽略的路径不会复制。

orca.yaml 里的共享目录追加到每位用户的工作树共享路径列表上，不会替换它。已经共享或链接过的路径，不会再从 .worktreeinclude 复制一次。

# orca.yaml (repo root)
worktree:
  sharedDirectories:
    - node_modules
    - .cache

# .worktreeinclude (repo root)
.env
.env.local
.vscode/settings.json

创建对话框：项目、运行位置、Agent 和任务链接

Create Workspace 对话框里，Project（项目）和 Run on（运行位置）是可输入筛选的下拉框：

  • 输入即过滤。按 Enter 确认当前高亮的那一行。
  • Project 底部始终固定着 Add a new project（添加新项目）。安装里还没有任何项目时，创建也不会走死。
  • Run on 列出已就绪的主机和环境配方，以及还要在那台机器上配好项目位置的主机。对需要配置的主机，选 Set project location（设置项目位置），不必离开创建表单。然后浏览到已有的检出或文件夹，或把 Git 仓库克隆到那台主机上。Orca 会选中刚就绪的位置，你可以继续创建工作树。断开的 SSH 或远程主机可以提供 Connect（连接），而不必把它们选成运行目标。Add host（添加主机）始终固定着，用来添加 SSH 或远程 Orca 服务器。
  • Agent 选择器（收起或展开）可以对你在创建时选的 Agent Set as default（设为默认），包括空白终端。
  • 从名称字段链接一个 GitHub PR、Linear 议题、GitLab MR，或 Jira 议题（粘贴 Jira URL，或在 Jira 模式里搜索）。链接的议题出现在工作树卡片上。见 Jira 议题抽屉。之后还可以从 Edit Worktree Details（编辑工作树详情）再附上更多评审和任务。

见 Orca 的几种运行方式。

带 emoji 的工作区名

在工作区名称字段里可以输入 Slack 风格的短码（:rocket:），并从短码建议浮层里挑选。双击重命名工作树、在 Edit Worktree Details 里编辑，或在工作树跳转面板（Cmd-J）里输入短码，用的是同一个选择器。显示名保留 emoji。Orca 从它推导 git 分支名时，会把认得的 emoji 改写成可读短码（例如 🚀 → rocket）。跳转面板可以用这个短码片段搜到带 emoji 的工作区。

给分支起名

默认情况下，Orca 用你输入的工作区名推导新工作树的分支名。工作区是从 GitHub PR、Linear / Jira 议题或 GitLab MR 创建的，就从那一项推导。想自己指定分支名，展开 Create Worktree 对话框里的 Advanced（高级），在 Branch name（分支名）字段里输入。占位是 feature/my-branch。

你输入的工作区名会用作分支名，即使 Auto-Rename Branch From Work（按工作自动重命名分支）开着。自动重命名只替换 Orca 自己生成的名字。

也可以从现有工作树的右键菜单创建子工作树。同一个抽屉里，可以把同一仓库里一棵活动的工作树选为新工作区的 Parent workspace（父工作区）。这只是在 Orca 侧栏里嵌套工作区，不改变 Git 历史或分支。Orca 会排除已归档的工作区，以及会造成环的选择。

从 Linear 议题创建时，Orca 用 Linear 为该议题准备的分支名（Linear 提供了的话）作为分支覆盖。这和 Linear 界面里会建议的名字相同，不只是把议题标题做成短名。

只有从你输入的工作区名或基准分支创建时，才提供 Branch name 字段。工作区绑着一项已跟踪的工作项时，分支从那一项推导。链接的 GitHub PR 在提交时还会再解析一次分支。Orca 藏起这个字段，免得你填了覆盖也被悄悄忽略。

侧栏布局

侧栏默认按项目给工作树分组。顶层行是项目（一个项目等于一个 Git 仓库，或一组相关仓库），展开后是你正在做的工作树。标题栏有自己的过滤输入，和全局搜索分开，不用离开侧栏就能缩小列表。

用侧栏控件收起或展开全部项目。添加项目时，发现的工作树一开始是隐藏的，可以从 Non-Orca worktrees（非 Orca 工作树）里显示它们。

侧栏标题的过滤菜单把主机和项目范围放在共用的 Show（显示）一节里（Hosts（主机）和项目是子菜单），然后是隐藏开关：

  • Sleeping（休眠）工作区。这个过滤器下，每个项目的入口 / 主工作区仍保持可见，免得那份检出空闲时项目行消失。
  • Except default branch（默认分支除外）。只在 Hide sleeping（隐藏休眠）开着时出现。休眠清扫时仍让默认分支上的工作区可见。休眠工作区已经显示时，这个子选项隐藏。
  • Default branch（默认分支）工作区，也就是主检出那一行。
  • Automation-created（自动化创建）的工作区。
  • CLI-created（CLI 创建）的工作区。由 orca worktree create 创建。悬停卡片可以看到 Orca CLI 的来源说明。
  • Other-client（其他客户端）的工作区。共享的远程 Orca 服务器上有从另一台已配对客户端创建的工作区时，会出现 Hide other-client workspaces（隐藏其他客户端的工作区）。打开后，这台设备的列表只留你在这里创建的工作区。空的 Cmd-J 最近项和数字快捷键走同一套过滤。输入查询后，仍能找到被藏起来的行。
  • Detached HEAD（游离 HEAD）工作区。检出停在某个提交上，而不是一条分支。

过滤控件上显示当前生效的过滤器数量。Clear（清除）只重置开着的过滤器。只被这些过滤器藏起来的工作区，文字搜索和工作树跳转面板（Cmd-J）在你输入查询后仍能到达。跳转面板自己也有主机 / 项目过滤（Tab）。

添加一个里面有多个 Git 仓库的父文件夹时，Orca 可以把选中的仓库分别导入，或归到一个项目组下。

侧栏顶部的 Search（搜索）按钮打开工作树跳转面板（Cmd-J）。是同一个表面，给不主要靠键盘导航的人用点击。状态栏直接显示 Agent 活动。未读工作树用粗体，不用角标。Orca 会记住左侧栏在重启之间是开还是关。从另一台客户端揭开一个远程工作区，不会展开你本机侧栏的分组。

可以把工作树固定到它所属项目的顶部，让长时间的工作留在视线里。右键工作树有归档、休眠、删除。要从键盘开始删除，悬停在工作树或文件夹工作区上，macOS 按 Cmd-Shift-Backspace，Windows / Linux 按 Ctrl-Shift-Backspace。正常的确认对话框仍会出现。按住 Cmd（Linux / Windows 用 Ctrl）点击，把工作树加进多选。按住 Shift 选一段连续范围。右键任一已选工作树，操作会作用到选择里的每一棵。仓库行本身可以拖动重排。拖动一行时，放入下层的目标是一块宽而稳定的投放区。拖到行的上方或下方用来排序。按 Escape 取消。

工作树有嵌套的子工作树时（例如来自编排，或带父级的 worktree create），右键菜单还可以提供 Sleep with Descendants (N)（连同后代一起休眠）和 Delete with Descendants…（连同后代一起删除）。连同后代休眠，会关掉所选工作区以及同一项目、同一仓库、同一主机上每个已核对的嵌套子工作区的活动面板。只有带活动终端或浏览器标签的工作区才会被休眠。连同后代删除把已有的级联删除说清楚，并在后台执行，确认不会挡住 Orca 的其他部分。过期的父子链接、成环的关系，以及跨主机或跨仓库的子工作区，都不会算进去。

父工作树在一枚 N children（N 个子工作区）标记下显示嵌套的子项。点这枚标记来显示或隐藏。要从键盘做同样的事，在 Settings → Shortcuts 里指定 Toggle Child Workspaces（切换子工作区）。它作用在指针下的工作树上；没有悬停时作用在当前工作树。效果和那张卡片上的标记相同。

双击侧栏里的工作树标题，就地重命名。双击卡片的其他地方，仍打开完整的编辑对话框。Edit Worktree Details 有一节 Linked work（关联的工作），可以把多项评审和任务附到一个工作区：粘贴 GitHub、GitLab、Linear 或 Jira 的 URL，或搜索已连接的提供方。附上的项分成 Reviews（评审，PR 和 MR）和 Issues & tasks（议题和任务），并带一个累计数量。工作区卡片把它们叠着显示。链接必须属于这个工作区的仓库。SSH 工作区的主机断开时，卡片标题行可以显示一个就地重连控件，见 SSH 工作树。

右键单个工作区，选 Copy Worktree Name（复制工作树名），复制 Orca 在工作树选择器里用的规范名称。顺序是自定义名、分支，再退到文件夹名。文件夹工作区，以及清掉了自定义名的工作区，也适用。

你自己重命名工作区之后，工作树的分支变了，Orca 仍保留这个显示名。清掉自定义名，就恢复按分支自动起名。Orca 生成的名字会继续跟着分支走。

用 Resource Manager 清理

需要先通览再删除时，用 Resource Manager → Clean up workspaces（资源管理器 → 清理工作区）。列表包括本机工作树、主工作树、文件夹工作区，以及断开的 SSH 主机上的工作区。文件夹工作区和项目组在这里用保存的显示名（例如 Documentation → Release notes），不用内部 ID。Orca 在重启时恢复会话之后也一样。可以搜索、过滤或排序，查看每个工作区的状态、最近活动、大小、Git 状态和关联的评审，然后再选择删除。

自动清理工作区

Settings → Workspace Cleanup 可以预览匹配的工作区、打开清理规则、立刻运行，并查看活动。规则默认关闭。启用一条规则之前先看删除预览：匹配的工作区可能在几秒内被删除。清理会删除工作区。没有自动归档。

也可以在 Settings → Workspace Cleanup 里打开：删除工作区时，一并删除其中的 Orca、Claude Code 和 Codex 聊天。这项默认关闭。打开时会要求确认，并包括以前删除的工作区。删掉的聊天不能恢复。见 Agent 会话历史。

在项目设置里，Workspace cleanup → Cleanup rules（工作区清理 → 清理规则）可以用主机的全局规则、为这个项目关掉清理，或使用自定义规则。自定义规则从全局规则出发。改动可以在几秒内生效，并且不显示全局页上的删除确认。文件夹项目不参与自动清理。

在终端里，orca workspace cleanup preview 显示当前规则会去掉或留下什么，以及原因。orca workspace cleanup run 运行这些规则。orca workspace cleanup report 显示活动和下一次运行。见 CLI 参考。

保留下来的分支

批量删除工作区（侧栏多选，或 Resource Manager 清理）仍会去掉磁盘上的文件夹。git 因为本地分支可能还有未合并的提交而拒绝丢掉它时，Orca 留下这些分支，并显示一条类似 Review N Branches（查看 N 条分支）的提示。打开它，列出留下的分支，你可以强制删除其中一些，留下另一些。没选的分支留在仓库里。工作区文件夹不会恢复。

多仓库项目组与文件夹工作区

导入一个里面有多个 Git 仓库的父文件夹时，Orca 可以把这些仓库归到侧栏里的一个项目组下。每个项目组有一套文件夹工作区流程：一个类似工作树的条目，住在父文件夹这一层，并把任务来源绑到下面的某一个仓库。这样，一项功能的 GitHub / GitLab / Linear / Jira 任务表面仍附在对的仓库上，工作区本身则和同组的其他项一起出现在侧栏。

要创建一个：悬停项目组的标题行，点 +（提示是 “Create workspace for group”）。编写框对话框（“Create Folder Workspace”）让你选工作区任务来源所用的源项目，给工作区起名，并可选附上一个关联的议题或 PR。提交后，文件夹工作区出现在项目组下，和按仓库划分的普通工作树并列。

文件夹项目的 setup 和 archive 脚本在共享的项目文件夹里运行：创建工作区时跑 setup，删除前跑 archive。同一个文件夹被它的各个工作区共用，写脚本时要把这一点算进去。

多仓库的文件夹工作区也可以作为其他工作区的父级，包括另一台机器上的工作区。用 Set Parent（设置父级），或把工作区拖到这个文件夹下面，在侧栏里把跨主机的相关工作归到一起。

删除项目组时，Orca 还提供一个复选框，在同一次操作里去掉这个组所包含的项目（底层的仓库登记）。不再使用的一组，一次确认就能清掉。

直接用 git

每棵 Orca 工作树都是一份真正的 Git 工作树。可以在里面开终端，用 git status、git rebase、git cherry-pick，以及其他任何 git 命令。Orca 在下一次渲染时接上这些变化。

你自己用 git worktree add 创建的工作树，在你于 Orca 里显示它们之前一直是外部的。仓库隐藏它们时，侧栏会显示一张 hidden worktrees（隐藏的工作树）卡片。点它打开 Non-Orca worktrees（非 Orca 工作树），再对想放进侧栏的工作树选 Show（显示）。

在 Settings → General → Workspace 里，给外部工作树的来源设全局默认：Claude Code 工作树、GSD 工作树、其他位置，以及你添加的任意自定义绝对路径。这些默认值作用于那台主机上当前和以后的工作树。新的全局自定义根一开始是隐藏的。在项目的 Non-Orca worktrees 对话框里，可以为这个项目覆盖某个来源，或把它退回全局设置，然后搜索并找回单个隐藏的工作树。

如果你在 CLI 里执行 git worktree remove，Orca 会注意到，并在下次刷新这个仓库时清掉自己的状态。

How Orca turns every feature or bug into its own git worktree.

Orca is worktree-native. Instead of branching and stashing on one checkout, every task gets its own on-disk copy of the repo via git worktree. This is what makes parallel agents safe — they never step on each other's files.

The model

  • Each repo has a base ref (usually origin/main).
  • Each worktree has a start-from ref — what it branches off.
  • Each worktree has its own branch, its own files on disk, and its own agent terminals.
  • Deleting a worktree removes both the directory and the branch (with confirmation). Letting git remove the checkout means removal does not stall chat or file operations. If git can't finish removing the checkout, the workspace stays listed so you can retry — Orca offers Force Delete when the failure allows it. Force Delete is a split control: its arrow menu offers Always force delete, which remembers the choice for all future workspace deletions. If git keeps a local branch because it may still have unmerged commits, Orca can offer a review step — see Preserved branches.

Per-feature lifecycle

  1. Create — task name, start-from picker, optional GitHub / Linear / Jira / GitLab link.
  2. Work — agent terminals, editor tabs, browser tabs, terminal panes all scoped to this worktree.
  3. Review — diff view against the start-from ref, Annotate AI Diff, Attribution.
  4. Ship — commit, push, open PR, wait on checks — all inline.
  5. Archive or delete — one click removes the worktree and branch.

Creation runs in the background

Submitting the Create Worktree dialog closes it immediately — the git fetch and git worktree add work continues in the background while you keep using Orca. The new worktree appears in the sidebar with a progress row, and the worktree's tab shows live setup status until the checkout finishes and opens the starting surface you selected. Choosing an agent launches that agent as the first tab; Blank Terminal seeds an ordinary shell instead. You can switch to other worktrees while a create is in flight, watch the progress, or cancel it from the in-tab panel. Creation stays fast in large repositories, and a create that finishes in the background does not pull you away from the workspace you switched to. If creation fails, the panel surfaces the error with a Retry.

Start-from picker

When creating a worktree, the Branch from control in the New Workspace composer sets the starting ref. This can be:

  • The repo's base ref (the fast path).
  • Another local branch — useful for stacking work on top of a PR in review.
  • A specific commit SHA.
  • An existing remote branch — Orca will fetch and check it out.

Shared directories & gitignored files

A brand-new worktree is a clean checkout. Dependencies, caches, and local secrets that live in gitignored paths are missing until you recreate them.

Orca fills that gap in three complementary ways:

  1. Worktree Shared Paths (per repo, in Settings → Repository) — paths materialize from the primary checkout into each new worktree (APFS clone-copy on macOS when possible, otherwise a symlink).
  2. worktree.sharedDirectories in orca.yaml — repo-checked-in list of gitignored directories to share the same way (symlink/share, not copy). Use this for large rebuildable trees like node_modules or .cache. Entries must exist as directories in the primary checkout and be gitignored; tracked or missing paths are skipped.
  3. .worktreeinclude at the repo root — list of gitignored files or directories to copy (not symlink) into each new worktree, so each worktree owns its copy. Typical entries: .env, local config under .vscode/. Blank lines and # comments are allowed. Only literal paths are supported today — globs and negation are skipped with a warning. Paths that are tracked, missing, or not gitignored are not copied.

orca.yaml shared directories add to the per-user Worktree Shared Paths list; they never replace it. Paths already shared/linked are not re-copied from .worktreeinclude.

# orca.yaml (repo root)
worktree:
  sharedDirectories:
    - node_modules
    - .cache
# .worktreeinclude (repo root)
.env
.env.local
.vscode/settings.json

Create dialog: Project, Run on, agent, and task links

The Create Workspace dialog uses type-ahead comboboxes for Project and Run on:

  • Type to filter; Enter commits the armed row.
  • Project always keeps Add a new project pinned at the bottom — create never dead-ends when the install has no projects yet.
  • Run on lists ready hosts and recipes, plus hosts that still need project setup on that machine. On a setup-needed host, choose Set project location without leaving the create form, then browse to an existing checkout/folder or clone the Git repository onto that host. Orca selects the newly ready location so you can continue creating the worktree. Disconnected SSH / remote hosts can offer Connect without selecting them as the run target. Add host stays pinned for SSH or Remote Orca Server targets.
  • The Agent selector (collapsed or expanded) can Set as default for the agent you pick on create, including Blank Terminal.
  • Link a GitHub PR, Linear issue, GitLab MR, or Jira issue from the name field (paste a Jira URL or search in Jira mode). Linked issues appear on the worktree card. See Jira items drawer. You can attach more reviews and tasks afterward from Edit Worktree Details.

See Ways to run Orca.

Emoji workspace names

In the workspace name field you can type Slack-style shortcodes (:rocket:) and pick from a shortcode suggestion popover. The same picker works when you double-click to rename a worktree, edit it in Edit Worktree Details, or type a shortcode in Worktree Jump Palette (Cmd-J). The display name keeps the emoji; when Orca derives a git branch name it rewrites known emoji to readable shortcodes (e.g. 🚀 → rocket). Jump Palette search matches emoji-named workspaces by that shortcode-derived fragment.

Naming the branch

By default Orca derives the new worktree's branch name from the workspace name you type, or from the linked GitHub PR, Linear/Jira issue, or GitLab MR when the workspace is created from one of those. To set an explicit branch name instead, expand the Advanced drawer in the Create Worktree dialog and type into the Branch name field (placeholder feature/my-branch).

A workspace name you type is used for the branch even when Auto-Rename Branch From Work is on; automatic renaming replaces only names Orca generated.

You can also create a child worktree from an existing worktree’s context menu. The same drawer lets you choose an active worktree from the same repository as the new workspace's Parent workspace. This only nests the workspaces in Orca's sidebar; it does not change Git history or branches. Orca excludes archived workspaces and choices that would create a cycle.

When you create from a Linear issue, Orca uses Linear's own branch name for that issue (when Linear provides one) as the branch override — the same name Linear would suggest in its UI — instead of only slugifying the issue title.

The Branch name field is only offered when you're creating from a typed workspace name or a base branch. When the workspace is tied to a tracked work item, the branch is derived from that item — a linked GitHub PR even re-resolves the branch at submit — so Orca hides the field to avoid a silently-ignored override.

Sidebar layout

The sidebar groups worktrees by project by default — top-level rows are projects (one project = one git repo or a related cluster), expanded into the worktrees you're actively working on. The header has its own filter input (separate from the global search) so you can narrow the list without leaving the sidebar.

Use the sidebar controls to collapse or expand all projects. When you add a project, discovered worktrees start hidden; you can show them from Non-Orca worktrees.

The sidebar header filter menu groups host and project scope under a shared Show section (submenu rows for Hosts and projects), then the hide toggles:

  • Sleeping workspaces — each project's entry-point / main workspace stays visible under this filter so the project row does not disappear when that checkout is idle
  • Except default branch (only while Hide sleeping is on) — keeps default-branch workspaces visible during the sleeping sweep; the sub-option is hidden when sleeping workspaces are already shown
  • Default branch workspaces (the main checkout row)
  • Automation-created workspaces
  • CLI-created workspaces — created via orca worktree create (hover the card for an Orca CLI provenance detail)
  • Other-client workspaces — Hide other-client workspaces appears when a shared Remote Orca Server has workspaces created from another paired client; turn it on to keep this device's list to workspaces you created here. Empty Cmd-J recents and numeric shortcuts follow the same filter; typing a query still finds hidden rows.
  • Detached HEAD workspaces — checkouts sitting on a commit rather than a branch

Active filter count shows on the filter control; Clear resets only the filters that are on. Text search and Worktree Jump Palette (Cmd-J) still reach workspaces hidden only by these filters once you type a query — the jump palette also has its own host/project filters (Tab).

When you add a parent folder that contains multiple Git repos, Orca can import the selected repos separately or group them under one project group.

A Search button at the top of the sidebar opens Worktree Jump Palette (Cmd-J) — same surface, click-driven for users who don't lean on the keyboard for navigation. The status bar shows agent activity inline; unread worktrees are bolded rather than badged. Orca remembers whether the left sidebar was open across restarts, and revealing a remote workspace from another client does not expand your local sidebar sections.

You can pin a worktree to the top of its project to keep long-running work in view, and right-clicking a worktree exposes archive / sleep / delete actions. To start deletion from the keyboard, hover a worktree or folder workspace and press Cmd-Shift-Backspace on macOS or Ctrl-Shift-Backspace on Windows / Linux; the normal confirmation dialog still appears. Hold Cmd (Ctrl on Linux/Windows) while clicking to add worktrees to a multi-selection, or hold Shift to select a contiguous range — right-clicking any selected worktree applies the action to every worktree in the selection. Repo rows themselves can be reordered by drag. When dragging a row, the nesting target is a broad, stable drop zone; drag above or below a row to reorder, and press Escape to cancel.

When a worktree has nested child worktrees (for example from orchestration or worktree create with a parent), the context menu can also offer Sleep with Descendants (N) and Delete with Descendants…. Sleep with descendants closes active panels on the selected workspace and every validated nested child in the same project, repo, and host — only workspaces with live terminals or browser tabs are targeted for sleep. Delete with descendants makes the existing cascading delete explicit and runs it in the background, so the confirmation does not block the rest of Orca. Stale lineage links, cycles, and children across host or repo boundaries are excluded.

A parent worktree shows its nested children under an N children chip; click the chip to show or hide them. To do the same from the keyboard, assign Toggle Child Workspaces under Settings → Shortcuts. It acts on the worktree under the pointer, or on the active worktree when nothing is hovered, and does exactly what that card's chip does.

Double-click a worktree title in the sidebar to rename it inline. Double-clicking elsewhere on the card still opens the full edit dialog. Edit Worktree Details has a Linked work section that attaches several reviews and tasks to one workspace — paste a GitHub, GitLab, Linear, or Jira URL, or search a connected provider. Attached items are grouped into Reviews (pull and merge requests) and Issues & tasks with a running count, and a workspace card shows them stacked; links must belong to the workspace's repository. For SSH workspaces whose host is disconnected, the card title row can show an inline reconnect control (see SSH worktrees).

Right-click a single workspace and choose Copy Worktree Name to copy the canonical name Orca uses for worktree selectors. It follows the custom name, branch, then folder fallback, so it also works for folder workspaces and workspaces whose custom name was cleared.

When you rename a workspace yourself, Orca keeps that display name when the worktree's branch changes. Clear the custom name to resume automatic branch-derived naming; names Orca generated continue to follow the branch.

Resource Manager cleanup

Use Resource Manager → Clean up workspaces when you need to review workspaces across your setup before removing them. The list includes local worktrees, main worktrees, folder workspaces, and workspaces on disconnected SSH hosts. Folder workspaces and project groups use their saved display names here (for example, Documentation → Release notes) instead of internal IDs, including after Orca recovers a session on restart. Search, filter, or sort the list to inspect each workspace's status, recent activity, size, Git state, and linked review before you select anything for removal.

Automatic workspace cleanup

Settings → Workspace Cleanup lets you preview matching workspaces, turn on cleanup rules, run them now, and review activity. Rules are off by default. Review the deletion preview before enabling a rule: matching workspaces can be deleted within seconds. Cleanup deletes workspaces; automatic archiving is not available.

You can also enable deletion of Orca, Claude Code, and Codex chats from deleted workspaces in Settings → Workspace Cleanup. This option is off by default. Enabling it asks for confirmation and includes previously deleted workspaces; deleted chats cannot be recovered. See Agent session history.

In a project's settings, Workspace cleanup → Cleanup rules can use the host's global rules, turn cleanup off for that project, or use custom rules. Custom rules start from the global rules; changes can take effect within seconds and do not show the global page's deletion confirmation. Folder projects are excluded from automatic cleanup.

From a terminal, orca workspace cleanup preview shows what the current rules would remove or keep and why. orca workspace cleanup run runs those rules, and orca workspace cleanup report shows activity and the next run. See the CLI reference.

Preserved branches

Bulk-deleting workspaces (sidebar multi-select or Resource Manager cleanup) still removes the on-disk folders. If git refuses to drop a local branch because it may contain unmerged commits, Orca keeps those branches and shows a toast such as Review N Branches. Opening it lists the kept branches so you can force-delete some and leave others. Unselected branches stay in the repository; workspace folders are not restored.

Multi-repo project groups & folder workspaces

When you import a parent folder that contains several Git repos, Orca can group those repos under a single project group in the sidebar. Each project group exposes a folder workspace flow — a worktree-like entry that lives at the parent-folder level and binds its task source to one of the repos underneath, so one feature's GitHub/GitLab/Linear/Jira task surface stays attached to the right repo even though the workspace itself is grouped with its siblings in the sidebar.

To create one, hover the project group's header row in the sidebar and click the + action (tooltip: "Create workspace for group"). The composer dialog ("Create Folder Workspace") asks you to pick the source project for the workspace's task source, name the workspace, and optionally attach a linked issue or PR. Submit, and the folder workspace appears under the project group alongside the regular repo-scoped worktrees.

Folder project workspace setup and archive scripts run in the shared project folder: setup runs when creating a workspace, and archive runs before removal. The same folder is shared by its workspaces, so account for that when writing scripts.

Multi-repo folder workspaces can also parent other workspaces, including workspaces on another machine. Use Set Parent or drag a workspace under the folder to group related work across hosts in the sidebar.

When deleting a project group, Orca also offers a checkbox to remove the group's contained projects (the underlying repo registrations) in the same action — so cleanup of a no-longer-used cluster is one confirmation, not several.

Using plain git

Every Orca worktree is a real git worktree. You can open a terminal in it and use git status, git rebase, git cherry-pick, and anything else — Orca picks up the changes next render.

Worktrees you create yourself with git worktree add stay external until you show them in Orca. If a repo hides them, the sidebar shows a hidden worktrees card. Click it to open Non-Orca worktrees, then choose Show for the worktrees you want in the sidebar.

In Settings → General → Workspace, set global defaults for external-worktree sources: Claude Code worktrees, GSD worktrees, other locations, and any custom absolute-path locations you add. Those defaults apply to current and future worktrees on that host; new global custom roots start hidden. In a project's Non-Orca worktrees dialog, you can override a source for that project or return it to the global setting, then search and recover individual hidden worktrees.

If you git worktree remove from the CLI, Orca will notice and clean up its own state the next time it refreshes that repo.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/model/worktrees

上一章Migrate from Conductor 下一章Tabs, panes & split layouts

Start Here · The Orca Model

标签、窗格与分屏

Tabs, panes & split layouts

把标签拖到窗格边缘就能分屏。标签组成组，边界一旦放下就固定。

窗格是为了同时看着多个 Agent，又不丢掉上下文。标签归进窗格，窗格再分成布局。

标签

一个标签只放一种内容：终端、编辑器缓冲、浏览器、diff 或 PR。标签住在一个标签组里。

  • 在组内上下拖动标签，用来排序。
  • 把标签拖到另一个组上，用来移动。
  • 右键终端、编辑器或浏览器标签，选 Copy Tab ID（复制标签 ID）。会复制一个稳定引用，例如 orcaTabId: abc123，用来让 Agent 对准这一个标签。未激活的标签和已固定的标签也可以复制。复制不会重命名、激活或关闭它。
  • 关掉当前工作树里的全部编辑器文件标签：macOS 用 Cmd+Option+W，Windows / Linux 用 Ctrl+Alt+W。
  • 一条当前标签色条标出哪个窗格有焦点。
  • 悬停在标签的任意位置，可以看到完整标题，下面一行是图标和程序。移到下一个标签时，卡片马上滑出来。标题被截断时，不必对准文字也能认出来。

切换标签

新安装上的默认组合键：

动作 macOS Linux / Windows
下一个 / 上一个标签（所有类型） Cmd+Shift+] / Cmd+Shift+[ Ctrl+Shift+] / Ctrl+Shift+[
下一个 / 上一个标签（同一类型） Cmd+Option+] / Cmd+Option+[ Ctrl+Alt+] / Ctrl+Alt+[
上一个最近标签 Ctrl+Tab Ctrl+Tab

到 Settings → Shortcuts 改键。已经装过的，自定义覆盖留在 ~/.orca/keybindings.json。

分屏

把标签拖到窗格边缘，就会分屏：

  • 右缘： 分成左右（horizontal split）。
  • 下缘： 分成上下（vertical split）。

空的分屏窗格里按 /，搜索 Agent 或文件，再用方向键和 Enter 打开。

分屏可以嵌套。左边放 Agent 终端，右上放 diff，右下放浏览器标签，可以同时在。

终端标签也可以在标签内部再分。用终端标签菜单里的 Split terminal right（向右拆分终端）或 Split terminal down（向下拆分终端）。当前终端窗格标题栏上的拆分按钮，用来向右拆。

固定边界

窗格边界留在你放下的位置。改变窗口大小不会打乱布局。边界位置按工作树保存。

跨工作树的标签组

每棵工作树有自己的标签布局。切换工作树时，整棵窗格树一起换。浏览器标签、终端和 diff 会按你离开时的样子出现。

Drag-to-split panes, tab groups, and pinned boundaries.

Orca's pane system is designed for watching multiple agents work without losing context. Tabs group into panes; panes split into layouts.

Tabs

Each tab holds one thing: a terminal, an editor buffer, a browser, a diff, a PR. Tabs live inside a tab group.

  • Drag a tab up-down within a group to reorder.
  • Drag a tab onto another group to move it.
  • Right-click a terminal, editor, or browser tab and choose Copy Tab ID to copy a stable reference (such as orcaTabId: abc123) for that exact tab — handy when you want to point an agent at the precise tab. It works on inactive and pinned tabs and does not rename, activate, or close the tab.
  • Close every editor file tab in the active worktree with Cmd+Option+W on macOS or Ctrl+Alt+W on Windows / Linux.
  • An active-tab color bar marks which pane is focused.
  • Hover anywhere on a tab to see its full title, with the tab's icon and program on a row below. Moving to the next tab shows its card right away with a short slide, so truncated titles are easy to identify without aiming at their text.

Switching tabs

Default chords on new installs:

ActionmacOSLinux / Windows
Next / previous tab (all types)Cmd+Shift+] / Cmd+Shift+[Ctrl+Shift+] / Ctrl+Shift+[
Next / previous tab (same type)Cmd+Option+] / Cmd+Option+[Ctrl+Alt+] / Ctrl+Alt+[
Previous recent tabCtrl+TabCtrl+Tab

Remap under Settings → Shortcuts. Existing installs keep customized overrides in ~/.orca/keybindings.json.

Split panes

Drag a tab to the edge of a pane to create a split:

  • Right edge — splits left/right (horizontal split).
  • Bottom edge — splits top/bottom (vertical split).

In an empty split pane, press / to search for an agent or file, then use the arrow keys and Enter to open it.

Splits nest. You can have an agent terminal on the left, a diff view on the top-right, and a browser tab on the bottom-right — all at once.

Terminal tabs can also split inside the tab. Use the terminal tab menu for Split terminal right or Split terminal down, or use the split button in the active terminal pane header for a right split.

Pinned boundaries

Pane boundaries stay where you put them. Resizing the window doesn't shuffle your layout; boundary positions are saved per worktree.

Tab groups across worktrees

Each worktree owns its own tab layout. Switching worktrees swaps the entire pane tree — your browser tab, terminal, and diff reappear exactly as you left them.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/model/tabs-panes-splits

上一章Worktrees 下一章Agents & sessions

Start Here · The Orca Model

Agent 与会话

Agents & sessions

一次 Agent 会话就是：一棵工作树里、一个终端中、正在跑的一个 CLI Agent。Orca 跟踪它的生命周期。哪些在工作、哪些空着，不用点进每个标签去看。

状态标记

Agent 标签和工作树行用同一套状态图形：

  • 转圈： 正在工作。
  • 琥珀色问号： 在等你（权限或需要输入）。侧栏「需要你处理」的计数用同一个图形。
  • 翠绿对勾（看板）或翠绿点（侧栏）：做完，或安静地处于活动状态。
  • 红点： 被挡住、被打断，或失败。
  • 灰点： 空闲。
  • 没有标记： 普通 shell，不是 Orca 认得的 Agent CLI。

状态来自终端的 OSC 标题序列，以及 Agent 钩子。Claude Code、Codex 和其他几种 Agent 会发出这些信号。

Agent 看板

打开 Settings → Experimental → Agent Dashboard。左侧栏会多一项 Agent Dashboard（Agent 看板），把各工作树里的 Agent 分成几列。

可以在 Settings → Shortcuts 里给 Toggle Agent Dashboard（切换 Agent 看板）指定快捷键。它没有默认键。焦点在终端、编辑器或浏览器标签上时也能用。

列

  • Needs You（需要你处理）：在等权限或问题。
  • Working（进行中）：正在跑。
  • Done（已完成）：已经结束、你可能还想再看的会话。
  • Idle（空闲）：安静、大约 30 分钟没有报告完成的 Agent。看板上默认隐藏。在窗口内或弹出窗口的 board settings（看板设置，齿轮）里打开 Show idle agents（显示空闲 Agent）。不要到全局的 Experimental 设置里找。窗口内和弹出窗口保持同步。

看板卡片会给 SSH 工作区和已配对的远程 Orca 服务器显示主机徽章。悬停或聚焦徽章，可以看到保存的主机名，例如 SSH host · openclaw 或 Remote Orca host · Build Mac。本机工作区不显示主机徽章。

打开方式

In-window（窗口内）：看板在侧栏旁边。Pop-out（弹出）：单独一个窗口。

搜索和过滤

看板工具栏里有：

  • Search（搜索）：按工作树、项目或 Agent 名称。有查询或过滤器时显示结果数量。
  • Filter（过滤）：可多选 Project（项目）、Workspace status（工作区状态）和 PR / MR status（PR / MR 状态）。状态值是 Open、Draft、Merged、Closed、No review。
  • 可逐个去掉的过滤标记，以及 Clear all filters（清除全部过滤）。

卡片

  • 页眉： Agent 图标、会话的 conversation name（对话名：重命名、生成的标题、AI Vault / Agent 会话标题，或退回工作树名），以及状态图形。
  • 预览： 有的话显示最后一条用户或 Agent 消息，否则显示任务摘要。
  • 页脚： 项目图标、工作树名（页眉已经是会话名时才显示），以及存在了多久；有缓存的评审状态时也显示。工作区状态只留在工具栏过滤器里，卡片上不再有每棵工作树自己的状态点。
  • Needs You（需要你处理）卡片着琥珀色，并可显示待回答问题的摘要。Done（已完成）卡片着绿色。其他状态保持中性，有颜色就表示「看这里」。
  • 点一张卡片，打开或聚焦那个 Agent 的实时终端。已配对远程 Orca 服务器上的 Agent 也一样，预览来自跑着它们的那台主机。
  • 嵌套的 Codex / Claude 子 Agent 可以出现在父行下面，展开查看。

实验看板关掉时，工作树卡片仍会用同一套图形内嵌 Agent 行。看不到状态标记时，那个会话里的 Agent CLI 不是 Orca 认得的。从 Agent 下拉框启动它，不要手敲二进制。

启动默认值

Orca 启动每个受支持的 Agent 时，都会预先带上它的完全自主权限参数：Claude 用 --dangerously-skip-permissions，Codex 用 --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox，Gemini 用 --yolo，选择器里的其他 Agent 用各自对应的参数。用意是工作树本身就是沙箱：Agent 可以做事，不必被逐项批准打断。

想改某个 Agent 的默认值，打开 Settings → Agents，展开它的那一行，编辑 launch arguments（启动参数）。Orca 会记住这次覆盖，以后每次启动都用它。字段旁边的 Reset（重置）会把出厂参数放回去。

Restart 标记

Agent 退出后（正常退出或崩溃），标签上会出现 Restart（重启）标记。点一下，用同一个工作目录把同一个 Agent 重新拉起来。Codex 的重启标记还会保留当前账号，见热切换 Codex 账号。

会话生命周期

  1. 启动。 从下拉框选一个 Agent，Orca 拉起 CLI。
  2. 工作。 OSC 标题更新状态。终端输出可以搜索、复制，并使用 Ghostty 主题。
  3. 空闲。 Orca 发现从工作中转到空闲，发出Agent 完成通知。
  4. 退出。 进程结束，出现 Restart 标记。

具体的检测规则，见 Orca CLI 里的 terminal wait --for tui-idle。

State dots, restart chips, and the lifecycle of an agent session.

An agent session is one CLI agent running in one terminal in one worktree. Orca tracks its lifecycle so you always know which sessions are working and which are idle — without you having to click into each tab to check.

State indicators

Agent tabs and worktree rows use shared status glyphs:

  • Spinner — working
  • Amber question mark — waiting on you (permission / needs input); sidebar “Needs You” counts use the same glyph
  • Emerald check (dashboard) or emerald dot (sidebar) — done / quiet active
  • Red dot — blocked, interrupted, or failed
  • Gray dot — idle
  • No indicator — plain shell, not a recognized agent CLI

State is detected from the terminal's OSC title sequence and agent hooks, which Claude Code, Codex, and several other agents emit.

Agent dashboard

Turn on Settings → Experimental → Agent Dashboard. Orca adds an Agent Dashboard entry in the left sidebar: a kanban of agents across worktrees.

You can assign a keyboard shortcut to Toggle Agent Dashboard under Settings → Shortcuts. It has no default binding, and works while a terminal, editor, or browser tab has focus.

Columns

  • Needs You — waiting on a permission or question
  • Working — actively running
  • Done — finished sessions you may still want to review
  • Idle — quiet agents that have not reported completion for about 30 minutes. Hidden by default on the board; enable Show idle agents from the dashboard board settings control (gear) on the in-window or pop-out surface — not from global Experimental settings. In-window and pop-out stay in sync.

Dashboard cards show a host badge for SSH workspaces and paired Remote Orca Servers. Hover or focus the badge to see the saved host name, such as SSH host · openclaw or Remote Orca host · Build Mac. Local workspaces do not show a host badge.

Open as

In-window board beside the sidebar, or Pop-out in a separate window.

Search and filters

The board toolbar includes:

  • Search — worktree, project, or agent name (result count shows when a query or filter is active)
  • Filter — multi-select Project, Workspace status, and PR / MR status (Open, Draft, Merged, Closed, No review)
  • Removable filter chips and Clear all filters

Cards

  • Header — agent icon, session conversation name (rename, generated title, AI Vault / agent session title, or fallback to the worktree name), state glyph
  • Preview — last user / agent message when available; otherwise the task summary
  • Footer — project icon, worktree name (when the header already shows a session name), and age; cached review state when present. Workspace status stays in the toolbar filter only — cards no longer show a per-worktree status dot.
  • Needs You cards tint amber (and can show the pending question summary); Done cards tint green; other states stay neutral so tint means “look here”
  • Click a card to open/focus that agent’s live terminal — including agents on a paired Remote Orca Server, whose preview comes from the host that runs them
  • Nested Codex/Claude subagents can appear as expandable children under the parent row

Worktree cards still inline agent rows with the same glyphs when the experimental board is off. If you don't see status indicators, the agent CLI in that session isn't one Orca recognizes — start it through the agent combobox rather than typing the binary by hand.

Launch defaults

Orca launches every supported agent with its full-autonomy permission flag pre-applied — Claude with --dangerously-skip-permissions, Codex with --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox, Gemini with --yolo, and the equivalent for each other agent in the picker. The intent is that the worktree itself is the sandbox: agents can do their thing without per-tool approval prompts breaking the flow.

If you want different defaults for an agent, open Settings → Agents, expand the agent's row, and edit its launch arguments — Orca remembers the override and applies it on every subsequent launch. A Reset button next to the field puts the shipped flag back if you want to revert.

Restart chip

When an agent exits (clean or crash), the tab shows a Restart chip. One click rehydrates the same agent with the same working directory. Codex's restart chip also preserves the current account (see Hot-swap Codex accounts).

Session lifecycle

  1. Launch — pick an agent from the combobox; Orca spawns the CLI.
  2. Work — OSC titles update state; terminal output scrolls with search, copy, and Ghostty theming.
  3. Idle — Orca detects the working→idle transition and fires an agent-finished notification.
  4. Exit — the process ends; the Restart chip appears.

For the exact detection rules, see the terminal wait --for tui-idle command in the Orca CLI.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/model/agents-sessions

上一章Tabs, panes & split layouts 下一章Session restore

Start Here · The Orca Model

会话恢复

Session restore

退出 Orca 再打开，会回到离开时的样子：工作树、分屏、回滚缓冲，以及当时聚焦的标签。

关掉 Orca 之后，下次启动会把整个工作区装回来：每棵打开的工作树、每一处分屏、每个窗格里的回滚缓冲，还有当时聚焦的标签。你不必记住哪个 Agent 跑在哪条分支上。

会恢复的内容

  • 打开的工作树。 侧栏里当时打开的每一棵。
  • 标签和分屏。 每棵工作树的窗格布局，包括嵌套分屏，以及哪个标签有焦点。
  • 正在跑的 Agent。 退出 Orca 时，正在跑的 Agent 继续跑。后台守护进程持有各个终端的 PTY，关掉窗口不会中途杀掉 Claude Code、Codex 或其他 Agent CLI。下次启动时，Orca 会重新接上同一批进程。
  • 终端回滚缓冲。 每个终端里的缓冲，包括 Orca 关闭期间新产生的输出。
  • 聚焦的工作树和标签。 打开后就是你关掉时的那个视图。

在 macOS 和 Linux 上，如果某个终端的 shell 已经停了，Orca 可以恢复它保存的输出，并在下面放一个新提示符。窗格也会记住上次 cd 到的目录，包括文件夹工作区，重启后仍然有效。

整机重启之后

守护进程跟着主机关掉。重启、系统更新、内核恐慌、硬断电，都会停掉每一个正在跑的 Agent。下次启动时，工作树、标签、分屏和最后记下的回滚缓冲还会回来，Agent 进程本身没有了。在任意标签里重新启动 Agent，就可以接着做。

Orca 已经关掉、守护进程自己崩溃时，它当时持有的会话同样会停。布局和回滚缓冲仍会在下次启动时恢复。

什么时候恢复

每次启动都会做会话恢复。差别只在这段空档里守护进程还在不在。

守护进程还在，Agent 继续跑：

  • Cmd-Q。 正常退出。Agent 在后台继续工作。
  • 自动更新后重新打开。 Orca 为了装更新而重启，Agent 不受影响。
  • 应用崩溃。 Orca 自己崩溃时，守护进程仍留着这些会话，下次启动可以重新接上。

守护进程没了，Agent 停了，布局仍会恢复：

  • 主机重启。 笔记本重启、系统更新、内核恐慌、硬断电。下次启动时，工作树、标签、分屏和最后持久化的回滚缓冲仍会回来。

想从空白开始

退出之前先明确关掉工作树。没有「以新会话打开」这种模式，Orca 总会恢复。关掉的工作树保持关闭。

下一步

  • Agent 与会话：状态点和 Agent 会话的生命周期。
  • 标签、窗格与分屏：你将要恢复的那套布局是怎么搭出来的。

Quit Orca, reopen it, and pick up exactly where you left off — worktrees, splits, scrollback, focused tab.

When you close Orca, the next launch rehydrates the whole workspace: every open worktree, every terminal split, the scrollback in each pane, and the tab you had focused. You shouldn't have to remember which agents you had running on which branches — that's Orca's job.

What gets restored

  • Open worktrees — every worktree you had open in the sidebar.
  • Tabs and splits — pane layout per worktree, including nested splits and which tab was focused.
  • Running agent processes — agents you had running keep running across an Orca quit. A background daemon owns the PTYs, so closing the app window doesn't kill Claude Code, Codex, or any other agent CLI mid-task. On next launch, Orca warm-reattaches to the same processes.
  • Terminal scrollback — the buffer in each terminal, including output produced while Orca was closed.
  • Focused worktree and tab — Orca opens to the same view you closed.

On macOS and Linux, if a terminal's shell has stopped, Orca can restore its saved output with a new prompt below it. Panes also remember the last directory you changed to across restarts, including in folder workspaces.

What doesn't get restored

The daemon dies when the host does, so anything that takes the whole machine down — reboot, OS update, kernel panic, hard power-off — ends every running agent. On next launch the worktrees, tabs, splits, and last-known scrollback come back, but the agent processes themselves are gone. Re-run the agent in any tab to keep going.

A daemon crash while Orca is closed has the same effect for any sessions it was holding, but the layout and scrollback still restore on next launch.

When restore runs

Session restore runs on every launch. The cases divide by whether the daemon survived the gap or not:

Daemon survives → agents keep running:

  • Cmd-Q — the normal way to quit. Agents keep working in the background.
  • Auto-updater relaunch — Orca restarts to install an update; agents are unaffected.
  • App crash — if Orca itself crashes, the daemon keeps your sessions alive for warm reattach on next launch.

Daemon dies → agents are gone, layout still restores:

  • Host reboot — laptop restart, OS update, kernel panic, hard power-off. Worktrees, tabs, splits, and the last-persisted scrollback still come back on next launch.
Starting fresh

If you want a clean slate, close worktrees explicitly before quitting. There is no "open in a new session" mode — Orca always restores. Closed worktrees stay closed.

Next steps

  • Agents & sessions — state dots and the lifecycle of an agent session.
  • Tabs, panes & split layouts — how the layout you'll be restoring is built in the first place.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/model/session-restore

上一章Agents & sessions 下一章Quick Open & Jump Palette

Start Here · The Orca Model

快速打开与工作树跳转面板

Quick Open & Jump Palette

工作树一多，找路就成了瓶颈。Orca 带了两个以键盘为主的导航工具。

快速打开（Cmd-P）

只在当前工作树里搜文件。输入一段文字，Orca 按最近使用加上匹配分数排序，在新的编辑器标签里打开。分成多段的查询可以分别命中路径的不同部分。结果如果带了位置，会打开文件并跳到那里。放弃的搜索会被取消，过期结果不会进列表。结果行先显示文件名，空间紧时截断父目录（悬停看完整路径）。被 Git 忽略的文件也在结果里：它们排在已跟踪匹配之后的第二轮，所以你常要快速打开的构建产物、env 文件仍找得到，又不会占满列表顶部。

新标签输入框（+）

标签条上的 + 输入框，在一个字段里搜已打开的标签、文件、URL 和 Agent。占位是 Search open tabs, files, URLs, agents…。文件行和快速打开一样，先显示文件名。已经打开的编辑器标签如果匹配上，优先用那个标签，不再给同一文件开第二份。

用图标行旁边的齿轮，可以重排新标签动作、隐藏选项，或指定快捷键。隐藏的动作仍能通过搜索和快捷键使用。

+ 输入框也会搜这棵工作树的浏览器历史。匹配的访问以 URL 和页面标题出现，按匹配程度和访问时间排序。

输入的是网页搜索、不是路径或 URL 时，会用你的 Default Search Engine（默认搜索引擎）在工作树浏览器里打开。单个词仍优先排文件匹配。多个词的短语会把搜索那一行提前。查询以 ? 开头时，跳过文件和标签匹配，立刻搜索。

工作树跳转面板（Cmd-J）

一次搜索里跨全部工作树和全部标签跳转。空输入的占位是 repo/worktree。输入任意一半，Orca 按那一半过滤。一开始打字，搜索就包括未归档的工作树，即使侧栏当前的过滤器把它们藏了起来。Slack 风格的 emoji 短码（:rocket:）用和工作区命名一样的建议浮层。

在面板里按 Tab，打开主机和项目的过滤菜单。选中的主机和项目会缩小结果，并显示成可以逐个去掉的标记。关掉面板会清掉过滤，下次打开不带范围。

结果包括：

  • Recent Chats & Terminals（最近的对话和终端）。查询为空时，最多六条最近的 Agent / 终端会话，按活动排序：先是需要你处理，然后是已完成，然后是空闲。你正在看的那个空闲标签会被省略，列表保持可操作。当前标签如果正在工作、在等你，或有未读，仍会出现。行上的标记和标签栏一样：转圈、问号、铃铛、对勾。还有更多最近会话时，点 See more（查看更多）展开完整列表。数字快捷键（macOS 为 Cmd-1–Cmd-6，Windows / Linux 为 Ctrl-1–Ctrl-6）直接跳到最前面的几行。面板一打开，有哪些行、什么顺序就固定，光标下面的行不会自己换位。
  • Recent Worktrees（最近的工作树）。空查询的下一组，按上次聚焦排序，有上限，方便扫读。
  • 项目和仓库组。按名字跳到侧栏的某一组。
  • 一开始打字，每棵工作树按仓库分组列出。
  • 已有缓存时，可以按 GitHub PR 的标题或编号（#123）、GitLab MR 的标题或编号（!123）匹配工作树。
  • 每个已打开的标签。先当前工作树，再全局。标题或内容里的匹配，排在只命中工作树、分支或仓库的匹配前面。同样强的匹配，优先你更近用过的标签。终端、浏览器和模拟器行，把仓库和工作树收成一枚紧凑的位置标记；较长的次要路径从开头省略，文件名和匹配到的结尾留得住。Orca 有最后活动时间时，行上会显示。输入 terminal 或 simulator 这类别名，仍能匹配那些标签类型，行标签本身不会因此变杂。

输入的查询同时命中已打开标签和工作树时，面板会把每一组的短预览交错排出来，两组主列表都不会被埋住。某一组还有更多匹配时，点它那行 N more 上的 See more（查看更多），一次再显示 20 条。改查询会收起已展开的组。只有一组结果时，保留一份有硬上限的完整列表。

在工作树上按 Shift-Enter，会在新分屏里打开它，而不是换掉当前窗格。

查询对不上任何现有工作树时，面板给出一行 Create worktree（创建工作树），用你输入的文字当名字。已有的匹配仍优先选中，所以还有真实结果时，按 Enter 仍是跳转。

快捷键可以在 Settings → Shortcuts 里改。

Cmd-J scoped jump across worktrees, recents, and tabs.

Once you have more than a handful of worktrees, navigation becomes the bottleneck. Orca ships two keyboard-first navigation tools.

Quick Open (Cmd-P)

File search scoped to the current worktree. Type a fragment; Orca ranks by recency plus match score and opens the file in a new editor tab. A multi-part query matches across path segments, and a result that names a location opens the file there. An abandoned search is canceled so stale results do not land in the list. Result rows lead with the filename and truncate the parent directory when space is tight (hover for the full path). Gitignored files are included in results — they're surfaced as a second pass after tracked matches, so the files you frequently quick-open (build outputs, env files) stay reachable without polluting the top of the list.

New-tab omnibox (+)

The tab strip + omnibox searches open tabs, files, URLs, and agents in one field (placeholder: Search open tabs, files, URLs, agents…). File rows use the same filename-first layout as Quick Open. Matching an already-open editor tab prefers that tab over a duplicate file result, so you jump to the open buffer instead of opening a second copy.

Use the gear beside the icon row to reorder new-tab actions, hide choices, or assign shortcuts. Hidden actions remain available through search and their shortcuts.

The + omnibox also searches this worktree's browser history. Matching visits appear as URL and page-title suggestions, ranked by how closely they match and how recently you visited them.

Type a web search instead of a path or URL to open it in the worktree browser with your Default Search Engine. A single token still ranks file matches first; a multi-word phrase promotes the search row. Prefix the query with ? to skip file and tab matching and search immediately.

Worktree Jump Palette (Cmd-J)

Jump across every worktree and every tab in one search. The placeholder in the empty input reads repo/worktree — type either half and Orca filters accordingly. Once you start typing, search includes non-archived worktrees even if they are hidden by the sidebar's current filters. Slack-style emoji shortcodes (:rocket:) use the same suggestion popover as workspace naming.

Press Tab in the palette for a host and project filter menu. Selected hosts and projects narrow the result set and show as chips you can remove one at a time; closing the palette clears the filter so the next open is unscoped.

Results include:

  • Recent Chats & Terminals — with an empty query, up to six recent agent/terminal sessions ranked by activity (needs-you first, then done, then idle). The idle tab you're already viewing is omitted so the list stays actionable; a current tab still appears when it is working, waiting on you, or has unread activity. Rows use the same live attention badges as the tab bar (spinner, question mark, bell, check). If more recent sessions are available, click See more to expand the full list; digit shortcuts (Cmd-1–Cmd-6 on macOS, Ctrl-1–Ctrl-6 on Windows / Linux) jump straight to the first rows. Membership and order freeze when the palette opens so rows don't shuffle under the cursor.
  • Recent Worktrees — the next empty-query section, ordered by last focus, capped so the list stays scannable.
  • Projects and repo groups, so you can jump to a sidebar section by name.
  • Every worktree grouped by repo once you start typing.
  • Worktrees matched by cached GitHub PR title or number (#123) and cached GitLab merge request title or number (!123) when that review metadata is already available.
  • Every open tab, scoped first by current worktree, then globally. A match in the tab's title or content ranks ahead of a match only in its worktree, branch, or repo; equally strong matches prefer the tab you used more recently. Terminal, browser, and simulator rows combine the repo and worktree into a compact location chip, while long secondary paths elide from the beginning so filenames and matched endings stay visible. Rows show the last-active age when Orca has one. Type aliases such as terminal or simulator still match those tab types without cluttering the row label.

When a typed query hits both open tabs and worktrees, the palette interleaves a short preview of each section so neither primary list is buried. If a section has more matches, click See more in its N more row to reveal 20 additional entries at a time. Changing the query resets the expanded sections. Single-section results keep a full hard-capped list.

Shift-Enter on a worktree opens it in a new split instead of swapping the current pane.

When the query does not match an existing worktree, the palette offers a Create worktree row using the typed text as the name. Existing matches stay selected first, so pressing Enter still jumps when a real result is available.

Shortcut bindings are remappable under Settings → Shortcuts.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/model/quick-open

上一章Session restore 下一章Supported agents

Start Here · Working with Agents

受支持的 Agent

Supported agents

Orca 预置的 Agent，开箱就能从下拉框启动。

Orca 能跑任何 CLI Agent。Agent 下拉框只是在终端里拉起一个进程。下面这些已经配在内置下拉框里，可以一键启动或完成初始配置。钩子、状态、用量和账号切换只在支持的 Agent 上提供，表里会注明。

默认权限

新启动时，Orca 会给每个受支持的 CLI 预填跳过确认的参数：

  • Claude：--dangerously-skip-permissions
  • Codex：--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox
  • Gemini、Cursor、Crush、Kimi、Rovo Dev、Hermes、GitHub Copilot、Command Code：--yolo
  • 其他 Agent 如果有等价 flag，也用它们自己的

工作树用完可以丢掉。Agent 在自己的检出里试命令，不必每条都再确认。合并之前，你仍可以挑选要留的 diff，或整份丢掉。

没改过启动参数的终端 Agent，要在 Yolo 和 Manual 之间切换，用 Settings → Agents → Agent Permissions。新的 Claude、Codex 终端会话和结构化对话，默认模式也在这里设。已经打开的对话保持自己的模式。某个 Agent 的启动参数或环境变量如果已经被你改过，Orca 不动它。全局开关不会清掉你的自定义命令。

只想让某一个 Agent 重新弹出确认：到 Settings 里改这个 Agent 的默认参数或环境变量。自定义值只要非空，Orca 就当成明确覆盖。以后再迁移权限模式时，不再改这个 Agent。

Every agent Orca ships with out of the box.

Orca works with any CLI agent — the agent combobox just launches a process in a terminal. The following ship preconfigured in the built-in agent picker with one-click launch/setup; deeper hooks, status, usage tracking, and account switching are noted where supported.

Permissions default

Orca pre-fills each supported CLI's permission-bypass flag for new launches — --dangerously-skip-permissions for Claude, --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox for Codex, --yolo for Gemini / Cursor / Crush / Kimi / Rovo Dev / Hermes / GitHub Copilot / Command Code, plus the equivalent flag for every other agent that exposes one. The reasoning is that worktrees are disposable: an agent running in its own checkout can experiment without you re-confirming every shell command, and you can still cherry-pick or discard the diff before merging.

Use Settings → Agents → Agent Permissions when you want to switch uncustomized terminal-agent launches between Yolo and Manual. Set the default for new Claude and Codex terminal and structured-chat sessions there as well; an existing chat keeps its own mode. If you already overrode a specific agent's launch arguments or environment, Orca leaves that agent alone so the global switch doesn't erase your custom command.

To restore prompts for one agent only, edit that agent's default arguments or environment in Settings. Orca treats a non-empty custom value as an explicit override and opts that agent out of future permission-mode migrations.
