上一章Quick Open & Jump Palette 下一章Claude Code in Orca

Start Here · Working with Agents

Orca 里的 Claude Code

Claude Code in Orca

Claude Code 是 Anthropic 的 Agent CLI。Orca 把它当成一等的终端 Agent 来跑：启动时认当前账号，跟踪用量，也可以热切换账号。

安装

  1. 安装 Claude Code（npm i -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code，或按 Anthropic 的文档）。
  2. 在任意终端登录一次。
  3. Orca 会自动读到 ~/.claude，不用再配。

启动

在任意工作树里打开终端，从 Agent 下拉框选 Claude Code。Orca 以这棵工作树为工作目录启动它，并挂上一条状态行钩子。钩子发出 OSC 标题事件，Orca 用它画状态点。

用量与速率限制

Orca 读取本机 ~/.claude 里的用量状态，在状态栏显示当前用量，以及离速率限制还有多远。见用量与速率限制。

热切换账号

Orca 支持多个 Claude 账号，一点就能切换，做法和 Codex 相同。已有 Claude 会话还在跑时也可以换。进行中的切换会被一道防护挡住，避免两次登录刷新叠在一起。切换会保留共用的 MCP 服务器登录态，换完不用重新登录 MCP。见热切换 Codex 账号。Claude 的步骤和那一页相同。

子 Agent 与团队

后台子 Agent，以及 Agent Teams 里的队友，可以显示在主 Agent 下面，作为可展开的子行。工作树的 Agent 列表和 Agent 看板 都是这样。选中子行，在主终端里看它的记录。再选父行就回去。眼睛图标标出当前显示的那份记录。Claude 状态钩子可用时，这项导航默认打开。想让主终端一直留在画面里，到 Settings → Agents → Claude subagent navigation（Claude 子 Agent 导航）关掉。改完这项之后，新开一次 Claude 会话。

钩子与记忆

Claude Code 支持按仓库配置的钩子和记忆文件。Orca 把它们放在Agent 钩子与记忆里。

Claude Code is Anthropic's agentic CLI. Orca runs it as a first-class terminal agent, with account-aware session start, usage tracking, and account hot-swapping.

Setup

  1. Install Claude Code (npm i -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code or follow Anthropic's docs).
  2. Log in once from any terminal.
  3. Orca picks up ~/.claude automatically — no extra config needed.

Launching

In any worktree, open a terminal and pick Claude Code from the agent combobox. Orca launches it with the worktree as the working directory and a status-line hook that emits OSC title events Orca uses for state dots.

Usage & rate limits

Orca reads your local ~/.claude usage state and shows current usage plus rate-limit proximity in the status bar. See Usage & rate-limit tracking.

Account hot-swap

Orca supports multiple Claude accounts and can swap between them in one click, matching the Codex flow. Switching accounts works even with live Claude sessions running — Orca holds the in-progress switch behind a guard so you don't trigger overlapping auth refreshes. A swap keeps your shared MCP server sign-ins, so you don't have to re-authenticate MCP servers after switching. See Hot-swap Codex accounts — the Claude flow is identical in shape.

Subagents and teams

Background subagents and Agent Teams teammates can show as expandable child rows under the lead agent in the worktree agent list and Agent Dashboard. Select a child to view its transcript in the lead terminal, then select the parent to return; an eye marks the transcript currently shown. This navigation is on by default when Claude status hooks are available. Turn it off under Settings → Agents → Claude subagent navigation if you prefer the lead terminal to stay in view. Start a fresh Claude session after changing this setting.

Hooks & memory

Claude Code supports per-repo hooks and memory files. Orca surfaces these under Agent hooks & memory.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/claude-code

上一章Supported agents 下一章How to use GLM-5.2 in Orca ADE

Start Here · Working with Agents

在 Orca 里使用 GLM-5.2

How to use GLM-5.2 in Orca ADE

先在你已经在用的 CLI Agent 里配好 GLM-5.2，再从 Orca 的 Agent 下拉框启动。模型访问来自你的订阅，不来自 Orca。

GLM-5.2 通过 Claude Code、OpenCode、Cline、Kilo Code、Roo Code、Droid、OpenClaw 或其他 CLI Agent 跑在 Orca 里。

Orca 提供隔离的工作树、终端窗格、浏览器标签、审阅流程和会话管理。模型访问来自你的 Z.ai CodePlan 订阅 和 Agent 自己的配置。

先决条件

在 Agent 里配置 GLM-5.2 之前，需要一份有效的 Z.ai CodePlan 订阅，并且带 GLM Coding Plan 权限。兼容 OpenAI 接口的 CLI Agent 还需要一把 Z.ai API key。Orca 不附带、也不转售 GLM 访问权限。

来源

下面的设置依照 Z.ai 的最新模型指南。提供方一侧的现行细节以那一页为准。

Claude Code

Claude Code 从 ~/.claude/settings.json 读取模型覆盖。要在 Orca 里用 GLM-5.2：

  1. 打开 ~/.claude/settings.json。
  2. 加上或更新下面的 env 块。
  3. 在 Orca 里重启 Claude Code 会话，让新环境生效。
  4. 在 Claude Code 里跑 /status，确认当前模型。
{
  "env": {
    "CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW": "1000000",
    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL": "glm-4.5-air",
    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL": "glm-5.2[1m]",
    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL": "glm-5.2[1m]"
  }
}

要用 GLM-5.2 的 1M 上下文变体，模型名带上 [1m]，并把 CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW 设为 1000000，让 Claude Code 的压缩窗口和这个上下文一样大。如果 Claude Code 说带 [1m] 的模型不存在，先更新 Claude Code，再试一次。

写代码时，Z.ai 建议用 /effort 把 Claude Code 的 effort 设为 max。Claude Code 里较低的 effort 会对应 GLM-5.2 的 high。xhigh、max 和 ultracode 对应 GLM-5.2 的 max。

OpenCode、Cline、Kilo Code、Roo Code 和 Droid

这些 CLI Agent 如果提供兼容 OpenAI 的提供方，按下面做：

  1. 在 Orca 里面或外面打开该 Agent 的设置。
  2. 选兼容 OpenAI 的提供方。这个 Agent 如果有 Z.ai 提供方，也可以直接选它。
  3. 把 base URL 设为 https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4。
  4. 填上你的 Z.ai API key。
  5. 把自定义模型名设为 glm-5.2。
  6. 如果有上下文字段，把上下文窗口大小设为 1000000。
  7. 关掉图片支持。只有该 Agent 明确写了这条提供方路径支持图片时，才打开。

保存后，从 Orca 的 Agent 下拉框启动它。Orca 会在所选工作树里跑这份已经配好的 CLI。

OpenClaw

如果 OpenClaw 的提供方模型列表里不能直接选 GLM-5.2，就在 ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json 里手工加上。

把 glm-5.2 加进 models.providers.zai.models：

{
  "id": "glm-5.2",
  "name": "GLM-5.2",
  "reasoning": true,
  "input": ["text"],
  "cost": {
    "input": 0,
    "output": 0,
    "cacheRead": 0,
    "cacheWrite": 0
  },
  "contextWindow": 1000000,
  "maxTokens": 131072
}

再设默认主模型：

{
  "model": {
    "primary": "zai/glm-5.2",
    "fallbacks": ["zai/glm-4.7"]
  }
}

再把模型写进 agents.defaults.models：

{
  "models": {
    "zai/glm-5.2": { "alias": "GLM" },
    "zai/glm-4.7": {}
  }
}

重启 OpenClaw gateway：

openclaw gateway restart

然后从 Orca 启动 OpenClaw，或在 Orca 终端里跑 openclaw tui，确认 GLM-5.2 已生效。

规则就一条：在这个 Agent 保存提供方和模型的地方配好 GLM-5.2，再让 Orca 在对应的工作树里启动它。

Configure Claude Code and other CLI agent harnesses to run GLM-5.2 inside Orca worktrees.

GLM-5.2 works in Orca through the agent harness you already use. Configure GLM-5.2 in Claude Code, OpenCode, Cline, Kilo Code, Roo Code, Droid, OpenClaw, or another CLI agent, then launch that agent from Orca's picker.

Orca supplies the isolated worktree, terminal panes, browser tab, review flow, and session management. Your Z.ai CodePlan subscription and agent config supply the model access.

Prerequisite

You need an active Z.ai CodePlan subscription with GLM Coding Plan access before configuring GLM-5.2 in an agent harness. OpenAI-compatible harnesses also need a Z.ai API key. Orca does not include or resell GLM access.

Source

These settings follow Z.ai's latest model guide. Check that page for the current provider-side details.

Claude Code

Claude Code reads model overrides from ~/.claude/settings.json. To use GLM-5.2 in Orca:

  1. Open ~/.claude/settings.json.
  2. Add or update the env block below.
  3. Restart Claude Code sessions in Orca so the new environment is loaded.
  4. Run /status inside Claude Code to confirm the active model.
{
  "env": {
    "CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW": "1000000",
    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL": "glm-4.5-air",
    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL": "glm-5.2[1m]",
    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL": "glm-5.2[1m]"
  }
}

Use the [1m] suffix when you want GLM-5.2's 1M context variant, and keep CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW set to 1000000 so Claude Code's compaction window matches that context size. If Claude Code says the [1m] model does not exist, update Claude Code and try again.

For coding tasks, Z.ai recommends setting Claude Code effort to max with /effort. Claude Code's lower effort settings map to GLM-5.2 high effort, while xhigh, max, and ultracode map to GLM-5.2 max effort.

OpenCode, Cline, Kilo Code, Roo Code, and Droid

For agent harnesses that expose an OpenAI-compatible provider:

  1. Open the harness settings outside or inside Orca.
  2. Choose the OpenAI-compatible provider option, or the Z.ai provider option if the harness has one.
  3. Set the base URL to https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4.
  4. Add your Z.ai API key.
  5. Set the custom model name to glm-5.2.
  6. Set context window size to 1000000 if the harness exposes that field.
  7. Disable image support unless the harness specifically documents image support for this provider path.

After saving, launch the harness from Orca's agent picker. Orca will run the same configured CLI in the selected worktree.

OpenClaw

If OpenClaw cannot select GLM-5.2 directly from its provider model list, add the model manually in ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json.

Add glm-5.2 to models.providers.zai.models:

{
  "id": "glm-5.2",
  "name": "GLM-5.2",
  "reasoning": true,
  "input": ["text"],
  "cost": {
    "input": 0,
    "output": 0,
    "cacheRead": 0,
    "cacheWrite": 0
  },
  "contextWindow": 1000000,
  "maxTokens": 131072
}

Then set the default primary model:

{
  "model": {
    "primary": "zai/glm-5.2",
    "fallbacks": ["zai/glm-4.7"]
  }
}

And add the model under agents.defaults.models:

{
  "models": {
    "zai/glm-5.2": { "alias": "GLM" },
    "zai/glm-4.7": {}
  }
}

Restart the OpenClaw gateway:

openclaw gateway restart

Then launch OpenClaw from Orca or run openclaw tui in an Orca terminal to confirm GLM-5.2 is active.

The key rule is simple: configure GLM-5.2 wherever the harness stores provider/model settings, then let Orca launch that harness in the right worktree.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/glm-agent

上一章Claude Code in Orca 下一章Codex in Orca

Start Here · Working with Agents

Orca 里的 Codex

Codex in Orca

Codex 是 OpenAI 的 Agent CLI。它是 Orca 里集成最深的之一：用量、热切换和重启都会保住账号身份。

安装

  1. 按 OpenAI 的文档安装 Codex。
  2. 在任意终端登录一次。
  3. Orca 从 ~/.codex 读取账号和凭据。

启动

在 Agent 下拉框里选 Codex。Orca 以这棵工作树为工作目录启动 codex，登录走当前选中的账号。

热切换账号

很多 Codex 用户用多个账号把速率限制摊开。Orca 的账号切换器可以换当前账号，不用重新登录，也不用改配置。见热切换 Codex 账号。

系统默认与额外账号

System default（系统默认）用你真实的 ~/.codex 登录，也就是在 Orca 外面直接跑 codex 会用的那份主目录。Orca 管理的额外账号，各自使用 Orca 账号数据下的主目录，凭据和 rollout 记录互相隔离。新启动的 Codex 跟随当前账号。已经在跑的会话继续用它启动时的主目录，直到你重启它。

Codex 0.157 及以后

如果 Orca 管理的账号主目录太长，放不下 Codex 后台守护进程的套接字，Orca 会关掉这份主目录上的守护进程，让 Codex 能启动。从 Agent 下拉框启动，或在 Orca 终端里直接输入 codex，都是这样。账号数据留在原地。系统里的 ~/.codex/config.toml 不会被改。

受影响的主目录里，codex agents 以及靠守护进程的恢复、续接都不可用。如果 Codex 仍报 path must be shorter than SUN_LEN，看故障排除。

每个 Codex 终端跑自己的服务

Codex 0.157 及以后，Orca 终端里的每个 Codex 标签默认跑自己的后台服务。这样每个标签的 Agent 状态才准，关掉一个 Codex 标签也不会把别的标签一起断掉。只作用于新终端。更新时已经开着的终端保持原行为，重新打开才变。

  • 按标签分开服务之后，Codex 的共享服务功能看不到在 Orca 里启动的会话。这些功能包括后台运行、codex agents，以及从 Codex 桌面应用或 IDE 打开的会话。
  • 想改回共享 Codex 的服务：打开新终端之前，关掉 Settings → Agents → Run each Codex terminal on its own server（每个 Codex 终端使用自己的服务），或设置 ORCA_CODEX_ISOLATE=0（例如在 shell 启动文件里 export ORCA_CODEX_ISOLATE=0）。共享服务上可以再次使用 Codex 的 agents 总览。
  • 你自己启动的 Codex 加入共享服务时，Orca 在该标签顶部显示一条提示。点 Fix（修复）会关掉共享。如果这个标签是更新前打开的，提示会改为提议新开一个终端。

嵌套的 Task 子 Agent

Codex 拉起 Task 子 Agent 时，Orca 可以在工作树的 Agent 列表和 Agent 看板 里，把它们显示成父 Agent 下面的子行。展开折叠箭头看每个子项。点一个子项，焦点回到父终端。子 Agent 没有自己的窗格。

交给另一个 Agent

在 Agent 终端的标题栏或右键菜单里选 Hand Off to Another Agent（交给另一个 Agent）。Orca 会新开一个 Agent 会话，CLI 可以相同，也可以不同，并把先前的记录或抓到的上下文收成一段范围有限的交接提示，放进新会话。原来的会话保持不动。这不是 codex resume。

重启提示

Codex 退出后，重启提示会用同一个账号再启动。如果会话中途换过账号，又想用新账号重启，先用账号切换器选好，再重启。

用量与速率限制

Orca 读取当前账号在本机的 Codex 用量，显示在状态栏。见用量与速率限制。

Windows 上的 Codex（WSL）

Windows 上的 Codex 和 macOS、Linux 用同一份真实的 ~/.codex 主目录。更新后第一次出现 Codex 终端时，Orca 会显示一次提示：Codex 可能再次要求信任文件夹或批准命令；只在 Orca 里加过的 MCP 服务器要再加一次。更新前已经开着的 Codex 终端，继续用 Orca 以前的主目录，重新打开才换。

在 Windows 上，Codex 可以跑宿主机上装的那份，也可以跑某个 WSL 发行版里的。从账号切换器添加一个放在 WSL 里的 Codex 账号时，Orca 会在该发行版里建一份隔离的账号主目录（~/.local/share/orca/codex-accounts/<id>/home）。读登录信息时，把它映射回宿主机路径 \\wsl.localhost\<distro>\...。启动、热切换和速率限制查询都走这个发行版。目标发行版里没装 Codex 时，Add account（添加账号）对话框会失败，并写明哪个发行版缺这个可执行文件。

Codex is OpenAI's agentic CLI. Orca's Codex integration is one of the deepest in the app — usage, hot-swap, and restart all preserve account identity.

Setup

  1. Install Codex per OpenAI's docs.
  2. Log in from any terminal.
  3. Orca reads ~/.codex for accounts and credentials.

Launching

Pick Codex from the agent combobox. Orca launches codex with the worktree as cwd and routes auth through the currently selected account.

Account hot-swap

Many Codex users run multiple accounts to stretch rate limits. Orca's account switcher swaps the active account without re-login or config edits. See Hot-swap Codex accounts.

System default vs extra accounts

System default uses your real ~/.codex login (the same home a bare codex outside Orca would use). Extra accounts Orca manages get their own home under Orca's account data so credentials and rollouts stay isolated. New Codex launches follow the active account; already-running sessions keep the home they started with until you restart them.

Codex 0.157 and later

If an Orca-managed account home is too long for Codex's background daemon socket, Orca turns off that daemon for the home so Codex can start. This applies whether you launch Codex from the agent picker or type codex in an Orca terminal. Orca keeps the account data in place and does not change your system ~/.codex/config.toml.

In affected homes, codex agents and daemon-backed resume or continuation are unavailable. If Codex still reports path must be shorter than SUN_LEN, see Troubleshooting.

Each Codex terminal runs its own server

On Codex 0.157 and later, each Codex tab in an Orca terminal runs its own background server by default. That keeps agent status accurate for each tab and prevents closing one Codex tab from dropping the others. It applies to new terminals; terminals already open when you update keep their previous behavior until you reopen them.

  • The trade-off: with per-tab servers, Codex's shared-server features (background runs, codex agents, and sessions opened from the Codex desktop app or an IDE) do not see sessions started in Orca.
  • To share Codex's server instead, turn off Settings → Agents → Run each Codex terminal on its own server before opening new terminals, or set ORCA_CODEX_ISOLATE=0 (for example export ORCA_CODEX_ISOLATE=0 in your shell startup file). Codex's agents overview is available again on the shared server.
  • When a Codex you started yourself joins the shared server, Orca shows a notice at the top of that tab. Its Fix turns sharing off, and on a tab opened before the update it offers to open a new terminal instead.

Nested Task subagents

When Codex spawns Task subagents, Orca can show them as child rows under the parent agent on the worktree agent list and Agent Dashboard. Expand the chevron to see each child; click a child to focus the parent terminal (subagents do not own a separate pane).

Hand off to another agent

From an agent terminal's header or context menu, choose Hand Off to Another Agent. Orca starts a fresh agent session (same or different CLI) and injects a bounded handoff prompt from the prior transcript or captured context. The original session is left alone — this is not codex resume.

Restart chip

When Codex exits, the restart chip relaunches the agent with the same account. If you swapped accounts mid-session and want to restart with the new one, use the account switcher first, then restart.

Usage & rate limits

Orca reads local Codex usage state for the active account and surfaces it in the status bar. See Usage & rate-limit tracking.

Codex on Windows (WSL)

Windows Codex runs on the same real ~/.codex home as macOS and Linux. The first time a Codex terminal appears after you update, Orca shows a one-time notice: Codex may ask again to trust folders or approve commands, and any MCP servers you added only inside Orca need to be added again. Codex terminals that were already open keep running on Orca's previous home until you reopen them.

On Windows, Orca can run Codex either from your host install or from a WSL distro. Add a WSL-hosted Codex account from the account switcher — Orca creates an isolated account home inside the distro (under ~/.local/share/orca/codex-accounts/<id>/home), maps it back to the host as a \\wsl.localhost\<distro>\... path for auth reads, and routes launches, hot-swap, and rate-limit fetches through the selected distro. If Codex isn't installed in the target distro, the Add account dialog fails with an actionable message telling you which distro is missing the binary.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/codex

上一章How to use GLM-5.2 in Orca ADE 下一章Cursor CLI in Orca

Start Here · Working with Agents

Orca 里的 Cursor CLI

Cursor CLI in Orca

Cursor CLI 是 Cursor 的命令行 Agent。Orca 一等支持它：从下拉框启动，完整识别 OSC 状态，退出后有重启提示。

安装

  1. 按 Cursor 的文档 安装 Cursor CLI。
  2. 登录一次。
  3. Orca 会在 PATH 上自动找到这个 CLI。

启动

在下拉框里选 Cursor。Orca 启动 CLI 时，工作目录就是这棵工作树。Cursor 的 TUI 会发出 Orca 画状态点所需的状态事件。

选择模型

在终端里，模型在 Cursor CLI 内部选。在对话界面里，从输入区选择模型和可用的 effort、Fast（快速）选项。Cursor 对话使用你现有的 Cursor 登录。没登录时选 Sign in（登录），走登录流程。

Orca 还会在状态栏和 Settings → Accounts → Cursor 里，显示这份登录对应的 Cursor 套餐用量。见用量与速率限制。

Cursor CLI is Cursor's command-line agent. Orca runs it with first-class support — launch from the combobox, full OSC state detection, and restart chip on exit.

Setup

  1. Install Cursor CLI per Cursor's docs.
  2. Log in once.
  3. Orca auto-detects the CLI on PATH.

Launching

Pick Cursor from the combobox. Orca launches the CLI scoped to the worktree. Cursor's TUI emits the state events Orca needs for agent state dots.

Model selection

In a terminal, configure model selection inside Cursor CLI. In Chat UI, choose a model and its available effort and Fast options from the composer. Cursor Chat uses your existing Cursor sign-in; if signed out, choose Sign in to run the login flow.

Orca also shows your Cursor plan usage from the existing Cursor sign-in in the status bar and Settings → Accounts → Cursor. See Usage & rate-limit tracking.

上一章Codex in Orca 下一章Hot-swap Codex accounts

Start Here · Working with Agents

热切换 Codex 账号

Hot-swap Codex accounts

一点就能换掉当前 Codex 账号。不用重新登录，也不用改配置。Claude Code 账号走同一套做法。

用多个 Codex 账号把 token 用满，是常见做法。

添加账号

  1. 每个 Codex 账号至少在终端里登录一次，让登录信息落在 ~/.codex 下。
  2. 打开 Settings → Agents → Codex Accounts。
  3. Orca 列出检测到的全部账号，以及各自的用量和当前限额。
  4. 给每个账号起一个好认的标签，例如 personal、work。

切换账号

点状态栏上的 Codex 标记，打开账号切换器。选一个账号。之后新开的 Codex 会话都用它。已经在跑的会话保持原账号，直到重启。

系统默认

System default（系统默认）这一行，是宿主机上当前的 Codex 登录，目录是 ~/.codex。在 Orca 里添加的账号不会改写这份登录，它们在各自隔离的主目录里跑。想让启动结果和 Orca 外面的终端 codex 一致时，选 System default。

改了配置却像没生效

对 Orca 管理的 Codex 账号，Orca 会把真实的 ~/.codex/config.toml 镜像到当前运行用的主目录。源文件缺失、是空的（例如云同步还在下载），或读不出来时，Accounts（账号）会显示警告：在源文件恢复正常之前，Codex 继续用上次成功同步的设置。按横幅里写的路径修好文件，然后重新启动，或重新选一次这个账号。

规则与容易踩的点

  • 切换是即时的。Orca 改的是当前生效的凭据指向，不会重新登录。
  • 已经在跑的 Codex 进程保持当前账号，直到重启。
  • 状态栏上的用量读数跟随当前账号。
  • 重启提示会保住重启那一刻的当前账号。

Claude Code 账号

Claude 的账号切换器做法相同。数据目录换成 ~/.claude，界面一样。

Running multiple Codex accounts to maximize tokens is common. Orca lets you hot-swap the active account in one click, with no re-login and no config editing. The same flow works for Claude Code accounts.

Add accounts

  1. Log into each Codex account from a terminal at least once, so the auth sits under ~/.codex.
  2. Open Settings → Agents → Codex Accounts.
  3. Orca lists all detected accounts with their usage and current limit.
  4. Give each one a friendly label — "personal", "work", etc.

Swap accounts

Click the Codex chip in the status bar to open the account switcher. Pick an account; any new Codex session launched after that uses it. Sessions already running keep their original account until restarted.

System default

The System default row is your current host Codex login under ~/.codex. Managed accounts (added in Orca) do not rewrite that login; they run in isolated homes. Select System default when you want launches to match a terminal codex outside Orca.

When config edits seem ignored

For managed Codex accounts, Orca mirrors settings from your real ~/.codex/config.toml into the active runtime home. If that source file is missing, empty (e.g. cloud-sync still downloading), or unreadable, Accounts shows a warning: Codex keeps the last successfully synced settings until the source is healthy again. Fix the file at the path named in the banner, then relaunch or reselect the account.

Rules & gotchas

  • Swapping is instant — Orca rewrites the active credential pointer, it does not re-authenticate.
  • Existing Codex processes keep their current account until restart.
  • Usage readouts in the status bar follow the currently-active account.
  • The restart chip preserves the active account at the time of restart.

Claude Code accounts

The Claude account switcher works identically — different data directory (~/.claude), same UX.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/codex-hot-swap

上一章Cursor CLI in Orca 下一章Chat UI (native chat)

Start Here · Working with Agents

对话界面（Chat UI）

Chat UI (native chat)

对话界面是受支持 Agent 会话上的一层可选界面。选 Chat 之后，符合条件的新会话会打开成结构化对话：一份记录加上输入区，会话状态留在 Orca 里。应用重启后，标签和对话都能回来。

结构化对话覆盖 Claude、Codex、Grok、OpenCode（含 2.x）、Pi、OMP、Antigravity、GitHub Copilot、Kimi Code、Cursor、Hermes、Kiro、Goose、Auggie、Gemini CLI、Factory Droid、Kilo Code 和 MiMo Code。没有结构化实现的 Agent，以及 WSL、SSH 会话，改用走终端的对话界面。

选 Chat 还是 CLI

到 Settings → Chat UI 或 Settings → Agents，为每个受支持的 Agent 选 Chat 或 CLI。初次设置时可以选 Chat UI 或 Terminal，再选一个 Agent，并为这套配置安装技能。第一次打开时，Chat 是预选的。已经在用的人保持自己的启动偏好。已经开着的对话留在原来的界面上。按 Agent 的这项选择，对从桌面发起的启动都生效，包括 CLI、编排，以及从手机转发过来的启动。没有单独选择的 Agent，也可以设一个默认值。

用标签的右键菜单，或 Agent 会话历史，可以把一段受支持的对话在对话界面和终端之间搬动。

结构化对话

选了 Chat 之后，符合条件的新的本机会话会打开成结构化对话标签，状态留在 Orca 里。应用重启后，标签和对话都能恢复。覆盖的 Agent 与上面那份名单相同。

结构化对话可用于：本机 macOS、Linux、原生 Windows，以及由已配对的 Orca 服务器持有的工作区。WSL 和 SSH 会话继续用走终端的对话界面。没有结构化实现的 Agent 留在终端里。已经开着的终端会话继续是终端会话。

Copilot Chat 需要 Copilot CLI 1.0.88 或更高版本，并且已登录。模型在启动之后才出现。套餐本身不提供模型选择时，用 Auto。不支持把 Copilot Session History 导入 Chat。

Kimi Chat 需要 Kimi Code 2.5.0 或更高版本，并且已登录。

Kiro Chat 需要 Kiro CLI 3.1.0 或更高版本，用的是主机上的 Kiro 登录。

Gemini Chat 需要 Gemini CLI 0.68.0 或更高版本。没登录时，先通过 CLI 登录。

Goose Chat 用主机上配置的提供方和凭据。

Auggie Chat 用主机上的 Auggie 登录，不提供权限模式。

Hermes Chat 需要工作区主机上的 Hermes 0.23.0 或更高版本。从 New Tab（新标签）菜单启动，或在会话历史里对一段终端对话用 Resume in New Chat（在新对话里恢复）。它会在原来的工作区和配置档里恢复。导入很长时，先显示最近的消息，完整对话仍留在 Hermes 里。SSH 工作区不支持 Hermes Chat。已配对的 Orca 服务器支持。Hermes gateway 停掉之后的第一次对话，可能要一分钟才启动。

Use your shell environment（使用你的 shell 环境）控制传给新建或恢复的结构化对话的变量。默认打开，所以它们会收到登录 shell 里 export 出来的变量。关掉之后，除了基本 shell 变量（如 PATH、locale、SSH_AUTH_SOCK），只允许你点名的变量。走终端的对话继续用它那个终端的 shell。

结构化对话启动 Agent 时，用的是 Settings → Agents 里为该 Agent 保存的 Command（命令）和 Arguments（参数）。对话字体、字号、代码字号、宽度和对比度在 Settings → Chat UI 里设。打开 Match terminal interface（匹配终端界面）后，跟随终端的字体和颜色，之后换主题也会跟上。同一页的 Inline visuals（行内图示）让 Agent 在回复里画图表、图示和草图，桌面和手机都可以。想看得更大时，可以把一张图开到自己的标签里。深色模式下，Mermaid 图跟随对话的主题。在 Settings → Agents 里，为新的 Claude、Codex 终端会话和结构化对话选 Manual、Auto 或 Yolo。Auto 让受支持的 Agent 自己批准日常动作。没有 Auto 的 Agent，用 Other agents（其他 Agent）选 Manual 或 Yolo。改默认值或切换模型时，已经打开的对话保持自己的模式。

Codex 的自动审阅拒绝了某个动作时，对话会说明原因，并提供 Approve once（批准这一次），只针对这个动作。

Codex 对话跑在 Ask（询问）模式时，工作区访问保持只读，直到 Codex 请求编辑。批准这次请求，编辑才会进行。这项工作适合更大权限时，可以用 Approve for me（替我批准）或 Full access（完全访问）来启动对话。权限选择作用于下一回合。已经在跑的工作仍用它开始时的权限。

Cursor 对话使用主机上的 Cursor 登录。需要时选 Sign in（登录），再在输入区选择模型和可用的 effort、Fast（快速）选项。Ask 模式会为命令弹出确认。Full access 会批准这些命令。见 Orca 里的 Cursor CLI。

Antigravity 对话用它自己的对话服务器。将要跑这段对话的主机上还没装这个服务器时，从它的 Providers（提供方）行，或从对话里的安装卡片选 Install（安装），再选一种登录方式。只有你选了 Install，运行时才会下载。在 Settings → Antigravity → Sign in with（登录方式）里，可以用 Google 账号、Gemini API key，或带 Application Default Credentials（ADC）的 Google Agent Platform。用 API key 时，粘贴并保存，或在启动环境里提供 GEMINI_API_KEY。用 ADC 时，在对话所在的主机上跑 gcloud auth application-default login。project 和 location 可选。Orca 把保存的 key 加密存在那台主机上。保存或删除 key 会重启正在跑的 Antigravity 对话。在已安装的 Providers 行上选 Remove（移除），会删掉已下载的运行时。这台主机上正在跑的 Antigravity 对话会停，历史仍保留。

新安装上，正在进行的对话会在 Orca 重启后自动恢复。已经在用的人保持自己保存的选择。从未做过这项选择的配置档保持关闭。到 Settings → Chat UI → Behavior 改这个行为。自动恢复关闭时，重新连上之后，Orca 会提议恢复被打断的结构化对话。可以先关掉这个对话框，以后从状态栏的 chats to resume（待恢复的对话）再打开。没有可恢复的对话时，这一项会消失。你关掉的对话标签保持关闭，不会在右侧再冒出来。

分叉一条回复，以及 Plan 模式

在受支持的结构化对话里，把指针放在一个已结束回合的最后一条回复上，选 Fork from this response（从这条回复分叉）。Orca 在原对话旁边打开一个新对话，内容到这一回合为止，工作区、Agent、模型和权限模式都相同。Claude 和 Codex 可以从任意一条已结束的回复分叉。OpenCode 和 OMP 在已安装版本支持时，可以从最新一条回复分叉。手机上，分叉动作在 Copy（复制）旁边。原对话保持不变。

用 /fork 从最新一条回复分叉。用 /btw 在旁边的分叉里问一个旁支问题，原对话继续跑。会打开 PR 或向 PR 推送的对话，可以在对话里显示这次检查的结果。

在 Claude 或 Codex 的结构化对话里，从斜杠菜单选 /plan，进入 Plan（计划）模式。这次选择作用于下一个新回合，重新打开对话后仍然在。用 Plan 胶囊上的退出动作回到 Build。Codex 还提供 /default。Plan 模式和权限是两件事，按这项工作选择合适的权限。对话偏好见 Settings → Chat UI。

输入区

  • 输入消息后发送，或停掉一个正在进行的回合。主机支持时可以附上文件和图片。从文件树或桌面把文件拖到输入区，或拖到对话窗格的任意位置，就会变成 Agent 能读的路径。更新后的本机 Codex 对话里，可以从应用菜单或剪贴板粘贴图片。发送前，待发图片显示成可以去掉的缩略图，点开可以看大图。附上的文件在输入区里显示成文件名胶囊。主机能够恢复时，未发送的草稿和附件会在断开、重连之后留在这个窗格里。同一会话重连时，已经稳定的记录会留在画面上，桌面和手机都是这样，不会先空白再等新的一次加载完成。
  • 在文件编辑器或终端里选中文字，从右键菜单选 Add to chat（加进对话）。或在 Markdown 预览里选中文字，用浮动的 Add to chat 按钮。选工作区里的一个 Agent，或选 New agent（新 Agent）。Orca 把带出处的引用插进目标输入区，先不发送。终端开了右键粘贴时，用 Ctrl+右键打开菜单。
  • 在草稿里输入 @，挑选工作区文件，并插入成 Agent 能读的路径。桌面和手机对话界面上的建议弹出层是同一个。
  • macOS 按 Cmd+F，Windows / Linux 按 Ctrl+F，在对话里查找文字。选中 Agent 回复的一部分，用 Add to chat 引用进输入区。在空草稿里按上、下方向键，可以召回你发出过的每一条提示。
  • 默认情况下，Agent 还在工作时按 Enter 发出的消息，会在输入区上方排成一张卡片。回合结束才发出。你可以 Steer（插入当前回合）把它插进正在跑的回合，也可以编辑或删除。Agent 正在向你提问时，排队消息也可以就地编辑。拖动卡片可以重排。编辑排队消息时用完整输入区，包括图片、技能和 @ 文件。手机输入区上方也有同样的卡片。按 Cmd+Enter / Ctrl+Enter 直接送进正在跑的回合。输入区为空时，这个快捷键会把第一条排队消息 Steer 进去。在 Settings → Chat UI → Behavior 里关掉 Queue follow-ups（排队后续消息），默认行为会反过来。手机上长按 Send（发送），选择 Queue（排队）或 Steer（插入当前回合）。点一下则跟随主机上的设置。更老的客户端或主机没有排队支持时，会立即发送。回合失败时，排队的后续消息停住，不会送进同一次失败。用 Resume（继续）发出下一条排队消息、单独发某一条，或发一条新消息。成功的回合会让剩下的队列继续。再失败会再次停住。排队消息可以在应用重启后继续。
  • Stop（停止）结束这一回合。对 Claude，会结束进程，包括后台命令和子 Agent。Codex 拒绝停止，或一直不回应停止请求时，Orca 也会结束它。对话告诉你没发出去的消息，会一直保持未发送，直到你选 Retry（重试）。被 Steer 过的消息不会再次发送。发出去的消息在主机确认前显示 Sending…（发送中）。送达失败时显示 Not sent（未发送），并附上原因。被 Orca 崩溃或退出截断的回复，会显示成一个已结束的回合，并附一句说明。Orca 不得不截断的回复会说明原因，并提供 Continue（继续）。它会先让 Agent 检查上一步是否做完。
  • 对已发送的消息使用 Rewind to here（撤回到这里），也就是悬停条上的撤销。对话回到那一点，这条消息回到输入区，你可以改完再发。撤回时 Agent 必须空闲，并且没有在等你。Codex 会撤回整个回合。
  • 更新后的结构化 Codex 对话里，正在进行的工具调用会显示实时进度。工具结束后，活动收成一行紧凑记录，展开可以看命令或结果输出。
  • 工具行写出它做了什么，包括 MCP 和网页搜索，不露出内部操作名。可以从某一行停掉单个正在跑的任务，这次会话里的其他任务继续。
  • 很长的已发送消息折在一个开关后面。两条回复之间的一串思考和工具调用，收成一行可展开的记录，忙的对话仍然好读。
  • Agent 用带编号的选项提问时，按对应数字键作答。Orca 会进到下一题。
  • 用窗格操作把对话界面分屏，或移到另一个窗格，会话保持同一个。
  • Claude 和 Codex 改成功的文件，可以在回合里显示成带行数的改动文件摘要。选一个文件，就地加载它的补丁。Agent 改过的文件也会显示成行内 diff 卡片。点记录里的文件路径和链接，在 Orca 里打开。
  • 在结构化对话里，/clear 在同一个窗格开始一段新对话，早前的对话留在历史里。/compact 让 Agent 压缩当前对话的上下文，便于接着做。Claude、Codex、Grok、OpenCode 和 OMP 支持它。Agent 还在工作时发出的 /compact 会排队，不会被拒绝。
  • Orca 或 Agent 在消息发送过程中重启时，Orca 会核对提供方的记录。能证明消息已经到达，就把它收成已发送。能证明没到达，Retry 就会可用。记录分不清结果时，Orca 把这次发送标成未确认，避免发成两条。
  • Orca 能起名时，新对话用第一条消息来命名。起不了名时，保持 Claude Chat 或 Codex Chat。默认由这段对话自己的 Agent 和账号起名。Settings → Chat UI → Chat naming → Advanced 可以改用另一个起名 Agent。也可以从标签操作给结构化对话标签改名。你设的名字会跟着这段对话，留在 Agent 会话历史里、手机上，以及关掉标签之后的其他窗口里。
  • 在草稿开头，或空白之后输入 /，打开一份分组列表，里面是斜杠命令和已发现的技能。选择器插入该 Agent 期望的记号：Claude 用 /skill，Codex 用 $skill。单独打 $ 不会再打开另一个选择器。在句子中间选一条命令，只会补全这个记号，不会把周围还没写完的草稿发出去。命令和技能目录会认当前 Agent，并和手机上的对话界面共用。在桌面上，Claude 的命令行会尽量用本机 CLI 报上来的说明和参数提示。
  • 在 Claude 和 Codex 的结构化对话里，选 /mcp 查看 MCP 服务器状态、登录、重连，或开关某一台服务器。在已配对的主机上，登录可能会要求你粘贴浏览器回调地址。见技能登记与 MCP。
  • 属于 Agent 自己的命令会交给 Agent，对话界面不会拦下。包括 Claude 的 /init、/review、/help，以及 Codex 的 /goal <objective>。Orca 仍处理自己的对话命令，例如 /clear 和 /compact。
  • Agent 有提供时，用 model（模型）和选项胶囊：思考档位、Fast mode（快速模式），以及其他 Agent 自己的会话选项。提供方模型很多时，用 Search models…（搜索模型…）过滤。Fast mode 会认提供方和模型。只有当前 Agent 报了支持，Orca 才显示它，并保住提供方支持的路由。Claude 的模型列表来自那台主机上已安装的 Claude CLI。更新 CLI 之后，新模型会出现。Codex 可以从胶囊直接选模型（发送 /model <id>）。单独的 /model 仍会打开 Agent 的选择器，并提供速度选项 Ultrafast。Grok 提供 model（启动时 -m，会话中 /model）和 reasoning effort（启动时 --reasoning-effort，会话中 /effort）。模型列表向已登录的 Grok CLI 查询（grok models），退役的 id 会从选择器里消失。effort 跟随所选模型：Grok 4.6 含 Extra high，Grok 4.5 到 High 为止。模型选择器不再等 Agent 进程启动。进程启动之前，输入区就显示这个 Agent 的模型和 effort，并在第一次启动时生效。会话已经在跑之后，只影响当前回合的胶囊会保持禁用或带说明，直到会话就绪。实时的值会送给这个标签里的 CLI。
  • 更新后的结构化 Claude 对话里，模型和 effort 控件旁边的环，表示这段对话用了多少上下文窗口。点开可以看到 token 数，以及消息、工具、记忆文件和其他上下文各占多少。回合进行中会更新。重新打开对话后仍然在。
  • Codex、Pi 和 OMP 会在你发送之前说明它们未登录，同时不禁用 Send（发送）。你可以先去修好登录，不必等一个失败的回合。

结构化对话很长时，记录边缘会出现一条消息轨，每条用户提示一个标记。悬停或聚焦可以预览提示，点一个就跳回那个回合。历史很长时会抽样，让这条轨保持紧凑。

在 Settings → Chat UI → Behavior 里，可以选择发送后是否移动滚动位置，以及空闲的对话 Agent 何时停下。选 Never（从不）会让它们一直跑。停掉空闲 Agent 不会删掉对话。窗格标题栏也有对话宽度控件。

向上滚动阅读更早的消息。还没到顶之前，对话界面就会加载更早的历史。某一页加载失败时，用那里的重试按钮。在桌面上，把指针放在已发送的文字消息上，时间戳旁边的按钮可以复制它。

同一段对话如果在一个可能仍在写入的终端里开着，对话界面不会再发一条抢着写的消息。先在那个终端里退出 Agent，或关掉终端，再从对话发送。对 Claude 和 Codex，一旦 Orca 能确认终端里的 Agent 已经退出，终端标签可以继续开着。

对话里的围栏代码块和 shell 命令会做语法高亮。每个围栏块有 Copy code（复制代码）按钮。它只复制这段代码，不包括周围的消息。

跟上 Agent 的工作

主回合进行中和结束后，结构化对话都会把委派出去的工作和后台工作留在可见处：

  • 有活在跑时，后台任务条保持可见。摘要会计数：Agent、shell、监视器、工作流和其他任务。展开后是分组的行，带名字、状态点和已用时间。每个 Claude 或 Codex 子项会一直可见，直到它自己的工作结束，展开后能看到任务和结果。Agent 子项也会在侧栏里显示成这条会话下面缩进的行。Codex 对话可以从某一行停掉一条后台命令或一个子 Agent，会话的其余部分继续。Grok 对话可以列出并停掉它留在后台的命令。Grok 和 Pi 也会把子 Agent 显示成子 Agent 行。
  • 在名册、活动条或侧栏里选一个子 Agent，会在单独的视图里打开它的任务、实时步骤和最终回复。用 Back to main agent（回到主 Agent）或 Escape 回到之前的记录位置。名册里还会显示它的模型、当前工作和结果。
  • Claude 子 Agent 停在权限确认上时，子行显示为等待。父会话显示 waiting（等待），而不是 blocked（被挡住）。这和 CLI 报告「子 Agent 正在请求批准」的方式一致。
  • 结构化对话在回合中途停下来要批准或输入时，Orca 会像其他正在等待的 Agent 一样发出提醒。你不用盯着记录。
  • Claude 的 TodoWrite 和 Codex 的 update_plan 更新，会渲染成行内清单。最新的计划也会出现在输入区上方一块可折叠的进度面板里。
  • 当前的 Codex goal 显示在输入区上方，并带上它现在的状态。用那里的 goal 控件暂停、恢复、清除或展开目标。输入 /goal，把下一条草稿当成 goal 发出。
  • 正在进行的回合，在记录末尾有一行活动。Thinking（思考中）表示这一回合正在产生推理。跑命令这类提供方活动优先显示。已完成或被打断的耗时，会在重新加载和应用重启之后留在这个回合上。Orca 如果没看到回合结束，就不写耗时，也不去估一个。
  • Codex 的 goal 被设置、暂停、挡住、完成、停止或清除时，会出现记录行。重复的记账更新不会再加重复行。
  • 回合落定后，标题可以汇总改过的不重复文件，以及增删行数。展开后是原有的 diff 卡片。已经回答过的批准和提问卡片会留下，作为回执，显示你选的答案。
  • 结束的回合收成它的答案。Agent 报告这一回合失败时，回合结束，不再继续显示为正在跑。

结构化对话在后台成功结束时，Orca 把该标签和工作区标成未读。这不会发桌面通知，也不会出声。其他需要你注意的控件见通知与收件箱。

从斜杠命令选择器里选中的技能，会在草稿里保持为一枚胶囊。你手工输入或粘贴的技能名仍是普通文字。安装和更新见 Orca 技能。

Agent 提出的问题

Claude 出示 AskUserQuestion（或类似的结构化权限、提问卡片）时，对话界面应在记录里渲染这张提问卡片。你从输入区这条路径回答，而不是把它当成一条折起来的工具调用。在卡片上用 Submit（提交）确认。跑在已配对的远程 Orca 服务器或无界面主机上的 Agent 也一样，不限于本机窗格。

在对话和终端之间搬动

右键标签，可以把受支持的会话在对话界面和 TUI 之间当场切换。

也可以从标签的右键菜单，或 Agent 会话历史 的一行里搬动：Resume in New Native Chat（在新的原生对话里恢复）把早前的对话开成结构化对话，Resume in New CLI（在新的 CLI 里恢复）把它分叉进终端。原来的对话保持原样。对话一旦划给结构化对话，这个会话就用对话界面，不要再用终端的 Resume（恢复）。

用对话标题栏或标签右键菜单里的 Hand Off（交接），把工作交给另一个 Agent。Claude 或 Codex 对话碰到用量上限，而且重置时间已知时，可以在 Settings → Chat UI → Behavior 里打开 Continue chats automatically when a usage limit resets（用量限制重置后自动继续对话）。重置之后 Orca 会发一条续接。在限额提示上选 Don't continue（不要继续），可以取消这一次对话的自动续接。这项设置属于正在跑 Agent 的那台主机。已关闭的对话或休眠的工作区要等重新打开或唤醒。重置时间早于 24 小时的，需要手动续接。

在哪些地方可用

对话界面在桌面端提供，覆盖受支持的本机会话，以及已配对服务器上的远程 Agent 会话。手机应用对同样这些已配对会话，复用对话式的记录呈现。

Optional chat surface over supported agent sessions — structured transcripts, skills, model pickers, and inline visuals.

Chat UI is a view for supported agent sessions. Choosing Chat for an agent opens eligible new sessions in Orca's structured native chat — a transcript + composer that keeps the session state in Orca, so the tab and its conversation can be restored after an app restart. Structured chat covers Claude, Codex, Grok, OpenCode (including 2.x), Pi, OMP, Antigravity, GitHub Copilot, Kimi Code, Cursor, Hermes, Kiro, Goose, Auggie, Gemini CLI, Factory Droid, Kilo Code, and MiMo Code. Agents without a structured implementation, and WSL or SSH sessions, use terminal-backed Chat UI instead.

Choose Chat or CLI

Open Settings → Chat UI or Settings → Agents to choose Chat or CLI for each supported agent. During onboarding you can choose Chat UI or Terminal, select an agent, and install skills for that setup. Chat is preselected on first-run onboarding; existing users keep their launch preferences, and existing conversations keep their surface. The per-agent choice applies to starts from the desktop, including CLI, orchestration, and launches relayed from your phone. You can also set a default for agents without an individual choice.

Use the tab's right-click menu or Agent Session History to move a supported conversation between chat and a terminal.

Structured chat

When Chat is selected, an eligible new local Claude, Codex, Grok, OpenCode, Pi, OMP, Antigravity, GitHub Copilot, Kimi Code, Cursor, Hermes, Kiro, Goose, Auggie, Gemini CLI, Factory Droid, Kilo Code, or MiMo Code session opens as a structured chat tab that keeps its state in Orca, so the tab and its conversation can be restored after an app restart.

Structured chat is available for eligible sessions on local macOS, Linux, and native Windows, and for workspaces on a paired Orca Server that owns them. WSL and SSH sessions continue to use terminal-backed Chat UI, and agents without a structured implementation stay on the terminal. Existing terminal sessions remain terminal sessions.

Copilot Chat requires Copilot CLI 1.0.88 or later and sign-in; models appear after startup, and plans without a model choice use Auto. Importing Copilot Session History into Chat is not supported. Kimi Chat requires Kimi Code 2.5.0 or later and sign-in.

Kiro Chat requires Kiro CLI 3.1.0 or later and uses the host’s Kiro sign-in. Gemini Chat requires Gemini CLI 0.68.0 or later; if signed out, sign in through the CLI first. Goose Chat uses the provider and credentials configured on the host. Auggie Chat uses the host’s Auggie sign-in and does not offer permission modes.

Hermes Chat requires Hermes 0.23.0 or later on the workspace host. Start it from the New Tab menu or use Resume in New Chat on a terminal conversation in Session History; it resumes in its original workspace and profile. Very long imports show recent messages while Hermes retains the full conversation. SSH workspaces do not support Hermes Chat; paired Orca Servers do. The first chat after the Hermes gateway stops can take a minute to start.

The Use your shell environment setting controls the variables passed to new or resumed structured chats. It is on by default, so they receive exports from your login shell. Turn it off to allow only selected variable names in addition to basic shell variables such as PATH, locale, and SSH_AUTH_SOCK. Terminal-backed chat continues to use its terminal shell.

Structured chats launch the agent with the Command and Arguments saved for that agent under Settings → Agents. Set the chat font, text size, code size, width, and contrast for structured chats under Settings → Chat UI, or turn on Match terminal interface to follow your terminal's font and colors — including later theme changes. Inline visuals in that pane lets an agent draw charts, diagrams, and mockups inside a reply, on desktop and phone; you can open a visual in its own tab when you want more room, and Mermaid diagrams follow the chat's theme in dark mode. Under Settings → Agents, choose Manual, Auto, or Yolo for new Claude and Codex terminal and structured-chat sessions. Auto lets supported agents approve routine actions themselves; use Other agents to choose Manual or Yolo for agents without Auto support. An existing chat keeps its own mode when you change the default or switch models.

If Codex automatic review denies an action, chat explains the denial and offers Approve once for that action.

In a Codex chat running in Ask mode, Orca keeps workspace access read-only until Codex requests an edit. Approve that request to let the edit proceed, or start the chat in Approve for me or Full access when that is appropriate for the work. A permission choice affects the next turn; work already running keeps the permission it started with.

Cursor Chat uses the Cursor sign-in on the host. Choose Sign in if needed, then select a model and its available effort and Fast options in the composer. Ask mode prompts for commands; Full access approves them. See Cursor CLI in Orca.

Antigravity chat uses its own chat server. If the server is not installed on the host that will run the chat, choose Install from its Providers row or from the chat's install card, then choose a sign-in method. The runtime is downloaded only after you choose Install. Under Settings → Antigravity → Sign in with, you can use a Google account, a Gemini API key, or Google Agent Platform with Application Default Credentials (ADC). For an API key, paste and save it, or supply GEMINI_API_KEY in the launch environment. For ADC, run gcloud auth application-default login on the chat host; project and location are optional. Orca stores a saved key encrypted on that host, and saving or removing it restarts running Antigravity chats. From its installed Providers row, choose Remove to remove the downloaded runtime; this stops running Antigravity chats on that host, while keeping their history.

Working chats resume automatically after an Orca restart on new installs. Existing users keep their saved choice; profiles that never chose remain off. Change this behavior under Settings → Chat UI → Behavior. If automatic resume is off, Orca offers to resume an interrupted structured chat when you reconnect. You can close that dialog and open it later from the chats to resume item in the status bar. The item disappears when there are no longer chats available to resume. A chat tab you close stays closed instead of reappearing on the right.

Fork a response and use Plan mode

In a supported structured chat, hover the last reply in a finished turn and choose Fork from this response. Orca opens a new chat next to the original, with the conversation through that turn and the same workspace, agent, model, and permission mode. Claude and Codex can fork any finished response; OpenCode and OMP can fork the latest response when the installed version supports it. On mobile, the fork action appears beside Copy. The original chat stays unchanged.

Use /fork to fork from the latest response. Use /btw to ask a side question in a fork beside the original chat while it keeps running. Chats that open or push to a pull request can show its CI result in the conversation.

In a Claude or Codex structured chat, choose /plan from the slash menu to enter Plan mode. The choice applies to the next fresh turn and stays with the chat when you reopen it. Use the Plan pill's exit action to return to Build; Codex also offers /default. Plan mode and permissions are separate, so choose the permission level appropriate for the work. See Settings → Chat UI for chat preferences.

Composer

  • Type a message and send (or stop a working turn). Attach files/images when the host supports it. You can drag files from the file tree or your desktop onto the composer—or anywhere in the chat pane—to add them as paths the agent can read. In updated local Codex chat, paste images from the app menu or clipboard; pending images appear as removable thumbnails before you send, and you can open a thumbnail for a larger preview. Files you attach show as filename pills in the composer. Unsent draft text and attachments stay with the pane across disconnect/reconnect when the host can restore them. A settled transcript also stays on screen while the same session reconnects, instead of blanking until the fresh load finishes (desktop and mobile share that retention).
  • Select text in a file editor or terminal and use Add to chat from the right-click menu, or select text in a Markdown preview and use its floating Add to chat button. Choose an agent in the workspace or New agent; Orca inserts the quoted selection with its source into the target input without sending it. In terminals with right-click paste enabled, use Ctrl+right-click to open the menu.
  • Type @ in the draft to pick workspace files and insert them as paths the agent can read. The same suggestion popover appears on desktop and in mobile Chat UI.
  • Press Cmd+F on macOS or Ctrl+F on Windows / Linux to find text in the chat. Select part of an agent reply and choose Add to chat to quote it into the composer, and press Up/Down in an empty draft to recall every prompt you sent.
  • By default, Enter queues a message you send while the agent is working as a card above the composer. It sends when the turn ends, unless you Steer it into the running turn, edit it, or delete it — you can edit a queued message in place even while the agent is asking you a question. Drag cards to reorder them. Edit a queued message with the full composer, including images, skills, and @files. The same cards appear above the mobile composer. Press Cmd+Enter / Ctrl+Enter to send directly into the running turn; with an empty composer, the shortcut steers the first queued message. You can also turn off Queue follow-ups in Settings → Chat UI → Behavior to reverse the default. On mobile, hold Send to choose Queue or Steer; a tap follows the host setting. Older clients or hosts without queue support send immediately. If a turn fails, queued follow-ups stay paused instead of sending into the same failure. Use Resume to send the next queued message, send an individual queued message, or send a new message. A successful turn lets the remaining queue continue; another failure holds it again. Queued messages can resume after an app restart.
  • Stop ends the turn. For Claude it ends the process, including background commands and subagents; if Codex refuses or never answers a stop request, Orca ends it too. A message the chat told you was not sent stays unsent until you choose Retry, and a steered message is never re-sent. An outgoing message reads Sending… until the host confirms it, and Not sent with the reason when delivery fails; a reply cut off by an Orca crash or quit reads as a finished turn with a single explanation. A reply Orca had to cut off says why and offers Continue, which first asks the agent to check whether its last step finished.
  • Use Rewind to here on a sent message (the undo action on its hover strip) to take the conversation back to that point; the message returns to the composer so you can edit and resend. Rewind needs an idle agent with nothing waiting on you, and Codex rewinds the whole turn.
  • In updated structured Codex chat, active tool calls show live progress while they run. When a tool finishes, its activity settles into a compact row that you can expand to inspect command or result output.
  • Tool rows identify the action they performed, including MCP and web-search work, instead of exposing internal operation names. You can stop an individual running task from its row without stopping the other tasks in the session.
  • Long sent messages fold behind a toggle, and a run of thoughts and tool calls between replies collapses into one expandable row, so a busy transcript stays readable.
  • When an agent asks a question with numbered choices, press the number key to answer; Orca advances to the next question.
  • Use the pane actions to split Chat UI or move it to another pane while keeping the same session.
  • Successful Claude and Codex edits can appear as a changed-file summary with line counts in the turn. Select a file to load its patch inline. Agent file edits also appear as inline diff cards. Click file paths and links in the transcript to open them in Orca.
  • In structured chat, /clear starts a fresh conversation in the same pane and keeps the earlier conversation in history. /compact asks the agent to reduce the current conversation context so you can continue working; Claude, Codex, Grok, OpenCode, and OMP support it. A /compact you send while the agent is working waits in line instead of being refused.
  • If Orca or the agent restarts while a message is being sent, Orca checks the provider transcript and settles the send when it can prove that the message arrived. If it can prove the message did not arrive, Retry becomes available; when the transcript cannot distinguish the outcome, Orca leaves the send marked unconfirmed rather than risking a duplicate.
  • New chats are named from their first message when Orca can generate a name; without one they stay Claude Chat or Codex Chat. By default, the chat’s own agent and account generate the name; Settings → Chat UI → Chat naming → Advanced lets you choose another naming agent, and you can rename a structured chat tab from its tab actions. A name you set stays with the chat in Agent Session History, on your phone, and in other windows after you close the tab.
  • Type / at the start of a draft or after whitespace to open one grouped list of slash commands and discovered skills. The picker inserts the token that agent expects (/skill for Claude or $skill for Codex); $ no longer opens a separate picker. Choosing a command mid-sentence completes the token without sending the surrounding draft. The command and skill catalog is agent-aware and shared with mobile Chat UI. On desktop, Claude command rows use descriptions and argument hints reported by the installed CLI when available.
  • In Claude and Codex structured chats, choose /mcp to see MCP server status, sign in, reconnect, or turn a server on or off. On a paired host, sign-in may ask you to paste the browser callback address. See Skills registry & MCP.
  • Agent-owned commands pass through to the agent instead of being rejected by the chat surface. That includes Claude /init, /review, and /help, plus Codex /goal <objective>; Orca still handles its own conversation commands such as /clear and /compact.
  • Use the model and options pills (thought level, Fast mode, and other agent-specific session options) when the agent exposes them. A Search models… field filters the picker when a provider exposes many models. Fast mode is a provider- and model-aware toggle; Orca shows it only when the active agent reports support and preserves the provider's supported routing. Claude model choices come from the installed Claude CLI on that host (not a hardcoded list), so new models show up after you update the CLI. Codex supports direct model selection from the pill (sends /model <id>), with bare /model still opening the agent picker, and offers Ultrafast as a speed choice. Grok exposes model (-m at launch, /model mid-session) and reasoning effort (--reasoning-effort at launch, /effort mid-session); the model list is probed from the signed-in Grok CLI (grok models) so retired ids drop out of the picker. Effort choices follow the selected model: Grok 4.6 includes Extra high, while Grok 4.5 stops at High. The model picker no longer waits for the agent to start: before the agent process starts, the composer shows the model and effort choices for that agent, and they apply on first start. After the session is live, pills that only affect a running turn stay disabled or labeled until the session is ready. Live values go to the CLI in that tab.
  • In updated structured Claude chat, the ring beside the model and effort controls shows how much of the context window the conversation uses. Open it to see token counts and the space used by messages, tools, memory files, and other context. It updates during a turn and stays available when you reopen the chat.
  • Codex, Pi, and OMP say when they are signed out before you send, without disabling Send, so you can fix the login instead of waiting on a failed turn.

For long structured conversations, a message rail appears along the transcript edge with one marker per user prompt. Hover or focus it to preview prompts, then select one to jump back to that turn; long histories are sampled so the rail stays compact.

In Settings → Chat UI → Behavior, choose whether sending moves the scroll position and when idle chat agents stop; select Never to keep them running. Stopping an idle agent does not delete its conversation. The pane header also offers chat width controls.

Scroll up to read older messages. Chat UI loads earlier history before you reach the top; if a page fails to load, use the retry button there. On desktop, hover a sent text message to copy it from the button beside its timestamp.

If the same conversation is open in a terminal that might still be writing to it, Chat UI does not send a competing message. Quit the agent in that terminal or close the terminal first, then send from the chat. For Claude and Codex, once Orca can establish that the terminal agent has exited, the terminal tab can remain open.

Fenced code blocks and shell commands in chat are syntax-highlighted, and each fenced block has a Copy code button. It copies only the snippet, not the surrounding message.

Follow agent work

Structured chat keeps delegated and background work visible while the main turn is active and afterward:

  • The background-task strip stays visible while work is live. Its summary counts agents, shells, monitors, workflows, and other tasks; expand it for grouped rows with names, state dots, and elapsed time. Each Claude or Codex child stays visible until its own work finishes, with its task and result in the expanded view; agent children also appear as indented rows beneath the session in the sidebar. A Codex chat can stop a background command or a subagent from its row without stopping the rest of the session, and a Grok chat can list and stop the commands it left running in the background. Grok and Pi show their subagents as subagent rows too.
  • Select a subagent in the roster, activity strip, or sidebar to open its task, live steps, and final reply in a dedicated view. Use Back to main agent or Escape to return to the previous transcript position. The roster also shows its model, current work, and result.
  • When a Claude subagent is waiting on a permission prompt, its child row reads as waiting, and the parent session reads waiting rather than blocked — matching how the CLI reports a subagent asking for approval.
  • If a structured chat stops mid-turn to ask for approval or input, Orca raises an alert for it like any other waiting agent, so you don't have to watch the transcript.
  • Claude TodoWrite and Codex update_plan updates render as inline checklists. The latest plan also appears in a collapsible progress panel above the composer.
  • The active Codex goal appears above the composer with its current state. Use the goal controls there to pause, resume, clear, or expand the objective; enter /goal to send the next draft as a goal.
  • A running turn has one activity row at the end of the transcript. Thinking means the current turn is producing reasoning; provider activity such as running a command takes precedence. Completed or interrupted durations stay with the turn across reloads and app restarts. If Orca did not observe the turn end, it omits the duration instead of estimating one.
  • Codex goal changes appear as transcript rows when a goal is set, paused, blocked, completed, stopped, or cleared. Repeated accounting updates do not add duplicate rows.
  • When a turn settles, its header can summarize the unique files changed and their additions/deletions. Expand it to reveal the existing diff cards. Answered approval and question cards remain as receipts showing your selected answer.
  • A finished turn collapses to its answer. If the agent reports a failed turn, the turn ends instead of continuing to show as running.

When a structured chat finishes successfully in the background, Orca marks its tab and workspace unread. This does not send a desktop notification or sound. See Notifications & Inbox for the other attention controls.

When you select a skill from the slash-command picker, it stays visible as a pill in the draft. A skill name you type or paste manually remains ordinary text. See Orca skills for installation and updates.

Questions from the agent

When Claude shows an AskUserQuestion (or similar structured permission/question card), Chat UI should render the question card in the transcript so you can answer from the composer path instead of treating it as a collapsed tool call. Confirm with Submit on the card. That includes agents running on a paired Remote Orca Server / headless host — not only local panes.

Move a conversation between chat and terminal

Right-click a tab to switch a supported session live between Chat UI and TUI.

You can move a conversation between structured chat and a terminal from the tab's right-click menu or from an Agent Session History row: Resume in New Native Chat opens the earlier conversation as a structured chat, and Resume in New CLI forks it into a terminal. The original conversation is left as it was. Once a conversation is reserved for structured chat, use the chat rather than terminal Resume for that session.

Use Hand Off in the chat header or the tab’s right-click menu to hand the work to another agent. When a Claude or Codex chat reaches a usage limit with a known reset time, you can opt in to Continue chats automatically when a usage limit resets under Settings → Chat UI → Behavior. Orca sends a continuation after the reset; choose Don't continue on the limit notice to cancel it for that chat. The setting belongs to the host running the agent, and closed chats or sleeping workspaces wait until reopened or awakened. Resets more than 24 hours old require manual continuation.

Availability

Chat UI ships on desktop for supported local and remote (paired server) agent sessions. The mobile companion reuses chat-style transcript patterns for the same paired sessions.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/native-chat

上一章Hot-swap Codex accounts 下一章Agent session history

Start Here · Working with Agents

Agent 会话历史

Agent session history

过去的 Agent 会话可以在右侧栏里浏览，并一键恢复。Orca 自己跑恢复命令，不用手写 --resume。

Orca 扫描受支持的 Agent CLI 留在磁盘上的会话记录，列在右侧栏的 Agent Session History（Agent 会话历史）里。选一次过去的会话，点 Resume（恢复）。Orca 在一个新终端里跑该 Agent 的恢复命令：同一个工作目录，同一个会话 ID。

打开面板

打开右侧栏，切到 Agents 标签。面板标题是 Agent Session History。

标题上有类似 12 shown · 47 recent 的计数，旁边是搜索框。输入文字，按会话标题、工作目录、分支、模型，或对话里的预览文字过滤。

会话历史也可以搜索其他已连接电脑，以及已配对的 Orca 服务器上编进索引的记录。到 Settings → Session History，为想搜索的电脑打开 Search inside sessions（在会话内搜索），或用 Enable on all computers（在所有电脑上启用）。某台电脑离线，或还没授权建索引时，Orca 会显示一条主机范围提示。可搜索的数据留在各自的电脑上。本机面板仍可以浏览还没进索引的会话。

脚本和终端里用 orca search，查的是同一份索引，不用打开面板。

范围

面板顶部的范围开关决定列出哪些会话：

  • Workspace（工作区）：当前工作区或工作树的会话，看当前工作区上下文。
  • Project（项目）：能对应到当前 Orca 项目的会话。
  • All（全部）：这台机器上、每个 Agent 找到的全部会话。

远程工作区可以浏览本机历史。恢复动作只在本机工作区执行。当前工作树是远程的时候，面板里会写明这一点。

视图选项

搜索框旁边的视图菜单，决定扫描哪些 Agent、会话怎么分组：

  • Agents：逐个打开或关掉 CLI（Claude、Codex、Muse Code、Hermes、Pi、OMP、Prime Agent、Cursor、Gemini、Antigravity、Rovo Dev、Copilot、OpenCode、Grok、OpenClaw、Devin、Droid、Kimi、Kiro、CodeBuddy、ZCode、Qoder）。关掉的 Agent 在扫描时跳过。在 Agents 标题上用 Select all（全选）或 Clear（清空）一次改全部。Clear 会一个都不选，这样你只打开关心的 CLI，不用在长列表里逐个取消。一个都没选时，显示 No agents selected（未选择 Agent），而不是平时的空过滤提示。
  • Group（分组）：Project、Folder（每个工作目录一个标题），或 Agent（每个 CLI 一个标题）。
  • Hide empty sessions（隐藏空会话）：去掉记录消息数为 0 的会话。

会话列表上方的排序，浏览时可以按 Last updated（最近更新）或 Created（创建时间）。搜索时，这个控件改成 Most relevant（最相关）或 Newest（最新）。相关度相同的，较新的排在前面。搜索排序和浏览排序分开记住。搜索碰到扫描上限时，在结果下方选 Show more（显示更多），继续往更早的记录搜。

恢复会话

点一行打开详情：工作目录、分支、模型、消息数、First prompt（第一条提示），以及最近的对话回合。展开详情时，First prompt 会从记录里加载未经截断的第一条用户消息。列表行只留一段短预览，供搜索用。在这张卡片上用 Copy（复制），把完整提问放到剪贴板，方便复用长提示，而不必再打开日志。也可以把已启用的会话行拖进工作区来恢复。行上的操作还有：

  • Resume（恢复）：在该会话的工作目录里打开一个新终端，并跑 Agent 的恢复命令。例如 claude --resume <id>、codex resume <id>、muse resume <session-id>、pi --session <session_file>、prime-agent --resume <path>、cursor-agent --resume <id>、acli rovodev run --restore <id>。Codex 会话如果当初设过 CODEX_HOME，恢复时会再次导出它。
  • Resume in New Native Chat（在新的原生对话里恢复）：为符合条件的会话打开一个新的结构化对话标签，并能看到之前的对话。Claude 必须在原来的工作区里恢复。Codex 可以在另一个工作区里恢复。从文件夹工作区开始的对话，仍在同一个文件夹里恢复。Resume in New CLI（在新的 CLI 里恢复）方向相反，把结构化对话分叉进终端。这两项也在标签的右键菜单里。对话一旦划给结构化对话，就不能再用终端的 Resume。可用性见对话界面。

Hermes 的终端会话可以在原来的工作区和配置档里用 Resume in New Chat（在新对话里恢复）。要求见 Hermes Chat 的要求。

Pi 从钩子报上来的磁盘会话文件恢复（--session <path>），不靠一个光秃的会话 id。文件不见了，即使还有会话 id，这一行也不能 Resume。

  • Copy resume command（复制恢复命令）：把同一条 shell 命令复制到剪贴板，拿到外部终端去用。
  • Copy session ID（复制会话 ID）/ Copy log path（复制日志路径）：给脚本用，或把记录附到 bug 报告里。
  • Open log（打开日志）/ Reveal log（在文件管理器中显示日志）：在 Orca 里打开原始记录，或跳到系统文件管理器里的位置。
  • Open cwd（打开工作目录）：把该会话的工作目录当成一个工作区打开。

恢复需要本机工作区

恢复时，Agent CLI 跑在正在显示 Orca 的那台机器上。如果你连的是远程工作区，先切回本机工作区（或用 Copy resume command 自己在远程跑），再点 Resume。

记录从哪来

Orca 读的是每个 Agent 自己留在磁盘上的会话存储，不用额外打开什么。CLI 只要写了记录，下次扫描后就会出现在面板里。点标题栏的 Refresh Session History（刷新会话历史）可以马上重扫。各 Agent 的位置：

  • Codex：~/.codex/sessions
  • Claude：~/.claude 里的历史
  • Muse Code：~/.local/share/muse/sessions
  • Cursor：它的会话日志
  • OpenCode：旧的会话文件，或 ~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db
  • CodeBuddy：项目下 .codebuddy/projects 里的记录
  • ZCode：它存下来的记录
  • Qoder：~/.qoder/projects 里的记录
  • 其他 Agent 各自还有对应位置

下一步

  • 热切换 Codex 账号：换掉当前会话背后的 Codex 登录，不用重启这次会话。
  • 钩子与记忆：控制 Agent 每次启动时带上什么上下文，恢复的会话也算。

Browse and resume past Claude, Codex, Muse Code, Cursor, Gemini, and other agent sessions from Orca's right sidebar.

Orca scans the on-disk session transcripts that supported agent CLIs leave behind and lists them in a right-sidebar panel called Agent Session History. Pick a past session, click Resume, and Orca runs the agent's resume command in a fresh terminal — same cwd, same session ID, no manual --resume flag wrangling.

Open the panel

Open the right sidebar and switch to the Agents tab. The panel header reads "Agent Session History".

The header shows a count like 12 shown · 47 recent and a search box. Type to filter by session title, working directory, branch, model, or preview text from the conversation.

Session History can also search indexed transcripts from other connected computers and paired Orca Servers. Turn on Search inside sessions for the computers you want under Settings → Session History (or use Enable on all computers). Orca shows a host-scope notice when a computer is offline or has not granted indexing access. Searchable data stays on each computer; the local panel still lets you browse sessions that have not been added to the index.

For scripts and terminal workflows, use orca search to query the same index without opening the panel.

Scope

The scope toggle at the top of the panel decides which sessions appear:

  • Workspace — sessions from the current workspace or worktree, depending on the active workspace context.
  • Project — sessions that resolve to the active Orca project.
  • All — every session Orca found across every agent on this machine.

Remote workspaces can browse local history, but resume actions only run from local workspaces — Orca tells you so in the panel when the active worktree is remote.

View options

The view-options menu (next to the search box) controls which agents are scanned and how sessions are grouped:

  • Agents — toggle individual CLIs on or off (Claude, Codex, Muse Code, Hermes, Pi, OMP, Prime Agent, Cursor, Gemini, Antigravity, Rovo Dev, Copilot, OpenCode, Grok, OpenClaw, Devin, Droid, Kimi, Kiro, CodeBuddy, ZCode, Qoder). Disabled agents are skipped during the scan. Use Select all / Clear in the Agents header to flip every agent at once — Clear leaves none selected so you can turn on only the CLI you care about without unchecking a long list. An empty selection shows No agents selected instead of the usual empty-filter message.
  • Group — Project, Folder (one heading per cwd), or Agent (one heading per CLI).
  • Hide empty sessions — drop sessions with zero recorded messages.

Use the sort control above the session list to order browsing by Last updated or Created. When you search, that control offers Most relevant or Newest instead. Equally relevant matches show the newer session first. Your search sort is remembered separately from the browsing sort. If a search reaches its scan limit, choose Show more below the results to search further back.

Resume a session

Click a session row to open its details: working directory, branch, model, message count, First prompt, and the latest conversation turns. First prompt loads the untruncated first user message from the transcript when you expand details (list rows only keep a short preview for search). Use Copy on that card to put the full ask on the clipboard — useful for reusing a long prompt without reopening the log. You can also drag an enabled session row into the workspace to resume it. From the row's actions you can:

  • Resume — opens a new terminal in the session's cwd and runs the agent's resume command (e.g. claude --resume <id>, codex resume <id>, muse resume <session-id>, pi --session <session_file>, prime-agent --resume <path>, cursor-agent --resume <id>, acli rovodev run --restore <id>). Codex sessions also re-export CODEX_HOME when the original session set one.

  • Resume in New Native Chat — opens a new structured chat tab with the previous conversation visible, for eligible sessions (Claude must resume in its original workspace; Codex can resume in another workspace; a chat that started in a folder workspace resumes in that same folder). Resume in New CLI does the reverse, forking a structured chat's conversation into a terminal. Both are also on the tab's right-click menu. Once a conversation is reserved for structured chat, terminal Resume is no longer available for it. See Chat UI for availability.

Hermes terminal sessions can use Resume in New Chat in their original workspace and profile. See Hermes Chat requirements.

Pi resumes from the on-disk session file reported by its hooks (--session <path>), not from a bare session id. If that file is missing, Resume is unavailable for that row even when a session id exists.

  • Copy resume command — copies the same shell command to the clipboard for use in an external terminal.
  • Copy session ID / Copy log path — for scripting or attaching transcripts to bug reports.
  • Open log / Reveal log — open the raw transcript file in Orca, or jump to it in your OS file manager.
  • Open cwd — open the session's working directory as a workspace.
Resume needs a local workspace

Resume runs the agent CLI on the machine where Orca is rendering. If you're connected to a remote workspace, switch back to a local one (or use Copy resume command and run it on the remote yourself) before clicking Resume.

Where the transcripts come from

Orca reads each agent's own on-disk session store — Codex's ~/.codex/sessions, Claude's ~/.claude history, Muse Code's ~/.local/share/muse/sessions, Cursor's session log, OpenCode's legacy session files or ~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db, CodeBuddy's project transcripts under .codebuddy/projects, ZCode's stored transcripts, Qoder's ~/.qoder/projects transcripts, and so on. There's nothing extra to enable; if the CLI writes a transcript, it shows up in the panel after the next scan. Use the Refresh Session History button in the header to rescan on demand.

Next steps

  • Hot-swap Codex accounts — switch the Codex login behind an active session without restarting it.
  • Hooks & memory — control what context an agent picks up on every launch (including resumed sessions).

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/session-history

上一章Chat UI (native chat) 下一章Agent hibernation

Start Here · Working with Agents

Agent 休眠

Agent hibernation

空闲太久的后台 Agent 终端可以被 Orca 停掉。下次打开这棵工作树时，同一次会话会自动恢复。

工作树开了几十个之后，空闲 Agent 会叠起来。每一个都是活的 PTY，内存里还占着一次模型会话。Agent 休眠让 Orca 在它们做完、又够久没人碰之后，悄悄停掉这些终端。下次你打开这棵工作树，再恢复同一次会话。

实验功能

Agent 休眠默认关闭。在 Settings → Experimental → Agent hibernation（Agent 休眠）里打开。安全模型还在调整。

哪些会被休眠

下面几条要同时成立，Orca 才会休眠一个 Agent 终端：

  • Agent 处于 done（已完成）：上一回合已经结束，没有在等输入。
  • 这个终端不在当前工作树里，也不在任何正在显示前台终端的工作树里。
  • Agent 做完之后，没有再收到按键。
  • 这个 Agent 有可恢复的会话：Claude、Codex、Gemini、Antigravity、OpenCode、Pi、MiMo Code、Droid、Grok、Devin 或 OMP。
  • 空闲时间至少达到所设的空闲窗口（默认 30 分钟）。
  • 没有手机会话正在操作这个终端。
  • 没有还没落地的编排 Dispatch（pending、dispatched 或未知）。运行时确认 completed、failed 或 circuit_broken 之后，才可以再休眠。
  • 窗格上没有还活着的子 Agent 或队友名单。提供方只报了 done 还不够，子项还挂着就不行。

任一条件不满足，终端就继续跑。一棵工作树有多个 Agent 窗格时，它们作为一组一起休眠，避免只停一半。

也可以从侧栏手动让工作树休眠。有嵌套下级时，用 Sleep with Descendants（连同下级一起休眠）。见工作树。手动休眠会保留 finished（已完成）和 interrupted（已中断）的可恢复会话。重新打开工作树时，仍能用同样的恢复参数拉起来。窗格已经不在前台，也不会因此清掉这些会话记录。

调整空闲时间

Agent hibernation 开关下面，Hibernate after（休眠前等待）决定一个已经做完的后台 Agent 要空闲多少分钟，Orca 才暂停它。

  • 默认 30 分钟。
  • 范围从 1 分钟到 24 小时。
  • 计时从 Agent 最后一次 done 更新开始。按键、新输出，或回到这个 Agent 的终端标签，都会重新计时。

空闲时间设得长，暂停更少，省下的内存也少。设得短，暂停更勤，下次切回来要多一次恢复。

找到休眠的工作区

侧栏的过滤菜单决定是否显示休眠工作区。如果你经常隐藏它们，在 Settings → Shortcuts 里给 Toggle Sleeping Workspaces（切换休眠工作区）设快捷键，就不用每次打开过滤菜单。

恢复

打开一棵休眠的工作树时，Orca 会用和 Agent 会话历史 里一样的恢复参数重新启动 Agent CLI，例如 claude --resume <id>、codex resume <id>。对话、工作目录和提供方会话会从停下的地方继续。不用点任何东西。终端回到前台时，恢复自动发生。

恢复还会复用 Orca 第一次打开这个 Agent 时记下的启动命令、参数和私有环境变量。

如果 Agent CLI 恢复不了这次会话（记录被删了，或提供方换了会话 ID），终端会开到一个新的提示符。之前的记录仍在这个 Agent 的会话历史里。

限制

只有上面列出的、能恢复会话的 Agent 会休眠。Cursor CLI、Hermes、Copilot、Trae，以及其他不能恢复的终端，会继续跑着。

下一步

  • Agent 会话历史：手动恢复过去的任意会话，包括 Orca 没有休眠过的。
  • 热切换 Codex 账号：换掉当前 Codex 登录，不用重启这次会话。这和休眠可以一起用。

Let Orca pause idle background agent terminals and auto-resume them when you reopen the worktree.

When you keep dozens of worktrees open, idle agents add up — each one is a live PTY holding a model session in memory. Agent hibernation lets Orca quietly stop those terminals once they've been done and untouched long enough, then resume the same session the next time you open the worktree.

Experimental

Agent hibernation is off by default. Turn it on under Settings → Experimental → Agent hibernation while we keep tuning the safety model.

What gets hibernated

Orca only hibernates an agent terminal when all of the following are true:

  • The agent is in a done state — it has finished its last turn and isn't waiting on input.
  • The terminal isn't in the active worktree or any worktree currently rendering a foreground terminal.
  • It hasn't received keystrokes since the agent finished.
  • The agent is one with a resumable session: Claude, Codex, Gemini, Antigravity, OpenCode, Pi, MiMo Code, Droid, Grok, Devin, or OMP.
  • It's been idle for at least the configured idle window (default 30 minutes).
  • No mobile session is currently driving the terminal.
  • No orchestration Dispatch is still unsettled (pending, dispatched, or unknown). Sleep is allowed again after runtime-confirmed completed, failed, or circuit_broken.
  • No live subagent / teammate roster remains on the pane (provider "done" alone is not enough while children are still attached).

A terminal that fails any check stays running. If a worktree has multiple agent panes, they hibernate together as a unit so a partially-paused worktree never ships.

You can also sleep worktrees manually from the sidebar (including Sleep with Descendants when nested children exist). See Worktrees. Manual sleep keeps finished and interrupted resumable sessions so reopening the worktree can still relaunch with the same resume flags — it does not wipe those session records just because the pane was no longer "live."

Tuning the idle window

Under the Agent hibernation toggle, Hibernate after sets how many idle minutes a finished background agent has to wait before Orca pauses it.

  • Default: 30 minutes.
  • Range: 1 minute to 24 hours.
  • The clock starts from the agent's last done update; any keystroke, new output, or returning to the agent's terminal tab resets it.

Longer windows trade memory savings for fewer pauses; shorter windows pause more aggressively but cost a resume next time you switch back.

Finding sleeping worktrees

The sidebar filter menu controls whether sleeping worktrees are shown. If you hide them often, assign Toggle Sleeping Workspaces under Settings → Shortcuts to show or hide them without opening the filter menu.

Resuming

When you open a hibernated worktree, Orca relaunches the agent CLI with the same resume flags it would use from Agent Session History — claude --resume <id>, codex resume <id>, and so on — so the conversation, working directory, and provider session pick up where they left off. There's nothing to click; the resume happens as part of bringing the terminal back into the foreground.

Resume also reuses the launch command, arguments, and private environment captured when Orca first opened the agent.

If the agent CLI can't resume the session for any reason (the transcript was deleted, the provider rotated the session ID), the terminal opens to a fresh prompt and the previous transcript stays available under the agent's session history.

Limitations

Only the listed resumable agents hibernate. Cursor CLI, Hermes, Copilot, Trae, and other non-resumable terminals are left running.

Next steps

  • Agent session history — manually resume any past session, including ones Orca didn't hibernate.
  • Hot-swap Codex accounts — swap the active Codex login without restarting the session, which works alongside hibernation.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/hibernation

上一章Agent session history 下一章Usage & rate-limit tracking

Start Here · Working with Agents

用量与速率限制

Usage & rate-limit tracking

用量和速率限制显示在状态栏里。Agent 因限额停住之前，你能先看到离限额还有多远。

Orca 跟踪 Claude Code、Codex、Muse Code、Gemini、Antigravity、OpenCode、Kimi Code、MiniMax、Cursor、ZCode 和 GLM Coding Plan 的用量。

显示什么

  • 当前用量，相对当前账号的套餐。
  • 距离重置还有多久。窗口包括 5 小时、每日、每周，以及 Claude Fable 的每周窗口（有这项才显示）。
  • 越过某一限额的 80% 时，出现警告标记。
  • 提供方如果报了余额，会和用量限额一起显示。包括 Claude 用量额度、OpenCode Zen 余额和 Codex 额度。提供方没有返回余额时，显示为不可用，不会显示成 0。

怎么取数

来源看提供方。能读本机 Agent 状态时，Orca 就读本机，包括 Muse Code 的本地会话日志。Muse 的用量是本机 token 记账，不含价格估算。OpenCode Go 的用量来自它的控制台 API，需要你在 Settings → Accounts 里提供完整的 opencode.ai Cookie 头，其中包括 __Host-console_session。只有一枚 auth cookie 时，可以找到工作区，取不到 Go 的用量。其他提供方可能要在 Settings 里单独配账号。

Cursor 读的是这台电脑上已有的 Cursor 登录。套餐的每月用量显示在状态栏、Usage（用量）弹出层，以及 Settings → Accounts → Cursor。Orca 不会替你登录。保存的会话过期后，自己跑 cursor-agent login。用量请求被拒绝时，显示为用量获取失败，和登录过期分开看。

Antigravity 的配额从 Antigravity 自己的 CLI 读取，包括它的模型组配额池。不需要再登录一份 Gemini CLI。查询本身不消耗配额。ZCode 的 Coding Plan（Coding Plan）配额，和它从磁盘记录里读到的会话历史一起显示。

也可以在 Settings → Accounts → GLM Coding Plan 里直接连一份 GLM Coding Plan：选套餐站点（Z.AI 或 Zhipu BigModel），保存套餐 API key。不需要 ZCode CLI 登录。保存的 key 优先于 ZCode CLI 的登录。这份套餐会和其他提供方一样出现在用量列表里。

多账号记账

状态栏始终反映当前账号。其他已配置的账号在账号切换器里，各自带用量。

用量列表

点状态栏上的用量段，打开 Usage 弹出层。它列出每个被跟踪的提供方：图标、名称、套餐、最近的一次重置、各窗口的条。最紧的限额排在最前。用标题上的刷新重新读取本机用量。

窗口很窄时，状态栏保持一行：先藏起小条和文字，再每个提供方只留一个百分比。还是挤，部分提供方会收成一枚 +N 标记。点它，在 Usage 弹出层里看全部提供方。用量到 80% 或更高的提供方，最后才被收进去。提供方报了余额时，余额显示在该提供方的限额旁边。在窄的 Compact（紧凑）段里，已经顶到套餐上限的提供方会露出还能花的余额。

  • Detailed（详细）：每个窗口的完整条、文字和百分比
  • Compact（紧凑）：每个提供方只留最紧的那个窗口

在 Settings → Appearance 里选择这些数字按 % used（已用百分比）还是 % remaining（剩余百分比）来读。

还没有实时数字的行，会显示一句短状态：Loading usage…（正在加载用量…）、not signed in（未登录）、Usage unavailable（用量不可用）、No usage data（没有用量数据），或提供方自己的错误。Claude 和 Codex 的行可以再点进账号切换。Manage accounts（管理账号）打开 Settings。

手机

在伴侣应用里，打开这台主机的 Accounts（账号）画面，看到的是同一套切换和用量。Codex 攒到了 rate-limit reset（速率限制重置）额度时，可以在这个画面花掉一份。见 Orca 手机应用。

估算费用（Stats）

Stats（统计）的拆分里，可能给已知模型族显示 estimated cost（估算费用），包括 Claude Opus 5.5、Fable 5.1，以及 Codex 的 GPT-5.6 和 GPT-6 Astra、Sol、Luna。Orca 用的是本机价格表，不是提供方的实时账单。• inferred pricing（推断价格）表示这个估算里的模型是 Orca 推断出来的。标了 • excludes unpriced models（不含未定价模型）的 Codex 费用，把这些模型排除在估算之外。Overview 也会标出这是一份不完整的合计。要看权威花费，用提供方自己的控制台。

Orca tracks usage for Claude Code, Codex, Muse Code, Gemini, Antigravity, OpenCode, Kimi Code, MiniMax, Cursor, ZCode, and GLM Coding Plans and surfaces it in the status bar, so you know how close you are to a rate limit before an agent stalls.

What's shown

  • Current usage against the active account's plan.
  • Time to reset for 5-hour, daily, weekly, and Claude Fable weekly windows (where applicable).
  • A warning chip when you cross 80% of a limit.
  • Provider credit balances when the provider reports one — Claude usage credits, OpenCode Zen balance, and Codex credits — alongside the usage limits. A provider that returns no balance reads as unavailable rather than zero.

How it works

The source depends on the provider. Orca reads local agent state where available, including Muse Code's local session logs; Muse usage is local token accounting and does not include a price estimate. OpenCode Go usage comes from its console API and needs your full opencode.ai Cookie header, including __Host-console_session, under Settings → Accounts. An auth cookie alone can find workspaces but cannot fetch Go usage. Other providers can require their own account setup in Settings.

For Cursor, Orca reads the existing Cursor sign-in on your computer and shows the plan's monthly usage in the status bar, Usage popover, and Settings → Accounts → Cursor. It does not sign you in; if the saved session expires, run cursor-agent login. A refused usage request reads as a usage failure, not an expired login.

For Antigravity, Orca reads the quota from Antigravity's own CLI — including its model-group pools — so you do not need a signed-in Gemini CLI, and checking does not spend quota. For ZCode, the Coding Plan quota appears alongside the session history it reads from on-disk transcripts.

You can also link a GLM Coding Plan directly under Settings → Accounts → GLM Coding Plan: pick the plan site (Z.AI or Zhipu BigModel) and save the plan API key. No ZCode CLI sign-in is required, and a saved key takes priority over the ZCode CLI sign-in. The plan appears in the usage roster like any other provider.

Multi-account accounting

The status bar always reflects the active account. Other configured accounts are visible in the account switcher with their own usage.

Usage roster

Click the usage segment in the status bar to open the Usage popover. It lists every tracked provider (icon · name · plan · soonest reset · per-window bars), sorted so the tightest limit is first. Use the header refresh control to re-read local usage state.

In a narrow window, the status bar keeps one line by hiding mini bars and labels, then showing one percentage per provider. If space is still tight, some provider chips become a +N chip. Click it to see every provider in the Usage popover. Providers at 80% usage or higher are collapsed last. When a provider reports a balance, it shows next to that provider's limits; in the narrow Compact segment, a provider that has hit a plan cap reveals its spendable balance.

  • Detailed — full bars, labels, and percentages for every window
  • Compact — only the tightest window per provider

Under Settings → Appearance, choose % used vs % remaining for how those numbers read.

Rows without live numbers show a short status instead: Loading usage…, not signed in, Usage unavailable, No usage data, or a provider-specific error. Claude and Codex rows can drill into account switching; Manage accounts opens Settings.

Mobile

On the companion app, open the host Accounts screen for the same switcher/usage readout. When Codex has earned rate-limit reset credits, spend one from that screen (see Mobile companion).

Estimated cost (Stats)

The Stats breakdown may show estimated cost for known model families, including Claude Opus 5.5, Fable 5.1, and Codex GPT-5.6 and GPT-6 Astra, Sol, and Luna. Orca uses a local price table, not a live bill from the provider. • inferred pricing marks an estimate where Orca inferred the model. A Codex cost marked • excludes unpriced models leaves those models out of the estimate; the Overview also flags a partial total. Prefer the provider console for authoritative spend.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/usage-tracking

上一章Agent hibernation 下一章Agent hooks & memory

Start Here · Working with Agents

Agent 钩子与记忆

Agent hooks & memory

Orca 沿用 Claude Code 和 Codex 已有的钩子和记忆约定：读它们、遵守它们，并把适合放在 IDE 里的部分做成界面。

仓库钩子

Orca 读取每个仓库的 .claude/ 和 .codex/ 配置。你已经写好的钩子，会在 Orca 于该仓库的工作树里启动 Agent 时运行。

工作树初始化钩子

工作树创建之后要自动跑的命令，在这里配。例如 pnpm install、direnv allow，或一段用来恢复 .env 的脚本。入口是 Settings → Repository → Hooks。

记忆文件

Claude 的 CLAUDE.md 和 Codex 的 AGENTS.md（仓库根目录或子目录）保持原样。它们属于 Agent。Orca 在文件浏览器里照常显示，你可以就地编辑。

Agent 状态钩子

Settings → Agents → Agent status hooks（Agent 状态钩子）控制 Orca 托管的那批钩子。它们把 working（进行中）、waiting（等待）、done（完成）报回界面。关掉这项会移除这些托管钩子，并不再重新安装。再打开时会装回去，不用重启 Orca。在 Windows 上，WSL 钩子中继走同一道实时开关。命令行对应：orca agent hooks status|on|off --json。Orca 只在有变化时才把 Codex 钩子写进 ~/.codex，不是每次启动都写，这样 Codex 不会反复要求你批准它。

重启之后还在

钩子端点写在磁盘上。POSIX 是 {userData}/agent-hooks/endpoint.env，Windows 是 endpoint.cmd。每次调用钩子都会重新读取。会话即使开了很久，应用重启之后仍能连上正在运行的 Orca，POST 打到当前端口。比上一轮会话活得更久的 PTY，不会再向已经失效的端口发 POST。

Orca CLI 暴露一个带注释的工作树状态字段，Agent 可以自己更新。见工作树检查点。

Orca plays nicely with the agent hook and memory conventions Claude Code and Codex already use — it reads them, respects them, and gives you a UI for the ones that make sense in an IDE context.

Per-repo hooks

Orca reads each repo's .claude/ and .codex/ configuration. Hooks you already have will run when Orca launches the agent in a worktree of that repo.

Worktree setup hooks

Configure commands to run automatically after a worktree is created — e.g. pnpm install, direnv allow, or a script that restores .env files. Set them under Settings → Repository → Hooks.

Memory files

Claude's CLAUDE.md and Codex's AGENTS.md (at repo root or nested) are left alone — they belong to the agent. Orca surfaces them in the file explorer like any other file so you can edit them inline.

Agent status hooks

Settings → Agents → Agent status hooks controls the Orca-managed hooks that report working / waiting / done into the UI. Turning the setting off removes those managed hooks and stops reinstalling them; turning it back on restores them without restarting Orca (on Windows, the WSL hook relay follows the same live gate). CLI equivalents: orca agent hooks status|on|off --json. Orca writes its Codex hook into ~/.codex only when something changed, instead of on every launch, so Codex stops re-prompting to approve it.

Surviving a restart

Hook endpoints are written to disk ({userData}/agent-hooks/endpoint.env on POSIX, endpoint.cmd on Windows) and re-sourced on every hook invocation, so long-lived agent sessions keep reaching the live Orca server even after an app restart — no more dead-port POSTs from a PTY that outlived the previous session.

The Orca CLI exposes a commented worktree status field agents can update themselves. See Worktree checkpoints.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/agents/hooks-memory

上一章Usage & rate-limit tracking 下一章Diff viewer

Start Here · Reviewing & Shipping Code

diff 查看器

Diff viewer

每棵工作树都有内置 diff，默认比较这棵工作树的起始引用（start-from）。用它认真看 AI 写出的代码。

功能

  • 合并 diff把已暂存、未暂存和未跟踪的文件放在一起看。
  • 左右两边都有行号，可以关掉。
  • 图片 diff 有三种看法：并排、滑动（swipe）、洋葱皮（onion-skin）。
  • **HTML 预览。**在 View all（查看全部）和合并 diff 里，工作树中还在的 HTML 段落，旁边有 Open Preview to the Side（在侧边打开预览），图标是一只眼睛。打开文件的控件一直都在。预览会在旁边的浏览器分屏里打开工作树中的 HTML。已删除的 HTML 不显示这只眼睛。只比较某次提交的合并 diff 界面也不显示。
  • 合并冲突有三方视图，可以在行内解决。
  • 可以按 diff 块或按行暂存，效果和 git add -p 一样，只是看得见。
  • Show Whitespace（显示空白）会显示空格、制表符和其他空白字符。在 diff 查看器的操作菜单里切换。
  • Collapse unchanged regions（折叠未改动区域）可以把文件 diff 里长段未改的上下文折起来，改动行留在视野里。在 diff 编辑器的操作菜单里打开。默认关闭。

比较范围

diff 默认比较工作树的起始引用。可以从 diff 工具栏改成和任意提交、任意分支，或基准引用比较。

自动换行

打开 diff 自动换行后，长行就地折行。宽 diff 从上往下读，不用横向滚动。默认关闭。在 diff 编辑器顶栏的 ⋯ 操作菜单里切换 Word Wrap（自动换行），或到 Settings → General → Diff Word Wrap 设全局默认值。两处是同一项设置。在编辑器里切换，所有地方一起变。

折叠未改动区域和自动换行只作用于单文件 diff。合并 diff 仍用自己的文件树和 diff 块控件。

文件树

合并 diff 可以在 diff 块旁边显示可折叠的文件树。拖树的宽度手柄来调宽度。手柄上也可以用方向键，按住 Shift 步长更大。宽度会跨会话记住。在合并 diff 的工具栏里可以收起或展开这棵树。

键盘快捷键

  • j / k：下一个 / 上一个有改动的文件。
  • n / p：下一个 / 上一个 diff 块。
  • F7 / Shift+F7：当前编辑器里的下一处 / 上一处改动。
  • s：暂存光标所在的 diff 块。
  • c：开始写评论。见批注 AI diff。

Orca's diff viewer is designed for serious review of AI-generated code — not a quick glance. Every worktree has a built-in diff against its start-from ref.

Features

  • Combined diff across all staged, unstaged, and untracked files.
  • Line numbers for both sides, toggleable.
  • Image diffs — side-by-side, swipe, and onion-skin modes for binary images.
  • HTML preview — in View all / combined diffs, HTML sections that still exist in the working tree show Open Preview to the Side (eye) next to the always-visible open-file control. Preview opens the working-tree HTML in a side browser split. Deleted HTML and commit-only combined surfaces skip the eye.
  • Merge-conflict UI with three-way view and inline resolution.
  • Staging by hunk or line — same as git add -p but visual.
  • Show Whitespace — reveal spaces, tabs, and other whitespace characters when reviewing a diff. Toggle it from the diff viewer actions menu.
  • Collapse unchanged regions — optionally fold long runs of context in a file diff so the changed lines stay in view. Turn it on from the diff editor actions menu; it is off by default.

Scoping

The diff shows changes against the worktree's start-from ref by default. You can switch to comparing against any commit, branch, or the base ref from the diff toolbar.

Word wrap

Long lines wrap in place when diff word wrap is on, so wide diffs read top-to-bottom without horizontal scrolling. It's off by default. Toggle Word Wrap from the ⋯ actions menu in the diff editor header, or set the global default under Settings → General → Diff Word Wrap. Both controls share the same setting — toggling from the editor flips it everywhere.
