上一章Terminal 下一章SSH worktrees

Remote & SSH

SSH 工作树

SSH worktrees

Orca 可以通过 SSH 在远程机器上驱动 Agent。长时间的构建、GPU 机器，或笔记本不适合跑这份工作的环境，都适合。

四种运行方式之一

SSH 是把 Agent 放到远程算力上的一种方式。本机、远程 Orca 服务器和短命虚拟机，见 Orca 的几种运行方式。

添加一个 SSH 目标

  1. 打开 Settings → SSH。
  2. 点 Add Target（添加目标）。主机表单在一个对话框里打开，不嵌在列表下面，所以主机很多时，Host、Advanced 和 Save 仍然够得着。编辑用同一个对话框，并显示目标标签加上 user@host:port。
  3. 填主机、用户、端口，以及可选的身份文件。也可以在同一个对话框里打开 OpenSSH config 选择器，搜索 ~/.ssh/config（包括 Include 进来的文件），选一台主机，预填表单。已经存在 Orca 里的主机会显示 In Orca（已在 Orca）标记。那个批量流程可用时，你仍然可以从配置一次性导入多台主机。
  4. 密钥有口令时，Orca 会在第一次提示你。
  5. 点 Test（测试）确认能连上，再点 Save（保存）。

添加或编辑的表单有未保存内容时，点到外面不会丢掉字段。要用 Escape、Cancel 或 × 才丢弃。

主机密钥核对

Orca 拿内置 SSH 连接去对你实际生效的 OpenSSH known_hosts 文件，以及它以前保存过的密钥。已经对上的会静默连上。默认策略下，第一次接触时 Orca 接受并记住这台主机，并显示它的指纹。StrictHostKeyChecking yes 会拒绝未知主机。no / off 会接受，但不保存信任记录。

密钥变了、被撤销了，或密钥类型和预期不同，Orca 会在向你要密码或密钥口令之前拒绝。你有意重建或重新制备了这台主机时，先核对新指纹，再按错误里的恢复路径做。known_hosts 对不上时，Orca 会给出合适的 ssh-keygen -R 命令（需要时包含带方括号的主机和端口）。

使用一个目标

建工作树时，选一个 SSH 目标，不选 Local（本机）。Orca 会：

  • 在远程主机上创建 Git 工作树。
  • 通过 SSH 连接在远程跑 Agent。
  • 同步文件事件，让编辑器、diff 和浏览器仍然像在本机。

高级连接选项

打开 Settings → SSH，编辑一个目标，需要网络代理、跳板机，或覆盖 SSH 多路复用时，展开 Advanced Connection（高级连接）。Orca 默认打开 Reuse SSH connection for faster setup（复用 SSH 连接，加快准备）。它在 macOS 和 Linux 上使用 OpenSSH 的连接复用，这样准备命令不用每次都重新握手。只有 SSH 策略拒绝多路复用会话的主机，才关掉它。

口令

口令在这次 Orca 会话期间留在内存里。关掉 Orca 就清掉。你可以在 SSH 设置里选择更长的存活时间。

多因素登录

主机要求验证码，或其他键盘交互提示（一次性密码、MFA 质询，或类似提示）时，内置连接可以用：Orca 把提示转给你，再把你的回答转回去。提示如果属于脚本化的准备步骤，会推迟到你能回答的那一步，而不是让连接失败。

内置客户端和主机没有兼容的 SSH 算法时，Orca 会改用你系统里的 ssh 再试。那条路可以用这套安装为那台主机配置的算法。

状态

远程工作树有一枚实时 SSH 状态标记：绿色已连接，黄色正在重连，红色已断开。SSH 主机还在连接时，Orca 可以用本机保存的元数据列出工作区，侧栏不用空等到实时提供方起来。连接稳定之后，这些行会刷新成权威的远程状态。断开不会杀掉正在跑的 Agent。Orca 会重连并重新接上，回放滚过的输出，并从已渲染的画面恢复全屏应用窗格，而不是还你一块空白或碎掉的画面。恢复中的 SSH 终端重新接上时，你打的键会保留，不会丢掉。主机掉线时，受影响的工作区卡片可以显示一行内联的 Connect（连接）或重连控件，不用打开终端浮层，也不用去状态栏里找。SSH 连接以 “Multiplexer disposed” 这类失败掉线时，Orca 会自动恢复，不会把窗格卡到你重启为止。Agent 状态（正在做 / 空闲 / 被挡住）通过 SSH 传播的方式和本机一样，所以侧栏和 Agent 动态 会实时反映远程 Agent。

关掉应用之后的会话

关掉桌面应用不再杀掉远程 PTY 会话。它们住在主机的托管运行时上，所以笔记本上的 Orca 关掉之后它们还在。你重新打开应用并连上目标时，会话会回到它们的标签，状态是 attached（已接上），回滚缓冲还在。托管运行时上的结构化对话，在回复还在跑或问题还在等时，会保持它的提供方已加载，所以桌面断开之后，批准还在。和主机失联，不能说明那边的工作停了。在同一棵工作树上再开第二个 Agent 之前，先看那台主机。

重连和重启

打开的文件、还活着的 Agent 标签，以及暂停中的 Claude 或 Codex 恢复提议，都能熬过 SSH 重连和应用重启，工作区会回到你离开时的样子。连接断开时你打的键，回来后会按顺序、恰好送达一次。窗格的回滚缓冲和尺寸也能熬过重连。托管运行时更新之后，打开的标签会重新接上。很早以前关掉的标签不会再变成空壳回来。你点一棵 SSH 工作树，而它的最后一个标签已经被你关掉时，会开一个新终端。

想让一台按需使用的 SSH 主机在 Orca 启动时保持断开，编辑那台主机，关掉 Advanced → Reconnect on launch（高级 → 启动时重连）。这个开关默认是开的。关掉期间，Orca 会等到你点 Connect，才为这次会话重连这台主机或它的托管运行时。

会打印文字的 shell 启动文件、有几万个条目的文件夹，以及 64 位 ARM Linux 服务器，不再会弄断或丢掉连接。两台机器共享一条路径时，文件、浏览器标签、链接和拖放都留在它们所属的那台机器上。

SSH 主机上的 orca CLI

默认情况下，在 SSH 主机上跑的 orca 命令只能碰到那台主机自己的终端和编排消息。那边的 Agent 仍然可以向派出它的协调者回报，但碰不到你的其他主机、桌面控制、账号或设置。要让那台主机上的 Agent 通过 orca 创建工作树、打开终端或派出工作，为那台主机打开 Allow this host's orca CLI to control Orca（允许这台主机的 orca CLI 控制 Orca）。位置是 Settings → SSH → 编辑主机 → Advanced Connection。从 v1.4.224 带过来的终端里没有 orca。在同一台主机上的新终端里跑 orca 命令。

Orca 在主机上删不掉某个 SSH 工作区时，Remove from Orca（从 Orca 移除）会把它从应用里去掉，文件留在主机上。

下载远程文件和文件夹

在 SSH 工作树的文件浏览器里右键：

  • File（文件）→ Download（下载）：本机保存对话框，把文件复制到笔记本。
  • Folder（文件夹）→ Download Folder（下载文件夹）：递归下载到你选的文件夹（仅桌面）。

Orca 用的传输路径和这条连接相同。只有连接宣告了递归文件夹传输时（通常是完整的 SFTP），才会出现文件夹下载。一直停在仅系统 OpenSSH 传输的连接，仍可能下载文件，但不显示 Download Folder。

这是仅桌面的动作。网页客户端不提供 Download，因为它依赖 Electron 的保存对话框。

在 VS Code 里打开远程工作区

对 SSH 工作树，工作树菜单的 Open in（打开方式）可以把远程路径交给 VS Code Remote-SSH。你配置的应用是 VS Code 或 VS Code Insiders 时可以（code / code-insiders，或这些启动器的直接路径）。

  1. 配置 Settings → General → Open in menu，让 VS Code 出现在列表里（预设或自定义命令）。
  2. 右键这棵 SSH 工作树（或用工作树的溢出菜单）→ Open in → VS Code。
  3. Orca 用 Remote-SSH 对着那台主机和工作树路径启动 VS Code（--remote ssh-remote+<host> <path>）。菜单项可能显示 Remote SSH 标记。

这条远程路径不支持： Cursor、Zed、组合的 shell 命令，或通过远程 Orca 服务器的活动运行时打开（那些保持 Local only，仅本机）。Finder / Explorer 那一项只用于本机路径。

Kerberos / GSSAPI

OpenSSH 配置里的主机设了 GSSAPIAuthentication yes 时，Orca 对那个目标优先用系统 OpenSSH 传输（内置的 ssh2 客户端不会说 GSSAPI）。连接之前保持一张有效的 Kerberos 票据（kinit，或你们组织的 SSO）。手工添加的目标，在配置成使用系统 SSH 时也可以启用 GSSAPI。

从配置导入的主机不用再拨一个单独的 “Kerberos mode” 开关。导入和 ssh -G 会把这个 flag 带过来。

FIDO2 / 安全密钥

由硬件支持的 OpenSSH 身份（ed25519-sk、ecdsa-sk，包括由 agent 持有的安全密钥）也走系统 OpenSSH，不走内置 ssh2 客户端。Orca 从你的身份文件判断密钥类型，把连接交给操作系统的 OpenSSH 二进制，这样触摸或 PIN 提示照常工作。普通的 Ed25519、ECDSA 和 RSA 密钥仍走内置传输。这台机器上没有 OpenSSH 时，这些 FIDO2 目标在装上并进入 PATH（或 Windows / macOS / Linux 的常见位置）之前无法认证。

托管运行时

第一次连接时，Orca 会在主机上装好自己的服务器：一份它随应用带上、并保持更新的固定运行时。连接不再因为主机的 Node 版本不对或没有构建工具而失败。没有开关可以关掉这项。

你的电脑和主机都下不了 Node.js 运行时，连接错误会提供 Use a Node.js mirror…（使用 Node.js 镜像…）。看一遍或换掉预填的镜像，然后选 Save and retry（保存并重试）。Orca 会用固定的校验和核对每次下载。配置的镜像也失败时，先到 Settings → Advanced → Network 改镜像，再重连。

  • 空主机会被装好，并连到它的托管运行时。
  • 已经有 Orca 项目、但没有打开终端的主机，会在下一次连接时把项目、文件夹、项目组和打开的编辑器标签挪到托管运行时上。
  • v1.4.224 留下还在跑的终端，升级和重连之后仍在原来的标签里工作。那台主机上的新终端在托管运行时上打开。
  • 托管运行时跑不了的主机，不再退回旧的 SSH 运行时。连接会失败并给出原因，Settings → SSH Hosts 会显示出来。要继续连那台主机，用更旧的 Orca 版本。

每台服务器在 Settings → Managed servers 里管理：看版本和正在跑的终端，Update（更新）或 Roll back（回退），Recover（恢复）一次中断的更新，或 Stop…（停止…）/ Forget…（忘掉…）。有终端在跑时，Update anyway（仍然更新）会更新服务器而不关掉它们。CLI 有同样的动作：orca environment status|update|rollback|recover|stop|forget。见 CLI 参考。

运行时选择

旧的按主机 Runtime（运行时）选择器（Auto / Orca-managed Node / Host Node）和只做中继的生命周期设置已经没有了。每条 SSH 连接都跑在托管运行时上。Orca 在某台主机上跑不了托管运行时时（不支持的操作系统或 CPU、noexec 的主目录，或拒绝端口转发的 SSH 服务器），连接会在 Settings → SSH Hosts 里说明原因，不再静默退回。

端口转发

远程工作树的右侧栏有一个 Ports（端口）标签（用 Cmd+Shift+I 开关）。Orca 扫描的是工作区自己的主机，也就是这棵工作树所在的托管 SSH 服务器或已配对服务器，不是你的笔记本。它把正在监听的端口列在 Detected（已发现）下面。点一下就转发给你，也可以在同一块面板里停掉一个已发现的端口。你也可以手工添加转发、编辑或去掉。保存的转发在托管主机上重连后会回来，并跨应用重启保留。特权远程端口会在本机自动改映射（例如远程 80 → 本机 10080）。保存的转发启动不了时，那一行会说明原因，并让你去掉它。在托管 SSH 主机上，对一个已发现的端口选 Forward（转发），会打开转发对话框。在共享的 Linux 主机上，已发现列表不含其他登录用户拥有的端口。root 和系统服务的端口仍然可见。

Orca can drive agents on remote machines over SSH — useful for long-running builds, GPU boxes, or any environment where your laptop isn't the right place to run the work.

One of four run modes

SSH is one way to put agents on remote compute. For local, self-hosted servers, and ephemeral VMs, see Ways to run Orca.

Add an SSH target

  1. Open Settings → SSH.
  2. Click Add Target. The host form opens in a modal (not inline under the list), so Host, Advanced, and Save stay reachable even with a long host list. Edit uses the same dialog and shows the target label plus user@host:port.
  3. Fill in host, user, port, and optional identity file — or open the OpenSSH config picker in the same dialog to search ~/.ssh/config (including Included files), pick one host, and prefill the form. Hosts already saved in Orca show an In Orca badge. You can still import hosts in bulk from config when that flow is available.
  4. If the key is passphrase-protected, Orca will prompt the first time.
  5. Click Test to verify connectivity, then Save.

Dirty add/edit forms ignore outside clicks so an accidental click away does not discard your fields; use Escape, Cancel, or × to discard.

Host key verification

Orca checks built-in SSH connections against your effective OpenSSH known_hosts files and keys it previously saved. Existing matches connect silently. With the default policy, Orca accepts and remembers a host on first contact and shows its fingerprint; StrictHostKeyChecking yes rejects unknown hosts, while no / off accepts without saving a trust record.

A changed, revoked, or unexpectedly different key type is rejected before Orca asks for a password or key passphrase. If you intentionally rebuilt or reprovisioned the host, verify its new fingerprint first, then follow the recovery path in the error. For a known_hosts mismatch, Orca provides the appropriate ssh-keygen -R command (including the bracketed host and port when needed).

Use a target

When creating a worktree, choose an SSH target instead of Local. Orca will:

  • Create the git worktree on the remote host.
  • Run agents remotely through the SSH connection.
  • Sync file events so the editor, diff, and browser still feel local.

Advanced connection options

Open Settings → SSH, edit a target, and expand Advanced Connection when the host needs a proxy, jump host, or SSH multiplexing override. Orca enables Reuse SSH connection for faster setup by default, which uses OpenSSH connection reuse on macOS and Linux so setup commands do not each pay a fresh SSH handshake. Turn it off only for hosts whose SSH policy rejects multiplexed sessions.

Passphrases

Passphrases are held in memory for the life of the Orca session. Closing Orca clears them. You can opt into a longer TTL under SSH settings.

Multi-factor sign-in

Hosts that ask for a verification code or another keyboard-interactive prompt (a one-time password, an MFA challenge, or a similar prompt) work over the built-in connection: Orca relays the prompt to you and forwards your answer. If the prompt is part of a scripted setup step, it is deferred to a step you can answer rather than failing the connect.

If the built-in client and host have no compatible SSH algorithm, Orca retries through your system ssh, which can use the algorithms configured for that installation.

Status

Remote worktrees show a chip with live SSH status — green connected, yellow reconnecting, red disconnected. While an SSH host is still connecting, Orca can list workspaces from persisted local metadata so the sidebar is not empty until the live provider comes up; those rows refresh to authoritative remote state once the connection settles. Disconnects don't kill running agents; Orca reconnects and re-attaches, replaying scrolling output and restoring full-screen app panes from their rendered frame instead of returning them blank or fragmented. Keystrokes you type while a restored SSH terminal reattaches are kept, not dropped. When a host drops, the affected workspace card can show an inline Connect / reconnect control so you can recover without opening a terminal overlay or hunting the status bar. If an SSH connection drops with a "Multiplexer disposed" style failure, Orca recovers automatically instead of leaving the pane stuck until you restart. Agent status (working / idle / blocked) propagates over SSH the same way it does locally, so the sidebar and Agents feed reflect remote agents in real time.

Sessions across app close

Closing the desktop app no longer kills your remote PTY sessions. They live on the host's managed Orca server, so they survive Orca closing on your laptop. When you reopen the app and reconnect to the target, the sessions are restored to their tabs in the attached state, with their scrollback intact. A structured chat on a managed server keeps its provider loaded while a reply is running or a question is waiting, so an approval is still there after the desktop disconnects. Losing contact with a host is not evidence that its work stopped, so check the host before starting a second agent on the same worktree.

Reconnect and restart

Open files, live agent tabs, and paused Claude or Codex resume offers survive an SSH reconnect and an app restart, so the workspace comes back the way you left it. Keys you type while the connection is down are delivered exactly once and in order when it returns, and a pane's scrollback and size survive the reconnect. Open tabs reattach after a managed server update. Tabs you closed long ago no longer return as empty shells, and clicking an SSH worktree whose last tab you closed opens a fresh terminal.

To leave an on-demand SSH host disconnected when Orca starts, edit that host and turn off Advanced → Reconnect on launch. The switch is on by default. While it is off, Orca waits until you select Connect before reconnecting the host or its managed server for that session.

Shell startup files that print text, folders with tens of thousands of entries, and 64-bit ARM Linux servers no longer break or drop the connection. When two machines share a path, files, browser tabs, links, and drag-and-drop stay on the machine they belong to.

The orca CLI on an SSH host

By default, orca commands run on an SSH host reach only that host's own terminals and orchestration messages. An agent there can still report back to a coordinator that dispatched it, but it can't reach your other hosts, desktop control, accounts, or settings. To let that host's agents create worktrees, open terminals, or dispatch work through orca, turn on Allow this host's orca CLI to control Orca for that host (Settings → SSH → edit the host → Advanced Connection). Terminals carried over from v1.4.224 do not have orca; run orca commands in a new terminal on the same host.

When deleting an SSH workspace that Orca cannot delete on the host, Remove from Orca removes it from the app and leaves its files on the host.

Downloading remote files and folders

Right-click in the SSH worktree file explorer:

  • File → Download — native save dialog; copies the file to your laptop.
  • Folder → Download Folder — recursive download into a folder you choose (desktop only).

Orca uses the same SSH transfer path as the connection. Folder download appears only when the connection advertises recursive folder transfer (typically full SFTP). Connections that stay on system OpenSSH-only transfer may still download files but not show Download Folder.

This is a desktop-only action — the web client doesn't expose Download because it relies on Electron's save dialog.

Open a remote workspace in VS Code

For SSH worktrees, the worktree menu's Open in list can hand the remote path to VS Code Remote-SSH when your configured app is VS Code or VS Code Insiders (code / code-insiders, or a direct path to those launchers).

  1. Configure Settings → General → Open in menu so VS Code is listed (preset or custom command).
  2. Right-click the SSH worktree (or use the worktree overflow menu) → Open in → VS Code.
  3. Orca launches VS Code with Remote-SSH against that host and the worktree path (--remote ssh-remote+<host> <path>). The menu entry may show a Remote SSH badge.

Not supported for this remote path: Cursor, Zed, compound shell commands, or opening via a Remote Orca Server active runtime (those stay Local only). Use the Finder/Explorer item only for local paths.

Kerberos / GSSAPI

If a host in OpenSSH config sets GSSAPIAuthentication yes, Orca prefers the system OpenSSH transport for that target (the built-in ssh2 client does not speak GSSAPI). Keep a valid Kerberos ticket (kinit / your org's SSO) before connecting. Manual targets can also enable GSSAPI when configured for system SSH.

You do not flip a separate “Kerberos mode” switch for config-imported hosts — import/ssh -G carries the flag through.

FIDO2 / security keys

Hardware-backed OpenSSH identities (ed25519-sk, ecdsa-sk, including agent-backed security keys) also use system OpenSSH rather than the built-in ssh2 client. Orca detects the key type from your identity files and hands the connection to the OS OpenSSH binary so the touch / PIN prompt works as usual. Regular Ed25519, ECDSA, and RSA keys stay on the built-in transport. If OpenSSH is not available on the machine, those FIDO2 targets cannot authenticate until it is installed and on PATH (or in the usual Windows/macOS/Linux locations).

Managed Orca server

On first connect, Orca sets up its own server on the host — a pinned runtime it ships and keeps up to date — so a connection no longer fails because the host has the wrong Node version or no build tools. There is no setting to turn this on.

If neither your computer nor the host can download the Node.js runtime, the connection error offers Use a Node.js mirror…. Review or replace the prefilled mirror, then choose Save and retry; Orca verifies each download against its pinned checksum. If the configured mirror also fails, open Settings → Advanced → Network to correct the mirror before reconnecting.

  • An empty host is set up and connects to its managed server.
  • A host that already has Orca projects but no open terminals moves its projects, folders, project groups, and open editor tabs onto the managed server on its next connect.
  • Terminals left running by v1.4.224 keep working in their existing tabs through the upgrade and reconnect; new terminals on that host open on the managed server.
  • A host the managed server cannot run on no longer falls back to the old SSH runtime. The connect fails with the reason, and Settings → SSH Hosts shows it — use an older Orca version to keep connecting to that host.

Manage each server under Settings → Managed servers: see its version and running terminals, Update or Roll back, Recover an interrupted update, or Stop… / Forget… it. If terminals are running, Update anyway updates the server without closing them. The CLI mirrors those actions — orca environment status|update|rollback|recover|stop|forget — see the CLI reference.

Runtime choices

The old per-host Runtime selector (Auto / Orca-managed Node / Host Node) and the relay-only lifecycle settings are gone: every SSH connection runs on the managed Orca server. If Orca cannot run the managed server on a host — an unsupported OS or CPU, a noexec home directory, or an SSH server that refuses port forwarding — the connect explains why in Settings → SSH Hosts instead of silently falling back.

Port forwarding

For remote worktrees, the right sidebar shows a Ports tab (toggle with Cmd+Shift+I). Orca scans the workspace's own host — the managed SSH server or paired server the worktree runs on, not your laptop — and lists listening ports under Detected; one click forwards them to you, and you can stop a detected port from the same panel. You can also add forwards manually, edit them, or remove them. Saved forwards return on managed hosts after a reconnect, persist across app restarts, and privileged remote ports auto-remap locally (e.g. remote 80 → local 10080). If a saved forward cannot start, its row explains why and lets you remove it. On managed SSH hosts, select Forward on a detected port to open the forwarding dialog. On shared Linux hosts, the detected list excludes ports owned by other login users; root and system-service ports remain visible.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/ssh

上一章Ways to run Orca 下一章Remote Orca Servers

Remote & SSH

远程 Orca 服务器

Remote Orca Servers

一台电脑干活，另一台电脑提供界面。服务器留着项目、工作树、终端、标签、提供方账号和 Agent 会话。笔记本连到那个正在运行的 Orca。

最省事的配法是两台电脑都用 Orca 桌面应用，通过 Tailscale 相连。这条路不用跑 orca serve。

Beta

远程 Orca 服务器是 Beta。服务器和客户端留在你掌控的私有网络路径上，例如同一个 Tailscale tailnet，或局域网。

什么跑在哪里

Client computer · Orca client
  Shows the UI and sends your input
                 │
                 │ private network route
                 ▼
Server computer · Orca server
  Stores repos and worktrees
  Runs terminals and agents

在服务器电脑上安装并登录 Codex、Claude Code、OpenCode、git，以及各个提供方的 CLI。笔记本上的登录不会自动带到服务器。

在无界面的 orca serve 主机上，从服务器的 shell 登记托管的 Claude / Codex 账号（远程客户端会禁用 Add account，添加账号）：

orca account add --agent claude
orca account add --agent codex
orca account list

没有 Settings 界面时，安装或刷新 Agent 技能：

orca skills install --skill orca-cli --skill orchestration
orca skills update --all

推荐：桌面应用加 Tailscale

你需要：

  • 两台电脑都装了并更新了 Orca
  • 两台电脑都装了 Tailscale
  • 两台电脑登录了同一个 tailnet
  • 服务器电脑醒着、在线，并且 Orca 在跑

Tailscale 给服务器一个通常以 100. 开头的私有地址。Orca 会把这个地址放在连接地址选择器的第一位。

1. 在服务器上创建访问链接

在应该继续跑会话的那台电脑上：

  1. 打开 Orca 桌面应用。
  2. 打开 Settings → Remote Orca Servers。
  3. 在 Advertise this app as a server（把这个应用宣告为服务器）下，点 New Link（新建链接）。
  4. 在 Connection address（连接地址）里选 Tailscale 地址。通常像 100.x.y.z。
  5. 点 Generate Access Link（生成访问链接）。
  6. 复制 Pair another Orca client（配对另一台 Orca 客户端）下面的链接。

没有 Tailscale 地址时，确认 Tailscale 已连接，再点 Connection address 旁边的刷新按钮。

访问链接不要外传

配对 URL 能进入这份 Orca 运行时。把它当成密码，只发给你打算配对的那台客户端。

2. 在笔记本上添加服务器

在你想当客户端的那台电脑上：

  1. 打开 Settings → Remote Orca Servers。
  2. 点 Add Server（添加服务器）。
  3. 起一个认得出来的名字，例如 Remote Server。
  4. 贴上服务器上的访问链接。
  5. 点 Add Server。
  6. 保存的服务器显示 Disconnected（已断开）时，点 Connect（连接）。

添加服务器只会保存它，不会把每个新项目都强行放到它上面。只有当你希望走服务器的项目、终端、提供方检查，以及浏览器或手机交接默认用那台服务器时，才打开 Advanced → Active Server（高级 → 当前服务器）。

使用远程服务器

服务器显示 Connected（已连接）之后，选它或它的一个项目，照常使用 Orca。终端、Agent 进程、文件、工作树和会话状态都在服务器电脑上。

多台已配对的客户端共用那台服务器时，侧栏过滤可以提供 Hide other-client workspaces（隐藏其他客户端的工作区），让这台设备只列出它自己创建的工作区。见 工作树 → 侧栏布局。

这意味着：

  • 客户端笔记本睡了或断开，Agent 继续跑。
  • 服务器上要有这些 Agent 用的仓库、工具和凭据。
  • 服务器要保持醒着，并连在 tailnet 上。
  • 客户端重连或重新打开 Orca，会回到服务器拥有的工作区、标签和可见窗格状态，不会把已配对的标签复制一份。已配对服务器的标签跨桌面重启保留。你关掉的标签保持关掉。
  • 主机应用重新启动之后，已配对的终端又能接受输入。网络掉线之后，远程浏览器标签会回来。orca serve 重启之后，手机仍保留它的终端。
  • 在远程主机上创建工作区，不会把其他已连接的桌面拉过去。
  • 你在服务器上删除的项目，会从每个已配对客户端的侧栏消失，不会留下永久的空行。

访问和安全

Orca 为每个已配对的客户端建一份单独的、可撤销的令牌。服务器在 Shared Server Access（已共享的服务器访问）下列出这些。

  • 点授权旁边的垃圾桶按钮可以撤销。正在用那份授权的客户端会立刻断开。
  • 再生成一条链接，会换掉上一条还没用过的链接。已经配对的客户端保持自己的授权，直到你撤销。
  • Tailscale 的 ACL 或授权尽量收窄到你的设置所允许的范围。
  • 不要把 Orca 的端口直接转发到公网。优先用 Tailscale、WireGuard、可信的局域网、SSH 转发，或经过认证的隧道。
  • 不要为另一台电脑选 127.0.0.1。那个地址只在服务器自己身上有效。

另一种：orca serve

主机应该不打开桌面窗口就跑时用 orca serve，例如无界面的 Linux 服务器，或由服务管理的虚拟机。你可以一直登录着的 MacBook 或台式机，用上面的应用内设置更简单。

在服务器上安装 Orca 和它附带的 CLI，然后运行：

orca serve --pairing-address <server-tailscale-ip-or-hostname>

例如：

orca serve --pairing-address 100.64.1.20

这条命令会：

  • 启动 Orca 运行时，不打开桌面窗口；
  • 在前台跑，直到你按 Ctrl-C；
  • 打印绑定的端点和一条运行时配对 URL；
  • --pairing-address 只填写客户端应该拨入的地址。

orca serve 默认跑在 Orca 自己的 Node 服务器（orcad）上。orcad 在那台电脑上服务不了时，Orca 会退回桌面应用的服务器，并打印一行原因。打包的 macOS 应用目前始终用桌面应用的服务器，这样已配对的客户端仍然能更新它。设 ORCA_SERVE_RUNTIME=electron 可以强制走那条路。

把打印出的配对 URL 贴进客户端的 Settings → Remote Orca Servers → Add Server。

防火墙、隧道或服务定义要求固定端口时，加上 --port 6768：

orca serve --port 6768 --pairing-address 100.64.1.20

一次只用一种主机模式。Orca 桌面应用已经在共享这台电脑时，不要再为同一套设置启动第二个 orca serve 进程。

从无界面服务器配对手机

给 Orca 手机应用要一个限定给手机的二维码和链接：

orca serve --pairing-address 100.64.1.20 --mobile-pairing

让手机留在同一个 tailnet 上，打开 Orca Mobile，选 Pair（配对），扫描终端里的二维码，或贴上打印出的链接。

把 orca serve 做成服务

要让服务器在服务管理器下一直跑，orca serve 可以监督自己，并安装自己的按用户服务：

orca serve service install --port 6768
orca serve service status
orca serve service uninstall

install 在 macOS 上写一个 LaunchAgent，在 Linux 上写一个 systemd 用户单元，为这个配置运行 orca serve --supervise --no-pairing。监督者用越来越长的延迟重启服务器，而不是在反复失败时打转，并在更新替换应用时等待。服务从不把配对链接打进日志。从桌面应用配对客户端，或在服务停着的时候在终端里跑 orca serve。Linux 上，除非打开 lingering（loginctl enable-linger），服务会在注销时停掉。install 会报告这一点。status 说明它是否在跑。uninstall 停掉它并去掉单元，终端守护进程和它的会话继续跑。这些服务命令在 Windows 上还不可用。

已经有别的服务管理器或容器在重启这个进程时，直接用 orca serve --supervise。

桌面应用，还是 orca serve？

服务器上的桌面应用 orca serve
适合 旧笔记本、Mac mini 或台式机 无界面的 Linux、虚拟机，或托管服务
设置 设置和按钮 终端命令和服务配置
服务器窗口 开着 没有
访问链接 New Link → Generate Access Link 打印在终端里
存活时间 桌面应用在跑的时候 前台进程或服务在跑的时候

远程 Orca 服务器，还是 SSH？

笔记本上的 Orca 应该拥有运行时，另一台机器只用来跑选中的工作树和终端时，用 SSH 工作树。

另一台机器应该拥有完整的 Orca 运行时，并为桌面、浏览器、手机或自动化客户端保留共享会话时，用远程 Orca 服务器。

完整比较见 Orca 的几种运行方式。

排错

列表里没有 Tailscale 地址

在服务器上确认 Tailscale 已连接，再点 Connection address 旁边的刷新按钮。两台电脑必须登录同一个 tailnet。Tailscale 的 IPv4 地址通常以 100. 开头。

服务器是断开的

确认服务器电脑醒着，Orca 还在跑，Tailscale 显示两台设备都在线。一台够不到另一台时，检查 tailnet 的 ACL 或授权。

服务器那一行报告协议版本不兼容时，两台电脑都更新 Orca。

访问链接给错了人

在服务器上打开 Settings → Remote Orca Servers → Shared Server Access，撤销那份授权。给打算配对的客户端生成一条新链接。

服务器找不到某个 Agent CLI

在服务器电脑上安装并登录那个 CLI。远程会话用的是服务器的 PATH、主目录和凭据，不是客户端的。

orca serve 宣告了错误的地址

停掉命令，用客户端够得着的地址重新启动：

orca serve --pairing-address <reachable-tailscale-ip-or-hostname>

远程客户端不要用通配地址或 127.0.0.1。

下一步

  • 在 Orca 的几种运行方式 里比较每一种运行方式。
  • 笔记本应该拥有 Orca 运行时时，用 SSH 工作树。
  • orca serve 的 flag 和自动化命令见 Orca CLI 参考。

Keep Orca running on another computer and connect from your laptop.

A Remote Orca Server lets one computer do the work while another computer provides the UI. The server keeps the projects, worktrees, terminals, tabs, provider accounts, and agent sessions. Your laptop connects to that running Orca instance.

The easiest setup is the Orca desktop app on both computers, connected through Tailscale. You do not need to run orca serve for this path.

Beta

Remote Orca Servers are beta. Keep the server and client on a private network path you control, such as the same Tailscale tailnet or LAN.

What runs where

Client computer · Orca client
  Shows the UI and sends your input
                 │
                 │ private network route
                 ▼
Server computer · Orca server
  Stores repos and worktrees
  Runs terminals and agents

Install and authenticate Codex, Claude Code, OpenCode, git, and any provider CLIs on the server computer. A login on your laptop does not automatically carry over to the server.

On a headless orca serve host, register managed Claude/Codex accounts from the server shell (the remote client disables Add account):

orca account add --agent claude
orca account add --agent codex
orca account list

Install or refresh agent skills without a Settings UI:

orca skills install --skill orca-cli --skill orchestration
orca skills update --all

Recommended: desktop app + Tailscale

You need:

  • Orca installed and updated on both computers
  • Tailscale installed on both computers
  • Both computers signed in to the same tailnet
  • The server computer awake, online, and running Orca

Tailscale gives the server a private address that usually starts with 100.. Orca puts that address first in its connection-address picker.

1. Create an access link on the server

On the computer that should keep the sessions running:

  1. Open the Orca desktop app.
  2. Open Settings → Remote Orca Servers.
  3. Under Advertise this app as a server, click New Link.
  4. For Connection address, select the Tailscale address. It usually looks like 100.x.y.z.
  5. Click Generate Access Link.
  6. Copy the link under Pair another Orca client.

If the Tailscale address is missing, confirm Tailscale is connected and click the refresh button beside Connection address.

Keep the access link private

The pairing URL grants access to this Orca runtime. Treat it like a password and send it only to the client you intend to pair.

2. Add the server on your laptop

On the computer you want to use as the client:

  1. Open Settings → Remote Orca Servers.
  2. Click Add Server.
  3. Enter a recognizable name, such as Remote Server.
  4. Paste the access link from the server.
  5. Click Add Server.
  6. If the saved server shows Disconnected, click Connect.

Adding a server saves it without forcing every new project onto it. Open Advanced → Active Server only when you want server-routed projects, terminals, provider checks, and browser or mobile handoff to use that server by default.

Use the remote server

Once the server shows Connected, select it or one of its projects and use Orca normally. Terminals, agent processes, files, worktrees, and session state live on the server computer.

When several paired clients share that server, the sidebar filter can offer Hide other-client workspaces so this device only lists workspaces it created. See Worktrees → Sidebar layout.

That means:

  • agents keep running when the client laptop sleeps or disconnects;
  • the server needs the repository, tools, and credentials used by those agents;
  • the server must stay awake and connected to the tailnet;
  • reconnecting the client or reopening Orca returns you to the server-owned workspace, tab, and visible-pane state without duplicating paired tabs; paired-server tabs persist across desktop restarts, while tabs you closed stay closed;
  • a paired terminal accepts input again after the host app relaunches, remote browser tabs come back after a network drop, and the phone keeps its terminals after orca serve restarts;
  • creating a workspace on a remote host does not pull other connected desktops to it;
  • projects you delete on the server disappear from every paired client's sidebar (no permanent ghost rows).

Access and security

Orca creates a separate, revocable token for each paired client. The server lists these under Shared Server Access.

  • Click the trash button beside a grant to revoke it. Active clients using that grant are disconnected immediately.
  • Generating another link replaces the previous unused link. Clients that already paired keep their own grants until you revoke them.
  • Keep Tailscale ACLs or grants as narrow as your setup allows.
  • Do not forward the Orca port directly to the public internet. Prefer Tailscale, WireGuard, a trusted LAN, SSH forwarding, or an authenticated tunnel.
  • Do not select 127.0.0.1 for another computer. That address only works on the server itself.

Alternative: orca serve

Use orca serve when the host should run without the desktop window—for example, a headless Linux server or a service-managed VM. For a MacBook or desktop you can leave signed in, the in-app setup above is simpler.

Install Orca and its bundled CLI on the server, then run:

orca serve --pairing-address <server-tailscale-ip-or-hostname>

For example:

orca serve --pairing-address 100.64.1.20

The command:

  • starts the Orca runtime without opening the desktop window;
  • runs in the foreground until you press Ctrl-C;
  • prints the bound endpoint and a runtime pairing URL;
  • uses --pairing-address only for the address clients should dial.

orca serve runs on Orca's own Node server (orcad) by default. If orcad can't serve on that computer, Orca falls back to the desktop app's server and prints one line saying why. A packaged macOS app currently always uses the desktop app's server so paired clients can still update it; set ORCA_SERVE_RUNTIME=electron to force that path.

Paste the printed pairing URL into Settings → Remote Orca Servers → Add Server on the client.

Add --port 6768 when a firewall, tunnel, or service definition requires a fixed port:

orca serve --port 6768 --pairing-address 100.64.1.20

Use only one host mode at a time. If the Orca desktop app is already sharing that computer, do not start a second orca serve process for the same setup.

Mobile from a headless server

For the Orca mobile app, request a mobile-scoped QR code and link:

orca serve --pairing-address 100.64.1.20 --mobile-pairing

Keep the phone on the same tailnet, open Orca Mobile, choose Pair, and scan the terminal QR code or paste the printed link.

orca serve as a service

To keep the server running under a service manager, orca serve supervises itself and can install its own per-user service:

orca serve service install --port 6768
orca serve service status
orca serve service uninstall

install writes a LaunchAgent on macOS or a systemd user unit on Linux that runs orca serve --supervise --no-pairing for this profile. The supervisor restarts the server with growing delays instead of looping on repeated failures, and waits while an update replaces the app. The service never prints a pairing link to its log — pair clients from the desktop app, or run orca serve in a terminal while the service is stopped. On Linux the service stops at logout unless lingering is on (loginctl enable-linger), which install reports. status says whether it is running, and uninstall stops it and removes the unit while leaving the terminal daemon and its sessions running. These service commands are not available on Windows yet.

Use orca serve --supervise directly when another service manager or a container already restarts the process.

Desktop app or orca serve?

Desktop app on the serverorca serve
Best forAn old laptop, Mac mini, or desktopA headless Linux box, VM, or managed service
SetupSettings and buttonsTerminal command and service configuration
Server windowOpenNone
Access linkNew Link → Generate Access LinkPrinted in the terminal
LifetimeWhile the desktop app is runningWhile the foreground process or service is running

Remote Orca Server or SSH?

Use SSH worktrees when Orca on your laptop should own the runtime and use another machine only to run selected worktrees and terminals.

Use a Remote Orca Server when the other machine should own the full Orca runtime and preserve shared sessions for desktop, browser, mobile, or automation clients.

See Ways to run Orca for the full comparison.

Troubleshooting

The Tailscale address is not listed

On the server, confirm Tailscale is connected, then click the refresh button beside Connection address. Both computers must be signed in to the same tailnet. A Tailscale IPv4 address usually starts with 100..

The server is disconnected

Confirm the server computer is awake, Orca is still running, and Tailscale shows both devices online. Check your tailnet ACLs or grants if one device cannot reach the other.

Update Orca on both computers if the server row reports an incompatible protocol version.

The access link was shared with the wrong person

On the server, open Settings → Remote Orca Servers → Shared Server Access and revoke that grant. Generate a new link for the intended client.

The server cannot find an agent CLI

Install and authenticate that CLI on the server computer. Remote sessions use the server's PATH, home directory, and credentials—not the client's.

orca serve advertises the wrong address

Stop the command and restart it with an address the client can reach:

orca serve --pairing-address <reachable-tailscale-ip-or-hostname>

Do not use a wildcard address or 127.0.0.1 for a remote client.

Next steps

  • Compare every run mode in Ways to run Orca.
  • Use SSH worktrees when the laptop should own the Orca runtime.
  • See the Orca CLI reference for orca serve flags and automation commands.

上一章SSH worktrees 下一章Orca CLI overview

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

Orca CLI 概览

Orca CLI overview

Orca CLI 是 orca 命令。从任意 shell 脚本化一个正在运行的 Orca：建工作树、看工作树、驱动 Agent 终端、打开文件和 diff、自动化内置浏览器、跑定时自动化、分享 HTML 或 Markdown 制品，并从脚本或 AI Agent 控制 Orca 自己的工具。

它随桌面应用附带。在 Settings → General → Orca CLI 里登记。即使 shell 启动文件又加了一个更旧的全局安装，Orca 也会把自己的 orca 放在终端 PATH 的前面。脚本和 Agent 调用到的，是和正在运行的应用匹配的那份 CLI。

Agent 可以装上对应的 Orca CLI 技能：

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-cli
# headless / no Settings UI:
orca skills install --skill orca-cli

每一种可安装的 Orca 技能，包括 orca skills install / orca skills update，见 技能与 MCP。

安装并核对

command -v orca
orca status --json

工作树命令

orca worktree ps --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name my-task --issue 123 --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name review-pr-123 --pr 123 --json
orca worktree current --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "reproduced bug" --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --gitlab-issue 42 --gitlab-mr 77 --json
orca worktree rm --worktree id:<id> --force --json

选择器、准备用的 flag、父子工作树，以及更全的命令图，见 Orca CLI 参考。

终端命令

orca terminal list --json
orca terminal read --json
orca terminal send --text "continue" --enter --json
orca terminal wait --for tui-idle --timeout-ms 30000 --json
orca terminal create --worktree path:/projects/app --command "npm test" --json
orca terminal split --direction vertical --command "npm run dev" --json

要跟踪的多 Agent 工作，用 编排，不用普通的终端提示。

文件命令

从 shell 在当前 Orca 工作树里打开文件和 diff：

orca file open src/App.tsx
orca file diff src/App.tsx --staged
orca file open-changed --mode both

shell 的当前目录不在目标工作树里时，用 --worktree <selector>。

浏览器配置文件

浏览器配置文件把标签的会话状态隔开，脚本或 Agent 可以用不同的 cookie、本地存储和登录身份来测。CLI 在 orca tab profile 下提供配置文件命令。从 orca tab profile list --json 开始，再按需要用 create、set、clone 或 use-default。

定时自动化

用 orca automations 从 shell 创建、查看、运行和删除 Orca 的定时任务。想让一段提示反复对着一个仓库或一棵已有工作树跑，从 定时自动化 开始。

制品

通过已登录的 Orca 账号，把 HTML 或 Markdown 分享成公开查看链接（orca artifacts share|update|list|delete）。发布要在 Settings → Artifacts 里另行打开。命令细节见 CLI 参考 → 制品。

浏览器自动化

CLI 也用「快照、交互、再快照」驱动内置浏览器：

orca goto --url https://example.com --json
orca snapshot --json     # returns refs like @e1, @e3
orca click --element @e3 --json
orca fill --element @e1 --value "[email protected]" --json
orca screenshot --json

做响应式检查时，把当前标签切到一个有名字的设备配置：

orca set device --name "iPhone 12" --json
orca screenshot --json

手机模拟器

CLI 也可以通过 Orca 的手机模拟器桥驱动 iOS Simulator。范围是当前工作树。Agent 和脚本可以从 orca emulator list 接上一台模拟器，点归一化坐标，输入文字，发送手势，旋转设备，再关掉它，不用离开 Orca。

orca emulator list --json
orca emulator attach "<device-name-or-udid>" --json
orca emulator tap 0.5 0.7 --json
orca emulator type "hello" --json
orca emulator gesture '[{"type":"begin","x":0.5,"y":0.8},{"type":"move","x":0.5,"y":0.4},{"type":"end","x":0.5,"y":0.2}]' --json
orca emulator rotate landscape_left --json
orca emulator kill --json

脚本需要明确目标时，用 --worktree <selector>、--device <udid-or-name> 或 --emulator <id>。

完整命令面，包括标签、等待、cookie 和 frame，见 Orca CLI 参考。然后装上 Orca CLI 技能（见 技能登记），让你的 Agent 去用它。

Use the Orca CLI to script Orca from a terminal, manage worktrees, control agent terminals, automate the built-in browser, and install agent skills.

The Orca CLI is the orca command-line interface for scripting a running Orca editor from any shell. Use it to create and inspect worktrees, drive agent terminals, open files and diffs, automate the built-in browser, run scheduled automations, share HTML/Markdown artifacts, and control Orca-native tools from scripts or AI agents.

It ships with the desktop app; register it under Settings → General → Orca CLI. Orca keeps its own orca command first on a terminal's PATH even when a shell startup file adds an older global install, so scripts and agents call the CLI that matches the running app.

Agents can install the matching Orca CLI skill with:

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-cli
# headless / no Settings UI:
orca skills install --skill orca-cli

See Skills registry & MCP for every installable Orca skill, including orca skills install / orca skills update.

Install & verify

command -v orca
orca status --json

Worktree commands

orca worktree ps --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name my-task --issue 123 --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name review-pr-123 --pr 123 --json
orca worktree current --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "reproduced bug" --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --gitlab-issue 42 --gitlab-mr 77 --json
orca worktree rm --worktree id:<id> --force --json

For selectors, setup flags, parent/child worktrees, and the broader command map, see Orca CLI reference.

Terminal commands

orca terminal list --json
orca terminal read --json
orca terminal send --text "continue" --enter --json
orca terminal wait --for tui-idle --timeout-ms 30000 --json
orca terminal create --worktree path:/projects/app --command "npm test" --json
orca terminal split --direction vertical --command "npm run dev" --json

For tracked multi-agent work, use Orchestration instead of plain terminal prompts.

File commands

Open files and diffs in the active Orca worktree from a shell:

orca file open src/App.tsx
orca file diff src/App.tsx --staged
orca file open-changed --mode both

Use --worktree <selector> when the shell's current directory is not inside the target worktree.

Browser profiles

Browser profiles isolate tab session state, so a script or agent can test with different cookies, local storage, and logged-in identities. The CLI exposes profile commands under orca tab profile; start with orca tab profile list --json, then use create, set, clone, or use-default as needed.

Scheduled automations

Use orca automations to create, inspect, run, and remove scheduled Orca tasks from a shell. Start with Scheduled automations when you want a recurring prompt to run against a repo or an existing worktree.

Artifacts

Share HTML or Markdown as public view links through the signed-in Orca account (orca artifacts share|update|list|delete). Publishing is opt-in under Settings → Artifacts. Command details: CLI reference → Artifacts.

Browser automation

The CLI also drives the built-in browser with a snapshot-interact-re-snapshot loop:

orca goto --url https://example.com --json
orca snapshot --json     # returns refs like @e1, @e3
orca click --element @e3 --json
orca fill --element @e1 --value "[email protected]" --json
orca screenshot --json

For responsive browser checks, switch the active tab to a named device profile:

orca set device --name "iPhone 12" --json
orca screenshot --json

Mobile emulator

The CLI can also drive an iOS Simulator through Orca's mobile emulator bridge. It is scoped to the active worktree, so agents and scripts can attach a simulator from orca emulator list, tap normalized coordinates, type text, send gestures, rotate the device, and shut it down without leaving Orca.

orca emulator list --json
orca emulator attach "<device-name-or-udid>" --json
orca emulator tap 0.5 0.7 --json
orca emulator type "hello" --json
orca emulator gesture '[{"type":"begin","x":0.5,"y":0.8},{"type":"move","x":0.5,"y":0.4},{"type":"end","x":0.5,"y":0.2}]' --json
orca emulator rotate landscape_left --json
orca emulator kill --json

Use --worktree <selector>, --device <udid-or-name>, or --emulator <id> when a script needs an explicit target.

For the full command surface including tabs, waits, cookies, and frames, see Orca CLI reference, then install the Orca CLI skill (see Skills registry) and point your agent at it.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/overview

上一章Remote Orca Servers 下一章Orca CLI reference

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

Orca CLI 参考

Orca CLI reference

orca CLI 对着一个正在运行的 Orca 运行时说话。shell 脚本或 Agent 要查看工作树、启动终端、打开文件、自动化内置浏览器，或把进度报回 Orca 时，用它。

核对运行时

先在 Settings → Experimental → CLI 里登记 CLI，再确认它够得着 Orca：

command -v orca
orca status --json

Orca 还没在跑时：

orca open --json
orca status --json

别的工具要解析结果时用 --json。给人看的输出用来在终端里快速看一眼。

选择器

多数命令接受选择器，不用很长的 ID：

orca repo show --repo id:<repoId> --json
orca worktree show --worktree active --json
orca worktree show --worktree path:/abs/path/to/worktree --json
orca worktree show --worktree branch:feature-name --json
orca worktree show --worktree issue:123 --json

active 和 current 从 shell 的当前目录或终端上下文，解析到包住它的那棵 Orca 管理的工作树。脚本可能跑在目标工作树外面时，用明确的选择器。远程运行时优先用完整的服务器端选择器，例如 id:<repoId>::<absolute-worktree-path> 或 path:<absolute-server-path>，因为本机 shell 的当前目录在运行时主机上可能不存在。

选一台主机

列出当前 Orca 主机可以指向的每台机器，以及各自的选择器：

orca host list --json

结果包括这台机器、它登记的 SSH 目标，以及已配对的远程 Orca 服务器。这台机器用 --host local，SSH 目标用 --host ssh:<target-id>，已配对的服务器用 --environment <server-name>。SSH 标签和已配对服务器的名字在唯一时也能解析。名字撞上时，用 host list 里的 ID。你把机器名放错了选择器时，Orca 会报告匹配的机器，以及应该改用的 flag，而不是返回空结果。

运行时命令

orca open --json
orca status --json
orca serve --port 6768 --pairing-address 100.64.1.20 --json
orca serve --supervise --json
orca serve service install --port 6768 --dry-run
orca serve service status --json
orca serve service uninstall --json
orca serve restore resume --json

orca serve 在前台启动一份运行时服务器，不打开桌面窗口。用于远程 Orca 服务器或无界面环境。用 Ctrl-C 停掉。

orca serve --supervise 给服务管理器用：服务器停掉时会用越来越长的延迟自己重启，而不是退出；更新替换应用时会等待；反复失败之后会放弃。orca serve service install 写一个按用户的 LaunchAgent（macOS）或 systemd 用户单元（Linux），为这个配置运行 orca serve --supervise --no-pairing。orca serve service status 报告它是否在跑。orca serve service uninstall 停掉并去掉它。orca serve restore resume 清掉自动终端恢复上的暂停，并列出更旧的客户端要求过的恢复。这些在 Windows 上还不可用。服务和监督命令要求这台机器上装了 Orca 应用。见 远程 Orca 服务器 → 把 orca serve 做成服务。

仓库

orca repo list --json
orca repo add --path /abs/path/to/repo --json
orca repo show --repo id:<repoId> --json
orca repo set-base-ref --repo id:<repoId> --ref origin/main --json
orca repo search-refs --repo id:<repoId> --query main --limit 10 --json

建很多工作树之前，先设好仓库的基准引用，新任务默认就从对的地方分出去。

会话搜索

从 shell 搜索已索引的 Agent 记录：

orca search --query "rate limit" --scope conversation --limit 20 --json
orca search --query "migration" --agent codex --since 30d --sort recent --json
orca search --index-status --json

用 --scope conversation|all 选择对话匹配，或全部已索引文本。--fresh 在搜索前刷新索引。--limit、--cursor、--agent、--path、--since 和 --sort 用来收窄或翻页。--index-status 只报告索引状态，不跑查询。那些主机已经授权时，结果可以包括已连接电脑和已配对服务器上的会话。

工作树

orca worktree list --repo id:<repoId> --json
orca worktree ps --json
orca worktree current --json
orca worktree show --worktree active --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name fix-login --json
orca worktree create --name child-task --agent codex --prompt "Investigate the flaky login test" --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "reproduced failure; testing token refresh fix" --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --workspace-status in-review --unread --json
orca worktree rm --worktree id:<worktreeId> --force --json

orca worktree create 加上 --branch <branch>，为新工作树指定 Git 分支，不带配置的前缀。名字冲突时 Orca 可能加一个数字后缀。--name 仍然是工作区的名字。

worktree create 从一棵 Orca 管理的工作树里面跑、并且能推断出关系时，Orca 会把新工作树记成子工作树。要明确就传 --parent-worktree active。新工作和原来无关时用 --no-parent。

worktree set --unread 在侧栏的工作区上放未读点，和 Agent 完成时 Orca 显示的是同一个。--read 清掉它。

Agent 启动用的 flag：

orca worktree create --name review-api --agent claude --setup run --json
orca worktree create --name quick-check --agent codex --prompt "Summarize the diff" --setup skip --json
orca worktree create --name hidden-setup --setup inherit --json

--agent 在第一个终端里启动所选的 Agent。--prompt 把最初的工作直接发给那个 Agent，包括跑在终端里的 Agent。--setup run|skip|inherit 控制仓库准备钩子。inherit 跟随仓库策略。

用工作树上已经存着的工作区元数据，链上议题和评审：

orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name review-task --pr 123 --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name gitlab-task --gitlab-issue 42 --gitlab-mr 77 --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --gitlab-issue '#42' --gitlab-mr '!77' --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --pr null --gitlab-mr null --json

--pr 是 GitHub 的 PR 编号。--gitlab-issue 接受议题编号或 #42。--gitlab-mr 接受 MR 编号或 !77。GitLab 的 flag 也接受 HTTP(S) 的议题或 MR URL，包括自己架设的实例和嵌套的组，只要 URL 的主机和项目对得上工作区已存的 GitLab 来源上下文，或仓库的远程。省略某个 flag，那条链接保持不变。在 set 上，字面量 null 只清掉点名的那条链接（create 拒绝 null）。每个提供方有各自的字段，所以设置 GitLab 链接会保留 GitHub 和 Linear 链接。

在已有工作区里启动 Agent

orca agent launch --agent codex --json
orca agent launch --agent codex --model gpt-6-astra --effort high --prompt "Write the design doc" --json
orca agent launch --agent claude --worktree path:/projects/myapp --focus --json

agent launch 启动 Agent 的方式和新开一个 Agent 标签一样：你的对话界面设置开着、并且 Agent 和主机都支持时，是结构化对话，否则是终端。结果会说明打开了哪种界面（outcome.kind）以及原因。--worktree 默认是包含当前目录的工作树或文件夹工作区。全新检出用 orca worktree create --agent。--model 和 --effort（后者要求有 --model）两种界面都适用。标签打开时不抢走焦点，除非你传了 --focus。启动回复丢了的话，在同一台主机的同一目录里用 --retry-launch <operationId> 重发同一条命令，不要再启动第二个 Agent。

引用

一个工作区可以链上多条评审和议题，包括仓库外面的来源：

orca reference list --worktree name:api --json
orca reference add https://github.com/acme/api/pull/5123 https://linear.app/acme/issue/STA-1234 --worktree name:api --json
orca reference remove https://github.com/acme/api/pull/5123 --worktree name:api --json
orca reference remove --key '<key-from-list>' --worktree name:api --json
orca worktree create --name fix --reference https://github.com/acme/api/pull/5123 --reference https://linear.app/acme/issue/STA-1234 --json
orca reference find STA-1234 --json
  • 写入用完整 URL。remove --key 也能处理 list 里没有 URL 的旧链接。重复添加和删除不存在的项都是空操作，无关的链接会保留。链上议题的 flag（--pr、--gitlab-issue、--linear-issue）和 --reference 不能出现在同一条命令里。
  • 查找接受完整 URL，或原生议题键，例如 STA-1234。光秃秃的数字会被拒绝。它只读已存的元数据（不向提供方发请求，也不搜记录），返回匹配的工作区，以及和它们关联的 Agent，带每个 Agent 的 linked 状态，以及终端或邮箱地址。搜对话文本用 orca search。

然后按明确地址给找到的 Agent 发消息：

orca terminal send --terminal '<terminal-from-find>' --text 'Status?' --enter
orca orchestration send --to '<mailbox-from-find>' --subject 'Status?' --type question

终端

orca terminal list --worktree active --json
orca terminal show --terminal <handle> --json
orca terminal read --terminal <handle> --json
orca terminal read --terminal <handle> --screen --json
orca terminal read --terminal <handle> --cursor <cursor> --limit 1000 --json
orca terminal send --terminal <handle> --text "continue" --enter --json
orca terminal wait --terminal <handle> --for tui-idle --timeout-ms 300000 --json
orca terminal create --worktree active --title "tests" --command "npm test" --json
orca terminal split --terminal <handle> --direction horizontal --command "npm run dev" --json
orca terminal rename --terminal <handle> --title "runner" --json
orca terminal switch --terminal <handle> --json
orca terminal close --terminal <handle> --json

省略 --terminal 时，目标是当前工作树里的活动终端。不确定终端在等什么时，先读再发送。

终端句柄

终端句柄的范围是这次运行时。Orca 重启了，或命令报告句柄已过期时，跑 orca terminal list --json，重新拿到句柄。

terminal list 在 Orca 能核实的时候，报告每个终端的 executionHostId，并在结果层面带一个 hostScope，里面是覆盖到的和省略的主机 ID。缺少主机身份或范围时，当成无法核实，不要当成本机。只有当它的执行主机列在 hostScope.hostIds 里时，缺少某个终端才说明它已经退出。

默认情况下，terminal read 返回累积的输出流，终端转义已去掉。会重绘行的程序因此可能看起来像叠在一起的碎片。需要当前渲染出的画面时用 --screen。响应的 source 标明是 stream、screen 还是 screen-unavailable。画面读取没有历史可翻页，所以 --screen 和 --cursor 互斥。

输出很长时用游标读取。从一次流读取里保存 nextCursor，再用 --cursor 传回去，只取新输出。

文件

orca file open src/App.tsx --worktree active --json
orca file diff src/App.tsx --staged --worktree active --json
orca file open-changed --mode both --worktree active --json

路径相对于所选工作树。open-changed 读 git 状态，按编辑、diff 或两种模式打开改过的文件。

这些命令把文件开成标签，不改变你的视图，即使那棵工作树就在屏幕上。传 --focus 才把打开的标签带到眼前。只有用户要求看这个文件时，Agent 才应该传它。

内置浏览器

浏览器命令控制所选工作树的 Orca 内置浏览器标签。它们不控制 Chrome、Safari，或 Orca 的桌面界面。对话 Agent 没有指定目标的命令，在 Orca 能解析时，用的是它的对话工作区，即使 Agent 跑在那个文件夹外面。工作区解析不了时，传 --worktree，或从打算用的工作区文件夹里运行。对已有页面做动作时，也可以用 --page，ID 来自 orca tab list --json。

用快照 → 操作 → 快照这一圈：

orca goto --url http://localhost:3000 --worktree active --json
orca snapshot --worktree active --json
orca click --element @e3 --worktree active --json
orca fill --element @e1 --value "[email protected]" --worktree active --json
orca wait --text "Welcome" --worktree active --json
orca screenshot --worktree active --json

@e3 这类引用来自 snapshot。导航、切换标签、会改变页面的点击，以及任何引用过期的错误之后，重新取快照。对受支持的页面动作，加上 --expect-url <absolute-url>，页面已经换到另一个 URL 时就拒绝这个动作。比较包含路径、查询、片段和末尾斜杠。这是 URL 前提，不是锁，也不是页面身份的证明。它不检查 iframe 内容。不能强制执行它的旧主机，会拒绝这个 flag。

标签和捕获命令：

orca tab list --worktree active --json
orca tab create --url http://localhost:3000 --worktree active --json
orca tab switch --index 1 --worktree active --json
orca capture start --worktree active --json
orca console --limit 50 --worktree active --json
orca network --limit 50 --worktree active --json
orca full-screenshot --worktree active --json
orca pdf --worktree active --json

只有还没有对应类型化 Orca 命令的浏览器动作，才用 orca exec --command "<agent-browser command>" --json。

浏览器设备仿真：

orca set device --name "iPhone 12" --worktree active --json
orca screenshot --worktree active --json

桌面本机操作

内置浏览器以外的本机桌面应用，用 orca computer：

orca computer permissions --json
orca computer list-apps --json
orca computer get-app-state --app com.apple.Safari --json
orca computer click --app com.apple.Safari --element-index 12 --json
orca computer paste-text --app com.apple.Safari --text "hello" --json

完整流程和权限设置见 本机操作。

手机模拟器

手机模拟器命令通过 Orca 按工作树划分的桥，控制 iOS Simulator 设备。Agent 在 Orca 里面操作时用它们，不用原始的 serve-sim 或 simctl，这样生命周期和当前设备状态仍然挂在当前工作树上。

orca emulator list --worktree active --json
orca emulator attach "<device-name-or-udid>" --worktree active --json
orca emulator tap 0.5 0.7 --worktree active --json
orca emulator type "hello" --worktree active --json
orca emulator gesture '[{"type":"begin","x":0.5,"y":0.8},{"type":"move","x":0.5,"y":0.4},{"type":"end","x":0.5,"y":0.2}]' --worktree active --json
orca emulator button home --worktree active --json
orca emulator rotate landscape_left --worktree active --json
orca emulator exec --command "tap 0.5 0.7" --worktree active --json
orca emulator kill --worktree active --json
orca emulator shutdown --worktree active --json

坐标从 0 归一化到 1。单次点按优先用 tap。拖拽或多步触摸用 gesture。脚本必须指向某一台模拟器，而不是工作树的当前模拟器时，传 --device <udid-or-name> 或 --emulator <id>。

Linear

orca linear 是 Agent 通过 orca-linear 技能使用的那一层（旧的安装名 linear-tickets 仍然可用）。优先用 --json。已链接的工作树用 --current 解析。

读取

orca linear issue --current --full --json
orca linear issue ENG-123 --comments --children --relations --activity --json
orca linear search "auth bug" --workspace all --json
orca linear list --filter assigned --limit 10 --json
orca linear list-issues --team ENG --state started --assignee me --json
orca linear list-issues --query auth --updated-at -P7D --cursor <cursor> --workspace <id> --json
orca linear team list --json
orca linear team states --team ENG --json
orca linear team labels --team ENG --json
orca linear project list --query launch --json

--full 展开评论、子项、附件、关联和活动。分节 flag（--comments、--children、--attachments、--relations、--activity）可以单独用。

按 MCP 方式写入

# Create or update (omit id/--current to create; requires --team and --title on create)
orca linear save-issue --team ENG --title "Fix auth" --priority high --json
orca linear save-issue ENG-123 --state "In Progress" --assignee me --json
orca linear save-issue --current --project null --due-date null --json

orca linear relation add ENG-1 --related ENG-2 --type blocks --json
orca linear relation remove ENG-1 --related ENG-2 --type related --json

save-issue 的标签会替换整组标签（Linear MCP 的 save_issue 语义）。字面量 null 清掉负责人、估算、截止日期、项目或父项。

字段辅助命令（仍然可用）

orca linear status set --current --to "In Progress" --json
orca linear assignee set --current --me --json
orca linear priority set ENG-123 --to high --json
orca linear estimate set --current --to 3 --json
orca linear due-date set --current --to 2026-08-01 --json
orca linear label add --current --label backend --json
orca linear comment add --current --body "Investigating regression" --json
orca linear comment edit <commentId> --body "Investigating the regression" --json
orca linear comment delete <commentId> --json
orca linear attach --current --url https://example.com/repro --title "Repro" --json
orca linear create --title "Flaky login test" --team ENG --priority high --json

comment edit 和 comment delete 针对确切的 Linear 评论 ID。comment rm 是删除的别名。替换文本来自文件或标准输入时，编辑用 --body-file <path|->。

跑 orca linear --help 或 orca skills get orca-linear，取和版本匹配的列表。脚本可能跑在没有链到 Orca 的工作树外面时，传一个明确的议题 id（例如 ENG-123）。

技能（本机，不需要运行时）

没有桌面 Settings 界面时，列出随应用附带的说明、打印和版本匹配的说明，或安装、更新混合技能包：

orca skills list
orca skills get orca-cli
orca skills get orchestration --full
orca skills install --skill orca-cli --skill orchestration
orca skills install --all --dry-run
orca skills update --all

install / update 会去调用 Settings 用的同一批 npx skills 命令。它们不联系 Orca 运行时。见 Orca 技能。

账号（主机本地的运行时）

在跑着 Orca 的无界面主机上（orca serve 或桌面应用），远程客户端用不了 Add account（添加账号）时（远程运行时范围会禁用那个按钮），在这里添加托管的 Claude / Codex 账号：

orca account list
orca account add                 # Claude by default
orca account add --agent codex

account add 在主机上的这个终端里跑 claude login / codex login，再把拿到的凭据登记到本地运行时。Codex 用设备授权，所以浏览器可以在另一台机器上完成。在拥有这些账号的那台机器上跑，不要通过只有客户端的远程会话来跑。

制品

通过已登录的 Orca 账号发布 HTML 或 Markdown。看公开链接不需要登录。创建、列出、更新、删除需要。发布默认是关的。必须有人在这台设备上启用 Settings → Artifacts → Allow publishing public artifact links（允许发布公开制品链接）。没有 CLI flag 能打开这道门。list、unshare 和 delete 仍然可用，这样关掉发布之后你还可以盘点或撤销链接。

orca artifacts share ./report.html --json
orca artifacts share ./notes.md --json
orca artifacts update ./notes.md --json
orca artifacts unshare ./notes.md --json
orca artifacts list --json
orca artifacts list --cursor <cursor> --json
orca artifacts delete <id> --json
  • 接受的文件：.html、.htm、.md、.markdown。
  • 共享制品内容每个文件限 10 MiB。
  • share 把编辑令牌存在当前 Orca 配置里，不打印出来。update / unshare 按当初分享这个文件的同一条本地路径和同一份配置来解析。
  • list 分页（nextCursor → --cursor）。delete 用 list 里的制品 id，不需要原来的文件。
  • 相对的 HTML 资源不会上传。分享自包含的 HTML，或绝对的资源 URL。
  • 发布或更新被拒绝时，失败码是 artifact_sharing_disabled。去改 Settings，不要重试。
  • 桌面：打开一个本地 HTML 或 Markdown 文件，用 Share as artifact（分享为制品），或从侧栏的 Artifacts（制品）页管理链接。

自动化、环境和钩子

定时提示：

orca automations list --json
orca automations create --name "Daily review" --trigger daily --time 09:00 --prompt "Review open changes" --provider claude --repo id:<repoId> --extra-agent-args="--model opus" --disabled --json
orca automations run <automationId> --json

--extra-agent-args 为一条自动化的全新运行设置模型或 effort，不改主机的默认值。见 定时自动化。

托管的 SSH 服务器：

orca environment list --json
orca environment status --environment <selector> --json
orca environment update --environment <selector> --json
orca environment rollback --environment <selector> --json
orca environment recover --environment <selector> --json
orca environment stop --environment <selector> --yes --json
orca environment forget --environment <selector> --yes --json

--environment 点名一台这台机器通过 SSH 部署的托管运行时（和 Settings → Managed servers 是同一批动作）。stop 停用一台服务器。forget 解除一台主机已经不在的服务器的关联。见 SSH 工作树 → 托管运行时。

远程运行时环境：

orca environment add --name work-laptop --pairing-code "orca://pair?code=..." --json
orca environment list --json
orca environment rm --environment <selector> --json

Agent 状态钩子：

orca agent hooks status --json
orca agent hooks on --json
orca agent hooks off --json

Agent 使用习惯

  • 自动化和 Agent 调用优先用 --json。
  • 优先用选择器，不要去解析界面标签。
  • 下一步输入不明显时，发送之前先读终端状态。
  • 用工作树备注做进度检查点。见 工作树检查点。
  • 要跟踪的多 Agent Dispatch 用 编排，不用临时的终端提示。

工作区清理

用 orca workspace cleanup preview 查看当前规则和原因，用 orca workspace cleanup run 应用它们，用 orca workspace cleanup report 看过去的活动和下一轮。加 --json 得到结构化输出。清理规则要另行打开。启用之前见 自动工作区清理。

Commands, selectors, and agent-friendly patterns for driving Orca from a shell.

The orca CLI talks to a running Orca runtime. Use it when a shell script or agent needs to inspect worktrees, launch terminals, open files, automate the built-in browser, or report progress back into Orca.

Verify the runtime

Register the CLI under Settings -> Experimental -> CLI, then check that it can reach Orca:

command -v orca
orca status --json

If Orca is not already running:

orca open --json
orca status --json

Use --json when another tool will parse the result. Human-readable output is for quick terminal checks.

Selectors

Most commands accept selectors instead of requiring long IDs:

orca repo show --repo id:<repoId> --json
orca worktree show --worktree active --json
orca worktree show --worktree path:/abs/path/to/worktree --json
orca worktree show --worktree branch:feature-name --json
orca worktree show --worktree issue:123 --json

active and current resolve to the enclosing Orca-managed worktree from the shell's current directory or terminal context. Use explicit selectors in scripts that may run outside the target worktree. For remote runtimes, prefer full server-side selectors such as id:<repoId>::<absolute-worktree-path> or path:<absolute-server-path> because the local shell's current directory may not exist on the runtime host.

Choose a host

List every machine the current Orca host can target and the selector for each one:

orca host list --json

The result includes this machine, its registered SSH targets, and paired Remote Orca Servers. Use --host local for this machine, --host ssh:<target-id> for an SSH target, and --environment <server-name> for a paired server. SSH labels and paired-server names also resolve when they are unique; use the IDs from host list when names collide. If you put a machine name on the wrong selector, Orca reports the matching machine and the flag to use instead of returning an empty result.

Runtime commands

orca open --json
orca status --json
orca serve --port 6768 --pairing-address 100.64.1.20 --json
orca serve --supervise --json
orca serve service install --port 6768 --dry-run
orca serve service status --json
orca serve service uninstall --json
orca serve restore resume --json

orca serve starts a runtime server in the foreground without opening the desktop window. Use it for Remote Orca Servers or headless environments, and stop it with Ctrl-C.

orca serve --supervise is for service managers: the server restarts itself with growing delays instead of exiting when it stops, waits while an update replaces the app, and gives up after repeated failures. orca serve service install writes a per-user LaunchAgent (macOS) or systemd user unit (Linux) that runs orca serve --supervise --no-pairing for this profile; orca serve service status reports whether it is running, and orca serve service uninstall stops and removes it. orca serve restore resume clears a pause on automatic terminal restores and lists restores an older client asked for. None of these are available on Windows yet, and the service and supervisor commands need the Orca app installed on the machine. See Remote Orca Servers → orca serve as a service.

Repos

orca repo list --json
orca repo add --path /abs/path/to/repo --json
orca repo show --repo id:<repoId> --json
orca repo set-base-ref --repo id:<repoId> --ref origin/main --json
orca repo search-refs --repo id:<repoId> --query main --limit 10 --json

Set the repo base ref before creating lots of worktrees so new tasks branch from the right place by default.

Session search

Search indexed agent transcripts from the shell:

orca search --query "rate limit" --scope conversation --limit 20 --json
orca search --query "migration" --agent codex --since 30d --sort recent --json
orca search --index-status --json

Use --scope conversation|all to choose conversation matches or all indexed text. --fresh refreshes the index before searching; --limit, --cursor, --agent, --path, --since, and --sort narrow or page the results. --index-status reports indexing state without running a query. Results can include sessions from connected computers and paired servers when those hosts have granted access.

Worktrees

orca worktree list --repo id:<repoId> --json
orca worktree ps --json
orca worktree current --json
orca worktree show --worktree active --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name fix-login --json
orca worktree create --name child-task --agent codex --prompt "Investigate the flaky login test" --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "reproduced failure; testing token refresh fix" --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --workspace-status in-review --unread --json
orca worktree rm --worktree id:<worktreeId> --force --json

Use --branch <branch> with orca worktree create to specify the Git branch for the new worktree without the configured prefix. Orca may add a numeric suffix if the name conflicts; --name still names the workspace.

When worktree create runs from inside an Orca-managed worktree, Orca records the new worktree as a child when it can infer the relationship. Pass --parent-worktree active to be explicit, or --no-parent when the new work is independent.

worktree set --unread puts the unread dot on the workspace in the sidebar, the same one Orca shows when an agent finishes; --read clears it.

Agent startup flags:

orca worktree create --name review-api --agent claude --setup run --json
orca worktree create --name quick-check --agent codex --prompt "Summarize the diff" --setup skip --json
orca worktree create --name hidden-setup --setup inherit --json

--agent launches the selected agent in the first terminal. --prompt sends initial work directly to that agent, including agents running in a terminal. --setup run|skip|inherit controls repo setup hooks; inherit follows the repo policy.

Link issues and reviews using the workspace metadata already stored on the worktree:

orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name review-task --pr 123 --json
orca worktree create --repo id:<repoId> --name gitlab-task --gitlab-issue 42 --gitlab-mr 77 --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --gitlab-issue '#42' --gitlab-mr '!77' --json
orca worktree set --worktree active --pr null --gitlab-mr null --json

--pr is a GitHub pull request number. --gitlab-issue accepts an issue number or #42; --gitlab-mr accepts a merge request number or !77. The GitLab flags also accept HTTP(S) issue or merge request URLs — including self-hosted instances and nested groups — as long as the URL's host and project match the workspace's stored GitLab source context or the repository's remote. Omitting a flag leaves its link unchanged; on set, literal null clears only the named link (create refuses null). Each provider keeps separate fields, so setting a GitLab link preserves GitHub and Linear links.

Launch an agent in an existing workspace

orca agent launch --agent codex --json
orca agent launch --agent codex --model gpt-6-astra --effort high --prompt "Write the design doc" --json
orca agent launch --agent claude --worktree path:/projects/myapp --focus --json

agent launch starts an agent the same way a new agent tab does: as a structured chat when your Chat UI setting is on and the agent and host support it, otherwise as a terminal. The result says which surface opened (outcome.kind) and why. --worktree defaults to the worktree or folder workspace containing the current directory; for a brand-new checkout use orca worktree create --agent. --model and --effort (which requires --model) apply to either surface, and the tab opens without stealing focus unless you pass --focus. If the launch reply is lost, re-issue the same command from the same directory on the same host with --retry-launch <operationId> rather than starting a second agent.

References

A workspace can link several reviews and issues, including sources outside its repository:

orca reference list --worktree name:api --json
orca reference add https://github.com/acme/api/pull/5123 https://linear.app/acme/issue/STA-1234 --worktree name:api --json
orca reference remove https://github.com/acme/api/pull/5123 --worktree name:api --json
orca reference remove --key '<key-from-list>' --worktree name:api --json
orca worktree create --name fix --reference https://github.com/acme/api/pull/5123 --reference https://linear.app/acme/issue/STA-1234 --json
orca reference find STA-1234 --json
  • Writes take full URLs; remove --key also handles older links from list that have no URL. Duplicate adds and absent removals are no-ops, and unrelated links are preserved. The linked-issue flags (--pr, --gitlab-issue, --linear-issue) and --reference cannot be combined on one command.
  • Find takes a full URL or a native issue key such as STA-1234 — bare numbers are rejected. It reads stored metadata only (no provider requests or transcript search) and returns matching workspaces plus the agents associated with them, with each agent's linked state and any terminal or mailbox address. Use orca search to search conversation text.

Then message the agent you found by its explicit address:

orca terminal send --terminal '<terminal-from-find>' --text 'Status?' --enter
orca orchestration send --to '<mailbox-from-find>' --subject 'Status?' --type question

Terminals

orca terminal list --worktree active --json
orca terminal show --terminal <handle> --json
orca terminal read --terminal <handle> --json
orca terminal read --terminal <handle> --screen --json
orca terminal read --terminal <handle> --cursor <cursor> --limit 1000 --json
orca terminal send --terminal <handle> --text "continue" --enter --json
orca terminal wait --terminal <handle> --for tui-idle --timeout-ms 300000 --json
orca terminal create --worktree active --title "tests" --command "npm test" --json
orca terminal split --terminal <handle> --direction horizontal --command "npm run dev" --json
orca terminal rename --terminal <handle> --title "runner" --json
orca terminal switch --terminal <handle> --json
orca terminal close --terminal <handle> --json

Omit --terminal to target the active terminal in the current worktree. Read before sending when you are not sure what the terminal is waiting for.

Terminal handles

Terminal handles are runtime-scoped. If Orca restarts or a command reports a stale terminal handle, run orca terminal list --json and reacquire the handle.

terminal list reports each terminal's executionHostId when Orca can verify it, plus a result-level hostScope with covered and omitted host IDs. Treat a missing host identity or scope as unverifiable, not local. A missing terminal is evidence that it exited only when its execution host is listed in hostScope.hostIds.

By default, terminal read returns the accumulated output stream with terminal escapes stripped. Programs that redraw lines can therefore appear as stacked fragments. Use --screen when you need the currently rendered frame; the response's source identifies stream, screen, or screen-unavailable. Screen reads have no history to page, so --screen and --cursor are mutually exclusive.

For long output, use cursor reads. Save nextCursor from one stream read, then pass it back with --cursor to fetch only new output.

Files

orca file open src/App.tsx --worktree active --json
orca file diff src/App.tsx --staged --worktree active --json
orca file open-changed --mode both --worktree active --json

Paths are relative to the selected worktree. open-changed reads git status and opens changed files in edit, diff, or both modes.

These commands open files as tabs without changing your view, even in the worktree on screen. Pass --focus to bring the opened tab into view — agents should pass it only when the user asked to see the file.

Built-in browser

Browser commands control Orca's embedded browser tab for the selected worktree. They do not control Chrome, Safari, or the Orca desktop UI. A chat agent's untargeted commands use its chat workspace when Orca can resolve it, even if the agent runs outside that folder. If the workspace cannot be resolved, pass --worktree or run from the intended workspace folder; commands acting on an existing page can instead use --page with an ID from orca tab list --json.

Use a snapshot -> act -> snapshot loop:

orca goto --url http://localhost:3000 --worktree active --json
orca snapshot --worktree active --json
orca click --element @e3 --worktree active --json
orca fill --element @e1 --value "[email protected]" --worktree active --json
orca wait --text "Welcome" --worktree active --json
orca screenshot --worktree active --json

Refs such as @e3 come from snapshot. Re-snapshot after navigation, tab switches, clicks that change the page, and any stale-ref error. For supported page actions, add --expect-url <absolute-url> to refuse the action if the page has moved to another URL. The comparison includes the path, query, fragment, and trailing slash. This is a URL precondition, not a lock or proof of page identity; it does not check iframe content. Older hosts that cannot enforce it refuse the flag.

Tab and capture commands:

orca tab list --worktree active --json
orca tab create --url http://localhost:3000 --worktree active --json
orca tab switch --index 1 --worktree active --json
orca capture start --worktree active --json
orca console --limit 50 --worktree active --json
orca network --limit 50 --worktree active --json
orca full-screenshot --worktree active --json
orca pdf --worktree active --json

Use orca exec --command "<agent-browser command>" --json only for browser actions that do not have a typed Orca command yet.

Browser device emulation:

orca set device --name "iPhone 12" --worktree active --json
orca screenshot --worktree active --json

Desktop computer use

Use orca computer for native desktop apps outside the built-in browser:

orca computer permissions --json
orca computer list-apps --json
orca computer get-app-state --app com.apple.Safari --json
orca computer click --app com.apple.Safari --element-index 12 --json
orca computer paste-text --app com.apple.Safari --text "hello" --json

See Computer use for the full workflow and permission setup.

Mobile emulator

The mobile emulator commands control iOS Simulator devices through Orca's worktree-scoped bridge. Use them instead of raw serve-sim or simctl when an agent is operating from inside Orca, so lifecycle and active-device state stay attached to the current worktree.

orca emulator list --worktree active --json
orca emulator attach "<device-name-or-udid>" --worktree active --json
orca emulator tap 0.5 0.7 --worktree active --json
orca emulator type "hello" --worktree active --json
orca emulator gesture '[{"type":"begin","x":0.5,"y":0.8},{"type":"move","x":0.5,"y":0.4},{"type":"end","x":0.5,"y":0.2}]' --worktree active --json
orca emulator button home --worktree active --json
orca emulator rotate landscape_left --worktree active --json
orca emulator exec --command "tap 0.5 0.7" --worktree active --json
orca emulator kill --worktree active --json
orca emulator shutdown --worktree active --json

Coordinates are normalized from 0 to 1. Prefer tap for single taps, and use gesture for drags or multi-step touch input. Pass --device <udid-or-name> or --emulator <id> when a script must target a specific simulator instead of the worktree's active emulator.

Linear

The orca linear surface is what agents use via the orca-linear skill (legacy install name linear-tickets still works). Prefer --json. Linked worktrees resolve with --current.

Read

orca linear issue --current --full --json
orca linear issue ENG-123 --comments --children --relations --activity --json
orca linear search "auth bug" --workspace all --json
orca linear list --filter assigned --limit 10 --json
orca linear list-issues --team ENG --state started --assignee me --json
orca linear list-issues --query auth --updated-at -P7D --cursor <cursor> --workspace <id> --json
orca linear team list --json
orca linear team states --team ENG --json
orca linear team labels --team ENG --json
orca linear project list --query launch --json

--full expands comments, children, attachments, relations, and activity. Section flags (--comments, --children, --attachments, --relations, --activity) work individually.

MCP-style write

# Create or update (omit id/--current to create; requires --team and --title on create)
orca linear save-issue --team ENG --title "Fix auth" --priority high --json
orca linear save-issue ENG-123 --state "In Progress" --assignee me --json
orca linear save-issue --current --project null --due-date null --json

orca linear relation add ENG-1 --related ENG-2 --type blocks --json
orca linear relation remove ENG-1 --related ENG-2 --type related --json

save-issue labels replace the full label set (Linear MCP save_issue semantics). Literal null clears assignee, estimate, due date, project, or parent.

Field helpers (still valid)

orca linear status set --current --to "In Progress" --json
orca linear assignee set --current --me --json
orca linear priority set ENG-123 --to high --json
orca linear estimate set --current --to 3 --json
orca linear due-date set --current --to 2026-08-01 --json
orca linear label add --current --label backend --json
orca linear comment add --current --body "Investigating regression" --json
orca linear comment edit <commentId> --body "Investigating the regression" --json
orca linear comment delete <commentId> --json
orca linear attach --current --url https://example.com/repro --title "Repro" --json
orca linear create --title "Flaky login test" --team ENG --priority high --json

comment edit and comment delete target the exact Linear comment ID; comment rm is an alias for delete. Use --body-file <path|-> with edit when the replacement text comes from a file or standard input.

Run orca linear --help or orca skills get orca-linear for the version-matched list. Pass an explicit issue id (e.g. ENG-123) when a script may run outside an Orca-linked worktree.

Skills (local, no runtime required)

List bundled guides, print a version-matched guide, or install/update hybrid skill packages without the desktop Settings UI:

orca skills list
orca skills get orca-cli
orca skills get orchestration --full
orca skills install --skill orca-cli --skill orchestration
orca skills install --all --dry-run
orca skills update --all

install / update shell out to the same npx skills commands Settings uses. They do not contact the Orca runtime. See Orca skills.

Account (host-local runtime)

On a headless host running Orca (orca serve or the desktop app), add managed Claude/Codex accounts when the remote client cannot use Add account (remote runtime scope disables that button):

orca account list
orca account add                 # Claude by default
orca account add --agent codex

account add runs claude login / codex login in this terminal on the host, then registers the captured credentials with the local runtime. Codex uses device authorization so the browser can finish on another machine. Run these on the machine that owns the accounts — not through a client-only remote session.

Artifacts

Publish HTML or Markdown through the signed-in Orca account. Viewing a public link does not require sign-in; create/list/update/delete do. Publishing is off by default — a human must enable Settings → Artifacts → Allow publishing public artifact links on the device. There is no CLI flag that grants the gate. list, unshare, and delete stay available so you can audit or revoke links after turning publishing off.

orca artifacts share ./report.html --json
orca artifacts share ./notes.md --json
orca artifacts update ./notes.md --json
orca artifacts unshare ./notes.md --json
orca artifacts list --json
orca artifacts list --cursor <cursor> --json
orca artifacts delete <id> --json
  • Accepted files: .html, .htm, .md, .markdown.
  • Shared artifact content is limited to 10 MiB per file.
  • share stores the edit token in the active Orca profile and does not print it. update / unshare resolve by the same local path and profile that originally shared the file.
  • list is paged (nextCursor → --cursor). delete takes the artifact id from list and does not need the original file.
  • Relative HTML assets are not uploaded — share self-contained HTML or absolute asset URLs.
  • Denied publish/update fails with artifact_sharing_disabled; fix Settings instead of retrying.
  • Desktop: open a local HTML or Markdown file and use Share as artifact, or manage links from the sidebar Artifacts page.

Automations, environments, and hooks

Scheduled prompts:

orca automations list --json
orca automations create --name "Daily review" --trigger daily --time 09:00 --prompt "Review open changes" --provider claude --repo id:<repoId> --extra-agent-args="--model opus" --disabled --json
orca automations run <automationId> --json

--extra-agent-args sets a model or effort for one automation's fresh runs without changing the host's defaults; see Scheduled automations.

Managed SSH servers:

orca environment list --json
orca environment status --environment <selector> --json
orca environment update --environment <selector> --json
orca environment rollback --environment <selector> --json
orca environment recover --environment <selector> --json
orca environment stop --environment <selector> --yes --json
orca environment forget --environment <selector> --yes --json

--environment names a managed Orca server this machine deployed over SSH (the same actions as Settings → Managed servers); stop decommissions a server and forget unlinks one whose host is gone. See SSH worktrees → Managed Orca server.

Remote runtime environments:

orca environment add --name work-laptop --pairing-code "orca://pair?code=..." --json
orca environment list --json
orca environment rm --environment <selector> --json

Agent status hooks:

orca agent hooks status --json
orca agent hooks on --json
orca agent hooks off --json

Agent habits

  • Prefer --json for automation and agent calls.
  • Prefer selectors over parsing UI labels.
  • Read terminal state before sending input unless the next input is obvious.
  • Use worktree comments for progress checkpoints. See Worktree checkpoints.
  • Use Orchestration for tracked multi-agent dispatches instead of ad hoc terminal prompts.

Workspace cleanup

Use orca workspace cleanup preview to inspect the current rules and their reasons, orca workspace cleanup run to apply them, and orca workspace cleanup report to see past activity and the next run. Add --json for structured output. Cleanup rules are opt-in; see Automatic workspace cleanup before enabling them.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/reference

上一章Orca CLI overview 下一章Orchestration

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

编排

Orchestration

编排是 Orca 里结构化的多 Agent 层：一个 Run（命名空间加协调者的收件箱）、任务、Dispatch、受监督的 Worker、消息，以及决策门。

需要归属、完成跟踪或一张 DAG 时用它。一次性提示用 orca terminal send。要完整交出归属、又不要监督时，用 orca-cli 技能里的工作树和终端命令。

实验功能

用这些命令之前，先在 Settings → Experimental 里启用编排。实验性的 Orchestration 面板和它的开关暂时不可用。CLI 对着正在运行的 Orca 运行时说话，所以要先让 orca status --json 成功。

旧命令已停用

orca orchestration run 和 run-stop（以及 coordinator-start / coordinator-stop）不做任何事。它们返回一段恢复文字，指向 orca skills get orchestration --full。用下面的 Run 加 worker-start 流程。

Worker 可以再启动帮手 Worker，深度到 Settings 里的 Nested worker depth（嵌套 Worker 深度）为止。默认是 4。以前设成 1 的配置会一次性改到 4，之后的选择会保留。停掉一个 Worker，也会停掉它的帮手。

核心模型

  • Run：持久的命名空间和主收件箱。它从不排程，也不放置 Worker。
  • 任务：一件带规格、依赖和状态的工作。状态是 pending、ready、dispatched、completed、failed 或 blocked。
  • Dispatch：一次把任务放到某个终端上的尝试。worker_done 和心跳的生命周期以它为准。
  • 消息：收件箱里的邮件（status、dispatch、worker_done、escalation、question、heartbeat 等）。
  • 决策门：协调者拥有的一个问题，解决之前挡住任务。

完成以谁为准，来自当前的 Dispatch 上下文。Worker 的完成和心跳消息都应同时带上 taskId 和 dispatchId。
