终端里打印的任务 ID，例如 task_...，是可点的链接。点一下会向 Orca 运行时要这个任务当前的 Dispatch，并聚焦到被指派的终端。任务在远程或 SSH 运行时里时也一样。

推荐的受监督循环

orca orchestration run-create --objective "Split checkout QA and summarize blockers" --json
orca orchestration task-create --spec "Audit billing settings for mobile layout" --task-title "Billing audit" --json
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree current --agent codex --json
# or new worktree:
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree new-child --name billing-audit --agent codex --setup run --json
# optional per-worker model / effort (Claude, Codex, Muse, Cursor, Antigravity, OpenCode; not with --terminal):
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree current --agent claude --model <opaque-model-id> --effort high --json
# Run Antigravity as a supervised worker in a local child worktree:
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree new-child --name research --agent antigravity --setup run --json

--model 接受不透明的提供方模型 ID，适用于 Claude、Codex、Muse、Cursor、Antigravity 和 OpenCode。Muse 传你的 Muse 安装接受的模型 id（例如 muse-spark-1.3）。Antigravity 用 agy models 报出的模型 slug。OpenCode Worker 请求的模型在执行主机上校验，并且只留给那个 Worker，所以不会改你其他启动的默认模型。--effort 要求同时有 --model，而且只在那个 Agent 或模型支持这个档位时生效。两个 flag 都不能和 --terminal（复用已有窗格）一起用。比 Orca 内置列表更新的 Codex 模型（例如 gpt-6.x），只要已安装的 Codex 支持，--effort 就接受已知的全部用词，包括 max 和 ultra。不认识的 effort 名字仍会被拒绝。覆盖只对那一次启动生效，并出现在启动回执的 launch.requested / launch.effective 里。跨主机启动需要一台宣告了启动偏好支持的 Worker 主机。

新工作树可以省略 --name，让 Orca 生成一个。

在 Settings → Agent Orchestration 里，可以关掉 Worker 心跳，或把间隔选成 1 到 60 分钟。默认 5 分钟。改动对新派出的 Worker 生效。已有的 Worker 保持原来的任务说明。更旧的主机保持 5 分钟默认。

对话界面 开着时，worker-start 启动受支持 Agent 的方式和新开一个 Agent 标签一样：Agent 和主机都支持时是结构化对话，否则是终端。--terminal 仍然复用已有窗格。

Orca 在启动本机 Antigravity 子工作树的 Worker 之前，会先为它准备信任。这套信任准备不支持通过 SSH 监督 Antigravity。它作为普通 Agent 的功能见 受支持的 Agent。

等完成（处理一次 Delivery 里的每条消息，再 ack）：

orca orchestration check --wait --types worker_done,escalation,question --timeout-ms 900000 --json
orca orchestration check --ack <deliveryId> --wait --types worker_done,escalation,question --timeout-ms 900000 --json

Worker 完成（从 Worker 窗格发出，带上注入的 ID）：

orca orchestration send \
  --type worker_done \
  --subject "Completed mobile audit" \
  --body "Fixed footer overlap; no follow-ups." \
  --task-id <taskId> \
  --dispatch-id <dispatchId> \
  --outcome succeeded \
  --files-modified "src/app/settings/Billing.tsx" \
  --json

worker_done 要求 --outcome succeeded|failed。

查看和恢复：

orca orchestration worker-show --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
orca orchestration worker-read --dispatch <dispatchId> --limit 50 --json
orca orchestration worker-stop --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
# After an accepted worker_done: reuse the same terminal for a follow-up Dispatch, or release it
# (archives inspectable output, then closes only that coordinator-owned agent terminal):
orca orchestration worker-release --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
# Keep a settled worker live for debugging when the user asked to retain it:
orca orchestration worker-retain --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
# retry placement is explicit — --retry-of does not inherit --on/worktree:
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --retry-of <dispatchId> --worktree current --agent codex --json

不要为了再读一遍输出，就把已完成的 Worker 终端开着。worker-release 之后用 worker-read。release 返回 release_pending 或 release_unknown 时，不要用一次宽泛的 terminal close 代替。按回执里的恢复动作做。

跨主机 Worker（可选）

orca orchestration worker-start \
  --task <taskId> \
  --on windows \
  --worktree new-top-level \
  --repo <exact_remote_repo_selector> \
  --name remote-worker \
  --agent codex \
  --setup run \
  --json
orca orchestration send --to dispatch:<dispatchId> --subject "Follow-up" --body "…" --json

之后的命令按 Dispatch ID 路由。不要再重复 --on。

底层 Dispatch（自定义拓扑）

orca worktree create --name billing-audit --agent codex --json
orca terminal wait --terminal <workerHandle> --for tui-idle --timeout-ms 60000 --json
orca orchestration dispatch --task <taskId> --to <workerHandle> --inject --json

消息注意点

  • 默认的 check 是所绑定 Run 里最老的、还没 ack 的 Delivery（先进先出）。一直重放，直到 --ack。
  • --peek / --all 不消耗邮件。
  • 组地址：@all、@idle、@claude、@codex、@opencode、@gemini、@droid、@grok、@cursor、@worktree:<id>。绝不要用于 worker_done 或心跳。
  • PowerShell 里给组地址加引号：--to "@all"。
orca orchestration send --to @all --subject "Heads up" --body "Pausing dispatches for a review." --json
orca orchestration send --to @idle --subject "Anyone free?" --json
orca orchestration send --to @codex --subject "Codex agents only" --json

等待进行中时，CLI 每 15 秒向 stderr 打一行很小的 JSON 心跳。stdout 仍然是命令的最终结果。

Worker 约定

被派出的 Worker 会收到一段开场说明，告诉它们怎么和协调者通信：

  • worker_done 恰好发一次，失败时也要发，并带上 --outcome。
  • --body 写短摘要：做了什么、发现了什么、还剩什么。
  • 同时带上任务 ID 和 Dispatch ID，免得过期的重试完成了错误的 Dispatch。
  • Dispatch 的任务说明要求时，在长时间的活动工作中发送 heartbeat。
  • 会挡住进度的问题用 orca orchestration ask，不用本机 TUI 提示。
orca orchestration ask \
  --to <coordinatorHandle> \
  --question "Should I update the shared component or only this page?" \
  --options "shared,page-only" \
  --timeout-ms 600000 \
  --json

加了 --json 时，ask 只打印一个 JSON 对象，Worker 可以把它用管道送给 jq -r .answer。

决策门

Worker 问协调者，用 ask。协调者已经建好任务 DAG、并且想在记下决议之前挡住一个任务时，用显式的门：

orca orchestration gate-create \
  --task <taskId> \
  --question "Merge the shared button change into the task branch?" \
  --options '["yes","no"]' \
  --json

orca orchestration gate-resolve --id <gateId> --resolution "yes" --json

恢复

orca orchestration dispatch-show --task <taskId> --json
orca orchestration dispatch-show --task <taskId> --preamble --json
orca orchestration task-list --json
orca orchestration task-update --id <taskId> --status blocked --result '{"reason":"waiting on credentials"}' --json

只有你有意放弃编排状态时才重置：

orca orchestration reset --tasks --json
orca orchestration reset --messages --json
orca orchestration reset --all --json

reset 影响的是运行时全局的编排状态。另一个协调者还在活动时不要跑，除非你就是要做这次清理。

选对命令

你正在看着的 Agent，给它一段轻量提示，用 orca terminal send。

Worker 必须回报 worker_done、通过协调者提问，并按任务 ID 被跟踪时，用 orca orchestration worker-start（或 dispatch --inject）。

想要一个持久的 Run 命名空间和受监督的多 Agent 循环时，用 orca orchestration run-create 加上任务和 Worker。不要用已经停用的 orchestration run。

完整说明

命令 flag 随应用变化。安装之后，Agent 应该运行：

orca skills get orchestration --full

Coordinate agents with Runs, tasks, supervised workers, messages, and decision gates.

Orchestration is Orca's structured multi-agent layer: a Run (namespace + coordinator inbox), Tasks, Dispatches, supervised workers, messages, and decision gates.

Use it when you need ownership, completion tracking, or a DAG. For one-off prompts, use orca terminal send. For full ownership handoffs without supervision, use worktree/terminal commands from the orca-cli skill.

Experimental

Enable orchestration under Settings → Experimental before using these commands. The experimental Orchestration panel and its switch are temporarily unavailable. The CLI talks to the running Orca runtime, so orca status --json should succeed first.

Legacy commands retired

orca orchestration run and run-stop (and coordinator-start / coordinator-stop) perform no effects. They return recovery text pointing at orca skills get orchestration --full. Use the Run + worker-start flow below.

Workers can start helper workers up to the Nested worker depth in Settings. The default is 4; profiles previously set to 1 move to 4 once, and later choices are kept. Stopping a worker also stops its helpers.

Core model

  • Run — durable namespace and home inbox. Never schedules or places workers.
  • Task — a work item with a spec, dependencies, and status: pending, ready, dispatched, completed, failed, or blocked.
  • Dispatch — one attempt of a task on a terminal; lifecycle authority for worker_done / heartbeat.
  • Message — inbox mail (status, dispatch, worker_done, escalation, question, heartbeat, …).
  • Decision gate — a coordinator-owned question that blocks a task until it is resolved.

Completion authority comes from the active dispatch context. Worker completion and heartbeat messages should include both taskId and dispatchId.

Task IDs printed in terminals, such as task_..., are clickable links. Clicking one asks the Orca runtime for the task's current dispatch and focuses the assigned terminal, including when the task lives in a remote or SSH runtime.

Preferred supervised loop

orca orchestration run-create --objective "Split checkout QA and summarize blockers" --json
orca orchestration task-create --spec "Audit billing settings for mobile layout" --task-title "Billing audit" --json
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree current --agent codex --json
# or new worktree:
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree new-child --name billing-audit --agent codex --setup run --json
# optional per-worker model / effort (Claude, Codex, Muse, Cursor, Antigravity, OpenCode; not with --terminal):
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree current --agent claude --model <opaque-model-id> --effort high --json
# Run Antigravity as a supervised worker in a local child worktree:
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --worktree new-child --name research --agent antigravity --setup run --json

--model accepts opaque provider model IDs for Claude, Codex, Muse, Cursor, Antigravity, and OpenCode. For Muse, pass the model id accepted by your Muse installation (for example muse-spark-1.3); for Antigravity, use a model slug reported by agy models. An OpenCode worker's requested model is validated on the execution host and kept to that worker, so it does not change the default model for your other launches. --effort requires --model and only applies when that agent/model supports the level. Neither flag can combine with --terminal (reuse an existing pane). For Codex models newer than Orca's built-in list (for example gpt-6.x), --effort accepts the full known vocabulary, including max and ultra, when the installed Codex supports them; unknown effort names are still refused. Overrides apply to that launch only and show under launch.requested / launch.effective in the start receipt. Federated starts need a worker host that advertises launch-preference support.

For a new worktree, omit --name to let Orca generate one.

Under Settings → Agent Orchestration, turn worker heartbeats off or choose an interval from 1 to 60 minutes. The default is 5 minutes. Changes apply to newly dispatched workers; existing workers keep their original brief, and older hosts keep the 5-minute default.

When Chat UI is on, worker-start launches supported agents the same way a new agent tab does: as a structured chat when the agent and host support it, otherwise as a terminal. --terminal still reuses an existing pane.

Orca prepares trust for a local Antigravity child worktree before starting its worker. Antigravity supervision over SSH is not supported by this trust setup. See Supported agents for its regular agent features.

Wait for completions (process every message in a Delivery, then ack):

orca orchestration check --wait --types worker_done,escalation,question --timeout-ms 900000 --json
orca orchestration check --ack <deliveryId> --wait --types worker_done,escalation,question --timeout-ms 900000 --json

Worker completion (from the worker pane; include injected IDs):

orca orchestration send \
  --type worker_done \
  --subject "Completed mobile audit" \
  --body "Fixed footer overlap; no follow-ups." \
  --task-id <taskId> \
  --dispatch-id <dispatchId> \
  --outcome succeeded \
  --files-modified "src/app/settings/Billing.tsx" \
  --json

worker_done requires --outcome succeeded|failed.

Inspect / recover:

orca orchestration worker-show --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
orca orchestration worker-read --dispatch <dispatchId> --limit 50 --json
orca orchestration worker-stop --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
# After an accepted worker_done: reuse the same terminal for a follow-up Dispatch, or release it
# (archives inspectable output, then closes only that coordinator-owned agent terminal):
orca orchestration worker-release --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
# Keep a settled worker live for debugging when the user asked to retain it:
orca orchestration worker-retain --dispatch <dispatchId> --json
# retry placement is explicit — --retry-of does not inherit --on/worktree:
orca orchestration worker-start --task <taskId> --retry-of <dispatchId> --worktree current --agent codex --json

Do not leave completed worker terminals open just to re-read output — use worker-read after worker-release. Do not substitute a broad terminal close when release returns release_pending or release_unknown; follow the receipt's recovery action.

Federated workers (optional)

orca orchestration worker-start \
  --task <taskId> \
  --on windows \
  --worktree new-top-level \
  --repo <exact_remote_repo_selector> \
  --name remote-worker \
  --agent codex \
  --setup run \
  --json
orca orchestration send --to dispatch:<dispatchId> --subject "Follow-up" --body "…" --json

Later commands route by Dispatch ID; do not repeat --on.

Low-level dispatch (custom topology)

orca worktree create --name billing-audit --agent codex --json
orca terminal wait --terminal <workerHandle> --for tui-idle --timeout-ms 60000 --json
orca orchestration dispatch --task <taskId> --to <workerHandle> --inject --json

Messaging notes

  • Default check is the bound Run's oldest unacked Delivery (FIFO). Replay until --ack.
  • --peek / --all do not consume mail.
  • Group addresses: @all, @idle, @claude, @codex, @opencode, @gemini, @droid, @grok, @cursor, @worktree:<id> — never for worker_done / heartbeat.
  • Quote PowerShell group addresses: --to "@all".
orca orchestration send --to @all --subject "Heads up" --body "Pausing dispatches for a review." --json
orca orchestration send --to @idle --subject "Anyone free?" --json
orca orchestration send --to @codex --subject "Codex agents only" --json

While a wait is active, the CLI emits small JSON heartbeat lines to stderr every 15 seconds. Stdout remains the final command result.

Worker contract

Dispatched workers receive a preamble that tells them how to communicate with the coordinator:

  • Send worker_done exactly once, even on failure, with --outcome.
  • Include a short --body summary: what was done, what was found, and what remains.
  • Include both task and dispatch IDs so stale retries cannot complete the wrong dispatch.
  • Send heartbeat messages during long active work when the dispatch brief requests them.
  • Use orca orchestration ask for blocking questions instead of local TUI prompts.
orca orchestration ask \
  --to <coordinatorHandle> \
  --question "Should I update the shared component or only this page?" \
  --options "shared,page-only" \
  --timeout-ms 600000 \
  --json

With --json, ask prints a single JSON object so workers can pipe it to jq -r .answer.

Decision gates

Use ask for worker-to-coordinator questions. Use explicit gates when the coordinator has created a task DAG and wants to block a task until a decision is recorded:

orca orchestration gate-create \
  --task <taskId> \
  --question "Merge the shared button change into the task branch?" \
  --options '["yes","no"]' \
  --json

orca orchestration gate-resolve --id <gateId> --resolution "yes" --json

Recovery

orca orchestration dispatch-show --task <taskId> --json
orca orchestration dispatch-show --task <taskId> --preamble --json
orca orchestration task-list --json
orca orchestration task-update --id <taskId> --status blocked --result '{"reason":"waiting on credentials"}' --json

Reset only when you are intentionally abandoning orchestration state:

orca orchestration reset --tasks --json
orca orchestration reset --messages --json
orca orchestration reset --all --json

reset affects runtime-global orchestration state. Do not run it while another coordinator is active unless that is the intended cleanup.

Choosing the right command

Use orca terminal send for a lightweight prompt to an agent you are watching.

Use orca orchestration worker-start (or dispatch --inject) when a worker must report worker_done, ask questions through the coordinator, and be tracked by task ID.

Use orca orchestration run-create + tasks + workers when you want a durable Run namespace and supervised multi-agent loop — not the retired orchestration run command.

Full guide

Command flags evolve with the app. After install, agents should run:

orca skills get orchestration --full

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/orchestration

上一章Orca CLI reference 下一章Scheduled automations

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

定时自动化

Scheduled automations

Orca 的自动化按时间表从 CLI 跑一段提示。分诊、审阅、维护这类重复的事，不用你亲手开一棵工作树。读完这一页，你会建一条先停着的自动化，看一看它，准备好了再跑。

先建一条安全的

调提示和目标时，先加上 --disabled：

orca automations create \
  --name "Weekday triage" \
  --trigger weekdays \
  --time 09:00 \
  --prompt "Triage new issues and summarize blockers" \
  --provider codex \
  --repo my-repo \
  --disabled \
  --json

--trigger 接受预设，例如 hourly、daily、weekdays、weekly，也接受 cron 表达式或 RRULE 字符串。时间表要跟某个具体的 IANA 时区，而不是运行时的默认时区时，用 --timezone <tz>。没有预设标签的多日规则，例如周二、周四、周六，会显示成存下来的那份时间表，而不是 Invalid schedule（无效时间表）。

选运行发生在哪里

每次运行都要在一个仓库里创建或选取工作时，用 --repo <selector>。自动化要在一棵已有的 Orca 工作树或文件夹工作区里跑时，用 --workspace <selector>：

orca automations create \
  --name "Nightly status" \
  --trigger "0 18 * * 1-5" \
  --prompt "Summarize today's changes" \
  --provider claude \
  --workspace active \
  --disabled

两个目标 flag 都省略时，Orca 能判断的话，会从当前 shell 目录找到包住它的那棵工作树。

定时运行发生在 Orca 服务器上一个已有工作区里时，对那些启动之后才读提示的 Agent（Aider、Goose、Amp 以及同类），Orca 会等它们就绪，再把自动化的提示打进去，而不是让它们空等。在命令行上就接提示的 Agent，例如 Claude 和 Codex，本来就不受影响。

跑之前先预检

便宜的 shell 探测失败时，跳过这次定时工作（非零退出会记成一次跳过的运行）：

orca automations create \
  --name "PR review" \
  --trigger hourly \
  --precheck "gh pr list --json number -q .[0].number" \
  --prompt "Review requested PRs" \
  --provider codex \
  --repo my-repo \
  --disabled \
  --json

项目主机 / 准备目标

自动化要跑在某个具体的项目主机准备上，而不是只靠 --repo / --workspace 时：

orca automations create \
  --name "Remote triage" \
  --trigger daily \
  --time 09:00 \
  --prompt "Triage open issues" \
  --provider claude \
  --project <projectId> \
  --host <hostId> \
  --disabled \
  --json

已经有准备 id 时，用 --project-host-setup <id>。可选的 --source-context '<json>' 把任务或提供方数据钉到某台主机或某个账号上（编辑时传 null 可以清掉）。

跨主机管理自动化

桌面的 Automations（自动化）页把这台电脑和已连接、且受支持的 Orca 主机上的时间表放进一张表。Host（主机）列显示每条自动化存在哪里、排在哪里。用 Filters → Host（过滤 → 主机）只看一台或多台，或回到 All hosts（全部主机）。

在桌面界面里创建时，Create on（创建在）先选主机，再选项目。项目选择器随后只显示那个目标上有的项目。需要更新 Orca 服务器的已连接主机仍然看得到，但是禁用的，并带一条 Update server（更新服务器），不会从选择器里消失。

错过运行的宽限

orca automations edit <automationId> --missed-run-grace-minutes 30 --json

沿用已有的自动化会话

目标是一棵已有工作树时，加上 --reuse-session。之后的运行会接着上一次还活着的自动化终端，而不是每次从空白终端开始：

orca automations create \
  --name "Inbox digest" \
  --trigger hourly \
  --prompt "Summarize unread mail" \
  --provider codex \
  --workspace active \
  --reuse-session \
  --disabled

用 orca automations edit <automationId> --fresh-session --json 改回每次运行都用新终端。

每次运行的模型和 effort

每条自动化都可以在每次运行时多带 Agent 参数。这样一条自动化可以用不同的模型或 effort，又不改主机上其他自动化的默认值：

orca automations create \
  --name "Docs tidy" \
  --trigger daily \
  --prompt "Tidy the docs" \
  --provider claude \
  --extra-agent-args="--model opus --add-dir docs" \
  --disabled

桌面自动化编辑器里，同一个字段是 Advanced → Extra agent arguments（高级 → 额外 Agent 参数）。每次全新运行，Orca 从主机上该 Agent 的默认 Arguments（参数）出发，再把额外的接上。额外参数里的模型或 effort 会换掉默认的。值以 -- 开头，所以用 = 传入。

额外参数只接受模型和 effort 选项，外加 Claude 的 --add-dir，适用于 Claude、Codex、CodeBuddy、Cursor、Grok 和 OMP。权限、配置、提示和会话选项会被拒绝。它们要求每次运行都是新会话，所以不能和 --reuse-session 一起用。清掉用 orca automations edit <automationId> --extra-agent-args=。

看过再启用

启用之前先列出并查看：

orca automations list --json
orca automations show <automationId> --json
orca automations edit <automationId> --enabled --json

桌面的自动化列表里，时间表很多时，用搜索框按名称、项目或提示文本过滤。住在 SSH 主机上的外部自动化，主机断开时仍然作为你可以管理的任务出现（包括删除）。删一条时间表不需要先有活动的 SSH 会话。

用 Filters（过滤）按 Host、Enabled（已启用）或 Paused（已暂停）、上次运行结果（Failed 失败、Succeeded 成功，或 Never ran 从未跑过），或一个或多个 Agents 收窄列表。Agent 子菜单很长时，在里面搜索。生效的过滤显示在表格上方，是可以去掉的药丸。表格显示每条自动化的主机、上次运行结果和相对时间。点 Name（名称）或 Last run（上次运行）列来排序。名称按字母；上次运行默认最新的在前。

自动化列表开着时，在搜索框里打字，按 ArrowUp 或 ArrowDown 在当前能看见的匹配行之间移动选择。列表会把选中行保持在视野里。正在修改或组字的输入仍按平常的光标行为。

键盘也可以在自动化详情里用：按 Enter 打开选中的自动化或运行，ArrowUp / ArrowDown 在运行历史里移动，ArrowLeft / ArrowRight 在 Overview（概览）和 Runs（运行）之间切换，Escape 从详情回到列表。在单次运行页上，用更早和更新的运行箭头，或 J / K，在运行之间移动。

用 edit 改名称、提示、提供方、目标、时间表或启用状态。remove 删掉一条自动化和它的运行历史。

需要时立刻跑

建好之后，先手动触发一次，核对提示和目标，再等下一个时间点：

orca automations run <automationId> --json
orca automations runs --id <automationId> --json

一次运行在打开工作区或重新连上目标之前就失败了，在 Orca 里打开那次运行，点 Rerun（再跑一次），为同一条自动化排一次新的手动运行。

下一步

  • Orca CLI 概览：工作树、终端和浏览器控制的其余 CLI。
  • 技能与 MCP：装上 Orca CLI 技能，Agent 就能调用同一批命令。

Orca automations run a prompt on a schedule from the CLI, so recurring triage, review, and maintenance tasks can start without you opening a worktree by hand. By the end of this page, you'll create a disabled automation, inspect it, and run it when you're ready.

Create a safe first automation

Start with --disabled while you tune the prompt and target:

orca automations create \
  --name "Weekday triage" \
  --trigger weekdays \
  --time 09:00 \
  --prompt "Triage new issues and summarize blockers" \
  --provider codex \
  --repo my-repo \
  --disabled \
  --json

--trigger accepts presets such as hourly, daily, weekdays, and weekly, plus cron expressions or RRULE strings. Use --timezone <tz> when the schedule should follow a specific IANA timezone instead of the runtime default. Multi-day rules without a preset label, such as Tuesday/Thursday/Saturday, display as their stored schedule instead of Invalid schedule.

Choose where runs happen

Use --repo <selector> when each run should create or select work in a repository. Use --workspace <selector> when the automation should run inside an existing Orca worktree or folder workspace instead:

orca automations create \
  --name "Nightly status" \
  --trigger "0 18 * * 1-5" \
  --prompt "Summarize today's changes" \
  --provider claude \
  --workspace active \
  --disabled

When you omit both target flags, Orca resolves the enclosing worktree from the current shell directory when it can.

When a scheduled run happens in an existing workspace on an Orca server, Orca types the automation's prompt into agents that read it after they start (Aider, Goose, Amp, and similar) once they are ready, instead of leaving them idle. Agents that take the prompt on the command line, such as Claude and Codex, were never affected.

Precheck before a run

Skip scheduled work when a cheap shell probe fails (non-zero exit records a skipped run):

orca automations create \
  --name "PR review" \
  --trigger hourly \
  --precheck "gh pr list --json number -q .[0].number" \
  --prompt "Review requested PRs" \
  --provider codex \
  --repo my-repo \
  --disabled \
  --json

Project host / setup targets

When the automation should run on a specific project host setup rather than only --repo / --workspace:

orca automations create \
  --name "Remote triage" \
  --trigger daily \
  --time 09:00 \
  --prompt "Triage open issues" \
  --provider claude \
  --project <projectId> \
  --host <hostId> \
  --disabled \
  --json

Use --project-host-setup <id> when you already have a setup id. Optional --source-context '<json>' pins task/provider data to a host/account (pass null on edit to clear).

Manage automations across hosts

The desktop Automations page loads schedules from this computer and supported connected Orca hosts into one table. The Host column shows where each automation is stored and scheduled; use Filters → Host to show one or more hosts, or return to All hosts.

When you create an automation in the desktop UI, Create on chooses its host before you choose a project. The project picker then shows projects available on that destination. Connected hosts that need a newer Orca server stay visible but disabled, with an Update server message, instead of disappearing from the picker.

Missed-run grace

orca automations edit <automationId> --missed-run-grace-minutes 30 --json

Reuse an existing automation session

For automations that target an existing worktree, add --reuse-session when later runs should continue in the previous live automation terminal instead of starting from a blank terminal each time:

orca automations create \
  --name "Inbox digest" \
  --trigger hourly \
  --prompt "Summarize unread mail" \
  --provider codex \
  --workspace active \
  --reuse-session \
  --disabled

Use orca automations edit <automationId> --fresh-session --json to switch the automation back to a fresh terminal per run.

Per-run model and effort

Each automation can pass extra agent arguments on every run, so one automation can use a different model or effort without changing the host's defaults for the others:

orca automations create \
  --name "Docs tidy" \
  --trigger daily \
  --prompt "Tidy the docs" \
  --provider claude \
  --extra-agent-args="--model opus --add-dir docs" \
  --disabled

In the desktop automation editor, the same field is Advanced → Extra agent arguments. On each fresh run, Orca starts from the host's default Arguments for that agent and appends the extras; a model or effort option in the extras replaces the default's. Pass the value with = because it begins with --.

Extras accept only model and effort options, plus Claude's --add-dir, for Claude, Codex, CodeBuddy, Cursor, Grok, and OMP. Permission, config, prompt, and session options are rejected. They require a fresh session for every run, so they can't be combined with --reuse-session; clear them with orca automations edit <automationId> --extra-agent-args=.

Review and enable

List and inspect automations before enabling them:

orca automations list --json
orca automations show <automationId> --json
orca automations edit <automationId> --enabled --json

In the desktop automations list, use the search field to filter by name, project, or prompt text when you have many schedules. External automations that live on an SSH host still appear as jobs you can manage (including delete) when that host is disconnected — you do not need an active SSH session just to remove a schedule.

Use Filters to narrow the list by Host, Enabled or Paused state, last-run outcome (Failed, Succeeded, or Never ran), or one or more Agents. Search inside the agent submenu when the list is long; active filters appear as removable pills above the table. The table shows the host, last-run result, and relative time for each automation. Click the Name or Last run column to sort; name sorts alphabetically, while last run defaults to newest first.

When the automation list is open, type in the search field and press ArrowUp or ArrowDown to move the selection through the visible matching rows. The list keeps the selected row in view; modified or composing input keeps its normal cursor behavior.

Keyboard navigation also works across the automation details: press Enter to open the selected automation or run, ArrowUp / ArrowDown to move through run history, ArrowLeft / ArrowRight to switch between Overview and Runs, and Escape to return from the detail view to the list. On an individual run page, use the older and newer run arrows or J / K to move between runs.

Use edit to change the name, prompt, provider, target, schedule, or enabled state. remove deletes an automation and its run history.

Run on demand

After creating an automation, trigger it manually to check the prompt and target before waiting for the next scheduled time:

orca automations run <automationId> --json
orca automations runs --id <automationId> --json

If a run fails before it opens a workspace or reconnects to its target, open that run in Orca and click Rerun to queue a fresh manual run for the same automation.

Next steps

  • Orca CLI overview — see the rest of the CLI surface for worktrees, terminals, and browser control.
  • Skills registry & MCP — install the Orca CLI skill so agents can call the same commands.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/automations

上一章Orchestration 下一章Computer use

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

本机操作

Computer use

orca computer 让 Agent 查看并操作本机桌面应用：列出正在跑的应用，读无障碍树，点击控件，填值，输入文字，滚动，截图。事情要操作操作系统或第三方应用，而不是终端或内置浏览器时，用它。

Beta

本机操作按平台带原生辅助程序，需要无障碍权限；macOS 上还要屏幕录制权限。命令面已经稳到可以让技能对着它写，但 flag 名仍可能变。

第一次设置

先看运行时和权限：

orca status --json
orca computer permissions --json
orca computer capabilities --json

permissions 报有缺的，就在系统设置里把无障碍（macOS 上还有屏幕录制）授给 Orca Computer Use，再跑一次 permissions --json 确认。

在 macOS 上后台使用

macOS 上，本机操作默认在后台做，所以 Agent 在另一个应用里干活时，你还可以继续用鼠标和键盘。有些动作仍然要那个应用到前台。前台模式用 --restore-window。在 Chromium 和 Electron 应用里拖拽必须用它。

快照，操作，再快照

每次交互都是同一圈：读应用的当前状态，对一个具体元素做动作，再读一次状态，核对结果。

orca computer list-apps --json
orca computer get-app-state --app com.spotify.client --json
orca computer click --app com.spotify.client --element-index 42 --json

元素序号只对最近一次 get-app-state 的结果有效，而且可能不连续。在 --json 输出里，从 result.snapshot.treeText 读这棵树。不要按 elementCount 编序号。导航、焦点变化、滚动，或应用任何一次重绘之后，先刷新状态，再复用序号。

多窗口应用

orca computer list-windows --app com.microsoft.edgemac --json
orca computer get-app-state --app com.microsoft.edgemac --window-id <id> --json
orca computer click --app com.microsoft.edgemac --window-id <id> --element-index 12 --json

列出的 id 不是 none 时，优先用稳定的 --window-id。否则用 --window-index。

选一个应用

优先用 list-apps 返回的 bundle ID：

orca computer get-app-state --app com.microsoft.edgemac --json

名字没有歧义时可以用应用名（--app Spotify）。bundle ID 和名字都撞上时，才用 --app pid:<number>。

可以做的动作

orca computer click --app <app> --element-index <i> --json
orca computer set-value --app <app> --element-index <i> --value "text" --json
orca computer type-text --app <app> --text "text" --json
orca computer press-key --app <app> --key Return --json
orca computer hotkey --app <app> --key CmdOrCtrl+A --json
orca computer paste-text --app <app> --text "text" --json
orca computer scroll --app <app> --element-index <i> --direction down --json
orca computer drag --app <app> --from-x 100 --from-y 100 --to-x 300 --to-y 300 --json
orca computer perform-secondary-action --app <app> --element-index <i> --action <name> --json

优先用语义动作（click、set-value、perform-secondary-action），少用原始的 type-text 或 press-key。语义动作直接打到无障碍元素上，焦点变了也还在；键盘输入则不行。

无障碍定位失败时，再小心地退回坐标：

orca computer click --app com.apple.Safari --x 120 --y 340 --json
orca computer drag --app <app> --from-element-index 3 --to-element-index 9 --json

敏感输入

机密从标准输入送进去，免得进 shell 历史：

printf '%s' "$TEXT" | orca computer set-value \
  --app com.apple.Safari --element-index 7 --value-stdin --json

type-text 和 paste-text 同样可以用 --text-stdin。

截图

get-app-state 返回无障碍树，默认还带一张截图。加了 --json 时，图片字节写到磁盘，路径在 screenshot.path 里，不嵌进响应。不需要像素时加 --no-screenshot（更快，载荷更小）。窗口藏着或最小化时，加 --restore-window，先把它带到眼前再截。

从 Agent 里用

随应用附带的 computer-use 技能把同一套命令和安全说明打在一起。把它装进 Agent 的技能目录：

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill computer-use

技能怎么被发现，见 技能与 MCP。

下一步

  • Orca CLI 概览：CLI 的其余部分（工作树、终端、浏览器）。
  • 技能与 MCP：把这套 CLI 交给 Agent。

Drive local desktop apps from an agent via accessibility trees, screenshots, and safe UI actions.

The orca computer CLI lets an agent inspect and control native desktop apps — list running apps, read accessibility trees, click controls, set values, type text, scroll, and take screenshots. Use it when a task needs to operate the OS or a third-party app rather than a terminal or the built-in browser.

Beta

Computer use ships native helpers per platform and requires Accessibility (and on macOS, Screen Recording) permission. The command surface is stable enough for skills to build against, but flag names may still shift.

First-time setup

Check the runtime and permissions:

orca status --json
orca computer permissions --json
orca computer capabilities --json

If permissions reports anything missing, grant Accessibility (and Screen Recording on macOS) to Orca Computer Use in System Settings, then re-run permissions --json to confirm.

Background use on macOS

On macOS, computer use acts in the background by default, so you can keep using your mouse and keyboard while an agent works in another app. Some actions still require the app to come forward. Use --restore-window for foreground mode; dragging in Chromium and Electron apps requires it.

Snapshot → act → snapshot

Every interaction follows the same loop: read the app's current state, act on a specific element, then re-read state to verify the result.

orca computer list-apps --json
orca computer get-app-state --app com.spotify.client --json
orca computer click --app com.spotify.client --element-index 42 --json

Element indexes are scoped to the latest get-app-state result and may be sparse. In --json output, read the tree from result.snapshot.treeText. Do not invent indexes from elementCount. Refresh state after navigation, focus changes, scrolling, or any app re-render before reusing an index.

Multi-window apps

orca computer list-windows --app com.microsoft.edgemac --json
orca computer get-app-state --app com.microsoft.edgemac --window-id <id> --json
orca computer click --app com.microsoft.edgemac --window-id <id> --element-index 12 --json

Prefer stable --window-id when the listed id is not none; otherwise use --window-index.

Selecting an app

Prefer bundle IDs returned by list-apps:

orca computer get-app-state --app com.microsoft.edgemac --json

App names work when unambiguous (--app Spotify). Use --app pid:<number> only when bundle ID and name both collide.

Available actions

orca computer click --app <app> --element-index <i> --json
orca computer set-value --app <app> --element-index <i> --value "text" --json
orca computer type-text --app <app> --text "text" --json
orca computer press-key --app <app> --key Return --json
orca computer hotkey --app <app> --key CmdOrCtrl+A --json
orca computer paste-text --app <app> --text "text" --json
orca computer scroll --app <app> --element-index <i> --direction down --json
orca computer drag --app <app> --from-x 100 --from-y 100 --to-x 300 --to-y 300 --json
orca computer perform-secondary-action --app <app> --element-index <i> --action <name> --json

Prefer semantic actions (click, set-value, perform-secondary-action) over raw type-text or press-key — they target accessibility elements directly and survive focus changes that keyboard input doesn't.

When accessibility targeting fails, fall back carefully to coordinates:

orca computer click --app com.apple.Safari --x 120 --y 340 --json
orca computer drag --app <app> --from-element-index 3 --to-element-index 9 --json

Sensitive input

Pass secrets through stdin so they don't land in shell history:

printf '%s' "$TEXT" | orca computer set-value \
  --app com.apple.Safari --element-index 7 --value-stdin --json

--text-stdin works the same way for type-text and paste-text.

Screenshots

get-app-state returns an accessibility tree and, by default, a screenshot. With --json, the image bytes are written to disk and the path is returned in screenshot.path rather than embedded in the response. Pass --no-screenshot when pixels aren't needed (faster, smaller payload). Pass --restore-window to bring a hidden or minimized window into view before capture.

Use it from an agent

The shipped computer-use skill packages the same command surface with safety guidance. Install it into the agent's skill directory:

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill computer-use

See Skills registry & MCP for how skills are picked up.

Next steps

  • Orca CLI overview — the rest of the CLI surface (worktrees, terminal, browser).
  • Skills registry & MCP — distributing this CLI to agents.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/computer-use

上一章Scheduled automations 下一章Worktree checkpoints

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

工作树检查点

Worktree checkpoints

每棵 Orca 工作树有一个轻量的自由文本 comment（备注），界面里看得到。它是这棵工作树眼下在做什么的一张快照。Agent 可以用 CLI 更新它。想让一起干活的人知道进度、又不必开一场对话，就用这个。

写法

orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "reproduced auth failure; testing credential-chain fix" --json

卡片状态（可选）

阶段变了，除了自由文本备注，还可以设工作区卡片的状态：

orca worktree set --worktree active \
  --comment "fix implemented; running integration tests" \
  --workspace-status in-progress \
  --json

状态：todo、in-progress、in-review、completed（或这棵工作区在用的自定义 id）。

适合写一笔的时候

  • 做完了有意义的一块实现。
  • 一个猜测被证实，或被否定。
  • 做完一次代码审阅。
  • 卡住了（在等外部输入、上游 bug、缺权限）。
  • 从调查转到修复，或从修复转到验证。

格式

第一行写动作：刚刚发生了什么、在哪里、状态或下一步。

orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "added debounce to SearchBar onChange (src/components/SearchBar.tsx); ready for review
goal: reduce redundant API calls per #298" --json

先读再写

备注里可能有人写过的上下文。先读一遍，免得盖掉目标和约束：

orca worktree current --json

还成立的留下，过时的去掉，再把你的更新织进去。

Every Orca worktree carries a lightweight, free-text comment field visible in the UI — a status snapshot of what the worktree is doing right now. Agents can update it from the CLI, and it's the pattern we recommend for keeping human collaborators in the loop without forcing chat.

The pattern

orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "reproduced auth failure; testing credential-chain fix" --json

Card status (optional)

Alongside the free-text comment, set the workspace card status when the phase changes:

orca worktree set --worktree active \
  --comment "fix implemented; running integration tests" \
  --workspace-status in-progress \
  --json

Statuses: todo, in-progress, in-review, completed (or a custom id your workspace uses).

Good checkpoint moments

  • Finished a meaningful slice of implementation.
  • Confirmed or refuted a hypothesis.
  • Completed a code review.
  • Hit a blocker (waiting on external input, upstream bug, missing access).
  • Transitioning from investigation to fix, or fix to verify.

Format

First line is the action: what just happened, where, and the status or next step.

orca worktree set --worktree active --comment "added debounce to SearchBar onChange (src/components/SearchBar.tsx); ready for review
goal: reduce redundant API calls per #298" --json

Reading before writing

If the comment might have user-written context, read it first so you don't clobber goals or constraints:

orca worktree current --json

Preserve what's still valid, drop what's stale, and weave in your update.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/worktree-checkpoints

上一章Computer use 下一章Orca skills registry & MCP

CLI & Automation · Orca CLI & Skills

Orca 技能与 MCP

Orca skills registry & MCP

Orca 带了一批技能，Agent 把它们装进自己的技能目录。公开安装包是混合占位：短的 SKILL.md，告诉 Agent 何时去用 Orca，以及怎样从正在运行的 CLI 取出完整说明。命令 flag 住在二进制里，所以不会和应用程序版本走散。

可安装的 Orca 技能

用 npx skills add，加上公开的 Orca 仓库和技能名。默认的 Agent 设置通常会装 orca-cli、computer-use 和 orchestration。

技能 安装 用来做
orca-cli npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-cli --global 工作树、终端、文件、自动化、内置浏览器。
orchestration npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orchestration --global 多 Agent 的 Run、任务、受监督的 Worker、消息、决策门。
computer-use npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill computer-use --global 通过无障碍树和安全的界面动作操作桌面应用。
orca-linear npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-linear --global 用 orca linear 读写 Linear 工单。
orca-emulator npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator --global 控制 iOS Simulator。
orca-emulator-android npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator-android --global 通过 adb 控制 Android 模拟器或设备。
orca-per-workspace-env npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-per-workspace-env --global 按工作区准备环境的配方（orca.yaml）。

混合占位和实时说明

npx skills add 之后，Agent 看到的是一段短占位，意思是：

  1. 解析这次会话的 CLI 可执行文件（ORCA_CLI_COMMAND、orca-dev、Linux 上的 orca-ide，否则是 orca）。
  2. 取出完整说明：orca skills get <topic>（长说明加 --full）。
  3. 优先用 --json，不要凭记忆编 flag。
orca skills list
orca skills get orca-cli
orca skills get orchestration --full
orca skills get orca-linear --json

Agent 要给自动化一份确定的输出时，加上 --json。skills show 是 skills get 的别名。

让技能保持最新

Orca 带出的技能包比全局已装的更新时，应用可以：

  • 显示 update available（有更新）提示。某一份副本过期、而更新器不能安全改写时，显示 Needs attention（需要处理）。
  • 打开 Update skills（更新技能）：列出各个放置位置和跳过原因，然后无界面地运行 npx --yes skills update <names> --global -y（不嵌入终端）。
  • 让这次运行留在后台：关掉对话框不会取消。状态栏有一段显示进度（跑的时候转圈，成功时短暂打勾，失败会一直留着，直到你处理）。点这一段可以重新打开对话框。
  • 在 Settings → Agents 上显示已装技能的新鲜程度（全局安装一栏），某一行需要复查时还有 Details（详情）。

手动等价命令（桌面 Settings 里仍是同一个 npx 形式）：

npx skills update orca-cli orchestration computer-use --global

无界面主机（SSH、容器、CI、orca serve）没有 Settings 界面时，用本机 CLI 包装。它们解析出同一批 npx 命令，加上非交互 flag，并且不需要正在运行的 Orca 运行时：

orca skills install                                      # list installable names
orca skills install --skill orca-cli --skill orchestration
orca skills install --skill orca-cli --agent claude-code,codex
orca skills install --all --dry-run
orca skills update --all
orca skills update --skill orca-cli --dry-run
  • 默认范围是全局（--global）。只要当前项目，传 --local。
  • install 的目标是 Orca 在这台主机上发现的 Agent（外加共享的 .agents/skills 目录）。用 --agent <name>[,<name>…] 或 --agent universal 覆盖。一个 Agent 都没发现时，必须给 --agent。
  • update 只刷新已经安装的技能。
  • --dry-run 打印解析出来的命令。--json 只在列出或 --dry-run 时有效。

Orca 主动提供应用内更新时，优先用它，这样应用扫到的那些全局放置位置会被改写。标成 Skipped（已跳过）的行会说明为什么不能自动更新（例如缺来源登记）。先修好放置位置，再查一次。

在 Skills 页安装和删除

应用内安装要你选工作区目标时，按工作区名称或种类（worktree 工作树 / folder 文件夹）搜索选择器。Agent 选择器把 Orca 总会带上的目录，和你可以另选的 Agent 目录分开，所以继续之前能看清确切的安装目标。

要去掉一个已扫描的技能，打开它的操作菜单，选 Delete…（删除…）。要删多个，打开页面的 More actions（更多操作），选 Delete skills…（删除技能…），再选符合条件的结果。某一份不能删时，Orca 会在你确认之前说明原因，例如它是随应用附带的、由插件管理的、只读的，或不在可删除的技能文件夹里。这和 Manage installs（管理安装）是分开的。后者管的是从共享链接装上的副本。

在主机之间分享私有技能

打开 Skills → Share skills（技能 → 分享技能），把一个技能或一捆技能发到一条不公开、可撤销的链接后面。发布需要你的 Orca 账号。发布前先看要包含的文件、脚本、可执行文件、摘要，以及可选的发布说明。每个已发布版本不可变，所以之后的本地修改不会悄悄改掉别人装到的内容。

任何持有有效链接的人都可以查看并安装这些共享技能，不用登录。把链接当成凭据。接收方先看确切版本，再选全部或其中一部分技能，然后在这台电脑、一份已配对的 Orca 运行时、WSL 或一台 SSH 主机上选择全局或工作区范围。已有且被改过的技能默认是 Keep local（保留本机这份）。用 Skills → Manage installs 更新、回退或去掉 Orca 装上的副本。用 Settings → Share Skills 复制或撤销你的有效链接。撤销链接会挡住以后的访问，已经装上的副本不会被删掉。

你先打开默认关闭的 Settings → Share Skills → Allow agents and the Orca CLI to publish skill links（允许 Agent 和 Orca CLI 发布技能链接）之后，Agent 可以从 CLI 发布一组你点名的技能：

orca skills installed --json
orca skills share --skill frontend --skill testing --bundle-name "Team Toolkit" --json

skills installed 返回安全的选择器，不暴露本机路径。skills share 接受确切的发现 ID，或没有歧义的名字。它不接受任意路径，也不接受 --all。

orca-cli

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-cli --global

装好之后，用 orca skills get orca-cli 取出和版本匹配的命令说明。见 Orca CLI。

orchestration

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orchestration --global

Agent 要通过 Run、任务、受监督的 Worker 和决策门去协调其他 Agent 时用这个。见 编排。改编排状态之前，始终先加载 orca skills get orchestration --full。旧的 orchestration run 命令已经停用。

computer-use

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill computer-use --global

Agent 要查看并操作本机桌面应用窗口时用这个。见 本机操作。

orca-linear

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-linear --global

Agent 改工单之前应先加载 orca skills get orca-linear。覆盖 issue --full、save-issue、list-issues、关联、完成时的附加加评论，以及不可信工单的规则。已有的 linear-tickets 安装仍然能解析到。见 CLI 参考 → Linear。

orca-emulator

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator --global

Agent 要从 Orca 里面用 orca emulator 控制 iOS Simulator 时用这个。

orca-emulator-android

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator-android --global

给 adb 连上的 Android AVD 或设备用：列出和启动、点按、滑动、输入、硬件按钮、安装和启动、权限、无障碍树、logcat。细节用 orca skills get orca-emulator-android 取。

orca-per-workspace-env

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-per-workspace-env --global

在 orca.yaml 里设置或排查按工作区准备的环境配方时用。见 Orca 的几种运行方式。

发现来源

Orca 的技能界面会扫描已安装的技能目录：Claude、Codex、Agent Skills，以及 OMP（~/.omp/agent/skills）。放在那些地方的技能不用手工符号链接就会出现。

加上你自己的技能

任何带 skills/<name>/SKILL.md 的仓库，都可以用 npx skills add 安装。把 Agent 指到内部仓库，它就有公司自己的能力。

MCP 服务器

模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器把外部工具暴露给兼容的 Agent。在 Settings → Integrations → MCP 登记 MCP 端点。这些工具会出现在支持 MCP 的 Agent CLI 里。

Install Orca agent skills with npx skills add. Hybrid stubs stay thin; orca skills get serves the version-matched guide. Orca can update skills in the background.

Orca ships skills that agents install into their skill directories. Public install packages are hybrid discovery stubs: short SKILL.md files that tell the agent when to engage Orca and how to load the full guide from the running CLI. Command flags live in the binary so they cannot drift from the app version.

Installable Orca skills

Use npx skills add with the public Orca repo and the skill name. Default agent setup usually installs orca-cli, computer-use, and orchestration.

SkillInstallUse it for
orca-clinpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-cli --globalWorktrees, terminals, files, automations, embedded browser.
orchestrationnpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orchestration --globalMulti-agent Runs, tasks, supervised workers, messages, gates.
computer-usenpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill computer-use --globalDesktop apps via accessibility trees and safe UI actions.
orca-linearnpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-linear --globalLinear ticket read/write through orca linear.
orca-emulatornpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator --globaliOS Simulator control.
orca-emulator-androidnpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator-android --globalAndroid emulator/device via adb.
orca-per-workspace-envnpx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-per-workspace-env --globalPer-workspace environment recipes (orca.yaml).

Hybrid stubs vs the live guide

After npx skills add, agents see a short stub that says:

  1. Resolve the CLI executable for this session (ORCA_CLI_COMMAND, orca-dev, Linux orca-ide, else orca).
  2. Load the full guide: orca skills get <topic> (or --full for the long guide).
  3. Prefer --json and do not invent flags from memory.
orca skills list
orca skills get orca-cli
orca skills get orchestration --full
orca skills get orca-linear --json

Add --json when an agent needs deterministic output for automation. skills show is an alias for skills get.

Keep skills up to date

When Orca ships a newer skill package than the one installed globally, the app can:

  • Show an update available nudge (and Needs attention when a copy is out of date somewhere the updater cannot safely rewrite).
  • Open Update skills — list of placements and skip reasons, then run npx --yes skills update <names> --global -y headlessly (no embedded terminal).
  • Keep the run in the background: closing the dialog does not cancel it. A status-bar segment shows progress (spinner while running, brief success check, persistent failure until you act). Click the segment to reopen the dialog.
  • Surface freshness on Settings → Agents for installed skills (global install rail), including Details when a row needs review.

Manual equivalent (desktop Settings still shows the same npx form):

npx skills update orca-cli orchestration computer-use --global

On headless hosts (SSH, containers, CI, orca serve) with no Settings UI, use the local CLI wrappers — they resolve the same npx commands, add non-interactive flags, and do not need a running Orca runtime:

orca skills install                                      # list installable names
orca skills install --skill orca-cli --skill orchestration
orca skills install --skill orca-cli --agent claude-code,codex
orca skills install --all --dry-run
orca skills update --all
orca skills update --skill orca-cli --dry-run
  • Default scope is global (--global); pass --local for the current project only.
  • install targets agents Orca detects on the host (plus the shared .agents/skills directory). Use --agent <name>[,<name>…] or --agent universal to override; if no agent is detected, --agent is required.
  • update only refreshes skills that are already installed.
  • --dry-run prints the resolved command; --json is only valid with listing / --dry-run.

Prefer the in-app updater when Orca offers it so the same global placements the app scanned get rewritten. Rows marked Skipped explain why that skill cannot be updated automatically (for example missing source registration) — fix the placement, then re-check.

Install and delete from the Skills page

When an in-app install asks for a workspace target, search the picker by workspace name or by kind (worktree / folder). The agent picker separates directories Orca always includes from additional agent directories you can select, so you can review the exact installation targets before continuing.

To remove a scanned skill, open its actions menu and choose Delete…. For several skills, open the page's More actions menu, choose Delete skills…, and select the eligible results. Orca explains why a copy cannot be deleted—for example because it is bundled, plugin-managed, read-only, or outside a removable skill folder—before you confirm. This is separate from Manage installs, which manages copies installed from shared links.

Share private skills between hosts

Open Skills → Share skills to publish one skill or a bundle behind one unlisted, revocable link. Publishing requires your Orca account. Review the included files, scripts, executables, digest, and optional release notes before you publish; each published version is immutable, so later local edits do not silently change what recipients install.

Anyone with an active link can inspect and install the shared skills without signing in, so treat the link like a credential. A recipient reviews the exact version, chooses all or a subset of its skills, then selects global or workspace scope on this computer, a paired Orca runtime, WSL, or an SSH host. Existing modified skills default to Keep local. Use Skills → Manage installs to update, roll back, or remove copies Orca installed, and Settings → Share Skills to copy or revoke your active links. Revoking a link blocks future access but does not remove copies already installed.

Agents can publish an explicitly named set from the CLI after you enable the separate, default-off Settings → Share Skills → Allow agents and the Orca CLI to publish skill links permission:

orca skills installed --json
orca skills share --skill frontend --skill testing --bundle-name "Team Toolkit" --json

skills installed returns safe selectors without exposing local paths. skills share accepts exact discovery IDs or unambiguous names; it does not accept arbitrary paths or --all.

orca-cli

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-cli --global

After install, load the version-matched command guide with orca skills get orca-cli. See Orca CLI.

orchestration

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orchestration --global

Use this when an agent should coordinate other agents through Runs, tasks, supervised workers, and decision gates. See Orchestration. Always load orca skills get orchestration --full before mutating orchestration state — the legacy orchestration run command is retired.

computer-use

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill computer-use --global

Use this when an agent needs to inspect and operate local desktop app windows. See Computer use.

orca-linear

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-linear --global

Agents should load orca skills get orca-linear before mutating tickets. Covers issue --full, save-issue, list-issues, relations, completion attach+comment flow, and untrusted-ticket rules. Existing linear-tickets installs still resolve. See CLI reference → Linear.

orca-emulator

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator --global

Use this when an agent should control an iOS Simulator from inside Orca through orca emulator commands.

orca-emulator-android

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-emulator-android --global

Use for adb-connected Android AVDs/devices: list/boot, tap/swipe/type, hardware buttons, install/launch, permissions, accessibility tree, logcat. Load details with orca skills get orca-emulator-android.

orca-per-workspace-env

npx skills add https://github.com/stablyai/orca --skill orca-per-workspace-env --global

Use when setting up or debugging per-workspace environment recipes in orca.yaml. See Ways to run Orca.

Discovery sources

Orca's skill UI scans installed skill homes for Claude, Codex, Agent Skills, and OMP (~/.omp/agent/skills), so skills placed there show up without a manual symlink.

Add your own skills

Any repo with a skills/<name>/SKILL.md file can be installed via npx skills add. Point your agent at internal repos to give it company-specific powers.

MCP servers

Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers expose external tools to compatible agents. Register MCP endpoints under Settings → Integrations → MCP; those tools appear inside agent CLIs that support MCP.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/cli/skills

上一章Worktree checkpoints 下一章Mobile companion

Mobile

手机伴侣

Mobile companion

Orca 的手机伴侣是 iOS / Android 应用，和桌面 Orca 配对之后，你可以在手机上查看并和 Agent 一起干活：回复提示、看源代码管理、切换 Agent 账号。Agent 会话归已配对的桌面所有。

Beta

手机伴侣是 Beta。iOS 从 App Store 安装。Android 从 Google Play 或当前 APK 安装。

如果你还有旧的 Android 应用，把新的 dev.orca.app 单独装上。它不能就地更新到新包名。

手机上能做什么

  • 用 Agents 标签看已连接桌面上的 Agent，标成已读，或从列表里清掉。活动状态来自每台桌面。
  • 看每棵工作树、它的 Agent，以及当前状态（正在做 / 完成 / 等输入）。手机把你连上的每台主机（本机桌面和远程 Orca 服务器）的工作树放在同一个视图里，切换不用换设备。SSH 主机上的工作区，在行标记上显示那台主机的健康状况，用词和桌面相同，所以掉线的机器一眼能看见。
  • 在工作区列表里看桌面的项目组。
  • 从手机浏览工作区的完整文件树，包括嵌得很深的路径，不用等桌面就能跳进任意文件。视频和音乐文件在手机上的预览里播放。
  • 受支持的 Agent 会话可以在 Chat UI（对话界面，一份聊天式记录）或原始终端里打开。设备默认在 Settings → Chat UI 里设。长按某个会话标签，只切换那一个标签。
  • 把最近的终端回滚缓冲取过来，这样你能读 Agent 做了什么或问了什么。终端流结束之后，视图会接受一份新的快照，不会停在旧的上面。聊天式视图里的 Markdown 可以渲染消息里的 Mermaid 图。原生对话显示的行内可视内容和子 Agent 行，和桌面相同。
  • 在手机终端视图里选择、复制和粘贴文字：长按选择，用分享页贴回一条回复。
  • 用终端辅助行放手机键盘上别扭的键，包括 Tab、Shift+Tab，以及 Shift、Ctrl 和 Alt 的锁定。可以打开 Smart rail（智能快捷条）做 Agent 动作，或切到 Live（实时），让每个打出来的字符直接进当前终端。
  • Agent 在等输入时，发一条短回复（continue、yes，或自由文本），附上照片或文件，或点麦克风口述。在 Live 模式下，口述的文字插入当前终端，不会替你按 Return。在对话界面里，附上的图片显示为你这条消息上的缩略图，可以带文字发，也可以不带。
  • 从会话标签条运行 Quick Commands（快捷命令）：保存的终端命令或 Agent 提示，和桌面列表同步。
  • 长按会话标签做批量关闭（Close Other Tabs 关闭其他标签、Close Tabs to the Left 关闭左侧标签、Close Tabs to the Right 关闭右侧标签）。未保存的 Markdown 标签和固定的标签会跳过。
  • 需要从手机看响应式页面时，用 Web（网页）或 Mobile（手机）视图打开浏览器会话。你在手机上打开的浏览器标签，桌面最小化或进入屏保时仍继续加载。
  • 为一棵工作树或文件夹工作区打开源代码管理，在手机上查看改过的文件、暂存或取消暂存，并提交。
  • 在外面切换当前 Agent 账号，并看用量和速率限制。Codex 攒到重置额度时，从账号屏幕花掉一次。
  • 从手机创建工作区，来源模式和桌面的 Smart 相同：Smart、GitHub、Linear、GitLab、Branch 和 Name。连着多台桌面时，New Workspace（新建工作区）会先问该由哪台主机创建（只连着一台时跳过选择）。
  • 长按一个工作区，选 Hand over（移交），把它交给一个受管理的 Agent，可以附带一个目标。Orca 会和那个 Agent 开一场新对话。
  • 从手机在一个工作区上启动 Agent。它的标签会立刻出现在桌面上，启动不会把桌面窗口挪到那个工作区。
  • 打开主机卡片的 ⋯ 菜单，做 Edit（编辑）、Connect（连接）、Remove（移除）以及相关动作（长按仍然是快捷方式）。
  • 改已保存主机的显示名或连接地址，不用重新配对（例如桌面在家里的局域网和 Tailscale 之间搬动）。
  • 在主机列表和打开的会话之间用滑动过渡移动。Back（返回）把这一页的主机栈滑回你刚才的地方。
  • 在 iPad 上，主机从一条侧栏始终够得着。你在主机列表和会话之间移动时，对话草稿会留着。
  • Agent 完成时收到推送通知，和桌面通知 对应。应用也会告诉你有更新的手机二进制可以安装，并链到那次发布。

手机应用有意不做完整编辑器。它是你已经在跑的那台桌面的遥控器。

配对

  1. 在桌面上，从账号或状态菜单打开 Orca 的配对流程。Orca 显示一个一次性配对码。
  2. 在手机上打开 Orca 伴侣应用，选 Pair（配对），然后贴上这个码。（也可以从桌面走一条深链，直接进手机的配对页。）
  3. 有 Orca Relay 时优先用它配对。只有 Relay 需要登录。Relay 上的本地网络地址选择器是可选的（想要一条局域网快路径时再展开）。局域网配对仍然需要一个地址。
  4. 这次配对交换为那部手机建立一枚设备令牌。让桌面在你选的那条路径上够得着。没用过的手机二维码 10 分钟后过期。需要时再生成一个。客户端和桌面都已更新时，已配对的设备一旦保存了自己的登录，这个码就失效。已经配对的设备不受影响。

关掉桌面应用会丢掉直连或局域网会话，直到你重新打开。走 Relay 的配对跟着桌面的 Relay 连接走。主机离线时重新打开桌面。

配对之后，手机用桌面的机器名给主机起名，并显示它的操作系统。你可以在桌面的 Settings → Remote Orca Servers 里改机器名。手机重连时会跟上这个变化。

对话界面

对受支持的 Agent，手机可以用 Chat UI 打开会话，不用原始终端。想法和桌面的对话界面 一样：可读的记录、输入框、权限和问题，以及图片附件。

  • 设备默认： 第一次打开时，手机会问这台手机上的会话该怎么打开。以后在 Settings → Chat UI → Open sessions in Chat UI（用对话界面打开会话）里改。你选对话界面之前，默认是终端。
  • 单个会话： 长按会话标签，选 Switch to chat view（切换到对话视图）或 Switch to terminal view（切换到终端视图）。这个覆盖只对那个标签生效，不改设备默认。
  • 输入框： 打一条回复，用 @ 提及工作区文件，用共享目录里按 Agent 区分的 / 斜杠命令（Claude 和 Codex 两套，带说明），附上照片，或口述。附上的图片显示为可以去掉的缩略图，发送时一起走（包括只发图片，以及多张图片）。
  • 模型和会话选项： 输入框显示一行药丸，是当前模型和会话选项。点它来选模型或选项（包括直接选 Codex 模型，以及当前模型支持时按提供方区分的 Fast mode 快速模式），不用掉进原始终端。
  • 桌面仍然拥有 Agent： 对话界面是已配对桌面会话上的一个视图，不是另一个云端 Agent。

在某个 Agent 的 Home（主页）上，Needs you（需要你处理）收集工具批准和访问请求。在那里打开一条请求，看过再回复。在 Agent 的设置里，Tools（工具）让你添加、检查、登录或去掉工具。有些登录会让你把浏览器回调地址贴回 Orca。

桌面够不着时，聊天发送会在手机上排队，重连后按顺序发出。手机重新连接时，最近的聊天消息会在重新打开后出现。对话草稿、附件、未发送的消息、自动化草稿、任务、新建工作区的草稿，以及提交、PR 和审阅文字，都能熬过应用重启。应用被杀掉之后，Orca 也会提议恢复未保存的文件和 Markdown 编辑。

手机口述可以在 Android 上在设备本地跑，iPhone 要 iOS 26 或更高，电脑作为后备。手机、桌面和已配对的网页客户端可以同时口述。一枚浮动的口述药丸让你在应用里换屏幕时继续说。这些功能要同时更新手机应用和桌面。

Quick Commands

从工作树会话里，在会话标签条上打开 Quick Commands（在新建终端旁边）。命令和桌面是同一份已保存列表，双向同步。

  • Terminal Command（终端命令）：在新标签里粘贴或运行一条 shell 命令（可以不按 Enter，方便你先改）。
  • Agent Prompt（Agent 提示）：用保存的提示正文打开一个选定的 Agent。
  • 从这一行的复制控件把命令正文复制到剪贴板（正文为空时禁用）。
  • 命令范围可以是全局，或当前项目。在这张表里添加、编辑和删除。桌面主机执行共享列表的上限。

已配对的桌面太旧时，启动器会禁用，直到你更新桌面。

会话标签

在会话条里长按一个标签，做标签动作，包括 Close（关闭）、Close Other Tabs、Close Tabs to the Left 和 Close Tabs to the Right。批量关闭会跳过有未保存改动的 Markdown 标签（手机关闭时没有保存提示）和固定的标签。能用对话的 Agent 标签，同一次长按表可以在对话界面和终端之间切换。

账号切换

手机应用提供和桌面状态栏相同的账号切换，包括非当前账号的行内用量。提供方报告了会话或每周窗口的重置时间戳时，账号屏幕会显示重置倒计时。在这里切换，就是在已配对的桌面上切换。

当前 Codex 账号攒到了 rate-limit reset（速率限制重置）额度时，账号屏幕会显示还有多少（以及下一次何时过期），并有一个 Use reset（使用重置）动作。确认会为当前 Codex 目标（主机或 WSL 发行版）花掉一次额度，并刷新符合条件的速率限制窗口。手机会记下这次尝试，所以连接不稳之后的重试不会花两次。结果包括成功、没有可重置的、没有剩余额度，或这次重置已经用过。另见 用量和速率限制跟踪。

创建工作区

从手机主屏幕打开 Create Workspace（创建工作区）/ New Workspace（新建工作区），选一种来源模式。Smart 会在受支持的任务提供方和分支里搜索。按提供方区分的模式把创建收窄到 GitHub、Linear、GitLab、一条分支，或一个普通的工作区名字。选好来源之后，用 Advanced（高级）做命名和分支控制，再在已配对的桌面上创建工作区。连着不止一台桌面时，先选主机（选择器显示每个端点，相近的名字也能分清）。没有已连接的桌面时，这个动作保持禁用。

编辑已配对的主机

在主机列表上，点主机卡片的 ⋯ 按钮（或长按卡片），选 Edit host（编辑主机）。

  • Name（名称）：只在这部手机上的显示名。它覆盖桌面的机器名。清掉就再用桌面的名字。
  • Address（地址）：IP、host:port，或 ws:// / wss://。缺端口时保持当前端口（或默认 6768）。改地址只改变这部手机连到哪里，不会重新配对。同一台桌面换了一条够得着的路径时用这个（例如家里的局域网和 Tailscale）。

保存地址变更会重连到新端点。配对令牌不变。

手机上的源代码管理

从工作树会话里，点分支图标打开源代码管理。用它做小的后续：看分支状态，检查改过的路径，暂存或取消暂存文件，暂存集合就绪后提交。浏览分支上的提交，并在 diff 查看器里打开它们改过的文件。在 diff 里可以在文件之间跳、折叠上下文，并在代码旁边读或添加 PR 审阅评论。你也可以解决冲突并完成一次合并。分支已经有 GitHub PR 时，从空的 PR 状态用 Link an existing PR（关联已有 PR）接上它，而不是新建一个。

浏览器链接

在手机的 Settings → Browser → Open links（设置 → 浏览器 → 打开链接）里，为对话和终端输出里的网页链接选择 Ask each time（每次询问）、Orca browser on desktop（桌面上的 Orca 浏览器）或 Phone browser（手机浏览器）。每次询问还会提供 Copy link（复制链接）。桌面开不了浏览器标签时，Orca 会退回手机浏览器。这项共享设置要同时更新手机应用和桌面。

手机正在控制桌面浏览器标签时，在桌面横幅上选 Take back all（全部收回），把控制权拿回来。

手机上的浏览器视图

手机浏览器窗格有一个 Web / Mobile 开关。用 Web 看正常页面，用 Mobile 向已配对的桌面浏览器要一个手机尺寸的响应式视口。

外观和语言

在手机的 Settings 里，Appearance（外观）下选 Light（浅色）、Dark（深色）或 System（跟随系统），Language（语言）下选显示语言。要用最新的手机设置，同时更新 Orca Mobile 和你的桌面或服务器。

终端设置

手机终端有单独的 Terminal settings（终端设置）屏幕（Settings → Terminal）：

  • Text size（文字大小）：50%–200% 的基准缩放，WebView 在适应宽度之上再应用。终端里的双指缩放会吸到同一组预设并保存。这项按设备，不改桌面终端。
  • Autocomplete & autocorrect（自动完成和自动改正）：默认关，这样操作系统不会改写命令栏里的命令、flag 或路径。想要手机式输入时打开。实时的键盘捕获输入无论开关，都按原始按键流出。

键盘盖住页面的方式和原生屏幕一样。页面会报告自己的高度，这样你打字时输入框和当前字段仍然看得见。

排错

  • 工作树上的转圈停住了： 手机跟踪的是桌面 Agent 进程的心跳。桌面说空闲、手机却显示正在做时，强制刷新这一行工作区，把状态同步过来。
  • 配对失败： 确认桌面和手机登录的是同一个 Orca 账号。配对码几分钟就过期。在屏幕上放太久了，就生成一个新的。
  • 认证失败 / 配对无效： 桌面不再接受这枚设备令牌（例如桌面清掉了已配对的设备）。先在横幅上试 Retry（重试）。短暂的拒绝可以恢复，不用重新配对。一直失败的话，从桌面 Re-pair（重新配对），或 Remove（移除）这台主机再配一次。
  • Update Orca Mobile / Update Orca on your computer（更新 Orca Mobile / 在电脑上更新 Orca）：手机和桌面说的是有版本的手机协议。任一边太旧时，手机会挡住那台主机，并把你指到 App Store（iOS）或 GitHub Releases（Android APK 或桌面），然后再回到主机。更新之后刷新连接。提示还在的话，去掉这台主机再配一次。
  • Quick Commands 不可用： 更新已配对的桌面，让它宣告 quick-commands 能力。
  • 够不着桌面： 手机和桌面必须共享一条网络路径（局域网、Tailscale，或你配对时用的那条路径）。关掉桌面应用会丢掉连接。重新打开桌面，手机就会自动重连。

下一步

把手机通知和桌面通知 配在一起，让 Agent 完成的提醒到达对的设备。无界面的机器用远程 Orca 服务器 的手机配对。在桌面和手机上跟踪提供方用量，见 用量和速率限制跟踪。

Pair the Orca mobile app to your desktop to monitor agents and unstick worktrees from your phone.

The Orca mobile companion is an iOS/Android app that pairs with your desktop Orca and lets you monitor and work with agents from your phone: reply to prompts, review source control, and switch agent accounts. The paired desktop owns the agent sessions.

Beta

The mobile companion is in beta. Install iOS from the App Store, and Android from Google Play or the current APK.

If you have the legacy Android app, install the new dev.orca.app separately; it cannot update in place to the new package.

What you can do from mobile

  • Use the Agents tab to see agents across connected desktops, mark them read, or clear them from the list. Activity status comes from each desktop.
  • See every worktree, its agent, and its current status (working / done / waiting on input). Mobile lists worktrees from every host you're connected to (local desktops and remote Orca servers) in a single view, so switching between them doesn't need a device change. A workspace on an SSH host shows that host's health on its row badge — the same wording the desktop uses — so a machine that dropped is visible at a glance.
  • See desktop project groups in the workspace list.
  • Browse the workspace's full file tree from mobile — including deeply nested paths — so you can jump into any file without waiting for the desktop. Video and music files play in previews on the phone.
  • Open supported agent sessions in Chat UI (a chat-style transcript) or the raw terminal. Set the device default under Settings → Chat UI, or long-press a session tab to switch that tab only.
  • Hydrate recent terminal scrollback so you can read what an agent did or asked. After a terminal stream ends, the view accepts a fresh snapshot instead of staying stale. Markdown in chat-style views can render Mermaid diagrams when the message includes them, and native chat shows the same inline visuals and subagent rows as the desktop.
  • Select, copy, and paste text from the mobile terminal view — long-press to select, share-sheet to paste back into a reply.
  • Use the terminal accessory row for keys that are awkward on a phone keyboard, including Tab, Shift+Tab, and Shift, Ctrl, and Alt latches. You can enable the Smart rail for agent actions, or switch to Live when each typed character should go straight to the active terminal.
  • Send a short reply (continue, yes, free-text) when an agent is waiting on input, attach a photo or file, or tap the microphone to dictate a response. In Live mode, dictated text is inserted into the active terminal without pressing Return for you. In Chat UI, attached images show as thumbnails on your message and can be sent with or without text.
  • Run Quick Commands from the session tab strip — saved terminal commands or agent prompts that sync with the desktop list.
  • Long-press a session tab for bulk close actions (Close Other Tabs, Close Tabs to the Left, Close Tabs to the Right). Unsaved markdown tabs and pinned tabs are skipped.
  • Open a browser session in Web or Mobile view when you need to check a responsive page from your phone. A browser tab you open on the phone keeps loading while the desktop is minimized or in its screen saver.
  • Open Source Control for a worktree or folder workspace to review changed files, stage or unstage, and commit from your phone.
  • Switch your active agent account and see usage / rate-limit state on the go. When Codex has earned reset credits, spend one from the accounts screen.
  • Create a workspace from mobile with the same Smart source modes as desktop: Smart, GitHub, Linear, GitLab, Branch, and Name. With multiple connected desktops, New Workspace asks which host should create it first (one connected host skips the picker).
  • Long-press a workspace and choose Hand over to give it to a managed agent, optionally with a goal. Orca opens a new chat with that agent.
  • Start an agent on a workspace from the phone; its tab appears on the desktop right away, and starting it does not move the desktop window to that workspace.
  • Open a host card's ⋯ menu for Edit, Connect, Remove, and related actions (long-press still works as a shortcut).
  • Edit a saved host's display name or connection address without re-pairing (for example when the desktop moves between home LAN and Tailscale).
  • Move between the hosts list and an open session with a slide transition; Back slides the page's host stack back to where you were.
  • On iPad, hosts stay reachable from a single sidebar and chat drafts are kept when you move between the host list and a session.
  • Get push notifications when an agent finishes, mirroring desktop notifications. The app also tells you when a newer mobile binary is available to install and links to that release.

The mobile app is intentionally not a full editor — it's a remote control for the desktop you already have running.

Pairing

  1. On desktop, open Orca's pairing flow from the account / status menu. Orca shows a one-time pairing code.
  2. On mobile, open the Orca companion app and choose Pair, then paste the code. (You can also follow a deep link from the desktop directly into the mobile pairing screen.)
  3. Prefer Orca Relay for pairing when it is available — sign-in is required for Relay only. The local network address picker is optional on Relay (expand it when you want a LAN fast path); LAN pairing still needs an address.
  4. The pairing exchange establishes a device token for that phone; keep desktop reachable on the path you chose. An unused phone QR code expires after 10 minutes; generate a new one if needed. With updated clients and desktop, the code stops working once the paired device saves its own sign-in. Existing paired devices are unaffected.

Closing the desktop app drops a direct/LAN session until you reopen it. Relay-backed pairing follows the desktop Relay connection — reopen desktop if the host goes offline.

After pairing, the phone names the host from the desktop's machine name and shows its operating system. You can change the machine name in desktop Settings → Remote Orca Servers; the phone picks up the change when it reconnects.

Chat UI

For supported agents, mobile can open the session in Chat UI instead of the raw terminal — the same idea as desktop's Chat UI: a readable transcript, composer, permissions/questions, and image attachments.

  • Device default — on first launch, mobile asks how sessions should open on this phone. Change it later under Settings → Chat UI → Open sessions in Chat UI. The default is terminal until you choose Chat UI.
  • Per-session — long-press a session tab and choose Switch to chat view or Switch to terminal view. That override sticks for that tab without changing the device default.
  • Composer — type a reply, @ mention workspace files, use agent-aware / slash commands from the shared catalog (Claude vs Codex sets, with descriptions), attach a photo, or dictate. Attached images appear as removable thumbnails and ride along on send (including image-only sends and multi-image attachments).
  • Model and session options — the composer shows a pill row for the current model and session options. Tap it to pick a model or option (including direct Codex model selection and provider-aware Fast mode when the active model supports it) without dropping into the raw terminal.
  • Desktop still owns the agent — Chat UI is a view over the paired desktop session, not a separate cloud agent.

On an agent’s Home, Needs you collects tool approvals and access requests. Open a request there to review it and respond. In the agent’s settings, Tools lets you add, check, sign in to, or remove tools; some sign-ins ask you to paste the browser callback address back into Orca.

When the desktop is unreachable, chat sends queue on the phone and send in order when it reconnects. Recent chat messages appear after a relaunch while the phone reconnects. Chat drafts, attachments, unsent messages, automation drafts, tasks, new-workspace drafts, and commit, PR, and review text survive an app restart. Orca also offers to restore unsaved file and Markdown edits after the app is killed.

Phone dictation can run on the device on Android and on iPhone with iOS 26 or later, with the computer as fallback. The phone, desktop, and paired web client can dictate at the same time. A floating dictation pill lets you continue speaking as you move to another screen in the app. Update the mobile app as well as the desktop for these features.

Quick Commands

From a worktree session, open Quick Commands from the session tab strip (next to new terminal). Commands are the same saved list as desktop and sync both ways.

  • Terminal Command — paste or run a shell command in a new tab (optionally without pressing Enter so you can edit first).
  • Agent Prompt — open a chosen agent with a saved prompt body.
  • Copy a command body to the clipboard from the row's copy control (disabled when the body is empty).
  • Scope commands globally or to the current project. Add, edit, and delete from the sheet; the desktop host enforces the shared list cap.

If the paired desktop is too old, the launcher is disabled until you update desktop.

Session tabs

Long-press a tab in the session strip for tab actions, including Close, Close Other Tabs, Close Tabs to the Left, and Close Tabs to the Right. Bulk close skips dirty markdown tabs (mobile has no save prompt on close) and pinned tabs. For chat-capable agent tabs, the same long-press sheet can switch Chat UI ↔ terminal.

Account switcher

The mobile app exposes the same account switcher as the desktop status bar, including inline usage for inactive accounts. The accounts screen shows reset countdowns when a provider reports reset timestamps for its session or weekly window. Switching here switches on the paired desktop.

When the active Codex account has earned rate-limit reset credits, the accounts screen shows how many are available (and when the next one expires) and a Use reset action. Confirming spends one credit for the current Codex target (host or WSL distro) and refreshes eligible rate-limit windows. The phone journals the attempt so a retry after a flaky connection does not double-spend. Outcomes include success, nothing eligible to reset, no credit left, or that a reset was already applied. See also Usage & rate-limit tracking.

Create a workspace

From the mobile home screen, open Create Workspace / New Workspace and choose a source mode. Smart searches across supported task providers and branches; the provider-specific modes narrow creation to GitHub, Linear, GitLab, a branch, or a plain workspace name. After selecting a source, use Advanced for naming and branch controls before creating the workspace on the paired desktop. If more than one desktop is connected, pick the host first (the picker shows each endpoint so similar names stay distinguishable). With no connected desktops, the action stays disabled.

Edit a paired host

On the hosts list, tap the host card's ⋯ button (or long-press the card) and choose Edit host.

  • Name — display name on this phone only. This overrides the desktop's machine name; clear it to use the desktop name again.
  • Address — IP, host:port, or ws:// / wss://. Missing port keeps the current port (or defaults to 6768). Address edits only change where this phone connects; they do not re-pair. Use this when the same desktop is reachable at a different path (for example home LAN vs Tailscale).

Saving an address change reconnects to the new endpoint. Pairing token stays the same.

Source control on mobile

From a worktree session, tap the branch icon to open Source Control. Use it for small follow-ups: check the branch state, inspect changed paths, stage or unstage files, and commit once the staged set is ready. Browse branch commits and open their changed files in the diff view. In diffs, jump between files, fold context, and read or add pull-request review comments alongside the code. You can also resolve conflicts and complete a merge. If the branch already has a GitHub pull request, use Link an existing PR from the empty PR state to attach it instead of creating a new one.

Browser links

Under phone Settings → Browser → Open links, choose Ask each time, Orca browser on desktop, or Phone browser for web links in chat and terminal output. Ask each time also offers Copy link. If the desktop cannot open a browser tab, Orca falls back to the phone browser. Update both the phone app and desktop for the shared setting.

When a phone is controlling desktop browser tabs, choose Take back all on the desktop banner to return control.

Browser view on mobile

The mobile browser pane has a Web / Mobile switch. Use Web to see the normal page and Mobile to ask the paired desktop browser for a phone-sized responsive viewport.

Appearance and language

In mobile Settings, choose Light, Dark, or System under Appearance, and choose a display language under Language. Update Orca Mobile as well as your desktop or server to use the latest mobile settings.

Terminal settings

The mobile terminal has a dedicated Terminal settings screen (Settings → Terminal):

  • Text size — a 50%–200% baseline zoom the WebView applies on top of fit-to-width. Pinch-to-zoom in the terminal snaps to the same presets and persists. The setting is per-device and doesn't change the desktop terminal.
  • Autocomplete & autocorrect — off by default so the OS never rewrites commands, flags, or paths in the command bar. Turn it on for phone-style typing; the live keyboard-capture input still streams raw keystrokes either way.

The keyboard covers the page the way it does on a native screen, and the page reports its height so the composer and active field stay visible while you type.

Troubleshooting

  • Stuck spinner on a worktree — the phone tracks a heartbeat from the desktop agent process. If the desktop says idle but the phone shows working, force-refresh the worktree row to sync state.
  • Pairing fails — make sure your desktop and phone are signed into the same Orca account. Pairing codes expire after a few minutes; generate a fresh one if it's been sitting on the screen.
  • Authentication failed / pairing invalid — the desktop no longer accepts this device token (for example after desktop cleared paired devices). Try Retry on the banner first (transient rejections can recover without re-pairing). If it keeps failing, use Re-pair from desktop, or Remove the host and pair again.
  • Update Orca Mobile / Update Orca on your computer — the phone and desktop speak a versioned mobile protocol. If either side is too old, mobile blocks that host and points you at the App Store (iOS), GitHub Releases (Android APK or desktop), then back to hosts. After updating, refresh the connection; if the message stays, remove the host and pair again.
  • Quick Commands unavailable — update the paired desktop so it advertises the quick-commands capability.
  • Can't reach desktop — phone and desktop must share a network path (LAN, Tailscale, or the pairing path you used). Closing the desktop app drops the connection; reopen desktop and the phone reconnects automatically.
Next steps

Pair mobile notifications with desktop notifications so agent-finished alerts reach the right device. For headless machines, use Remote Orca Server mobile pairing. Track provider usage on desktop and mobile under Usage & rate-limit tracking.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/mobile

上一章Orca skills registry & MCP 下一章Install Orca Mobile on Android

Mobile

在 Android 上安装 Orca Mobile

Install Orca Mobile on Android

从 Google Play 安装最省事。Google Play 用不了时，按这一页直接装 APK。

Orca Mobile 在 Google Play 上，安装和更新都从那里走最简单。这一页是直接装 APK 的做法。

安装 Orca Mobile

  1. 用 Chrome 或其他完整浏览器打开 Android APK 下载。Orca 下载页 上也能找到。
  2. 下载完成后，打开浏览器的 Downloads（下载），或设备上的 Files → Downloads（文件 → 下载），点 app-release.apk。
  3. 按 Android 的安装提示做。如果系统询问，只允许这个浏览器或 Files 应用安装未知应用。Orca 装好后，把这项权限关掉。

排错

下载没开始，或一直下不完

  1. 链接如果开在 Discord、GitHub 或其他应用里面，选 Open in browser（在浏览器中打开）或 Open in Chrome（在 Chrome 中打开），也可以把链接复制到完整浏览器里。
  2. 重新打开 Android 下载，等到它下完。

Samsung Galaxy：Auto Blocker

Galaxy 提示 Auto Blocker（自动拦截）拦住了安装时，打开 Settings → Security and privacy → Auto Blocker（设置 → 安全和隐私 → 自动拦截），先暂时关掉，装上 Orca，再打开。不要关 Play Protect。

Install Orca Mobile on Android from Google Play or the APK.

Orca Mobile is on Google Play, which is the simplest way to install and update it. Use this guide to install the APK directly instead, for example where Google Play is not available.

Install Orca Mobile

  1. Open the Android APK download in Chrome or another full browser. You can also find it on the Orca download page.
  2. When the download finishes, open your browser's Downloads or your device's Files → Downloads, then tap app-release.apk.
  3. Follow Android's installation prompt. If asked, allow only the browser or Files app to install unknown apps, then turn that permission off after Orca is installed.

Troubleshooting

The download never starts or never finishes

  1. If the link opened inside Discord, GitHub, or another app, choose Open in browser / Open in Chrome, or copy it into a full browser.
  2. Start the Android download again and wait for it to finish.

Samsung Galaxy: Auto Blocker

If your Samsung Galaxy says Auto Blocker prevented the install, open Settings → Security and privacy → Auto Blocker, temporarily turn it off, install Orca, then turn it back on. Do not disable Play Protect.
