上一章Mobile companion 下一章Notifications & Inbox

Notifications & Inbox

通知与收件箱

Notifications & Inbox

Orca 跑的是 Agent，不只是终端，所以它分得清 Agent 是做完了，还是只是停了一下。通知用的就是这个信号。你可以排上三个 Agent，走开，等第一个做完再回来。

Agent 做完时的提醒

Agent 从工作中变成空闲时，Orca 会发通知：系统通知、声音，以及工作树上的一枚标记。一个 Agent 刚结束、下一个马上结束时，完成音仍会响，不会被吞掉。

对更新后的结构化对话，成功的一轮只把对应标签和工作区标成未读。它不发系统通知，也不响。失败或取消的一轮不加未读标记。

顶栏铃铛

顶栏铃铛显示所有工作树的未读通知。点一条通知，会跳到对应的工作树和窗格。

在 macOS 上，同一个未读数会出现在 Dock 图标的角标上。Orca 不必放到前台，也能看到还有 Agent 在等你。进入那个工作区后，角标会清掉。

标回未读

右键一条通知，把它标回未读。已经分拣过、还想稍后回来看时用这个。

调整

在 Settings → Notifications 里，可以分别关掉系统通知、声音，以及工作树上的完成标记。

按工作区静音

右键一个工作区，把它的通知静音，少被打断。这个工作区仍会显示未读点。

按机器

在 Settings → Notifications → Machines 里，选择哪些机器可以在这台电脑上显示通知：这台电脑、SSH 目标，以及已配对的 Orca 服务器。已配对服务器的开关，也覆盖你经它触达的工作。静音的机器记在一份退出名单里，以后新加的机器仍会通知。这些开关只影响这台电脑上的桌面通知，手机推送不变。

要改一台已配对服务器自己发出的提醒（包括手机推送），在设置的主机选择器里选那台服务器，再打开 Notifications。可以改它的总通知、Agent 完成、Agent 等待输入，以及终端铃。这些控件不可用时，先更新那台较旧的服务器。服务器上的这些设置，和桌面上的按机器开关是分开的。

自定义声音

在 Settings → Notifications 里，每个类别可以指定自己的桌面通知声音。指到磁盘上的任意音频文件，或从 Orca 自带的一组里选。适合让 Agent 做完的声音和系统邮件、Slack 区分开。

支持的格式：MP3、WAV、OGG、M4A、AAC、FLAC。选定的那一个文件会用于所有实际送出的桌面通知。

用了自定义声音后，在同一设置页里调它的播放音量。

Orca runs agents, not just terminals, so it knows when an agent actually finishes versus when it's just paused. Notifications lean on that signal so you can queue three agents, walk away, and come back when the first one is done.

Agent-finished pings

When an agent transitions from working to idle, Orca fires a notification — system notification, sound, and a chip on the worktree. A completion sound still plays when one agent finishes right after another instead of being dropped.

For an updated structured chat, a successful turn instead marks its tab and workspace unread. It does not send a system notification or play a sound. Failed or canceled turns do not add an unread mark.

Persistent bell

The header bell shows unread notifications across all worktrees. Clicking a notification jumps to the matching worktree and pane.

On macOS, the same unread count is mirrored as a badge on the Dock icon, so you can spot pending agent pings without bringing Orca to the foreground. The badge clears when you visit the workspace.

Mark unread

Right-click a notification to mark unread — useful when you've triaged something but want to come back to it later.

Tuning

Turn off specific categories (system, sound, chip-only) under Settings → Notifications.

Per-workspace mute

Right-click a workspace and mute its notifications when you want fewer interruptions. The workspace still shows its unread dot.

Per-machine

Under Settings → Notifications → Machines, choose which machines can show notifications on this computer — this computer, SSH targets, and paired Orca Servers. A paired server's switch also covers the work you reach through it. Muted machines are stored as an opt-out list, so a machine you add later still notifies. These switches only affect desktop notifications on this computer; phone push is unchanged.

To change the alerts a paired server itself sends, including phone pushes, select that server in the Settings host picker and open Notifications. You can change its overall notifications, agent completion, agent input, and terminal bell switches. Update an older server if those controls are unavailable. These server settings are separate from the per-machine switches on your desktop.

Custom sounds

Pick a custom desktop notification sound per category under Settings → Notifications — point at any audio file on disk, or pick from Orca's built-in set. Useful when you want agent-finished pings to stand out from system mail and Slack.

Supported formats: MP3, WAV, OGG, M4A, AAC, FLAC. One file applies to all delivered desktop notifications.

When you use a custom sound, set its playback volume from the same settings pane.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/notifications

上一章Install Orca Mobile on Android 下一章Agents feed

Notifications & Inbox

Agent 动态

Agents feed

侧栏的 Agents 把每棵工作树里的 Agent 事件收成一条按时间排列的动态：做完、被问题挡住、未读，以及新建工作树。

它和按工作树分开的通知配合使用。通知是分散的，这里是一处按时间分拣的表面。

这个入口默认打开。自你上次来过之后有新事件时，它显示未读角标。点一条，会跳到对应的工作树和窗格，和点系统通知是同一次跳转。

macOS 用 Cmd+F，Windows 和 Linux 用 Ctrl+F，把焦点放到动态的过滤器上。焦点已经在内嵌终端里时，同一快捷键留给终端。

动态标题反映当前过滤器。右键一行 Agent 可以看到它的操作。按住 Cmd（Windows / Linux 用 Ctrl）可以多选几行，一次对它们做同一个操作。对行做了操作之后，你仍留在这份动态里。

会出现什么

动态里的每一条是下面之一：

  • Agent 结束一轮：变成空闲，或卡在一个问题上。
  • 新建了一棵工作树。
  • Agent 等输入等得够久，被标成阻塞。
  • Agent 最近一条回复的短预览。扫动态时不必打开每一条线程。

线程按状态分组。正在跑的 Agent 固定在顶部，一眼能看到还在工作的那些。

和通知怎么配合

顶栏铃铛和系统通知仍会在 Agent 做完时发出。这份动态用来补看你离开 Orca 之后发生了什么：不必逐棵工作树去翻，扫一遍这里即可。

下一步

  • 通知与收件箱：系统提醒、顶栏铃铛，以及按类别调整。
  • Agent 与会话：这份动态所依据的工作中、空闲、阻塞，Orca 是怎么跟踪的。

Threaded feed of agent completions, blocking states, response previews, and worktree creation events across every worktree.

The Agents entry in the sidebar opens a threaded feed of agent events across every worktree — completions, blocking questions, unread state, and worktree creation. It complements the per-worktree notifications view by giving you one chronological surface to triage from.

The entry is on by default. It shows an unread badge when new events have arrived since you last visited; clicking an entry jumps you to the matching worktree and pane — the same handoff Orca does from a system notification.

Use Cmd+F on macOS or Ctrl+F on Windows and Linux to focus the feed filter. When the embedded terminal has focus, the same shortcut stays with the terminal instead.

The feed header reflects the active filter. Right-click an agent row for its actions, and hold Cmd (Ctrl on Windows / Linux) to select several rows and apply one action to all of them at once; acting on rows keeps you in the activity view.

What shows up

Each entry in the feed is one of:

  • An agent finishing a turn, either idle or blocked on a question.
  • A new worktree being created.
  • An agent that's been waiting on input long enough to surface as blocking.
  • A short preview of the agent's most recent response, so you can skim the feed without opening every thread.

Threads are grouped by status — running agents stay pinned at the top so you can spot the ones still working at a glance.

Interplay with notifications

The Agents feed doesn't replace the header bell or system notifications — those still fire on agent-finished pings. The feed is the catch-up surface when you've been away from Orca: instead of scrolling each worktree to find what changed, scan it.

Next steps

  • Notifications & Inbox — system pings, the header bell, and per-category tuning.
  • Agents & sessions — how Orca tracks the working/idle/blocked transitions this feed surfaces.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/activity

上一章Notifications & Inbox 下一章Race three agents on the same task

Notifications & Inbox · Recipes

三个 Agent 抢同一件事

Race three agents on the same task

同一件事交给多个 Agent 同时做，是 Orca 最值得用的做法。同一段提示，三条分支，留下更好的那份。

步骤

  1. 从同一条起始引用建三棵工作树。名字用 fix-bug、fix-bug-2、fix-bug-3。
  2. 每棵里启动一个不同的 Agent：Claude Code、Codex、Cursor CLI。
  3. 把同一段提示贴进三个里面。
  4. 把窗格分成能同时看见的样子：把标签拖到边缘。
  5. 它们停下来之后，分别看 diff。对要留下的那份用 批注 AI diff。
  6. 在胜出的那棵工作树上提交、推送、开 PR。
  7. 删掉另外两份。点一次，工作树和分支一起去掉。

为什么有效

不同的 Agent 会犯不同的错。同一件事并行做，比一次次重试更省，分歧本身也是信号。三方一致的地方，多半是对的。分开的地方，就是难的那一块。

Running the same task across multiple agents in parallel is Orca's killer move. Same prompt, three branches, pick the winner.

Steps

  1. Create three worktrees from the same start-from ref. Name them fix-bug, fix-bug-2, fix-bug-3.
  2. Launch a different agent in each — Claude Code, Codex, Cursor CLI.
  3. Paste the same prompt into all three.
  4. Split the panes so you can see them work — drag tabs to edges.
  5. When they finish, review each diff. Use Annotate AI Diff on the winner.
  6. Commit, push, open PR from the winning worktree.
  7. Delete the two losers — one click removes the worktree and branch.

Why it works

Different agents make different mistakes. Running the same task in parallel is cheaper than sequential retries and surfaces disagreement as a signal. Where three agents agree, the answer is probably right. Where they split, you've found the hard part.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/recipes/parallel-agents

上一章Agents feed 下一章Review an AI diff line-by-line

Notifications & Inbox · Recipes

逐行审阅 AI diff

Review an AI diff line-by-line

AI 的 diff 看得仔细，快和出错才分得开。按下面这个循环做。

步骤

  1. 打开这棵工作树的 diff 查看器。
  2. 用 j / k 一个文件一个文件看。每一段 diff 块都问：这处改动有必要吗？够小吗？和文件其余部分一致吗？
  3. 想改的地方按 c 留下评论。完整的句子最好用。
  4. 整份 diff 看完后，点 Send to agent（发给 Agent）。Orca 会把所有评论收成一段提示。
  5. 看 Agent 修改。状态点会变成黄色（还在等更多输入）或绿色（正在做）。
  6. 它空下来之后，重新打开 diff。你的评论还钉在原地。已经修好的标成解决，其余的再追一条。
  7. 重复到干净，再提交。

Reviewing an AI diff well is the difference between shipping fast and shipping bugs. Here's the loop.

Steps

  1. Open the worktree's diff view.
  2. Go file-by-file with j / k. For each hunk, ask: is the change necessary? is it minimal? does it match the rest of the file?
  3. Drop comments with c on anything you want changed — full sentences work best.
  4. When you've been through the whole diff, click Send to agent. Orca batches all comments into one prompt.
  5. Watch the agent revise. The state dot will go yellow (waiting for more input) or green (working).
  6. When it's idle, re-open the diff. Your comments are pinned; resolve the ones that are fixed and leave follow-ups on the rest.
  7. Repeat until clean, then commit.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/recipes/review-ai-diff

上一章Race three agents on the same task 下一章Jump between 10 worktrees

Notifications & Inbox · Recipes

在十棵工作树之间跳转

Jump between 10 worktrees

十棵工作树要来回找。工作树跳转面板、Restart 提示和 Agent 状态点就是为这个规模准备的。

步骤

  1. Cmd-J 打开工作树跳转面板。输入任务名的一段。Enter 跳过去；Shift-Enter 在分屏里打开。列表很长时，按 Tab 按主机或项目过滤。
  2. 扫一眼侧栏。有 Agent 在跑的工作树带绿点。先去等输入的那些（黄点）。
  3. 在每棵工作树里，Restart（重新启动）提示会把已经退出的 Agent 再拉起来。笔记本睡醒后要成批恢复时很好用。
  4. 用常驻铃 清掉「Agent 已完成」那一串。点一条通知，就会跳到那棵工作树。

保持干净

合并过的工作树及时删。Orca 把这件事做得很便宜：点一次，工作树和分支都没了。留下几十棵已合并的，跳转面板会变慢。

Ten worktrees is a lot to juggle. The Jump Palette + Restart chip + agent state dots are built for this exact scale.

Steps

  1. Cmd-J opens the jump palette. Type a fragment of the task name. Enter jumps; Shift-Enter opens in a split. Press Tab to filter by host or project when the list is large.
  2. Scan the sidebar — worktrees with active agents have a green dot. Go to the ones that need input (yellow) first.
  3. In each worktree, the Restart chip relaunches any agent that exited. Great for mass-resuming after a laptop sleep.
  4. Use the persistent bell to drain the "agent finished" queue — click a notification, it jumps you to the worktree.

Hygiene

Delete merged worktrees aggressively. Orca makes this cheap — one click, worktree and branch both gone. Leaving dozens of merged worktrees around just slows down the palette.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/recipes/jump-worktrees

上一章Review an AI diff line-by-line 下一章Fix a UI bug with Design Mode

Notifications & Inbox · Recipes

用设计模式修界面问题

Fix a UI bug with Design Mode

设计模式把「这个按钮看起来不对」到「提交修好」收成不到一分钟。

步骤

  1. 打开这棵工作树的浏览器窗格，进到有问题的那一页。
  2. 打开设计模式。
  3. 点那个坏掉的元素。它会作为一条带内容的附件进到 Agent 对话里。
  4. 写下要改什么：「这里内边距太紧，加大到和上面的卡片一样。」
  5. Agent 会改源码。热重载会刷新浏览器。
  6. 再点一次这个元素核对。还不对就再来一轮。
  7. 对了就提交。

为什么快

不用截图，不用在 DOM 里找，也不用复制选择器。Agent 拿到的是 HTML、计算后的 CSS，以及你指的那个元素的裁切图。这和人审阅时想要的上下文是同一份。

Design Mode collapses the "that button looks wrong" → "fixed commit" loop to under a minute.

Steps

  1. Open the worktree's browser pane. Navigate to the page with the bug.
  2. Toggle Design Mode on.
  3. Click the broken element. It lands in the agent chat as a rich attachment.
  4. Type what you want fixed: "this padding is too tight, increase to match the cards above."
  5. The agent edits the source. Hot reload refreshes the browser.
  6. Click the element again to verify — if still wrong, repeat.
  7. When it's right, commit.

Why it's fast

No screenshot, no DOM hunting, no selector copying. The agent gets the HTML, computed CSS, and a cropped image of the exact element you pointed at — the same context a human reviewer would want.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/recipes/design-mode-fix

上一章Jump between 10 worktrees 下一章Work on a remote machine over SSH

Notifications & Inbox · Recipes

通过 SSH 在远程机器上干活

Work on a remote machine over SSH

把 Orca 指到任意 SSH 目标：更强的开发机、GPU 主机，或带 SSH 的云沙箱。用起来像一棵本机工作树。编辑器、diff 查看器和 Agent 都一样，算力换了一台。远程仓库可以开，任意远程文件夹也可以开。本机、SSH、服务器、一次性虚拟机怎么选，见 Orca 的几种运行方式。

准备

  1. 在 Settings → SSH 里加上这台主机。
  2. 测一下连接。要在仓库里干活，先确认主机上有 git。
  3. 把仓库加进 Orca，位置选这个 SSH 目标。也可以从文件选择器直接打开任意远程文件夹。

开跑

  1. 建一棵工作树。Orca 会在远程执行 git worktree add。
  2. 启动一个 Agent。它跑在远程主机上，不在你的笔记本上。
  3. 就地编辑文件。Orca 会把保存传到远程文件系统。
  4. 在笔记本上照常看 diff、提交、推送。

断开之后

笔记本睡了，或 Wi-Fi 断了，Agent 仍在远程继续跑。Orca 会重连，并把终端重新接上。已经做的不会丢。

Point Orca at any SSH target — a beefier dev box, a GPU host, a cloud sandbox — and it feels like a local worktree. Same editor, same diff view, same agents, different compute. You can open remote repos or just arbitrary folders. For the full menu of local / SSH / server / ephemeral-VM modes, see Ways to run Orca.

Setup

  1. Add the host under Settings → SSH.
  2. Test the connection. Make sure git is installed on the host if you're working with a repo.
  3. Add the repo to Orca, selecting the SSH target as its location — or open any remote folder directly from the file picker.

Run

  1. Create a worktree. Orca runs git worktree add on the remote.
  2. Launch an agent — it runs on the remote host, not your laptop.
  3. Edit files inline — Orca streams saves to the remote filesystem.
  4. Review the diff, commit, and push from your laptop as usual.

Disconnects

Laptop sleeps, Wi-Fi drops — the agent keeps running remotely. Orca reconnects and re-attaches the terminal. Nothing lost.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/recipes/remote-worktrees

上一章Fix a UI bug with Design Mode 下一章Settings reference

Settings Reference

设置参考

Settings reference

设置按页分组。按 Cmd-, 再输入关键词，可以搜到这里的每一项。文档里的功能页会直接链到对应的设置页。

General（通用）

  • Orca CLI： 为 shell 和 Agent 登记随应用附带的命令行。
  • Automatic updates（自动更新）：在 General → About 下打开。打开后会自己下载、安装并重新打开。默认关闭，并按你的检查频率、通道和延迟来做。见 安装 → 更新。
  • Updates（更新）：检查并安装更新。Update Delay（更新延迟）在发布之后等待 None（默认）、1、3、7、14 或 30 天，再提供这次更新。被按住的更新会显示它何时可用。选 Skip Delay Once（这次跳过延迟）可以不等待就检查。延迟是 14 或 30 天时，一次发布可能在变得符合条件之前就离开了更新源。对 Check for Updates（检查更新）按住修饰键再点：
修饰键 效果
Shift+click 把最新的 RC 预发布版也纳入
Cmd+click（macOS）/ Ctrl+click（Windows / Linux） 最新带 perf 标签的预发布版
Option+click（仅 macOS） 选一个已通过兼容性检查的本地 macOS 构建。失败时显示 “Could Not Use Local Build”，并带 Choose Another Build（另选一个构建）
  • Open in menu（Open in 菜单）：选择出现在工作树 Open in（用外部应用打开）菜单里的应用。VS Code / Insiders 会为 SSH 工作树启用 Remote SSH 打开。其他编辑器仍只打开本地路径。
  • UI zoom（界面缩放）：这一次安装的界面比例。
  • Default new-worktree name（新工作树的默认名）：自定义前缀，或海洋生物。
  • Format on Save（保存时格式化）：默认关闭。打开后，为每个项目配置格式化器和包含模式。见 Monaco 编辑器 → 保存时格式化。
  • Default Markdown view（Markdown 的默认视图）：选择 Markdown 文件怎么打开。见富文本 Markdown 编辑器。
  • Insert Final Newline（插入末尾换行）：保存文件时可以补上最后一行换行。
  • Editor Word Wrap（编辑器自动换行）：文件编辑器的默认换行，默认打开。从文件标签的 ⋯ 菜单或 Alt+Z 切换。和 Diff Word Wrap（diff 自动换行）是分开的。
  • Reuse a preview tab when browsing files（浏览文件时复用预览标签）：默认打开。关掉后，单击打开的每个文件用自己的标签，不再替换当前预览标签。
  • Collapse Unchanged Regions（折叠未改区域）：在单文件 diff 里折起大段未改内容，周围仍留一点上下文。需要整份文件时展开那一段。View all changes（查看全部改动）保持它自己的折叠布局。

Appearance（外观）

  • 主题、强调色、密度。主题选择器可以按名字搜索。已安装的插件可以把应用主题和终端主题加进选择器。一个应用主题也可以带上它的终端配色。
  • 界面字体。编辑器字体要另行打开（空着表示跟随终端字体；填了值，只覆盖文件编辑器和 diff）。
  • 编辑器缩略图开关。
  • 状态栏开关，包括 Resource Manager（资源管理器）：CPU、内存、会话、守护进程控件、工作区磁盘扫描。项目在已配对的 Orca 服务器上时，它会在服务器名下列出那台服务器的进程。
  • Usage percentages（用量百分比）：状态栏名册上，把提供方限额显示成 % used（已用百分比）或 % remaining（剩余百分比）。名册现在默认用更短的 Compact（紧凑）视图，除非你已经选过一种。同一选择器里仍有 Detailed（详细）。
  • App Icon（应用图标）：在 Classic、Watercolor 和 Blue 之间切换 Dock 和窗口切换器里的图标。
  • Language（语言）：主要的界面控件。在 System（跟随系统）、English、中文（简体）、繁體中文、한국어、日本語、Español、Deutsch、Русский、Türkçe 之间切换。设置搜索也会匹配 “language” 的各语言写法（语言 / 語言 / 언어 / 言語 / Idioma），界面仍是英文时也能找到 Language。

Chat（对话）

在这里，或在 Agents 下，选择默认的 Chat 或 CLI 启动表面，以及按 Agent 的覆盖。首次引导时预选 Chat。已有的启动偏好会保留。见对话界面。

  • Behavior（行为）：后续消息是否排队、发送时是否移动滚动位置、空闲的对话 Agent 何时停下、重启后正在工作的对话是否继续，以及碰到用量限制的对话在限额重置后是否自动继续。新会话的权限默认在 Agents 里设置。已有的对话保留它自己的模式。
  • Agents： 为每个受支持的 Agent 选择 Chat 或 CLI。选择作用于从这台桌面发起的启动，包括 CLI、编排，以及经中继的手机启动。Antigravity 的对话运行时没装时，它的那一行提供 Install（安装）。Remove（移除）会停掉它正在跑的对话并去掉运行时，聊天历史保留。
  • Appearance（外观）：选择对话的 Font family（字体，带预览，并从已安装字体给建议）、以像素计的 Text size（文字大小）和 Code text size（代码文字大小）、Width（宽度）和 Contrast（对比度）。打开 Match terminal interface（匹配终端界面），整段对话使用终端的字体和颜色，以后改了终端主题也会跟着变。这项开着时，字体和大小控件不可用。Reset chat appearance（重置对话外观）恢复默认。
  • Inline visuals（内嵌画面）：让 Agent 在回复里显示图表、示意图和草图。作用于新开始的对话。
  • Chat naming（对话命名）：新对话默认用它自己的 Agent 和账号来生成名字。在 Advanced（高级）里可以换一个命名用的 Agent。以前保存的命名命令和参数仍然有效。

Git

  • 默认的基准引用解析方式。
  • 提交签名选项。
  • 外部 git 工具用的编辑器。
  • Auto-Rename Branch From Work（按工作自动重命名分支）：Agent 开始工作后，重命名 Orca 生成的海洋生物分支。
  • GitHub API Budget（GitHub API 配额）：来自本机 gh CLI 的剩余 REST（core）、Search 和 GraphQL 配额。PR 检查或 Tasks 不再刷新时用得上。数字看着正常、GitHub 仍在限制实时调用时，见排查 GitHub 错误。

Terminal（终端）

  • 字体、主题、光标样式、内边距。
  • Ghostty 导入。
  • Warp 主题导入：用 Import themes from Warp（从 Warp 导入主题）带入 Warp 的 YAML 主题（按系统自动找到 Warp 的主题文件夹），或用 Import from YAML（从 YAML 导入）导入任意一文件夹的 Warp 格式主题文件。
  • JIS Yen (¥) to Backslash ()：给 macOS 日文键盘，把日元符号换成反斜杠。
  • Default shell（默认 shell）：新的本机终端标签用哪个 shell。留空则用系统默认。Windows 上在可用时还提供 PowerShell、Command Prompt 和 WSL。
  • Shell arguments（shell 参数）：给自定义的 Unix 默认 shell，在 Default shell → Advanced 下。选 -l (default) 作为登录 shell，或选 Custom args（自定义参数），一行一个参数。自定义列表为空时，不传参数。
  • Running terminal limit（正在运行的终端上限）：在 Advanced 下按电脑设置。默认是系统终端上限的一半，最多 4096。Orca 读不到系统上限时是 256。改它不会关掉正在跑的终端。更新之后，新开一个终端才会用上新的终端宿主。较旧的远程服务器要先更新，才能改它们的上限。
  • Allow TUI Clipboard Writes (OSC 52)（允许 TUI 写剪贴板）：默认打开。让 Zellij、tmux、Neovim、fzf、Grok（以及同类工具）经 PTY 写系统剪贴板，包括经 SSH。想要以前那种锁定，就关掉。

Quick Commands（快速命令）

  • 保存的终端命令和 Agent 提示预设，范围是全局或某个项目。每一行有一个复制控件，用来复制命令正文。
  • 查看和编辑命令列表时的范围过滤。同一份列表会同步到手机端。
  • 有远程或多主机时，命令按拥有它们的 Orca 主机分组（Saved on（保存在））。主机归属和 Global / Project 范围是分开的，也和命令在哪里运行是分开的。见 终端 → 快速命令。

Agents

  • Installed agents（已安装的 Agent）：检测到的 CLI，可以启用或停用。
  • 按 Agent 的启动默认：覆盖某个 Agent 的二进制路径或名字，并编辑它默认的 Command（命令）和 Arguments（参数），以及环境。结构化的原生对话使用同一套保存的命令和参数。这些启动设置在工作区自己的主机上编辑，所以远程或 SSH 工作区用的是跑着它的那台机器上的设置。
  • 启用或停用检测到的 Agent，让启动菜单只显示你要用的 CLI。
  • Agent permissions（Agent 权限）：为新的终端会话和结构化对话选择 Manual、Auto 或 Yolo。Auto 让受支持的 Agent（Claude、Claude Agent Teams 和 Codex）自己批准日常动作。选了它之后，用 Other agents（其他 Agent）给不支持 Auto 的 Agent 设 Manual 或 Yolo。已有的对话保留当前模式。带自定义启动参数的 Agent 保持不变，直到你用一套预设替换那些参数。
  • Folder trust（文件夹信任）：第一次在一个新仓库里启动 Agent 时，选择 Orca 是否为 Agent 信任这个仓库，或让每个 Agent 自己问。你已经在用的仓库保持以前的行为。可以在仓库设置里改这个选择。见 Orca 里的 Codex 和支持的 Agent。
  • Share Codex history across accounts（在账号之间共享 Codex 历史）：默认打开。切回系统默认时，Orca 管理的账号里的历史也会出现在 ~/.codex。账号在下一次启动前会迁到共享的对话存储，第一次启动可能因此变慢。正忙的账号稍后迁移。关掉共享后，已经共享的历史留在原地。共享开着时开始的长对话，要重新打开这项才能继续。较旧的 SSH 或 WSL 主机要先更新，这项才会在那里可用。
  • Run each Codex terminal on its own server（每个 Codex 终端使用自己的服务）：默认打开。让 Orca 对 Codex 标签的状态和关标签保持正确。关掉则使用 Codex 的共享服务和它的 Agent 总览。作用于新终端。你自己启动的 Codex 共用一份服务时，会附带一条 Warn when a Codex tab shares a server（Codex 标签共用服务时警告）。见 Orca 里的 Codex。
  • Claude、Codex、OpenCode、Devin、Antigravity 和 GLM Coding Plan 的账号列表。在 Linux 上，没能加密的已存凭据会在这里标出警告。每个 Claude 账号有自己的配置文件夹。添加时，如果你的默认浏览器已经登录了另一个 Claude 邮箱，会提供 Copy sign-in link instead（改为复制登录链接）。Antigravity 账号行也显示它的对话运行时的安装状态，并让你在那台主机上安装或移除。
  • OpenCode Go 的会话 cookie：粘贴完整的 opencode.ai Cookie 头，包括 __Host-console_session，用来显示 Go 的速率限制。只有 auth cookie 不够。
  • 每个 Agent 的启动钩子。
  • Agent status hooks（Agent 状态钩子）：在 Orca 里显示工作中 / 等待 / 完成。切换不用重启应用就生效，包括 Windows 上的 WSL 钩子中继。CLI：orca agent hooks on|off|status。
  • Keep computer awake（保持电脑醒着）：On（一直醒着）、Agent（有 Agent 在工作时醒着）或 Off（关闭）。桌面状态栏上的同一控件叫 Caffeinate（咖啡杯图标）。在已配对的网页客户端上隐藏。
  • Skill freshness（技能是否需要更新）：Agents 页和技能卡片仍显示完整状态。侧栏导航只给需要处理的技能加角标（Update available（有更新）、Needs attention（需要留意）/ 复核）。健康、加载中，以及可选但未安装的行保持安静。在对话框里用 Update（更新），在后台刷新全局技能，不用终端。进度出现在状态栏。关掉对话框不会取消这次运行。见 Orca 技能。

Browser（浏览器）

  • 配置文件，见浏览器配置文件。
  • Password manager（密码管理器）：启用已保存的登录，管理或导入它们。见密码管理器。
  • Default Zoom（默认缩放）：新打开的浏览器标签用的缩放级别。标签上用 Cmd 加滚轮做的调整会另外记住。
  • 设计模式的默认值。
  • 开发者工具要另行打开。
  • Remote server workspaces（远程服务器工作区）：选 This device（这台设备），在这台桌面上渲染新的已配对运行时浏览器页面；或选 Server (streamed)（服务器，串流），在服务器上渲染。流量始终经过远程服务器。这个选择只作用于新页面。见工作树浏览器 → 远程工作区。
  • Browse through SSH workspace hosts（经 SSH 工作区主机浏览）：把浏览器流量和 DNS 送到每个工作区的 SSH 主机。关掉则从这台设备浏览。
  • Link Routing（链接路由）：把终端、Markdown 和编辑器里的 http(s) 链接在 Orca 的浏览器或系统浏览器里打开。嵌套的 Hold Shift…（按住 Shift……）把这个默认对这一次点击反过来（⇧⌘-click / Shift+Ctrl+click）。见工作树浏览器。
  • Terminal URL clicks（终端里点击 URL）：选择普通的 URL 点击是打开操作菜单、直接打开，还是需要修饰键。中键点击 URL 的行为另外配置。
  • Show terminal link actions（显示终端链接操作）：默认打开。在终端链接上普通点击，打开一个紧凑的操作浮层。关掉后，需要 ⌘-click / Ctrl-click。见 终端 → 链接操作。
  • Default Search Engine（默认搜索引擎）：在浏览器地址栏或新标签输入框里输入非 URL 文字时使用。

Artifacts（制品）

  • Orca account（Orca 账号）：登录（和 Orca Relay 是同一套账号）后，才能发布和管理分享出去的制品。
  • Allow publishing public artifact links（允许发布公开制品链接）：默认关闭。这是整台设备的总开关。打开后，你、Agent 和这台机器上的 orca CLI 可以上传最大 10 MiB 的 HTML / Markdown，并生成公开查看链接。关掉不会删除已有链接。
  • Show Artifacts（显示制品）/ 侧栏快捷方式：打开制品列表，搜索、预览、复制或删除账号拥有的链接。
  • Ask Before Deleting Artifacts（删除制品前询问）：断开一条公开链接之前，可以要求确认。
  • 从打开的本地 HTML 页面或 Markdown 编辑器用 Share as artifact（作为制品分享），或用 orca artifacts …。见 CLI 参考 → 制品。

Integrations（集成）

  • GitHub OAuth。
  • Linear API 令牌。
  • Jira：Cloud（邮箱 + API 令牌），或自建的 Server / Data Center（PAT 或用户名 / 密码）。见 Jira 议题抽屉。
  • Bitbucket Cloud：用 Email & API token（邮箱和 API 令牌，默认）或 Access token（访问令牌）点 Connect（连接）。Orca 在保存前验证凭据。ORCA_BITBUCKET_* 环境变量优先，并藏起 Connect / Disconnect。保存的凭据留在这台机器上。在远程 Orca 服务器上，把环境变量设在服务器上。见托管评审。
  • MiniMax：粘贴 MiniMax 的会话 cookie（来自 platform.minimax.io/console/usage），为 MiniMax CLI 开启本机用量和速率限制跟踪。可选的 group ID 和用量模型字段，会覆盖从 cookie 里取出的默认值。
  • MCP 服务器。仓库的 MCP 设置留在你选定它们的那台主机上。

Notifications（通知）

  • 在设置的主机选择器里选一台已配对的服务器，查看并修改它为自己的 Agent 和终端发出的通知，包括手机推送。较旧的服务器要先更新，才能编辑这些开关。本机的声音和按机器的控件作用于这台电脑。见通知与收件箱。
  • Agent 完成：系统通知、声音、工作树上的标记。
  • 每个类别的自定义桌面通知声音。
  • Machines（机器）：选择哪些机器可以在这台电脑上显示通知（这台电脑、SSH 目标、已配对的 Orca 服务器）。已配对服务器的开关也覆盖经它触达的工作。新加的机器仍会通知。见通知与收件箱。
  • PR 检查失败。
  • 有可用更新。

Voice（语音）

  • Set up（设置）：没有选中可用模型时，Voice 会为你的语言建议一个本机模型。设置会选中并下载它，请求麦克风权限，并启用听写。也可以从列表里另选模型。已经下载的本机模型可能需要更新。依赖离线听写之前，从 Voice 设置或听写条开始这次更新。

  • Microphone（麦克风）：选择听写用哪个输入设备。默认是系统麦克风。不想用系统默认时，选一个具体设备（例如耳机）。所选麦克风拔掉或找不到时，Orca 退回系统默认，并显示一条不打断的提示。

  • Test（测试）：录一段麦克风样本并回放。语音模型就绪时，Orca 也显示它的转写。

  • Stop After Silence（静音后停止）：在 Voice → Input 下，选 30 秒、一分钟、两分钟或 Off。听写停下时，Orca 保留已经捕获的文字。

  • Dictation mode（听写模式）：Toggle（按快捷键开始 / 停止）、Hold（按住快捷键说话）或 Auto（按住说话，或点一下切换）。

  • Dictation language（听写语言）：Whisper、SenseVoice 和 OpenAI 听写模型使用的语言。

  • Speech Model（语音模型）：下载或选择一个本机模型，或在粘贴 API 密钥后使用云端的 OpenAI 模型：

    • Parakeet TDT v3（建议）：欧洲多语，离线。
    • Parakeet TDT v2： 英语，更快。
    • Parakeet Unified EN： 仅英语的流式。在 Voice 设置里下载。
    • Nemotron 3.5： 流式转写，文字实时出现。
    • Zipformer 系列：中英双语流式；中文流式；Zipformer Streaming KO（韩语）。Zipformer Streaming EN 已不再提供。已有的选择会转到 Parakeet v2，你可能需要下载它。
    • Paraformer Bilingual： 中文方言加英语。
    • Parakeet TDT-CTC JA： 日语。
    • SenseVoice： 中文 / 英文 / 日语 / 韩语 / 粤语，自动检测语言。
    • Whisper Tiny： 90 多种语言，准确度较低。
    • GPT Transcribe： 云端，需要在同一页填写 OpenAI 密钥。已保存的 GPT-4o Transcribe 选择会转到 GPT Transcribe。

  • 也可以配置兼容 OpenAI 的转写服务，选择本机模型是否使用 GPU，添加个人或仓库词表，以及去掉填充词。

  • 启用语音后，Orca 会在你第一次听写之前加载本机语音模型。用 Unload Speech Model（卸载语音模型）决定何时释放这块内存。

  • AI cleanup（AI 整理）可选，默认关闭。它可以在插入前修正标点和口误，并在把听写文字、词表和说明发给你选的 Agent 之前先问。整理失败或超时，Orca 插入原来的词。

  • 听写条显示已提交和待定的词，并让你完成或取消。本机听写在你停顿时插入短语。转写失败可以重试这段录音。听写过程中按 Enter 或 Send，会等词出来再发送。也可以用 Toggle 或 Auto 模式，对浏览器里聚焦的字段听写。Hold 模式在页面内部检测不到按键松开。

SSH

  • SSH 工作树、目标、口令、默认身份文件，以及批量管理主机。选中多台主机，一起 Test（测试）或 Remove（移除）。从 OpenSSH 配置列表里移除的主机保持移除，直到你选 Restore removed hosts（恢复已移除的主机）。
  • 高级：代理 / 跳板主机；Reuse SSH connection for faster setup（复用 SSH 连接以加快准备，系统 OpenSSH 多路复用，默认打开）；按主机的 Allow this host's orca CLI to control Orca（允许这台主机的 orca CLI 控制 Orca）。
  • Managed servers（托管运行时）：Orca 在一台空的 SSH 主机上安装并运行自己的服务器，并保持更新。可以查看它的版本和终端，对每一台 Update（更新）、Roll back（回滚）、Recover（恢复），或 Stop…（停止……）/ Forget…（忘记……）。每台 SSH 主机都跑在这份托管运行时上。旧的 Runtime 选择器已经没有了。跑不了的主机会显示原因，并且不会连接。见 SSH 工作树 → 托管运行时。
  • Kerberos 主机：OpenSSH 配置里的 GSSAPIAuthentication 驱动系统 OpenSSH 认证。见 SSH 工作树。

Remote Orca Servers（远程 Orca 服务器）

  • 在一份服务器列表里配对并连接远程 Orca 运行时。用 Add server（添加服务器）粘贴访问链接、装到一台 SSH 主机上，或设置一台 Cloud VM。每一行显示状态，较不常用的操作放在它的 ⋯ 菜单里。
  • 把这台桌面应用宣告成服务器，并创建可撤销的访问链接。
  • 为已配对的手机主机设置这台桌面的 Machine name（机器名）。留空则用检测到的电脑名。见手机端。
  • 为经服务器路由的项目、终端和提供方检查，做高级的默认运行时选择。

Shortcuts（快捷键）

  • 完整键位图，每条绑定都可以改。
  • Toggle Sleeping Workspaces（切换休眠工作区）出厂时未绑定。想要直接的快捷键来切换侧栏的休眠工作树过滤，在这里指定。
  • Toggle Child Workspaces（切换子工作区）出厂时未绑定。在这里指定后，用来显示或隐藏父工作树的嵌套子项，和点它的 N children（N 个子工作区）标记相同。目标是指针下的工作树；没有悬停时是当前工作树。
  • Toggle Workspace Board（切换工作区看板）出厂时未绑定。在这里指定后，一个快捷键打开或关闭 Workspace Board。workspace.openBoard 的已有绑定仍然有效。
  • 关闭全部编辑器标签的默认是 macOS 的 Cmd+Option+W，Windows / Linux 的 Ctrl+Alt+W。
  • 标签导航默认值（新安装）： 跨所有类型的下一个 / 上一个标签是 Cmd+Shift+] / Cmd+Shift+[（Linux / Windows 用 Ctrl）。同一类型的下一个 / 上一个是 Cmd+Option+] / Cmd+Option+[。上一个最近标签是 Ctrl+Tab。已有安装的自定义覆盖留在 ~/.orca/keybindings.json。
  • Add Review Note（添加评审笔记）默认是 Cmd+Shift+A（macOS）/ Ctrl+Shift+A（Windows / Linux），可以改。
  • Send Review Notes to Agent（把评审笔记发给 Agent）出厂时未绑定。在这里指定后，不用鼠标就能打开当前工作树 diff 笔记的发送菜单。
  • Delete workspace（删除工作区）默认是 macOS 的 Cmd+Shift+Backspace，Windows / Linux 的 Ctrl+Shift+Backspace。悬停在侧栏里要删的那个工作区上。Orca 仍会要求确认。

Repository（仓库）

  • 每个仓库的基准引用和钩子。
  • 创建工作树时自动运行的命令。
  • 侧栏用的仓库图标：选一个图标、完整可搜索的 emoji 选择器、上传的图片、网站 favicon 或 GitHub 头像，再选预设或自定义的十六进制徽章颜色。
  • 源代码管理里，对提交说明、PR 详情和分支名的 AI 覆盖。见 AI 动作配方。
  • 工作树共享路径： 从主检出放进每棵新工作树的被 Git 忽略路径。macOS 上能用 APFS 克隆复制就用它，否则用符号链接。和随仓库提交的 orca.yaml 里的 worktree.sharedDirectories，以及 .worktreeinclude 互补。见工作树。

Floating Workspace（浮动工作区）

  • Enable Floating Workspace（启用浮动工作区）：给终端、浏览器和 Markdown 标签用的全局表面，这些标签不绑在某一棵仓库工作树上。
  • Terminal Directory（终端目录）：新的浮动终端标签的起始目录（~ 是主目录）。
  • Toggle Button Location（切换按钮位置）：浮动工作区的切换按钮出现在哪里。键盘快捷键与按钮放在哪里无关。

Plugins（插件）

  • 打开 Settings → Plugins，用状态栏按钮，或在文本字段之外按 Cmd-Shift-X / Ctrl-Shift-X。浏览目录，按类别或安装状态过滤，启用或停用已安装的插件。
  • 插件可以添加主题、图标主题、语法高亮、自定义编辑器和其他能力。也可以添加一个 git 市场来源，并管理已安装的版本。
  • 插件系统默认打开。安装插件（包括经 orca://plugins 链接）会打开一个复核画面。会运行代码的插件，安装前需要你的同意。同意之后，它们对你的电脑有完整访问。只安装你信任的插件。插件 Worker 跑在这台电脑上。SSH 工作区的动作仍经 Orca 路由。

Experimental（实验）

  • 动态页：Slack 风格的工作树动态，用来看 Agent 事件。
  • 紧凑工作树卡片：布局仍在实验中时，藏起侧栏里多余的第二行。
  • Agent 休眠：暂停空闲的后台 Agent，重新打开时自动继续。
  • Agent Dashboard（Agent 看板）：需要你处理 / 进行中 / 已完成（可选空闲），带搜索以及项目 / 工作区 / PR 过滤。可以在窗口内打开，或作为弹出窗口。Show idle agents（显示空闲 Agent）在看板上的 board settings（看板设置）里，不在这里。见 Agent 与会话。
  • Browser extensions（浏览器扩展）：重启后，在本机浏览器配置文件里启用 Chrome 网上应用店扩展。见工作树浏览器 → Chrome 扩展。
  • Cloud VM： 显示仓库自有、按需环境（云沙箱、虚拟机或本机 Docker）的设置控件，以及工作区的 Run on（运行位置）目标。设置指南和配方安装在这个实验开关下面。见 Orca 的几种运行方式。
  • 尚未稳定的功能，行为可能变化。

Settings are grouped into panes. Everything here is searchable with Cmd-, then typing a keyword. Feature pages in these docs link directly to the relevant pane.

General

  • Orca CLI — register the bundled command-line tool for shells and agents.
  • Automatic updates — under General → About, opt in to download, install, and relaunch automatically. It is off by default and follows your check cadence, channel, and delay. See Install → Updates.
  • Updates — check for and install updates. Update Delay waits None (the default), 1, 3, 7, 14, or 30 days after a release is published before offering it. A held update shows when it becomes available; choose Skip Delay Once to check without waiting. With a 14- or 30-day delay, a release may leave the feed before it becomes eligible. Modifier clicks on Check for Updates:
ModifierEffect
Shift+clickInclude the latest RC prerelease
Cmd+click (macOS) / Ctrl+click (Windows/Linux)Latest perf-tagged prerelease
Option+click (macOS only)Pick a validated local macOS build (compatibility-checked). Failures surface as “Could Not Use Local Build” with Choose Another Build.
  • Open in menu — choose apps on the worktree Open in menu. VS Code / Insiders enable Remote SSH open for SSH worktrees; other editors remain local-path only.
  • UI zoom — per-install UI scale.
  • Default new-worktree name — custom prefix or marine creatures.
  • Format on Save — off by default. Enable it and configure a formatter and include patterns for each project. See Monaco editor → Format on save.
  • Default Markdown view — choose how Markdown files open. See Rich markdown editor.
  • Insert Final Newline — optionally add a final newline when saving files.
  • Editor Word Wrap — default wrap for file editors (on by default). Toggle from a file tab's ⋯ menu or Alt+Z. Separate from Diff Word Wrap.
  • Reuse a preview tab when browsing files — on by default. Turn it off to open each single-clicked file in its own tab instead of replacing the current preview tab.
  • Collapse Unchanged Regions — fold long unchanged bands in single-file diffs while keeping a little surrounding context visible. Expand a band when you need the full file; View all changes keeps its own collapsed layout.

Appearance

  • Theme, accent color, density. Search the theme pickers by name. Installed plugins can add app and terminal themes to the theme pickers; an app theme may offer its terminal colors too.
  • Font family for UI; editor font is opt-in (empty = follow terminal font; set a value to override file editors and diffs only).
  • Editor minimap toggle.
  • Status bar toggles, including Resource Manager (CPU/memory/sessions, daemon controls, workspace disk scans; when your projects live on a paired Orca server, it lists that server's processes under the server's name).
  • Usage percentages — show provider limits as % used or % remaining on the status-bar roster. The roster now defaults to a shorter Compact view unless you already chose one; Detailed is still available from the same selector.
  • App Icon — cycle between Classic, Watercolor, and Blue for the icon shown in the Dock and window switcher.
  • Language — primary Interface control. Switch between System (follows the OS), English, 中文（简体）, 繁體中文, 한국어, 日本語, Español, Deutsch, Русский, or Türkçe. Settings search also matches native words for “language” (语言 / 語言 / 언어 / 言語 / Idioma) so you can find Language while the UI is still in English.

Chat

Choose the default Chat or CLI launch surface and per-agent overrides here or under Agents. On first-run onboarding, Chat is preselected; existing launch preferences are preserved. See Chat UI.

  • Behavior — choose whether follow-ups queue, whether sending moves the scroll position, when idle chat agents stop, whether working chats resume after a restart, and whether a usage-limited chat continues automatically after its limit resets. Set permission defaults for new sessions under Agents; an existing chat keeps its own mode.
  • Agents — choose Chat or CLI for each supported agent. Choices apply to starts from this desktop, including CLI, orchestration, and relayed phone launches. If Antigravity’s chat runtime is not installed, its row offers Install; Remove stops its running chats and removes the runtime while keeping chat history.
  • Appearance — choose a chat Font family (with a preview and suggestions from installed fonts), Text size and Code text size in pixels, Width, and Contrast. Turn on Match terminal interface to use your terminal's font and colors for the whole chat, and stay in sync if you change your terminal theme later; the font and size controls are disabled while it is on. Reset chat appearance restores the defaults.
  • Inline visuals — let the agent show charts, diagrams, and mockups inside its replies. Applies to newly started chats.
  • Chat naming — new chats use their own agent and account to generate a name by default. Choose a different naming agent under Advanced; previously saved naming commands and arguments continue to work.

Git

  • Default base ref resolver.
  • Commit signing options.
  • Editor for external git tools.
  • Auto-Rename Branch From Work — rename Orca-generated creature branches after an agent starts work.
  • GitHub API Budget — remaining REST (core), Search, and GraphQL quota from the local gh CLI. Useful when PR checks or Tasks stop refreshing; see Troubleshooting GitHub errors if the numbers look fine but GitHub still rate-limits live calls.

Terminal

  • Font, theme, cursor style, padding.
  • Ghostty import.
  • Warp theme import — bring in your Warp YAML themes with Import themes from Warp (auto-discovers Warp's themes folder per OS) or Import from YAML for any folder of Warp-format theme files.
  • JIS Yen (¥) to Backslash (\) for macOS Japanese keyboards.
  • Default shell for new local terminal tabs. Leave it blank to use the system default; Windows also offers PowerShell, Command Prompt, and WSL when available.
  • Shell arguments for a custom Unix default shell, under Default shell → Advanced. Choose -l (default) for a login shell, or Custom args and enter one argument per line. An empty custom list passes no arguments.
  • Running terminal limit — set a limit per computer under Advanced. The default is half the system terminal limit, up to 4096, or 256 when Orca cannot read the system limit. Changing it does not close running terminals. After updating, open a new terminal to use the new terminal host; older remote servers must update before their limit can be changed.
  • Allow TUI Clipboard Writes (OSC 52) — on by default. Lets Zellij, tmux, Neovim, fzf, Grok (and similar) write the system clipboard over the PTY, including over SSH. Turn off if you prefer the older lockdown.

Quick Commands

  • Saved terminal commands and agent-prompt presets, scoped globally or to a project. Each row has a copy control for the command body.
  • Scope filters for reviewing and editing the command list. The same list syncs to the mobile companion.
  • With remote or multi-host setups, commands are grouped by the Orca host that owns them (Saved on). Host ownership is separate from Global/Project scope and from where the command runs — see Terminal → Quick Commands.

Agents

  • Installed agents — detected CLIs you can enable or disable.
  • Per-agent launch defaults — override an agent's binary path or name, and edit its default Command and Arguments (and environment). Structured native chats use the same saved command and arguments. These launch settings are edited on the workspace's own host, so a remote or SSH workspace uses the settings from the machine that runs it.
  • Enable or disable detected agents so launch menus only show the CLIs you want to use.
  • Agent permissions — choose Manual, Auto, or Yolo for new terminal and structured-chat sessions. Auto lets supported agents (Claude, Claude Agent Teams, and Codex) approve routine actions themselves; when you choose it, use Other agents to set Manual or Yolo for agents that do not support Auto. Existing chats keep their current mode, and agents with custom launch arguments remain unchanged until you replace those arguments with a preset.
  • Folder trust — the first time you start an agent in a new repository, choose whether Orca trusts the repository for agents or lets each agent ask for itself. Repositories you already use keep their previous behavior. You can change the choice in the repository settings. See Codex in Orca and Supported agents.
  • Share Codex history across accounts — on by default. History from Orca-managed accounts also appears in ~/.codex when you switch back to system default. Accounts migrate to a shared conversation store before their next launch, which can delay the first launch; busy accounts migrate later. Turning sharing off leaves shared history in place, and long conversations started while sharing was on need it enabled to resume. Older SSH or WSL hosts need an update before this setting is available there.
  • Run each Codex terminal on its own server — on by default. Keeps Orca's status and tab-closing correct for Codex tabs; turn it off to use Codex's shared server and its agents overview. Applies to new terminals. A companion Warn when a Codex tab shares a server notice appears when a Codex you started yourself shares a server. See Codex in Orca.
  • Account lists for Claude, Codex, OpenCode, Devin, Antigravity, and the GLM Coding Plan. On Linux, a saved credential that could not be encrypted is flagged here with a warning. Each Claude account keeps its own config folder, and adding one offers Copy sign-in link instead when your default browser is already signed in to another Claude email. The Antigravity account row also shows its chat runtime's install status and lets you install or remove it for that host.
  • OpenCode Go session cookie — paste the full opencode.ai Cookie header, including __Host-console_session, to show Go rate limits. An auth cookie alone is insufficient.
  • Startup hooks per agent.
  • Agent status hooks — show working / waiting / done states in Orca. Toggle takes effect without restarting the app (including the Windows WSL hook relay). CLI: orca agent hooks on|off|status.
  • Keep computer awake — On (stay awake continuously), Agent (stay awake while an agent is working), or Off. The same control lives on the desktop status bar as Caffeinate (coffee icon). Hidden on paired web clients.
  • Skill freshness — the Agents pane and skill cards still show full status. Sidebar navigation only badges skills that need action (Update available, Needs attention / review); healthy, loading, and optional not-installed rows stay quiet. Use Update in the dialog to refresh global skills in the background (no terminal). Progress appears on the status bar; closing the dialog does not cancel the run. See Orca skills.

Browser

  • Profiles (see Browser-use profiles).
  • Password manager — enable saved logins, manage or import them; see Password manager.
  • Default Zoom — zoom level applied to newly opened browser tabs (per-tab adjustments via Cmd-wheel are remembered separately).
  • Design Mode defaults.
  • Devtools opt-in.
  • Remote server workspaces — choose This device to render new paired-runtime browser pages on this desktop, or Server (streamed) to render them on the server. Traffic always goes through the remote server, and the choice applies to new pages only. See Per-worktree browser → Remote workspaces.
  • Browse through SSH workspace hosts — send browser traffic and DNS through each workspace's SSH host. Turn it off to browse from this device instead.
  • Link Routing — open http(s) links from the terminal, markdown, and editor in Orca's browser or the system browser. Nested Hold Shift… inverts that default for one click (⇧⌘-click / Shift+Ctrl+click). See Per-worktree browser.
  • Terminal URL clicks — choose whether a plain URL click opens the action menu, opens directly, or requires a modifier; configure middle-click URL behavior separately.
  • Show terminal link actions — on by default. A plain click on a terminal link opens a compact action popover. Turn off to require ⌘-click / Ctrl-click. See Terminal → Link actions.
  • Default Search Engine — used when you type non-URL text in the browser address bar or the new-tab omnibox.

Artifacts

  • Orca account — sign in (same account family as Orca Relay) to publish and manage shared files.
  • Allow publishing public artifact links — off by default. Device-wide gate: when on, you, agents, and the orca CLI on this machine can upload HTML/Markdown up to 10 MiB and mint public view links. Turning it off does not delete existing links.
  • Show Artifacts / sidebar shortcut — open the Artifacts list to search, preview, copy, or delete account-owned links.
  • Ask Before Deleting Artifacts — optional confirmation before breaking a public link.
  • Share from an open local HTML page or Markdown editor with Share as artifact, or via orca artifacts …. See CLI reference → Artifacts.

Integrations

  • GitHub OAuth.
  • Linear API token.
  • Jira — Cloud (email + API token) or self-hosted Server/Data Center (PAT or username/password). See Jira items drawer.
  • Bitbucket Cloud — Connect with Email & API token (default) or an Access token. Orca verifies the credential before saving. ORCA_BITBUCKET_* environment variables take precedence and hide Connect / Disconnect. Saved credentials stay on this machine — on a Remote Orca Server, set the env vars on the server instead. See Hosted reviews.
  • MiniMax — paste a MiniMax session cookie (from platform.minimax.io/console/usage) to enable local usage and rate-limit tracking for the MiniMax CLI. Optional group ID and usage models fields override the defaults picked from the cookie.
  • MCP servers. Repository MCP settings stay with the host you selected them on.

Notifications

  • Choose a paired server in the Settings host picker to view and change the notifications it sends for its agents and terminals, including phone pushes. Older servers must be updated to edit these switches. Local sound and per-machine controls apply to this computer. See Notifications & Inbox.
  • Agent-finished: system, sound, chip.
  • Custom desktop notification sound per category.
  • Machines — choose which machines can show notifications on this computer (this computer, SSH targets, paired Orca Servers). A paired server's switch also covers work reached through it; a newly added machine still notifies. See Notifications & Inbox.
  • PR check failures.
  • Update available.

Voice

  • Set up — when no usable model is selected, Voice suggests an on-device model for your language. Set up selects and downloads it, requests microphone access, and enables dictation. You can choose another model from the list. Existing local model downloads may need an update; start it from Voice settings or the dictation pill before relying on offline dictation.
  • Microphone — pick which input device dictation uses. Defaults to the system mic; choose a specific device (for example a headset) when you do not want the OS default. If the chosen mic is unplugged or missing, Orca falls back to the system default and shows a non-blocking notice.
  • Test — record and play back a microphone sample. When the speech model is ready, Orca also shows its transcript.
  • Stop After Silence — under Voice → Input, choose 30 seconds, one minute, two minutes, or Off. Orca keeps the text captured when dictation stops.
  • Dictation mode — Toggle (press shortcut to start/stop), Hold (hold shortcut to talk), or Auto (hold to talk or tap to toggle).
  • Dictation language — choose the language used by Whisper, SenseVoice, and OpenAI dictation models.
  • Speech Model — download/select an on-device model, or use cloud OpenAI models after pasting an API key:
    • Parakeet TDT v3 (recommended) — multilingual European languages, offline.
    • Parakeet TDT v2 — English, faster.
    • Parakeet Unified EN — English-only streaming; download it in Voice settings.
    • Nemotron 3.5 — streaming transcription with live text.
    • Zipformer family — bilingual ZH+EN streaming; streaming ZH; Zipformer Streaming KO (Korean). Zipformer Streaming EN is no longer offered; existing selections move to Parakeet v2, which you may need to download.
    • Paraformer Bilingual — Chinese dialects + English.
    • Parakeet TDT-CTC JA — Japanese.
    • SenseVoice — ZH / EN / JA / KO / Cantonese with auto language detection.
    • Whisper Tiny — 90+ languages, lower accuracy.
    • GPT Transcribe — cloud; needs an OpenAI key under the same pane. Saved GPT-4o Transcribe selections move to GPT Transcribe.
  • You can also configure an OpenAI-compatible transcription service, choose whether to use the GPU for on-device models, add personal or repository vocabulary, and remove filler words.
  • With voice enabled, Orca loads the on-device speech model ahead of your first dictation. Use Unload Speech Model to choose when to free that memory.
  • AI cleanup is optional and off by default. It can fix punctuation and spoken corrections before insertion, and asks before sending dictated text, vocabulary, and instructions to your selected agent. If cleanup fails or times out, Orca inserts the original words.
  • The dictation pill shows committed and pending words and lets you finish or cancel. On-device dictation inserts phrases as you pause. If transcription fails, you can retry the recording. Pressing Enter or Send while dictating waits for the words before sending. You can also dictate into a focused field in the browser using Toggle or Auto mode; Hold mode cannot detect key release inside a page.

SSH

  • SSH worktrees, targets, passphrases, default identity file, and bulk host management. Select several hosts to Test or Remove them together; hosts removed from the OpenSSH-config list stay removed until you choose Restore removed hosts.
  • Advanced: proxy / jump host; Reuse SSH connection for faster setup (system OpenSSH multiplexing; default on); per-host Allow this host's orca CLI to control Orca.
  • Managed servers — Orca installs and runs its own server on an empty SSH host and keeps it up to date; view its version and terminals, Update, Roll back, Recover, or Stop… / Forget… each one. Every SSH host runs on this managed server — the old Runtime selector is gone, and a host it cannot run on shows the reason and does not connect. See SSH worktrees → Managed Orca server.
  • Kerberos hosts: GSSAPIAuthentication from OpenSSH config drives system OpenSSH auth (see SSH worktrees).

Remote Orca Servers

  • Pair and connect to remote Orca runtimes in one server list. Use Add server to paste an access link, install on an SSH host, or set up a Cloud VM; each row shows its status and puts less-common actions in its ⋯ menu.
  • Advertise this desktop app as a server and create revocable access links.
  • Set this desktop's Machine name for paired mobile hosts; leave it blank to use the detected computer name. See Mobile companion.
  • Advanced default runtime selection for server-routed projects, terminals, and provider checks.

Shortcuts

  • Full keymap — every binding remappable.
  • Toggle Sleeping Workspaces ships unbound; assign it here if you want a direct shortcut for the sidebar sleeping-worktree filter.
  • Toggle Child Workspaces ships unbound; assign it here to show or hide a parent worktree's nested children, the same as clicking its N children chip. It targets the worktree under the pointer, or the active worktree when nothing is hovered.
  • Toggle Workspace Board ships unbound; assign it here to open or close the Workspace Board with one shortcut. Existing bindings for workspace.openBoard continue to work.
  • Close all editor tabs defaults to Cmd+Option+W on macOS and Ctrl+Alt+W on Windows / Linux.
  • Tab navigation defaults (new installs): next/previous tab across all types is Cmd+Shift+] / Cmd+Shift+[ (Ctrl on Linux/Windows). Same-type next/previous is Cmd+Option+] / Cmd+Option+[. Previous recent tab is Ctrl+Tab. Existing installs keep customized overrides under ~/.orca/keybindings.json.
  • Add Review Note defaults to Cmd+Shift+A (macOS) / Ctrl+Shift+A (Windows / Linux); remappable.
  • Send Review Notes to Agent ships unbound; assign it here to open the send-notes menu for the active worktree's diff notes without using the mouse.
  • Delete workspace defaults to Cmd+Shift+Backspace on macOS and Ctrl+Shift+Backspace on Windows / Linux. Hover the sidebar workspace you mean to delete; Orca still asks for confirmation.

Repository

  • Per-repo base ref and hooks.
  • Auto-run commands on worktree create.
  • Repo icons for the sidebar: choose an icon, full searchable emoji picker, uploaded image, website favicon, or GitHub avatar, then pick a preset or custom hex badge color.
  • Source Control AI overrides for commit messages, pull request details, and branch names. See AI action recipes.
  • Worktree Shared Paths — gitignored paths to materialize from the primary checkout into each new worktree (APFS clone-copy on macOS when possible, otherwise symlink). Complements repo-checked-in worktree.sharedDirectories in orca.yaml and .worktreeinclude (see Worktrees).

Floating Workspace

  • Enable Floating Workspace — global surface for terminal, browser, and markdown tabs that are not tied to a repo worktree.
  • Terminal Directory — start directory for new floating terminal tabs (~ = home).
  • Toggle Button Location — where the floating-workspace toggle appears; the keyboard shortcut works regardless of button placement.

Plugins

  • Open Settings → Plugins, use the status bar button, or press Cmd-Shift-X / Ctrl-Shift-X outside text fields. Browse the catalog, filter by category or install state, and enable or disable installed plugins.
  • Plugins can add themes, icon themes, syntax highlighting, custom editors, and other capabilities. You can also add a git marketplace source and manage installed versions.
  • The plugin system is on by default. Installing a plugin, including through an orca://plugins link, opens a review screen. Plugins that run code require your consent before installation and, once approved, have full access to your computer. Install only plugins you trust. Plugin workers run on this computer; SSH workspace actions still route through Orca.

Experimental

  • Activity Page — Slack-style worktree feed for agent events.
  • Compact worktree cards — hide redundant second lines in the sidebar while the layout is still experimental.
  • Agent hibernation — pause idle background agents and auto-resume them on reopen.
  • Agent Dashboard — kanban of Needs You / Working / Done agents (optional Idle), with search and project/workspace/PR filters; open in-window or as a pop-out. Show idle agents lives on the dashboard board settings control, not here. See Agents & sessions.
  • Browser extensions — enable Chrome Web Store extensions in local browser profiles after a restart. See Per-worktree browser → Chrome extensions.
  • Cloud VM — show setup controls and workspace Run on targets for repo-owned, on-demand environments (cloud sandboxes, VMs, or local Docker). Setup guide and recipe install live under this experimental toggle. See Ways to run Orca.
  • Features not yet stable — behavior may change.

上一章Work on a remote machine over SSH 下一章Privacy & Telemetry

Privacy & Telemetry

隐私与遥测

Privacy & Telemetry

这一页讲的是打包版 Orca 桌面构建里、可选的产品使用遥测。账号、手机连接、通知和其他在线功能有各自的数据流，写在下面，也写在隐私政策里。

摘要

  • 假名标识。 产品遥测使用随机的安装标识和会话标识。有些使用摘要还用一个假名的对话标识。这些标识能把同一次安装、同一次会话或同一段对话里的事件串起来，所以是假名，不是匿名。账号和其他托管功能保留它们自己的记录。
  • 内容边界。 产品遥测在设计上不包含文件内容、提示词、Agent 回复、终端输出，以及随意填写的本地路径。你通过已开启的托管功能、或你自己配置的 AI 提供方发出去的内容，不在这条说明里。
  • 退出开关。 对受支持的启动，DO_NOT_TRACK=1 或 ORCA_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 会关闭产品遥测。也可以在 Settings → Privacy 里关掉。

产品遥测里有什么

事件包括 Orca 版本、操作系统（例如 darwin、win32 或 linux）、CPU 架构、粗略的系统版本、发布通道（stable 或 rc），以及随机的安装标识和会话标识。遥测事件的结构限制了接受哪些字段，并限制字段的取值。

类别包括：

  • 生命周期和功能使用。 应用启动、引导（包括系统和所选界面语言）、功能交互，以及仓库或工作区是怎么添加或创建的。这些事件描述动作，不描述仓库名、URL、路径或内容。已配对的手机应用打到你桌面主机上的动作（例如发送一条聊天消息）可以记进那台主机的产品遥测。
  • Agent。 Agent 种类、从哪里启动、有界限的活动和用量摘要，以及粗略的错误类别。令牌用量摘要包括提供方、输入 / 输出 / 缓存令牌数、一个假名对话标识和一个修订号。摘要里没有对话正文。
  • 公开插件的使用。 从官方或社区目录安装的插件的标识和版本，以及安装 / 更新和触发信息。私有目录、本地文件夹和开发中的插件不进这条事件。
  • 设置和隐私开关。 一份允许名单里的偏好变化，以及遥测同意的选择。自由填写的设置值不在这些事件里。

桌面端的 PostHog 传输不会把你的账号名或邮箱加进这些事件，并关闭了 SDK 的 GeoIP 充实。PostHog 和网络提供方在事件送出时仍会收到请求信息，例如来源 IP。随机的事件标识去不掉这一层请求处理。

不属于产品遥测的部分

  • 账号和登录在你使用这些功能时处理身份、组织、认证和安全记录。这些记录和产品使用事件是分开的。
  • 手机和 Relay处理配对、设备、主机、路由和连接元数据。已配对运行时的载荷加密，不会藏起全部服务元数据。
  • 推送通知处理设备令牌和投递信息，可以包含通知标题、正文和标识。Apple Push Notification service 或 Firebase Cloud Messaging 会收到投递所需的信息。设备上的通知预览设置改变的是设备上显示什么，不改变那些服务收到什么。
  • 分享、插件和连接器处理你所开启功能需要的上传内容、包文件、集成凭据、消息或事件。公开分享的内容，拿到公开链接的人都可以访问。
  • Cloud Sandboxes处理你选择在那里运行的托管工作区、文件、凭据和 Agent 活动。
  • 支持和诊断处理你提交的材料，里面可以含有项目或个人信息。分享之前先看一遍诊断包、截图和其他附件。
  • 你选的 AI 提供方和 Agent按它们自己的条款和你的配置，收到这次推理所需的上下文。我们不用客户的代码或提示词训练自己的模型。Orca 的遥测设置不会改变另一家提供方的训练或保留设置。

关掉可选的产品遥测，不会关掉单独开启的功能所需要的处理，不会抹掉已经存在的记录，也不会停用你配置的 AI 提供方。网站分析的范围也是分开的，见隐私政策。

怎么退出

下面任一开关，就足以对相应的桌面启动关闭产品遥测。常见的 CI 环境标记，例如 CI，也会关闭它：

  1. 在应用里。 Settings → Privacy，关掉 “Share anonymous usage data”（分享匿名使用数据）。存下的偏好会跨启动保留。在应用里关掉时，可以在停止发送之前再送出一条记录这次退出的事件。

  1. DO_NOT_TRACK=1。 对那一次启动关闭产品遥测。去掉它之后，下次启动恢复你存下的偏好。
  2. ORCA_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1。 覆盖行为和 DO_NOT_TRACK 相同。

产品遥测送到哪里

  • 提供方： PostHog Cloud 的美国接收端点。
  • 保留和删除： 由 PostHog 项目设置和适用的服务安排决定。这一页不写最长保留期。关掉遥测不会抹掉已经送出的事件。
  • 联系： 隐私请求或对本页的问题，写 help@stably.ai 或 neil@stably.ai。

What Orca product telemetry contains, how to opt out, and how it differs from hosted features.

This page describes optional product-usage telemetry in packaged Orca desktop builds. Accounts, mobile connections, notifications and other online features have separate data flows, described below and in our Privacy Policy.

Summary

  • Pseudonymous identifiers. Product telemetry uses random installation and session identifiers. Some usage summaries use a pseudonymous conversation identifier. These identifiers can link events from the same installation, session or conversation, so they are pseudonymous rather than anonymous. Accounts and other hosted features keep their own records.
  • Content boundaries. Product telemetry is designed to exclude file contents, prompts, agent responses, terminal output and free-form local paths. This does not describe content you send through an enabled hosted feature or to an AI provider you configure.
  • Opt-out controls. DO_NOT_TRACK=1 or ORCA_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 disables product telemetry for supported launches. You can also turn it off in Settings → Privacy.

What product telemetry contains

Events include the Orca version, operating system (for example, darwin, win32 or linux), CPU architecture, coarse OS release, release channel (stable or rc), and random installation and session identifiers. The telemetry event schema restricts accepted fields and bounds their values.

The categories include:

  • Lifecycle and feature use — app launches, onboarding (including system and selected interface language), feature interactions, and how repositories or workspaces are added or created. These events describe the action rather than the repository name, URL, path or contents. Actions from a paired mobile app that reach your desktop host, such as sending a chat message, can be recorded in that host's product telemetry.
  • Agents — agent kind, launch surface, bounded activity and usage summaries, and coarse error categories. Token-usage summaries include provider, input/output/cache token counts, a pseudonymous conversation identifier and a revision number; they do not include the conversation text.
  • Public plugin use — identifiers and versions of plugins installed from the official or community catalogs, with installation/update and trigger information. Private catalogs, local folders and development plugins are excluded from this event.
  • Settings and privacy controls — changes to an allowlisted set of preferences and telemetry consent choices. Free-form setting values are not part of these events.

The desktop PostHog transport does not add your account name or email to these events and disables SDK GeoIP enrichment. PostHog and network providers still receive request information, such as the source IP address, when events are sent. A random event identifier does not remove that request-level processing.

What is separate from product telemetry

  • Accounts and sign-ins process identity, organization, authentication and security records when you use those features. These records are separate from product-usage events.
  • Mobile and Relay process pairing, device, host, routing and connection metadata. Paired-runtime payload encryption does not conceal all service metadata.
  • Push notifications process device tokens and delivery information and can include notification titles, bodies and identifiers. Apple Push Notification service or Firebase Cloud Messaging receives information needed for delivery. Your device's notification-preview settings change what is shown on the device, not what those services receive.
  • Sharing, plugins and connectors process the uploaded content, package files, integration credentials, messages or events needed for the features you enable. Publicly shared content can be accessible to anyone with its public link.
  • Cloud Sandboxes process the hosted workspace, files, credentials and agent activity you choose to run there.
  • Support and diagnostics process material you submit, which can contain project or personal information. Review diagnostic bundles, screenshots and other attachments before sharing them.
  • Customer-selected AI providers and agents receive the context needed for your requested inference under their own terms and your configuration. We do not use customer code or prompts to train our own models; Orca telemetry settings do not change another provider's training or retention settings.

Turning off optional product telemetry does not turn off processing needed for separately enabled features, erase historical records or disable your configured AI providers. Website analytics also has a separate scope; see the Privacy Policy.

How to opt out

Any one of these controls is sufficient to disable product telemetry for the applicable desktop launch. Common CI environment markers, such as CI, also disable it:

  1. In the app. Settings → Privacy → turn "Share anonymous usage data" off. The stored preference persists across launches. Turning it off in the app can send one final event recording the opt-out before transmission stops.
  1. DO_NOT_TRACK=1 — disables product telemetry for that launch. Removing it restores your stored preference on the next launch.
  2. ORCA_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 — has the same override behavior as DO_NOT_TRACK.

Where product telemetry goes

  • Provider: PostHog Cloud's United States ingestion endpoint.
  • Retention and deletion: the PostHog project settings and applicable service arrangements determine retention; this page does not state a maximum retention period. Disabling telemetry does not erase events already sent.
  • Requests: contact [email protected] or [email protected] for privacy requests or questions about this page.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/telemetry

上一章Settings reference 下一章Troubleshooting & FAQ

Troubleshooting & FAQ

故障排除与常见问题

Troubleshooting & FAQ

Agent 起不来

  • 在 Orca 外面的普通终端里运行这个 Agent 的 CLI。那里也失败，就查 CLI 的安装和登录。只在 Orca 里失败，看下面的说明。
  • 确认 CLI 在 Orca 看得到的 PATH 上（查看 Settings → Agents）。
  • 试标签上的 Restart（重启）标记。

Codex 报告 path must be shorter than SUN_LEN

把 Orca 更新到 v1.4.212 或更高。Codex 0.157 及以后，在 Orca 管理的账号主目录里，后台守护进程的套接字路径太长时会碰到这个错误。Orca 现在会自动调整受影响的主目录。你不必搬走账号，也不必编辑系统的 ~/.codex/config.toml。见 Orca 里的 Codex。

Orca 问你用哪一份配置副本

配置的 JSON 副本和 SQLite 副本都能读、但内容不一致时，Orca 会显示每一份上次保存的时间。选 Use SQLite (Recommended)（使用 SQLite，建议）或 Use JSON（使用 JSON）来保留那一份。Orca 应用你的选择并重启。需要先查看这些副本时，选 Quit（退出）。其他配置恢复错误仍可能单独给出命令说明。

diff 看起来不对，或卡住了

  • 点 diff 工具栏上的刷新图标。Orca 会重新读取这棵工作树。
  • 外部的 git 操作（rebase、reset）可能落在两次刷新之间。

创建工作树失败

  • 起始引用可能还没 fetch。在仓库里开一个终端，运行 git fetch origin。
  • 目标目录里可能已经有那条分支的工作树。删掉它，或换一个分支名。

Orca CLI 说 “command not found”

在 Settings → General → Orca CLI 里登记 CLI。macOS 上它会把一个垫片装进 ~/.local/bin，确认这个目录在你的 shell 的 PATH 上。Windows 上，orca 是安装程序加进 PATH 的原生程序。

SSH 连上了，远程终端失败

  • Orca 在主机上准备自己的托管运行时，所以错误的 Node 版本或缺少构建工具不再挡住连接。Settings → SSH Hosts 会说明一台主机跑的是哪份服务器。Settings → Managed servers 可以更新、回滚或恢复它。见 SSH 工作树 → 托管运行时。
  • 托管运行时跑不了的主机，不再退回旧的 SSH 运行时。Settings → SSH Hosts 会说明原因，连接就带着这个原因失败。要继续连那台主机，用较旧的 Orca 版本。
  • 从 v1.4.224 带过来的终端仍接受输入和输出，但它们没有 orca CLI。在同一台主机上新开一个终端，再运行 orca 命令。

SSH 能传文件，不能 “Download Folder”

下载文件夹需要递归的 SFTP 传输。只走系统 SSH 的连接上，下载单个文件可能仍然可以。退路是在终端里用 tar / scp。

“Open in VS Code” 不可用，或只有 Local

  • 用一棵 SSH 工作树（不是把远程 Orca 服务器当成当前运行时）。
  • 把 Open-in 命令设成 VS Code / Insiders，不要设成 Cursor，也不要设成带多个参数的 shell 命令。
  • 主机被移除过的话，刷新 SSH 目标。

Kerberos 登录失败

  • 确认 klist 显示了该主机领域的有效票据。
  • 确认 OpenSSH 配置里那个 Host 有 GSSAPIAuthentication yes，然后在 Settings → SSH 里重新导入或重新测试这个目标。

浏览器说 browser_no_tab

当前工作树里没有打开的标签。用 orca tab create --url ... 开一个，或手动打开浏览器窗格再导航。

性能和内存

  • 关掉你没有在用的工作树。每棵工作树都会让文件监视保持活动。
  • 带很多浏览器标签的分屏最吃内存。关掉不需要的浏览器。

GitHub PR 面板、检查和 Tasks 报错

速率限制、错误的 gh 登录、缺少的权限范围，以及仓库访问问题，都会出现在源代码管理和 PR Checks 面板里。完整对照（包括为什么设置里的 GitHub API Budget 看着正常，REST 仍被挡住）见 排查 GitHub 错误。

Orca 说没安装 GitHub CLI，但 gh 在你的终端里能用时，多半是 PATH 上的一个 gh 启动器卡住了。Orca 会越过卡住的启动器，改用通过了版本检查的那一份，而不是把 CLI 报成缺失。

快速检查：

gh auth status -h github.com
gh api user
gh api rate_limit --jq '.resources.core'

日志

Help → Open Logs（帮助 → 打开日志）打开放着 Orca 日志的目录。提交 bug 时附上这些日志。

报告问题

  • Help → Send Feedback（帮助 → 发送反馈，应用内）：把截图粘贴或拖进对话框，或选择图片文件。提交前会显示缩略图。bug 难复现时附上日志。
  • GitHub Issues：bug 和功能请求。
  • Discord：即时帮助。

Agent won't start

  • Run the agent's CLI in a regular terminal outside Orca. If it fails there too, check the CLI installation and login. If it fails only in Orca, check the guidance below.
  • Make sure the CLI is on the PATH Orca sees (check Settings → Agents).
  • Try the Restart chip on the tab.

Codex reports path must be shorter than SUN_LEN

Update Orca to v1.4.212 or later. Codex 0.157 and later can hit this error when the background daemon's socket path is too long in an Orca-managed account home. Orca now adjusts affected homes automatically; you do not need to move the account or edit your system ~/.codex/config.toml. See Codex in Orca.

Orca asks which profile copy to use

If both the JSON and SQLite copies of your profile are readable but disagree, Orca shows when each was last saved. Choose Use SQLite (Recommended) or Use JSON to keep that copy; Orca applies your choice and restarts. Choose Quit if you need to inspect the copies first. Other profile recovery errors can still show separate command guidance.

Diff view looks wrong / stuck

  • Click the refresh icon on the diff toolbar — Orca re-reads the worktree.
  • External git operations (rebase, reset) can land between refreshes.

Worktree creation fails

  • The start-from ref may not be fetched. Open a terminal in the repo and run git fetch origin.
  • The target directory may already have a worktree for that branch — delete it or pick a new branch name.

Orca CLI says "command not found"

Register the CLI under Settings → General → Orca CLI. On macOS it installs a shim into ~/.local/bin; make sure that's on your shell's PATH. On Windows, orca is a native program the installer adds to your PATH.

SSH connects but remote terminals fail

  • Orca sets up its own managed server on the host, so a wrong Node version or missing build tools no longer blocks the connection. Settings → SSH Hosts says which server a host runs, and Settings → Managed servers can update, roll back, or recover it. See SSH worktrees → Managed Orca server.
  • A host the managed server cannot run on no longer falls back to the old SSH runtime: Settings → SSH Hosts explains why, and the connect fails with that reason. Use an older Orca version to keep connecting to that host.
  • Terminals carried over from v1.4.224 keep accepting input and output, but they do not have the orca CLI. Open a new terminal on the same host for orca commands.

SSH works for files but not “Download Folder”

Folder download needs recursive SFTP transfer. File download may still work on system-SSH-only connections. Use a terminal tar/scp as a fallback.

“Open in VS Code” is disabled or Local only

  • Use an SSH worktree (not a Remote Orca Server active runtime).
  • Set the Open-in command to VS Code / Insiders, not Cursor or a multi-arg shell command.
  • Refresh SSH targets if the host was removed.

Kerberos login fails

  • Ensure klist shows a valid ticket for the host realm.
  • Confirm GSSAPIAuthentication yes for that Host in OpenSSH config, then re-import or re-test the target in Settings → SSH.

Browser says browser_no_tab

No tab is open in the current worktree. Open one with orca tab create --url ... or open the browser pane manually and navigate.

Performance & memory

  • Close worktrees you're not actively using. Each worktree keeps file watchers alive.
  • Split layouts with many browser tabs are the biggest RAM users — close browsers you don't need.

GitHub PR panel / checks / Tasks errors

Rate limits, bad gh auth, missing scopes, and repo access issues all show up in Source Control and the PR Checks panel. See Troubleshooting GitHub errors for the full matrix (including why GitHub API Budget in Settings can look fine while REST is still blocked).

If Orca says the GitHub CLI is not installed even though gh works in your terminal, a gh launcher on your PATH is likely hanging. Orca keeps looking past a stuck launcher and uses the copy that passes its version check, instead of reporting the CLI as missing.

Quick checks:

gh auth status -h github.com
gh api user
gh api rate_limit --jq '.resources.core'

Logs

Help → Open Logs opens the directory with Orca's logs. Attach these when filing a bug.

Reporting issues

  • Help → Send Feedback (in-app) — paste or drag screenshots into the dialog, or pick image files; thumbnails show before you submit. Attach logs when the bug is hard to reproduce.
  • GitHub Issues — bugs and feature requests.
  • Discord — real-time help.

英文原文：English source: orca.dev/docs/troubleshooting

上一章Privacy & Telemetry 下一章Troubleshooting GitHub errors

Troubleshooting & FAQ

排查 GitHub 错误

Troubleshooting GitHub errors

Orca 通过你机器上（或远程 Orca 主机上）的 GitHub CLI（gh） 和 GitHub 说话。PR 状态、检查、议题或 Tasks 刷新失败时，原因几乎总是 GitHub 的登录、权限或 API 速率限制。PR 面板本身通常没有坏。

这一页讲最常见的错误，以及怎么处理。

先分拣

你看到的 可能原因 先试这个
“GitHub is rate-limiting requests” / “rate limit exceeded (core)” 你这个用户的 GitHub REST（core）配额用完了 等到重置；停掉额外的 gh、Agent 和 Orca 用量；查看 Settings → Git → GitHub API Budget
“GitHub authentication is unavailable” / gh auth 在提示登录 gh 没登录、令牌过期，或 GITHUB_TOKEN 不对 gh auth status，然后 gh auth login
“GitHub did not allow access” / HTTP 403（不是速率限制） 缺少权限范围，或没有这个仓库的访问权 带上 repo（以及需要的组织 SSO）重新登录；确认你能在浏览器里打开这个 PR
“repository is unavailable” / HTTP 404 远程不对、私有仓库没有权限，或仓库改了名 看 git remote -v，以及浏览器里能不能打开
“GitHub is unreachable” / 超时 网络、代理、VPN，或 GitHub 故障 看 githubstatus.com；关掉 VPN 再试
“GitHub CLI is unavailable” Orca 用的 PATH 里没有 gh 安装 gh，重启 Orca

速率限制（最常见）

GitHub 给每个已登录用户一份共用的每小时配额。这个账号上的每个工具都在用它：Orca、终端里的 gh、会调用 gh 的 Claude / Codex / Grok Agent、CI 脚本、浏览器扩展，以及其他应用。

Orca 关心的配额桶

桶 覆盖什么 已登录时的典型上限
REST (core) 大多数 PR / 议题 / API 调用（gh pr view、检查元数据、许多 REST 端点） 每小时 5,000
GraphQL Project / Tasks，以及一些更丰富的 PR 查询 每小时 5,000 点
Search 由搜索驱动的列表 每分钟 30

主桶用完时，GitHub 返回 HTTP 403，消息类似 API rate limit exceeded。Orca 把它归成速率限制，能留就留下上次已知的 PR 状态，并在短时间里不再新开 gh 调用，免得一次限额变成一串失败。

为什么设置看着正常，PR 面板却被挡住

Settings → Git → GitHub API Budget 读的是 GitHub 专门的 rate_limit 端点。这次探测不计入速率限制，有时仍报告还有剩余配额，而同一用户的真实 REST 调用已经返回 remaining: 0。

两边不一致时，按这个顺序相信：

  1. PR / Checks 面板上的错误（实时请求失败了）
  2. 下面这种真实的 CLI 检查
  3. 设置里的配额数字（有用，但只是探测）

也别把 GitHub API Budget 和 Accounts 下面的 Claude / Codex / Grok 用量混在一起。那是 AI 提供方的限额，不是 GitHub 的 REST 配额。

在终端里确认是速率限制

# Probe (does not consume quota; can look healthier than reality)
gh api rate_limit --jq '.resources | {core, graphql, search}'

# Real REST call — this is what PR refresh depends on
gh api user -i 2>&1 | head -40

如果 gh api user 返回 403，并带有 API rate limit exceeded 和 X-Ratelimit-Remaining: 0，这个账号的 REST 会被挡住，直到 X-Ratelimit-Reset（Unix 纪元秒）。

配额通常是怎么烧完的

  • 同时开着许多 Orca 窗口或 electron-dev 构建（每一个都可能刷新 PR / Tasks）
  • Agent 在自动跑 gh（指派 PR / 议题、轮询检查、大批量 GraphQL）
  • Tasks 很重，或多仓库扇出，同时还在刷新 PR 面板
  • 其他应用用了同一个 GitHub 用户令牌

怎么做

  1. 等到错误里或 X-Ratelimit-Reset 所示的每小时重置。
  2. 减少同时用着 GitHub 的客户端。 退出多余的 Orca 实例，暂停大批量的 gh 自动化。
  3. 被限制时，不要在 PR 面板上反复刷新。Orca 已经在后退。
  4. 脚本里优先把 GraphQL 合成一批。不要在个人账号上对一大组 PR 逐个循环 REST。
  5. 重置之后，PR 刷新仍失败，再查登录（下一节）。

登录问题

Orca 继承那台主机上 gh 正在用的登录。

查看状态

gh auth status -h github.com
gh api user --jq '{login, id}'

正常的输出：已登录，令牌有效，gh api user 返回你的登录名。

常见踩坑

shell 配置里的 GITHUB_TOKEN / GH_TOKEN。 如果导出了它们（例如在 ~/.bash_profile 或 ~/.zshrc 里写 export GITHUB_TOKEN=$(gh auth token)），gh 会优先用它们，而不是系统密钥环。过期或错误的环境令牌会让登录或速率限制的表现很难懂。清掉它们再重新登录：

unset GITHUB_TOKEN GH_TOKEN
gh auth logout -h github.com
gh auth login -h github.com

令牌过期或被撤销。 gh auth status 可能把令牌显示为无效。运行 gh auth login（或 gh auth refresh），然后重启 Orca，让它用上新凭据。

组织的 SAML SSO。 私有组织仓库可能要求给令牌做 SSO 授权。从 GitHub 令牌设置里打开该组织的 SSO 授权链接，然后再试。

远程 / SSH 工作树。 GitHub 登录是按主机的。在笔记本上登录，不会让远程机器上的 gh 也登录。SSH 到那台主机，在那里运行 gh auth login，或在 Orca 里看那份环境的远程服务器 GitHub 配额视图。

权限和仓库错误

现象 含义
HTTP 403，且没有 “rate limit” 令牌缺少权限范围，或你不能看这项资源
HTTP 404 / “could not resolve to a Repository” 仓库不存在、改了名，或这个令牌看不到它
“resource not accessible by integration” 应用或令牌类型不能做这个动作

处理：

  • 确认用和 gh api user 相同的用户登录时，PR / 仓库能在浏览器里打开
  • 用包含 repo 的经典权限范围重新登录（用到那些功能时再加上 read:org / project）
  • GitHub Enterprise 上，确认 gh 登录的是那个主机名（gh auth login --hostname …）

网络和 GitHub 故障

超时、“could not resolve host” 或 “GitHub is unreachable” 是连通性问题：

  • 查看 GitHub Status
  • 试着不用 VPN 或公司代理
  • 在远程主机上，确认到 api.github.com 的出站 HTTPS 是通的

没有 GitHub CLI

Orca 报告 GitHub CLI 不可用时：

  1. 安装 gh
  2. 在普通终端里确认 which gh 能用
  3. 完全退出再打开 Orca（让 PATH 对上）
  4. 在 Windows 上，装到和 Orca 启动的是同一环境（WSL 或原生）

GitHub 失败时 Orca 怎么表现

  • 速率限制或故障： PR 和 Checks 面板优先显示上次已知状态，加上一条短横幅，而不是把界面清掉。
  • 硬性的登录或权限失败： 你会看到明确的空状态或横幅，告诉你去修登录或访问权。
  • 熔断： 主配额桶返回 403 之后，Orca 会短时间拒绝为那个桶（core、search 或 graphql）再新开 gh。应用保持能响应，也不会把这次限额再耗下去。

在 Orca 里查看 GitHub API 配额

打开 Settings → Git，找到 GitHub API Budget：

  • 来自 GitHub 探测的 REST / Search / GraphQL 剩余数量
  • 等限额过去之后再刷新
  • 在远程 Orca 服务器上，用远程的高级配额视图看服务器自己的 gh 身份（本机设置只显示桌面客户端）

还是卡住

  1. 在 Orca 所用的同一台机器、同一个用户下，用 gh pr view 或 gh api user 在终端里复现一次。
  2. 收集日志：Help → Open Logs（帮助 → 打开日志）。
  3. 提交问题时带上归类后的错误文字（不要带密钥）、打过码的 gh auth status 输出，以及终端里的 gh 是否同样失败。
  • GitHub Issues
  • Discord

相关

  • 托管评审、议题与 Actions：依赖 GitHub 的 PR 和 Checks 功能
  • 设置参考：Integrations 和 Git 设置页
  • 用量与速率限制跟踪：AI 提供方限额（Claude / Codex），不是 GitHub API
  • 故障排除与常见问题：Orca 的一般问题

Diagnose rate limits, auth failures, and other GitHub CLI errors in Orca PR checks, Tasks, and Source Control.

Orca talks to GitHub through the GitHub CLI (gh) on your machine (or remote Orca host). When PR status, checks, issues, or Tasks fail to refresh, the cause is almost always GitHub auth, permissions, or API rate limits — not a broken PR panel by itself.

This page covers the errors you’ll see most often and how to fix them.

Quick triage

What you seeLikely causeFirst thing to try
“GitHub is rate-limiting requests” / “rate limit exceeded (core)”GitHub REST (core) quota exhausted for your userWait for reset; stop extra gh / agent / Orca usage; check Settings → Git → GitHub API Budget
“GitHub authentication is unavailable” / gh auth promptsgh not logged in, expired token, or bad GITHUB_TOKENgh auth status, then gh auth login
“GitHub did not allow access” / HTTP 403 (not rate limit)Missing scopes or no access to the repoRe-auth with repo (and needed org SSO); confirm you can open the PR in the browser
“repository is unavailable” / HTTP 404Wrong remote, private repo without access, or renamed repoCheck git remote -v and browser access
“GitHub is unreachable” / timeoutsNetwork, proxy, VPN, or GitHub outageCheck githubstatus.com; retry off VPN
“GitHub CLI is unavailable”gh missing from PATH Orca usesInstall gh and restart Orca

Rate limits (most common)

GitHub gives each authenticated user a shared hourly budget. Every tool on that account shares it: Orca, gh in terminals, Claude/Codex/Grok agents that call gh, CI scripts, browser extensions, and other apps.

Buckets Orca cares about

BucketWhat it coversTypical limit (authenticated)
REST (core)Most PR/issue/API calls (gh pr view, checks metadata, many REST endpoints)5,000 / hour
GraphQLProject/Tasks and some richer PR queries5,000 points / hour
SearchSearch-driven lists30 / minute

When a primary bucket is exhausted, GitHub returns HTTP 403 with a message like API rate limit exceeded. Orca classifies that as rate-limited, keeps the last known PR status when it can, and stops spawning more gh calls for a short window so a single limit doesn’t turn into a storm of failures.

Why Settings can look “OK” while the PR panel is blocked

Settings → Git → GitHub API Budget reads GitHub’s special rate_limit endpoint. That probe is exempt from rate-limit accounting and sometimes still reports remaining quota while real REST calls for the same user already return remaining: 0.

Trust this order when they disagree:

  1. The error on the PR / Checks panel (live request failed)
  2. A real CLI check (below)
  3. The Settings budget numbers (useful, but probe-only)

Also don’t confuse GitHub API Budget with Claude / Codex / Grok usage under Accounts — those are AI provider limits, not GitHub’s REST quota.

Confirm a rate limit from a terminal

# Probe (does not consume quota; can look healthier than reality)
gh api rate_limit --jq '.resources | {core, graphql, search}'

# Real REST call — this is what PR refresh depends on
gh api user -i 2>&1 | head -40

If gh api user returns 403 with API rate limit exceeded and X-Ratelimit-Remaining: 0, the account is blocked for REST until X-Ratelimit-Reset (Unix epoch seconds).

What usually burns the quota

  • Many Orca windows or electron-dev builds open at once (each may refresh PRs / Tasks)
  • Agents automating gh (assign PRs/issues, poll checks, bulk GraphQL)
  • Heavy Tasks / multi-repo fan-out while also refreshing PR panels
  • Other apps using the same GitHub user token

What to do

  1. Wait for the hourly reset shown in the error or in X-Ratelimit-Reset.
  2. Reduce concurrent GitHub clients — quit extra Orca instances and pause bulk gh automations.
  3. Avoid hammering refresh on the PR panel while limited; Orca is already backing off.
  4. Prefer batching GraphQL in scripts; don’t loop REST one-PR-at-a-time across large sets on a personal account.
  5. After reset, if PR refresh still fails, re-check auth (next section).

Authentication problems

Orca inherits whatever gh uses on that host.

Check status

gh auth status -h github.com
gh api user --jq '{login, id}'

Healthy output: logged in, token valid, gh api user returns your login.

Common footguns

GITHUB_TOKEN / GH_TOKEN in your shell profile

If these are exported (for example export GITHUB_TOKEN=$(gh auth token) in ~/.bash_profile or ~/.zshrc), gh prefers them over the keyring. A stale or wrong env token produces confusing auth or rate-limit behavior. Unset them and re-login:

unset GITHUB_TOKEN GH_TOKEN
gh auth logout -h github.com
gh auth login -h github.com

Expired or revoked token

gh auth status may show the token as invalid. Run gh auth login (or gh auth refresh) and restart Orca so it picks up the new credentials.

Org SAML SSO

Private org repos may need SSO authorization for the token. Open the org’s SSO authorize link from the GitHub token settings, then retry.

Remote / SSH worktrees

GitHub auth is per host. Logging in on your laptop does not log in gh on a remote machine. SSH into the host and run gh auth login there, or use Orca’s remote-server GitHub budget view for that environment.

Permission and repository errors

SymptomMeaning
HTTP 403 without “rate limit”Token lacks scope or you’re not allowed to see the resource
HTTP 404 / “could not resolve to a Repository”Repo missing, renamed, or invisible to this token
“resource not accessible by integration”App/token type can’t perform that action

Fixes:

  • Confirm the PR/repo opens in the browser while logged in as the same user as gh api user
  • Re-auth with classic scopes that include repo (and read:org / project if you use those features)
  • For GitHub Enterprise, ensure gh is authenticated to that hostname (gh auth login --hostname …)

Network and GitHub outages

Messages about timeouts, “could not resolve host”, or “GitHub is unreachable” are connectivity issues:

  • Check GitHub Status
  • Try without VPN / corporate proxy
  • On remote hosts, confirm outbound HTTPS to api.github.com works

GitHub CLI missing

If Orca reports the GitHub CLI is unavailable:

  1. Install gh
  2. Confirm which gh works in a normal terminal
  3. Fully quit and reopen Orca (so PATH matches)
  4. On Windows, install for the same environment Orca launches (WSL vs native)

How Orca behaves when GitHub fails

  • Rate limit / outage: PR and Checks panels prefer last known status plus a short banner instead of wiping the UI.
  • Hard auth / permission failures: You’ll get clear empty-state or banner copy telling you to fix login or access.
  • Circuit breaker: After a primary rate-limit 403, Orca briefly refuses new gh spawns for that bucket (core, search, or graphql) so the app stays responsive and doesn’t dig a deeper hole.

Check GitHub API Budget in Orca

Open Settings → Git and find GitHub API Budget:

  • REST / Search / GraphQL remaining counts from GitHub’s probe
  • Refresh after waiting out a limit
  • On remote Orca servers, use the remote advanced budget view for the server-owned gh identity (local Settings only shows the desktop client)

Still stuck?

  1. Reproduce once in a terminal with gh pr view or gh api user from the same machine/user Orca uses.
  2. Collect logs: Help → Open Logs.
  3. File an issue with the classified error text (not secrets), gh auth status redacted output, and whether terminal gh fails the same way.
  • GitHub Issues
  • Discord
